saṃvartāmaṇḍalānte kramapadanihitānandaśaktiḥ subhīmā saṃsṛjyādyaṃ catuṣkamakulakulagataṃ pañcakaṃ cānyaṣaṭkam  |
catvāraḥ pañcako'nyaḥ punarapi caturastattvato maṇḍaledaṃ saṃsṛṣṭaṃ yena tasmai namata guruvaraṃ bhairavaṃ śrīkujeśam  || 1.1 ||

śrīmaddhimavataḥ pṛṣṭhe trikūṭaśikharāntagam  |
santānapuramadhyagamanekākārarūpiṇam  || 1.2 ||

tryasraṃ vai triprakāraṃ tu triśaktitriguṇojjvalam  |
candrasūryakṛtālokaṃ vahnidedīpyavarcasam  || 1.3 ||

trisandhyāveṣṭitaṃ divyaṃ prākāratripathānvitam  |
dvārapālatrayopetaṃ trikapāṭārgalānvitam  || 1.4 ||

anekaratnasandīptamudyānavanamaṇḍitam  |
vasantaguṇasampannaṃ satatānandapūritam  || 1.5 ||

santānabhuvanaṃ divyaṃ divyādivyairniṣevitam  |
tatra taṃ bhuvaneśānaṃ vyaktāvyaktaṃ sanātanam  || 1.6 ||

kāryakāraṇabhāvena kiñcitkālamapekṣayā  |
tiṣṭhate bhairavīśāno maunamādāya niścalam  || 1.7 ||

tatra devagaṇāḥ sarve sakinnaramahoragāḥ  |
kurvanti kalakalārāvaṃ samāgatya samīpataḥ  || 1.8 ||

śrutvā kalakalārāvaṃ ko bhavān kimihāgataḥ  |
himavān tu prasannātmā gacchāmo'nveṣaṇaṃ prati  || 1.9 ||

yāvatsa paśyate tatsthaṃ  śivajñānāvalokanāt |
tāvatpaśyati śrīnāthamāgataṃ tu mamāśrame  || 1.10 ||

gatastūrṇaṃ prayatnena yatrāste bhagavān prabhuḥ  |
sakuṭumbaḥ stutiṃ divyāṃ himavān vākyamabravīt || 1.11 ||

adya me saphalaṃ janma adya me saphalaṃ tapaḥ  |
adya me saphalaṃ sthānaṃ jīvitaṃ saphalaṃ mama  || 1.12 ||

adya dhanyaḥ kṛtārtho'hamadya me saphalā gatiḥ  |
adya me saphalaṃ sarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  || 1.13 ||

yannātha bhavadaṅghribhyāmaṅkitaṃ mastakaṃ mama  |
tena vikhyātakīrtistu bhaviṣyāmi jagattraye  || 1.14 ||

tvatprasādena deveśa sarvajñatvaṃ labhāmyaham  |
kimanena na paryāptaṃ yadāyāta-m-iha prabhuḥ  || 1.15 ||

kiṃ kurmaḥ kā gatirmahyamādeśo dīyatāṃ prabho  |
himavantavilāpo'yaṃ śrutvaivaṃ sakuṭumbinaḥ  || 1.16 ||

uvāca bhagavānnāthaḥ prahasyemāṃ giraṃ śubhām  |
himavanta gire sādhu tuṣṭo'haṃ tava klinnayā  || 1.17 ||

prārthayasva varaṃ kiñciddāsyāmo manasepsitam  |
himavanta mahāprājña tuṣṭo'haṃ paramārthataḥ  || 1.18 ||


himavānuvāca

kiṃ kṛtaṃ me maheśāna svakīyadayayā prabho  |
rucitaṃ kuru deveśa himavānabravīdidam  || 1.19 ||

parvatollapitaṃ śrutvā uvācedaṃ sureśvaraḥ  |
prasannagirayā divyaṃ varaṃ dātuṃ samudyataḥ  || 1.20 ||

prathamaṃ tāvattubhyaṃ hi pañca ślokān paṭhettu yaḥ  |
sannidhānaḥ prayatnena bhaviṣyāmo hyavaśyataḥ  || 1.21 ||

dvitīyaṃ sannidhāno'haṃ bhaviṣyāmi tavādhvare  |
tṛtīyaṃ sarvaśailānāṃ rājatvaṃ cakradhāriṇaḥ  || 1.22 ||

caturthaṃ mama tulyatvaṃ pañcamaṃ mokṣadaṃ nṛṇām  |
evaṃ pañca varāstubhyaṃ himavanta punarvada  || 1.23 ||


himavānuvāca

kimanyena mahādeva ātmatulyastvayā kila  |
kṛto'haṃ tatkimanyena kiṃ tu devābhayaṃ dada  || 1.24 ||

evaṃ brūtha punaḥ kiñcidyatte manasi rocate  |
tadarpayāmyahaṃ sarvaṃ pūrvamevoditaṃ mayā  || 1.25 ||


himavānuvāca

āśrame sati sarvatra prāticāraṃ vinā na hi  |
tatra ḍikkarikā mahyaṃ kariṣyatyupalepanam  || 1.26 ||

sā ca dharmapravṛttā ca yena tatkriyatāṃ prabho  |
iṣṭā sā mama deveśa kālikā ca kumārikā  || 1.27 ||

evaṃ babhūva tasmādvai tatrasthā guṇaśālinī  |
prasādayati deveśaṃ vinayādyairanekadhā  || 1.28 ||

vinayenopasaṅgamya stutistotrairanekadhā  |
kālena bahunā kālīmuvācedaṃ kuleśvaraḥ  || 1.29 ||

tuṣṭo'haṃ kālike tubhyaṃ brūhi kiñcinmanepsitam  |
yattvayā dhāritaṃ citte tatprārthaya hyaśaṅkitā  || 1.30 ||
labdh[v]ā praṇayasadbhāvaṃ tyaktalajjā manotsukā  |
vadate nātha nāthastvaṃ bhavāsmākaṃ surārcitaḥ  || 1.31 ||

evaṃ śrutvā maheśāno vākyamānandasambhavam  |
tataḥ sampāditaṃ śīghramājñānandaguṇojjvalam  || 1.32 ||

ājñāsanasamārūḍhaṃ preritānantaśambhunā  |
darśitaṃ nikhilaṃ sarvaṃ pūrvasantānagocaram  || 1.33 ||

tataḥ prabuddhabhāvātmā vadatyevaṃ kuleśvarī  |
darśitaṃ nikhilaṃ mahyaṃ kimāścaryaṃ kujeśvara  || 1.34 ||

viditaṃ nātha me sarvaṃ kriyākāraṇagocaram  |
yasmātsampadyate hyevaṃ tadācakṣva kujeśvara  || 1.35 ||

ājñāto guṇamaiśvaryaṃ sañjātaṃ parameśvara  |
asya tantrārthasadbhāvaṃ brūhi me paramārthataḥ  || 1.36 ||

dṛṣṭaṃ samastaparyantaṃ bhavadājñāṣaḍadhvaram  |
brūhi nirdeśataḥ sarvaṃ yadi tuṣṭaḥ kujeśvara  || 1.37 ||

ājñāto guṇasadbhāvaṃ brūhi deva guṇodayam  |
yathā drakṣyāpitaṃ sarvamājñādvāreṇa me'khilam  || 1.38 ||

pūrvavṛttāntasadbhāvaṃ pūrvapāṭhaśrutaṃ ca yat |
pūrvakalpārthanirdeśamājñāto jñāpitaṃ tvayā  || 1.39 ||

pūrvasandarśitaṃ deva ājñāguṇamahodayam  |
tadbhraṃśādbhraṃśamutpannamato'rthaṃ kathaya sphuṭam  || 1.40 ||

kalpe kalpe tvayā deva saṃhitārthamanekadhā  |
mantratantrakriyāyogāḥ kathitā nāvadhāritāḥ  || 1.41 ||

idānīṃ saṃsphuṭaṃ sarvamājñāguṇamahodayam  |
yasmātsañjāyate sarvaṃ tatprabhāvaṃ vada prabho  || 1.42 ||

ānandaścāvaliścaiva prabhuryogī tathaiva ca  |
atītaścaiva pādaśca ṣaṭprakārāḥ kathaṃ sthitāḥ  || 1.43 ||

śrībhairava uvāca

sādhu sādhu mahābhāge mahānandavidhāyini  |
pṛcchitaṃ yattvayā vākyamatyadbhutamanāmayam  || 1.44 ||

gopitaṃ sarvarudrāṇāṃ vīrāṇāṃ bhairaveṣu ca  |
siddhakramaṃ nirācāraṃ tathāpi kathayāmi te  || 1.45 ||

siddhamārgakramāyātaṃ siddhapaṅktivyavasthitaṃ  |
gopitaṃ sarvamārgeṣu tavādya prakaṭīkṛtam  || 1.46 ||

pūrvasañcodito devi tvayāhaṃ tvaṃ mayā punaḥ  |
atra kalpe mayā tubhyaṃ tvaṃ punarmama dāsyasi  || 1.47 ||

ārādhayantaṃ deveśaṃ na jānāti tapotkaṭā  |
tataḥ stavena divyena devenānandabhṛdgirā  |
divyastotraṃ samārabdhamaśeṣārthaprabodhakam  || 1.70 ||


āfter70 f āBEFṅcontinue with:

evaṃ samyagvidhānena rudraśaktiḥ svayambhunā  |
nirmitā svāṅgajairvarṇairnādiphāntasvarūpiṇī  || 1.71 ||

sarvākṣaramayī devī sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitā  |
utpannā sumahātejā bhairavābhimukhe sthitā  || 1.72 ||

vadate mālinī kastvaṃ devo'haṃ kimupāgataḥ  |
māṃ tvaṃ kathaṃ na jānāsi devi tvaṃ kena nirmitā  || 1.73 ||

sṛṣṭikrīḍāvatārārthe mayā utpāditā priye  |
tvamevotpāditaḥ kena brūhi vākyaṃ tu bhairava  || 1.74 ||

varṇarāśirahaṃ bhadre svayambhūrjagataḥ patiḥ  |
mamāṅgasambhavairbījaistvamevotpāditā mayā  || 1.75 ||

vīrāvalīti tena tvaṃ rudraśaktiḥ praśasyase  |
vadate mālinī kruddhā tvatsvakīyaiḥ śarīrajaiḥ  || 1.76 ||

varṇairutpāditāhaṃ te gṛhṇa varṇān svakāniha  |
prasārya varṇamālāṃ tu tattvākāraṃ svarūpiṇam  || 1.77 ||

pūrvabījatanurbhūtvā prasuptāmṛtakuṇḍalī  |
kutaḥ sarve gatā varṇā bhrāntacittaḥ sureśvaraḥ  || 1.78 ||

paraṃ vismayamāpannaḥ kṣaṇamekaṃ vitarkitaḥ  |
lolībhūtāstu te sarve jīvatattve layaṃ gatāḥ  || 1.79 ||

aho devyāḥ prabhāvastu iti cintā jagatpateḥ  |
stunoti vividhaiḥ stotrairdevo bhuvanamālinīm  || 1.80 ||

kāvarṇā kāmarūpe pureva purigatā jālapīṭhe jikā yā ṣaḍbhinnā madhyapīṭhe tripathapadagatā tvaṃ ca śṛṅgātakārā  |
siddhairyā veṣṭitāṅgī parivṛtacaturaiḥ ṣaṣṭibhiryogivṛndairyuktā hṛtpaṅkajena ḍaralakasahajā pātu māṃ rudraśaktiḥ  || 1.81 ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate candradvīpāvatāro nāma prathamaḥ paṭalaḥ


*************************************************************************


śrībhairava uvāca

jaya tvaṃ mālinī devī nirmale malanāśinī  |
jñānaśaktiḥ prabhurdevī buddhistvaṃ tejavardhanī  || 2.1 ||

jananī sarvabhūtānāṃ saṃsāre'smin vyavasthitā  |
mātā vīrāvalī devī kāruṇyaṃ kuru vatsale  || 2.2 ||

ḍaṇḍaka

(1)                 jayati paramatattvanirvāṇasambhūtitejomayī niḥsṛtā vyaktarūpā
(2)                 parā jñānaśaktistvamicchā kriyā ṛjvirekhā punaḥ suptanāgendravat
(3)                 kuṇḍalākārarūpā prabhurnādaśaktistu saṅgīyase bhāsurā
(4)                 jyotirūpā surūpā śivā jyeṣṭhanāmā ca vāmā ca raudrī manākhyāmbikā
(5)                 bindurūpāvadhūtārdhacandrākṛtistvaṃ trikoṇā o-ma-kāra i-kāra
(6)                 e-kārasaṃyojitaikatvamāpadyase tattvarūpā bhagākāravatsthāyinī
(7)                 āditattvodbhavā yonirūpā ca śrīkaṇṭhasambodhanī rudramātā
(8)                 tathānantaśaktiḥ susūkṣmā trimūrtyāmarīśārghinī bhārabhūtis
(9)                 tithīśātmikā sthāṇubhūtā harākhyā ca jhaṇṭīśabhauktīśa-
(10)                sadyātmikānugraheśārcitā krūrasaṅge mahāsenasambhoginī
(11)                ṣoḍaśāntāmṛtā bindusandohaniṣyandadehaplutāśeṣasamyakparānanda-
(12)                nirvāṇasaukhyaprade bhairavī bhairavodyānakrīḍānuṣakte
(13)                parā mālinī rudramālārcite rudraśaktiḥ khagī siddhayogeśvarī
(14)                siddhamātā vibhuḥ śabdarāśīti yonyārṇavī vāgviśuddhāsi vāgeśvarī
(15)                mātṛkāsiddhamicchā kriyā maṅgalā siddhalakṣmī vibhūtiḥ subhūtir
(16)                gatiḥ śāśvatā khyāti nārāyaṇī raktacaṇḍā karālekṣaṇā bhīmarūpā
(17)                mahocchuṣmayāgapriyā tvaṃ jayantyājitā rudrasammohanī
(18)                tvaṃ navātmānadevasya cotsaṅgayānāśritā
(19)                mantramārgānugairmantribhirvīrapānānuraktaiḥ subhaktaiśca
(20)                sampūjyase devi pañcāmṛtairdivyapānotsavairekajanmadvijanma-
(21)                trijanmacatuḥpañcaṣaṭsaptajanmodbhavaistaiśca nāraiḥ
(22)                śubhaiḥ phalguṣaistarpyase madyamāṃsapriye
(23)                mantravidyāvratodbhāṣibhirmuṇḍakaṅkālakāpālibhir
(24)                divyacaryānurūḍhairnamaskāra oṃkārasvāhāsvadhākāravauṣaḍvaṣaṭ-
(25)                kāraphaṭkārahūṃkārajātībhiretaiśca mantrākṣaroccāribhir
(26)                vāmahastasthitaiścākṣasūtrāvalījāpibhiḥ sādhakaiḥ putrakair
(27)                mātṛbhirmaṇḍale dīkṣitairyogibhiryoginīvṛndamelāpakai
(28)                rudrakrīḍālasaiḥ pūjyase yogināṃ yogasiddhiprade devi tvaṃ
(29)                padmapattropamairlocanaiḥ snehapūrṇaistu yaṃ paśyase
(30)                tasya divyāntarīkṣasthitā saptapātālasatkhecarī siddhiravyāhatā
(31)                vartate. bhaktito yaḥ paṭheddaṇḍakamekakālaṃ dvikālaṃ trikālaṃ
(32)                śuciḥ saṃsmaredyaḥ sadā mānavaḥ so'pi śastrāgnicaurārṇave
(33)                parvatāgre'pi saṃrakṣase devi putrānurāgānmahālakṣmi ye
(34)                hemacaurānyadārānuṣaktāśca brahmaghnagoghnā mahādoṣaduṣṭā
(35)                vimuñcanti saṃsmṛtya devi tvadīyaṃ mukhaṃ pūrṇacandrānukāraṃ
(36)                sphuraddi vyamāṇikyasatkuṇḍalodghṛṣṭagaṇḍasthalaṃ
(37)                ye'pi baddhā dṛḍhairbandhanairnāgapāśairbhujābaddha-
(38)                pādārgalaiste'pi tvannāmasaṅkīrtanāddevi muñcanti
(39)                ghorairmahāvyādhibhiḥ saṃsmṛtya pādāravindadvayaṃ te
(40)                mahākāli kālāgnitejaḥprabhe skandagovindabrahmendracandrārka-
(41)                puṣpāyudhairmaulimālālisatpadmakiñjalkasatpiñjaraiḥ sevyase
(42)                sarvavīrāmbike bhairavī bhairavaste śaraṇyāgato'haṃ
(43)                kṣamasvāparādhaṃ kṣamasvāparādhaṃ śive
evaṃ stutā mahādevī bhairaveṇa mahātmanā  |
tato liṅgaṃ vinirbhidya nirgatā parameśvarī  || 2.3 ||

nīlāñjanasamaprakhyā kubjarūpā vṛkodarā  |
īṣatkarālavadanā barbarordhvaśiroruhā  || 2.4 ||

surūpā ca virūpā ca anekākārarūpiṇī  |
vāmaprasāritakarā vāmadevī-m-uvāca ha  || 2.5 ||

ājñānandasamāviṣṭā stutyānandākulīkṛtā  |
na vedmi ko'tra māṃ stauti kāhaṃ kasya varapradā  || 2.6 ||

uvācaivaṃ mahāsattvā dṛṣṭipāto madīyakaḥ  |
āśīviṣeva duṣprekṣyaḥ sa kathaṃ dhāritastvayā  || 2.7 ||

prārthayasva tadā kiñcidyatte manasi rocate  || 2.8 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

prasādāya mahādevi dadājñānugrahaṃ mama  |
tapasā tava cogreṇa mama hāniḥ kujāmbike  || 2.9 ||

sañjātā tena me devi pūrvamuktamidaṃ mayā  |
evaṃ śrutvā mahādevī salajjā gadgadekṣanā  || 2.10 ||

kiṃ te siddhaṃ mahādeva yena lajjāpitā vayam  || 2.11 ||

śrībhairava uvāca

pūrvamuktaṃ mayā tubhyamājñāsamayagocare  |
mattulyānugṛhītvā tu paścādbhava gaṇāmbikā  || 2.12 ||

kasyedaṃ siddhasantānaṃ pāramparyakramāgatam  |
matsakāśātpunastubhyaṃ tvatsakāśātpunarmama  || 2.13 ||

evaṃ tadbhairavaṃ vākyaṃ śrutvā devī parāṅmukhī  |
sañjātā kubjikārūpā lajjāto rabhasoditā  || 2.14 ||
kiṃ tu lajjāyase devi pūrvamājñā mayā tava  |
idānīṃ dada me śīghraṃ mā śaṅkā mā vilambaya  || 2.15 ||


śrīkubjikā uvāca

aprabuddhapramattena yadā tadrabhasoditam  |
tatkiṃ nigrahabuddhyā vā yuktaṃ tvedaṃ kujeśvara  || 2.16 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

sarvānugrahake devi kiṃ na budhyasi cātmani  |
na mayā rahitaṃ kiñcinna tvayā rahitaṃ kvacit || 2.17 ||

anyonyaguṇayogena kāryakāraṇayogataḥ  |
tvaṃ gururmama deveśi ahaṃ te na vicāraṇāt || 2.18 ||

rudrabhairavavīrāṇāmeṣā cājñā na kasyacit |
yadi śiṣyaṃ na manyetha mitratvena tadā dada  || 2.19 ||

evaṃ brūte tadā devyā sarvametadbhaviṣyati  |
paścimedaṃ kṛtaṃ deva pūrvabhāgavivarjitam  || 2.20 ||

candradvīpaṃ manoramyaṃ deva tyaktuṃ na me manaḥ  |
paścimaṃ sarvamārgāṇāṃ tvaṃ tāvadanuśīlaya  || 2.21 ||

paścimāmnāyamārgo'yaṃ siddhānāmakhilaṃ dada  |
gacchāmyahaṃ punastatra bhārate kulaparvatam  || 2.22 ||

anādiyugaparyantaṃ kīrtayāmāsa tadvidām  |
śrīparvataṃ kumārākhyaṃ chāyāchatravibhūṣitam  || 2.23 ||

evamuktvā gatā tūrṇaṃ śrīmatkaumāraparvatam  |
tatra chāyātmikā devī avyaktā vyaktarūpiṇī  || 2.24 ||

kṣapitvā kālaparyāyaṃ yāvadālokayeddiśām  |
uttarāṃ tāvattatsarvaṃ liṅgapūrṇaṃ mahāvanam  || 2.25 ||
aśītiyojanāyāmaṃ samantātparimaṇḍalam  |
caturdvārasamopetaṃ tīrthakoṭibhirāvṛtam  || 2.26 ||

anekasiddhasaṃchannaṃ manoramyamanopamam  |
tamoguṇagaṇākīrṇamanekāścaryasaṃkulam  || 2.27 ||

devyādṛṣṭinipātena akasmācchrīrupasthitā  |
tena śrīśailamuddiṣṭaṃ devyānāmapratiṣṭhitam  || 2.28 ||

aṅguṣṭhena kṛtā rekhā svasthānasya ca tasya vai  |
tatra jātā nadī divyā sāsīmā ubhayorapi  || 2.29 ||

tacchāyāṃ niścalāṃ kṛtvā ājñāṃ dattvā tu śāmbhavīm  |
atra yo viśate kaścitsa me tulyo bhaviṣyati  || 2.30 ||

hartā kartā svatantro'sau bhraṣṭajñānaprakāśakaḥ  |
ājñāto guṇamaiśvaryaṃ trailokye sacarācare  || 2.31 ||

evamākṣepayitvā tu gatā trikūṭaparvatam  |
tatra kālaṃ kṣapitvā tu kiṣkindhākhyamanugrahet || 2.32 ||

tasya cājñāvibhūtiṃ tu dattvānugṛhya rākṣasān  |
yena tiṣṭhāmyahaṃ tīre samudrasya tvaśaṅkitā  || 2.33 ||

tatra kanyākumārī tvaṃ gatvā kālasya paryayam  |
samudramanugṛhītvā daradaṇḍīṃ gatā punaḥ  || 2.34 ||

tatra chāyādharī devī avyaktaguṇacetasā  |
lokānugrahahetvarthaṃ tatrājñāṃ mocayetpunaḥ  || 2.35 ||

pūrvasthāne tu yā vācā sā tvatraiva bhaviṣyati  |
evamuktvā gatā dūraṃ paścimaṃ himagahvaram  || 2.36 ||

yatra olambikā nāma tiṣṭhate vanapallikā  |
raktāmbaradharā raktā raktasthā ratilālasā  || 2.37 ||

tatrasthā gahvarāntasthā guhāgahanavāsinī  |
yāvatsantiṣṭhate kālaṃ tāvadyogimayaṃ khilam  || 2.38 ||
taistu santoṣitā devī nayopāyairanekadhā  |
tataḥ prasannagambhīrā uvācedaṃ kujeśvarī  || 2.39 ||

anekopāyaracanā vivekaguṇaśālinī  |
oḍḍitā yena aṅghribhyāṃ tenedamoḍḍiyānakam  || 2.40 ||

bhaviṣyati purāvasthamaṣṭakoṭiguṇāśrayam  |
āgatya khecarīcakrāttvamoghājñāprasādataḥ  || 2.41 ||

aṣṭau te mānasāḥ putrā bhaviṣyanti ca ṣaḍguṇāḥ  |
śākinyaṣṭakamātā tvamaṣṭasiṃhāsanādhipāḥ  || 2.42 ||

rudrāṇī rudraśākī ca gomukhī sumukhī tathā  |
vānarī kekarī caiva kālarātrī ca bhaṭṭikā  || 2.43 ||

vāmano harṣaṇaścaiva siṃhavaktro mahābalaḥ  |
mahākālaikavīraśca bhairavaśca pracaṇḍakaḥ  || 2.44 ||

caturbhujo gaṇādhyakṣo gajavaktro mahotkaṭaḥ  |
airāvato vināyakṣaḥ ṣaḍete prāticārakāḥ  || 2.45 ||

putrīputrāṣṭakopetā nivṛttisthā niyāmikā  |
anekasṛṣṭikartā ca susampūrṇaguṇojjvalaḥ  || 2.46 ||

kṛte coḍḍamaheśāno mitrānandaḥ patistava  |
aṣṭau putrāḥ kariṣyanti adhikāraṃ paścimānvaye  || 2.47 ||

adhikāraṃ kariṣyanti ṣaṭkulādhipatīśvarāḥ  |
yuge yuge bhaviṣyanti pṛthaksaṃjñākramodayāḥ  || 2.48 ||

evaṃ te sūcitaṃ sarvaṃ kramaughaḥ kulapaddhatiḥ  |
bhaviṣyadraktacāmuṇḍe gamiṣyāmo yathepsitam  || 2.49 ||

evaṃ dattvā varaṃ tebhyaḥ karālaṃ ca samāgatā  |
mahājvālālisandīptaṃ dīptatejānalaprabham  || 2.50 ||

mahājvālāvalīṭopaṃ devyāstejo mahādbhutam  |
dhṛtaṃ yena pratāpo'syāstena tajjālasaṃjñakam  || 2.51 ||
kiñcitkālasya paryāye prabuddhakiraṇojjvalā  |
vicitraracanānekaṃ paśyatyagrendrajālavat || 2.52 ||

kasyaiṣā racanā divyā pūrvamāsīdihādhvare  |
mattejasaḥ pratāpena bhraṣṭā tvaṃ na palāyitā  || 2.53 ||

karālavadane tubhyaṃ māyājālaprasārike  |
jālandharādhipatyatvaṃ bhaviṣyatyacireṇa tu  || 2.54 ||

āgatya khecarīcakrācchrīsiddhakauṇḍalīśvaraḥ  |
aśeṣārthavido nāthaḥ sa te nātho bhaviṣyati  || 2.55 ||

bhaviṣyanti karālinyo daśaiva duhitā tava  |
bhaviṣyantyuttarānandā daśaite guṇavattarāḥ  || 2.56 ||

prāticārāstu ṣaḍbhadre bhaviṣyantyanugocare  |
ājñānandasamekatvaṃ karālīduhitājanam  || 2.57 ||

mālā śivā tathā durgā pāvanī harṣaṇī tathā  |
jayā tu suprabhā caiva prabhā caṇḍā ca rugminī  || 2.58 ||

śakuniḥ sumatirnando gopālaśca pitāmahaḥ  |
pallavo meghanirghoṣaḥ śikhivaktro mahādhvajaḥ  || 2.59 ||

kālakūṭo daśaivaite putrāḥ siṃhāsanādhipāḥ  |
bhaviṣyanti bhave tubhyaṃ meghavarṇādito gaṇāḥ  || 2.60 ||

bṛhatkukṣaikadaṃṣṭraśca gaṇeśo vighnarāṭprabhuḥ  |
mahānandaḥ ṣaḍevaite bhaviṣyanti gaṇeśvarāḥ  || 2.61 ||

uttarānandamīśānāḥ kariṣyanti yuge yuge  |
jñānabhraṃśāvasāne tu saṃjñābhedān punaḥ punaḥ  || 2.62 ||

karālī tava santāne bhaviṣyanti mamājñayā  |
evamuktvā maheśānī gatā sahyaṃ mahāvanam  || 2.63 ||

sampūrṇamaṇḍalārcībhiḥ pūrayantī jagattrayam  |
niḥśeṣaṃ nikhilaṃ viśvaṃ lokālokāntasaṃsthitam  || 2.64 ||
yāvatsantiṣṭhate tatsthā nayopādairanekadhā  |
tāvaccaṇḍākṣī balavatparicaryāmanekadhā  || 2.65 ||

kurvantī vividhopāyaiḥ saukaryaracanān bahūn  |
tejobhābhiḥ pradīpyante caṇḍākṣīguṇapūritāḥ  || 2.66 ||

yasminnadrau sthitā devī dedīpyārcirghanojjvalā  |
tatpradeśaṃ sthiraṃ jātamanyaddagdhaṃ carācaram  || 2.67 ||

āpūritamidaṃ sarvamanekaracanādibhiḥ  |
paśyate parvataṃ mātā kālānte muditekṣaṇā  || 2.68 ||

tāvaccaṇḍākṣiṇītyagre paśyatyamitatejasā  |
viśvāmṛtaiḥ pūrayantī divyaughaguṇalālasā  || 2.69 ||

uvācedaṃ mahādevī sādhu pūrṇamanorathe  |
yenedaṃ pūritaṃ sthānaṃ tena tvaṃ pūrṇarūpiṇī  || 2.70 ||

bhaviṣyatyādhipatyatvaṃ parvato'yaṃ tavodbhavaḥ  |
viṣuvena tu yogena yenedaṃ saṃskṛtaṃ tvayā  || 2.71 ||

tena pīṭheśvarī tvaṃ vai bhaviṣyasi yuge yuge  |
tejaskandhāsanaṃ tubhyaṃ dvāparāntādhikāriṇī  || 2.72 ||

bhaviṣyati bhave'vaśyaṃ cakrānandaḥ patistava  |
sampūrṇamaṇḍalākāro granthādhāraḥ kuleśvaraḥ  || 2.73 ||

dvādaśaiva bhave tubhyaṃ bhaviṣyanti kumārikāḥ  |
tābhyastvekaikakoṭiśca ādhipatyādhikārikāḥ  || 2.74 ||

bhaviṣyanti tathā putrāḥ prāticārāstadardhataḥ  |
āgantuṃ khecarīcakrātpreritāstu mamājñayā  || 2.75 ||

yena te nāmato brūmi yathā te'haṃ prasāditā  |
haṃsāvalī sutārā ca harṣā vāṇī sulocanā  || 2.76 ||

mahānandā sunandā ca koṭarākṣī vṛkānanā  |
yaśovatī viśālākṣī sundarī dvādaśī tathā  || 2.77 ||
siṃhāsanādhipatye tāḥ pūrṇādrau kulakanyakāḥ  |
valirnando daśagrīvo hayagrīvo hayastathā  || 2.78 ||

sugrīvo gopatirbhīṣmaḥ śikhaṇḍī khaṇḍalastathā  |
śakraścaṇḍādhipaḥ siddhāḥ sarvānugrahakārakāḥ  || 2.79 ||

haṃsabhedādimārgasya bhaviṣyanti prakāśakāḥ  |
āmodaśca pramodaśca sumukho durmukhastathā  || 2.80 ||

avighno vighnakartā ca tava mārgeṣu rakṣakāḥ  |
etatsarvaṃ yathānyāyaṃ caṇḍākṣī puratastava  || 2.81 ||

bhaviṣyati mamājñāto gacchāmaḥ kāmikaṃ yathā  |
evamuktvā gatā śīghraṃ yatrocchuṣmā nadī śubhā  || 2.82 ||

mahocchuṣmavanāntasthā divyādivyaughavāhinī  |
mahocchuṣmahradaṃ yatra yatra nīlo mahāhradaḥ  || 2.83 ||

tatra sā ramate devī divyājñāguṇaśālinī  |
ubhayostaṭayostasthā ramitvā kālaparyayam  || 2.84 ||

yāvatpaśyati viśvāṅgī tattvāṅgī tāvatpaśyati  |
kāmabhogakṛtāṭopāṃ vasantatilakojjvalām  || 2.85 ||

dravayantīṃ dravantīṃ tāmicchayā bhuvanatrayam  |
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā prahasitā mātā kā tvaṃ kasmādihāgatā  || 2.86 ||

tāṃ dṛṣṭvā mohitā mātā jānantyapi na jānatī  |
viśramya ca muhūrtaikaṃ yāvadālokayetpunaḥ  || 2.87 ||

tāvocchuṣma ihāyātā mamāgre śokavāhinī  |
sādhu kāmini sarvatra yattvayā darśitaṃ mama  || 2.88 ||

kāmānandaphalāvāptistena kāmeśvarī bhava  |
kāruṇyātkāmarūpaṃ tu mamāgre vividhaṃ kṛtam  || 2.89 ||

tenedaṃ kāmarūpaṃ tu mahatpīṭhaṃ tavādhvaram  |
bhaviṣyati kalau prāpte candrānandaḥ patistava  || 2.90 ||
vāyuskandhopaviṣṭo'sau ātmabhedaprakāśakaḥ  |
aśeṣārthavido nāthaḥ sarvajñaḥ parameśvaraḥ  || 2.91 ||

kāmike kāmukastubhyaṃ kāmadevo bhaviṣyati  |
bhaviṣyanti mahānandāstrayodaśa guṇānvitāḥ  || 2.92 ||

yoginyo yogasampannāstava ḍikkarikāḥ śubhāḥ  |
putrāstrayodaśā hyevaṃ saptaite prāticārakāḥ  || 2.93 ||

bhaviṣyanti jagaddīpā jagadānandakārakāḥ  |
prabhā prasūtiḥ śāntābhā bhānuvatyā ca śrībalā  || 2.94 ||

hārī ca hāriṇī caiva śālinī kandukī tathā  |
muktāvalī tathā cānyā gautamī kauśikī tathā  || 2.95 ||

śākodarī ca vikhyātā rājñāḥ siṃhāsanādhipāḥ  |
bhānuranantahetuśca surājaḥ sundarastathā  || 2.96 ||

mahāvaktrārjuno bhīmo droṇako bhasmako'ntakaḥ  |
ketudhvajo viśālākṣaḥ kalyāṇaścaturānanaḥ  || 2.97 ||

eṣo'vatāro vividhaḥ kalau prāpte bhaviṣyati  |
lampaṭo ghaṇṭakarṇaśca sthūladanto gajānanaḥ  || 2.98 ||

bṛhatkukṣiḥ surānandaḥ saptamastu balotkaṭaḥ  |
saptaite viṣamāḥ kruddhāḥ sarvasantānapālakāḥ  || 2.99 ||

pīṭhopapīṭhasandohe kṣetre kṣetre mahābalāḥ  |
sarvasādhāraṇā hyete bhaviṣyanti kalau yuge  || 2.100 ||

anyatkāmāmbike kiñcidbhaṇiṣyāmaḥ kariṣyatha  |
sarvasādhāraṇaṃ tacca caturṇāṃ tu vijānatha  || 2.101 ||

bhaviṣyati kalācakraṃ maccharīrasamudbhavam  |
parāparavibhāgajñaṃ mātaṅgakulasambhavam  || 2.102 ||

nīlasyottarabhāge tu mahocchuṣmavanāntagam  |
parāparaṃ tu tenedaṃ pañcamaṃ pīṭhanāyakam  || 2.103 ||
mātaṅginīkulāntasthamādyaṃ caivātha pañcamam  |
tena jātaṃ jagatsarvaṃ tatsañjātaṃ kulākulam  || 2.104 ||

maccharīrāṅgasambhūtaṃ bhaviṣyanti tavādhvare  |
kāryadṛṣṭau praśastaṃ tu apraśastamitare jane  || 2.105 ||

madhyadeśasthitaṃ tacca matsamīpe vyavasthitam  |
siddhapālakasaṃyuktaṃ bhaviṣyatyavatārakam  || 2.106 ||

nirācāraṃ jagatsarvaṃ nirācāravivarjitam  |
nirācāreṇa yogena kariṣyanti nirākulam  || 2.107 ||

hārikā hāri gāndhārī vīrā caiva nakhī tathā  |
jvālinī sumukhī caiva piṅgalī ca sukeśinī  || 2.108 ||

śrīphalaḥ kaṣmalaścaṇḍaścaṇḍālaśceṭakastathā  |
mātaṅgo bāhuko vīro avyakto navamaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 2.109 ||

herambo dhūlisaṃjñastu piśācaḥ kubjavāmanaḥ  |
parāparaṃ tu tatpīṭhaṃ kāmapīṭhordhvamadhyagam  || 2.110 ||

triśrotraṃ pūritaṃ yasmāttriśrotrā tvaṃ tathā bhava  |
nadīrūpāsi māṅgalye bhava tvaṃ kāmarūpiṇī  || 2.111 ||

mātaṅgānāṃ kulotpanne yastvāṃ nityābhivādayet |
teṣu kṣemakarī nityaṃ na manyante kṣayaṅkarī  || 2.112 ||

tvāṃ muktvā yo'nyavarṇastu yo'tra pīṭhe bhaviṣyati  |
tasyāpadakarī nityaṃ bhaviṣyasi kulāmbike  || 2.113 ||

evaṃ tiṣṭha mamānande jagānandakarī ciram  |
bhaviṣyati purāvasthamamoghājñāprasādataḥ  || 2.114 ||

evamuktvā gatā śīghraṃ devīkoṭaṃ kṛtakṣaṇāt |
ālokanena mahatā aṭṭahāso'ṭṭahāsataḥ  || 2.115 ||

kolāgiryāṃ tathojjenī prayāgavaraṇādikam  |
virajekāmrakādyaṃ ca anyaccānyaṃ carācaram  || 2.116 ||
yatra yatra gatā devī yatra yatrāvalokayet |
tatra sandohatīrthaṃ ca upakṣetrāṇyanekadhā  || 2.117 ||

kṛtaṃ tu bhārate varṣe ātmakīrtikumārikā  |
tena kaumārikākhaṇḍaṃ sañjātaṃ puṇyapāvanam  || 2.118 ||

pūrvasantānadevena yaduktaṃ bhārataṃ vraja  |
tadāvasāne kubjeśi ubhābhyāṃ melakaṃ tviha  || 2.119 ||

tatkṛtaṃ sakalaṃ devyā ājñānandāvabodhakam  |
āgatā tu punastatra pūrvarūpānuyāyinī  || 2.120 ||

devo'pi pūrvasantāne śiṣyaḥ suravarārcite  |
śrīmadoḍramaheśānaṃ kṛtvā cājñāṃ punardadet || 2.121 ||

vraja tvaṃ bhārate varṣe itaḥ prabhṛtyanugrahaḥ  |
uḍḍapīṭhe punaḥ sthātuṃ kuru sṛṣṭimanekadhā  || 2.122 ||

evamuktvā punastatra trikūṭaśikharāntagaḥ  |
adṛṣṭavigraheśānaścāntardhānamabhūtkṣaṇāt || 2.123 ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate ājñāparyāyakaumāryādhikāro nāma dvitīyaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************



śrīkubjikā uvāca

tvayā sārdhaṃ mahādeva vivāho jāyate yathā  |
kiṃ nimittaṃ ca kasyārthe tanme nigada bhairava  || 3.1 ||

śrībhairava uvāca

tvameva devi sā bhadre gatāsi pitṛmandiram  |
krauñcasya ca vadhārthāya devaistvārādhitā vayam  || 3.2 ||

sa ca krauñco yathotpannastatsarvaṃ kathayāmi te  |
sthānātsthānaṃ kramantyāśu prasvedaḥ patitaḥ kvacit || 3.3 ||

tatrāsau dānavo jātaḥ krauñcākhyo baladarpitaḥ  |
tena devagaṇāḥ sarve saptalokāntasaṃsthitāḥ  || 3.4 ||

upadrutāstu balinā gatā brahmapuraṃ tu te  |
brahmāpi taiḥ samaṃ devi viṣṇoḥ pārśvamupāgatāḥ  || 3.5 ||

viṣṇunā saha ālocya kiṃ kurvāma upadrutāḥ  |
krauñcāsureṇa balinā tasyopāyaṃ vada prabho  || 3.6 ||

sphoṭanārthaṃ garutmīśa tenāhamidamāgataḥ  |
hariṇāpi punaścoktaṃ vadhituṃ tasya na kṣamaḥ  || 3.7 ||

devadevīsutaṃ muktvā kasmātso'pi tadudbhavaḥ  |
tata evaṃ samālocya kva sthānasthaḥ kujeśvaraḥ  || 3.8 ||

devīdehojjhito deva uvācedaṃ pitāmahaḥ  |
prahasya pālako hyevamuvācedaṃ pitāmahaḥ  || 3.9 ||

idānīṃ kimasau dakṣo nirvapedutthito'nalaḥ  |
tasya kopānalāddagdhaḥ kāryotpanne kutastu saḥ  || 3.10 ||

mahādarpavaśādbhraṣṭā naṣṭā yūyaṃ divaukasaḥ  |
evopalambhitāḥ sarve hariṇā brahmaṇoditāḥ  || 3.11 ||

ūcustvevaṃ punaḥ paścādupāyaḥ ko'sti sāmpratam  |
himavadgirerduhitā tiṣṭhatyekā subhāvitā  || 3.12 ||

jagannāthāṅghriniratā jagannātho hi tatra ca  |
evamuktvā vasantasya kāmasya guṇaśālinaḥ  || 3.13 ||

devaiḥ pracoditau tau dvau devadevyormanoharau  |
evaṃ tai racitaṃ sarvaṃ puṣpapallavakādibhiḥ  || 3.14 ||

kokilārāvajhaṅkāraṃ ṣaṭpadonmattasaṅkulam  |
vasantamuditaṃ dṛṣṭvā prasannagirayā kila  || 3.15 ||

uvācedaṃ tadā kāle kāmo vidhyati bhairavam  || 3.16 ||

madālasānandabhṛtekṣaṇekṣitaḥ prapaśyatāmeva kumārikorum  |
dhṛtvā karotkaṇṭhitayā ca kaṇṭhe āliṅgayantyā ca diśaṃ vilokya  || 3.17 ||

lajjāyamānena sakopanena trailokyasaṃhāramahānalena  |
sandīpito'sau patitaḥ kṣaṇena kāmo hataḥ kāmanirīkṣaṇena  || 3.18 ||

kāmānande dagdhe prītiratī rodanātmike duḥsaham  |*
dṛṣṭvā te rudamāne nānaṅgaḥ patirbhavati mā rudathaḥ  || 3.19 ||*

nigrahītvā tu taṃ kāmaṃ trinetrarūpadhāriṇā  |
nigrahānugrahaścaiva bhairavecchā pravartate  || 3.20 ||

etadantaramāsādya brahmaviṣṇupuraḥsarāḥ  |
sarve devagaṇāḥ prāptā ṛṣisiddhāḥ saguhyakāḥ  || 3.21 ||

stutistotraravairdivyaistoṣayitvā kujeśvaram  |
uvācedaṃ harirbrahmā deva cotkaṇṭhitā vayam  || 3.22 ||

bhavatpādavinirmuktā devadevā hyupadrutāḥ  |
pracaṇḍabalinākrāntāḥ krauñcena parameśvara  || 3.23 ||

prasīda dayayā nātha bruvāmastvabhayaṃ dada  |
devīmudvāhyatāṃ nātha kleśāyāsaprapīḍitām  || 3.24 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

kutra tiṣṭhati kasyaiṣā kā mātā kaḥ pitāmahaḥ  |
ko me dadāti ko yācyaḥ kiṃ kurvāmaḥ kulojjhitāḥ  || 3.25 ||

uktaṃ tu brahmaṇā hyevaṃ yācyo'haṃ yācakā vayam  |
yajñayājī himantākhyo adhvaryuḥ parameśvaraḥ  || 3.26 ||

evamuktvā tu vṛddhena vasiṣṭhapramukhānṛṣīn  |
preṣitā vada śīghredaṃ sampradānakriyāṃ kuru  || 3.27 ||

tairgatai rucitaṃ sarvamādeśaṃ śirasā dhṛtam  |
bahuvittaprabhāreṇa vivāhānandakṛddhyabhūt || 3.28 ||

sarvamaṅgalamāṅgalyamānandānandapūritam  |
tadā prabhṛti sarvedamabhūtpūrṇamanoratham  || 3.29 ||

bhairave mathanāsakte jagadyoniḥ sadoditā  |
trailokyasṛṣṭihetvarthaṃ manthānastho bhavettada  || 3.30 ||

krīḍāvinodairatilālasasthaṃ kulāmṛtānandavidhau pravṛttam  |
kuleśvaraṃ kubjibhṛtānurāgaṃ sampṛcchatedaṃ praṇatā kujeśī  || 3.31 ||

praṇayena tu yogena drāvitāṅgaṃ tvayā mama  |
kubjenaiva tu rūpeṇa pīḍitātīva bhairava  || 3.32 ||


śrībhairava uvāca


vinodakuśale devi anekārthavidhāyinī  |
toṣito'dya tvayā nāthe pṛccha pṛccha sudurlabham  || 3.33 ||

śrīkubjikā uvāca

pūrvakāle tvayā mahyaṃ prasādo yaḥ kṛtaḥ prabho  |
kubjatvaṃ śabdarūpeṇa pūrvaṃ vyāharitaṃ yataḥ  || 3.34 ||

tena kāryeṇa deveśa kālasthānaṃ na me prabho  |
pṛcchāmi praṇayāviṣṭā ajñānaguṇaśālinī  || 3.35 ||

kathaṃ me kubjikā nāma kiṃ khañjī pūrva sūcitā  |
etadācakṣva yatnena sarvopāyasamanvitam  || 3.36 ||

paramārthaṃ yadā deva tadā syātsiddhisādhanam  |
atha cettanmṛṣā vākyaṃ tatkiṃ nāma pratiṣṭhitam  || 3.37 ||

kathayasva prasādena samācāro guruḥ katham  |
sādhanaṃ sarvavastūnāṃ yenaikena prapadyate  || 3.38 ||
mantratantreṇa yogena ājñātaḥ sampravartate  |
tatsarvaṃ helayā nātha ekoccārādvada prabho  || 3.39 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

krīḍānandasvarūpeṇa pṛṣṭo'haṃ klinnacetase  |
tena te kledanāmārgaṃ kathayāmi surārcite  || 3.40 ||

nityānandaprakartāraṃ kalyāṇārthaprabodhakam  |
gurumanveṣayedyatnātsubhagaṃ priyadarśanam  || 3.41 ||

śubhajātisuvṛttisthaṃ śubhadeśasamudbhavam  |
jñānavijñānasampannaṃ samastārthaviśāradam  || 3.42 ||

kālajñaṃ nipuṇaṃ dakṣaṃ sāmarthajñamakutsitam  |
sarvāvayavasampannaṃ vyaṅgadoṣavivarjitam  || 3.43 ||

vedhaghaṭṭanirodhajñaṃ lokamārgaviśāradam  |
kriyākāṇḍarataṃ śāntaṃ subhaktaṃ guruvatsalam  || 3.44 ||

susantuṣṭamalobhiṣṭhaṃ tapasvijanavatsalam  |
pratipannajanānandaṃ śauryavantaṃ dṛḍhavratam  || 3.45 ||

vidyāmabhayadātāraṃ laulyacāpalyavarjitam  |
ācārapālakaṃ dhīraṃ samayeṣu kṛtāspadam  || 3.46 ||

āgataṃ na tyajedvastuṃ yo gatvā na parigrahet |
sa gururna manuṣyānāṃ devānāmapi durlabhaḥ  || 3.47 ||

śaktihīnaṃ guruṃ prāpya śiṣye muktiḥ kutaḥ priye  |
mūlacchinne yathā vṛkṣe kutaḥ puṣpaphalādikam  || 3.48 ||

evaṃvidhaṃ guruṃ prāpya ko na mucyeta bandhanāt |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvabhāvena śiṣyaścārādhayedgurum  || 3.49 ||

ātmanā ca dhanenaiva dāsatvena bhajettu tam  |
tāvadārādhayeddevi prasanno yāvatsa guruḥ  || 3.50 ||
prasanno dadate dīkṣāṃ yayā pāśakṣayo bhavet |
prabodho bhavate tasya gṛhṇāti yadi tatkramāt || 3.51 ||

akramāddadate yastu akramādgṛhṇate tu yaḥ  |
dvāvetau niścitau baddhau pāśaiḥ kulasamudbhavaiḥ  || 3.52 ||

yāvadaṣṭau tathā pañca trīṇyabdāni subhāvitaḥ  |
tāvanna kārayeddīkṣāṃ niṣiddhastu kulānvaye  || 3.53 ||

atha cedgurusāmarthyāddadate dayayā śiśoḥ  |
tathāpi tena kartavyaṃ dāsatvaṃ tu guroḥ kule  || 3.54 ||

ākruṣṭaḥ śatadhā vāpi tāḍitastu sahasradhā  |
evaṃ kṛte na yasyāsti virāgastasya yogyatā  || 3.55 ||

guruṇā roṣito vātha yo dadyāduttaraṃ kvacit |
sa tu naśyati duṣṭātmā ajīrṇe bhojanaṃ yathā  || 3.56 ||

guroḥ kopaṃ na kartavyaṃ vāṅmanaḥkāyakarmabhiḥ  |
tasya kopāddahiṣyanti prāptajñānaṃ marīcayaḥ  || 3.57 ||

martyalokaṃ samāsādya kiñcijjñā guravo yadi  |
tadā jñānasya kā rakṣā jñānacauraṃ haranti tāḥ  || 3.58 ||

kṣamāśīlaṃ guruṃ matvā yadi śiṣyo'pamānayet |
prāptaṃ me jñānasadbhāvaṃ gacchāmaḥ kathanaṃ vinā  || 3.59 ||

tasya rodhādikā devyo mūkatvaṃ janayanti vai  |
na rohati yathā bījaṃ dagdhaṃ tadvadidaṃ priye  || 3.60 ||

ājñāyogaṃ kriyāmantraṃ muṣitvā yaḥ palāyate  |
na ca tena samaṃ yāti tatraivāyāti niścitam  || 3.61 ||

sa kathaṃ tiṣṭhate mūḍho bhuktodgīrṇe vapuryathā  |
nābubhukṣā bubhukṣā vā ghṛṇī kṣīṇatanurbhavet || 3.62 ||

śubhaṃ vā aśubhaṃ vātha kurvāṇaṃ na hasedgurum  |
hasanāddhvaṃsamāyāti hasite hiṃsito hi saḥ  || 3.63 ||
sāmānyapratipattyā vā na vadedguruṇā saha  |
mukhe hastaṃ pradattvā tu dadādeśamiti bruvan  || 3.64 ||

aṅgarakṣā na kartavyā na śāṭhyaṃ guruṇā saha  |
uktānukteṣu kāryeṣu upekṣāṃ naiva kārayet || 3.65 ||

śaṭhastu duṣṭabhāvaśca mṛṣāvādyapravādakaḥ  |
antaraṅgī na sadbhāvī sa naṣṭaḥ kañjinī yathā  || 3.66 ||

dvidhābhāvābhipannasya bhinnabhāva itastataḥ  |
ya evaṃ vartate mūḍhaḥ sa naṣṭaḥ kañjikaṃ yathā  || 3.67 ||

ājñāsphurantamānandaṃ guruṃ tyaktvānyamāśrayet |
sanniruddhastu sarvatra rājyabhraṣṭo yathā nṛpaḥ  || 3.68 ||

śarīraṃ dravyavijñānaṃ vastravāhanabhūṣaṇam  |
gurvarthaṃ dhārayedyastu sa vai saṃskāramarhati  || 3.69 ||

gururmānyo guruḥ pūjyaścārādhyo guravaḥ sadā  |
gurau santoṣite sarvaṃ toṣitaṃ sacarācaram  || 3.70 ||

guroḥ samo naiva hi martyaloke tathā viśeṣeṇa tu cāntarikṣe  |
yastārayedduḥkhamahārṇavaughātkiṃ tasya kartuṃ sa karoti śiṣyaḥ  || 3.71 ||

na mātā na pitā caiva na bhrātā naiva bāndhavāḥ  |
upakāraṃ hi kurvanti kurute yādṛśaṃ guruḥ  || 3.72 ||

evaṃ matvā varārohe duḥkhe duḥkhī sukhe sukhī  |
gurorvairodhikaṃ sthānaṃ pramādādapi na vrajet || 3.73 ||

upaviṣṭasya pārśve tu kartavyaṃ mārjanādikam  |
bhikṣāpātraṃ nivedyeta puṣpadhāṭīṃ vahetsadā  || 3.74 ||

antaraṅgaṃ na kartavyaṃ vāṅmanaḥkāyakarmaṇā  |
yatkiñcidgurave kāryaṃ tatkartavyamaśaṅkitaiḥ  || 3.75 ||

ya evaṃ vartate śiṣyaḥ sukhaduḥkhasamāśrayī  |
tasya siddhirna dūrasthā mokṣaḥ svādhīnatāṃ gataḥ  || 3.76 ||
guruṇāpāditaṃ sarvamupadeśaṃ prapūjayet |
tasmādevaṃ viditvā tu gururdevo na cānyathā  || 3.77 ||

triṣkālaṃ praṇipātena dhyānayogena taṃ yajet |
adṛṣṭavigraheśānamupalabhyeta nānyathā  || 3.78 ||

mantradhyānatapopāyaiścaryāyogairanekadhā  |
na paśyanti paraṃ śambhuṃ yāvannopāsayedgurum  || 3.79 ||

dhyāyanto'pi sadā bhaktyā madrūpaṃ guruṇoditam  |
tathāpi na bhavetsaukhyaṃ śāmbhavaṃ paramārthataḥ  || 3.80 ||

yāvanmūrdhnopari pādā ājñāyuktaḥ subhāvitaḥ  |
tāvanna jāyate śīghramadṛṣṭaguṇalakṣaṇam  || 3.81 ||

gururūpavidhau yadi niścalatā tadupāsati mūrdhni dhṛtāṅghriyugam  |
acireṇa bhavatyupaladbhiguṇā aṇimādiguṇāṣṭakaśambhupadam  || 3.82 ||

ājñāhīne parokṣatvaṃ trayāṇāṃ darśitaṃ mayā  |
rudrabhairavavīrāṇāṃ kathanādyogaḥ pravartate  || 3.83 ||

siddhe siddhaṃ vinirdiṣṭaṃ  pratyakṣaguṇalakṣaṇam  |
ājñātaḥ sampravarteta sā cājñā guravo viduḥ  || 3.84 ||

śāstre śāstre smṛtaṃ jñānaṃ mayānekavidhānataḥ  |
pratyakṣamantranihitā siddhājñā siddhagocare  || 3.85 ||

tenedaṃ siddhasantānaṃ gurudevopalakṣitam  |
yasya cājñānipātena sambodhaḥ śāmbhavo bhavet || 3.86 ||

pratyakṣaṃ guravaṃ tyaktvā jñānarūpaṃ kuleśvaram  |
kathamārādhanānyatra kurute mohitātmanaḥ  || 3.87 ||

nirvāṇāgnau jvaladdīpte yo'nyatrāgnau vrajetkudhīḥ  |
devāgāraṃ guruṃ tyaktvā vrajatyadhobhavaṃ tu saḥ  || 3.88 ||

pūrvakarmaviśuddhasya śaktipātaḥ sunirmalaḥ  |
tīvraśaktinipāto'sya śīghrameva prapadyate  || 3.89 ||
malakāyaprapūrṇasya mandaṃ mandaṃ pravartate  |
abhāgyasyāpi ṣaṇmāsāttīvratvaṃ samprapadyate  || 3.90 ||

yāvanna sarvabhāvena martyalokamupāgatam  |
gurumūrtidharaṃ śambhuṃ tāvatpāto na śāmbhavaḥ  || 3.91 ||

madvīryaḥ pārado yadvatpatitaḥ sphuṭitaḥ kaṇaiḥ  |
tadvacca deśikendrāṇāṃ rūpeṇa prabhramāmyaham  || 3.92 ||

mama vīryaprasūtāste ācāryāḥ sūtakeva hi  |
vindhanti saṃskṛtāḥ santo bhaktyoṣadhisujāraṇāt || 3.93 ||

ahamekaḥ kulālo vai khecarādau guṇojjvalaḥ  |
sṛjāmi nikhilaṃ sarvaṃ gurutve saṃvyavasthitaḥ  || 3.94 ||

sādākhyaḥ khecarāṇāṃ ca piṅgo'haṃ pavanodbhavaḥ  |
tejase'nantarūpo'haṃ'nugrahīśo jalodbhavaḥ  || 3.95 ||

śrīkaṇṭho'haṃ nivṛt[t]yante kulīśo'haṃ kṣitītale  |
'nughrahāmyakhilaṃ sarvameko'pyanekadhā sthitaḥ  || 3.96 ||

yena yena hi bhāvena pṛcchito'haṃ yathā yathā  |
tathā tathā mayā sarvaṃ gurutve sampradarśitam  || 3.97 ||

atraiva siddhasantāne pratyakṣo'haṃ vyavasthitaḥ  |
gurumūrtau sthito nityaṃ yasyājñā sampravartate  || 3.98 ||

suvarṇasya yathākārāḥ saṃjñābhedairanekadhā  |
kaṭakaṅkaṇakeyūraiḥ kaṇṭhīmudrāṅgulīyakaiḥ  || 3.99 ||

tathā te guravo jñeyā mamājñāṅgasamudbhavāḥ  |
rasavadvedhakā jñeyā stokaṃ stokaṃ bahuṃ bahum  || 3.100 ||

palakoṭipalānāṃ ca guñjādevaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  |
evaṃ vibhūtirākhyātā yugarūpānusāriṇī  || 3.101 ||

palena vihito vedhaḥ kiṃ guñjāto na vidhyati  |
saṃskāre sati sarvatra bahustokaṃ na cintayet || 3.102 ||
parāparavibhāgena kālabhāvavaśena ca  |
bahustokaṃ na mantavyaṃ pratyayaścātra kāraṇam  || 3.103 ||

rasaviddhaṃ yathā tāmraṃ na bhūyastāmratāṃ vrajet |
ājñāviddhastathāpyevaṃ na saṃsāramanukramet || 3.104 ||

sā cājñā vidyate yasya mama tulyaḥ kujāmbike  |
palamātraraso bhavyaṃ guñjāmātrarasena kim  || 3.105 ||

palamātraraso hyahaṃ guñjāmātrastatodbhavaḥ  |
evaṃ matvā gurūṇāṃ ca na vikalpo vibhūtaye  || 3.106 ||

ājñāto bhukti muktiśca sarvaṃ sādhayate kṣaṇāt |
vāñchitaṃ labhate sarvaṃ yadi bhaktiḥ suniścalā  || 3.107 ||

ājñā tu dvividhā proktā sādhakānugrahātmikā  |
samarthācārayuktasya tatastāṃ tu pramocayet || 3.108 ||

prāthamikasya yā ājñā; sā viśuddhiprabodhikā  |
adhikāranimittārthaṃ punaścājñāṃ dadedguruḥ  || 3.109 ||

ājñāmātreṇa santuṣṭo anyasyājñāṃ dadāti ca  |
nehatre tu sukhaṃ tasya paratre bādhyate tu saḥ  || 3.110 ||

vācāsiddhiḥ purakṣobhaṃ yāvajjñātaṃ na yoginaḥ  |
tāvanna kārayeddīkṣāmityājñā pārameśvarī  || 3.111 ||

jñātvāmnāyaṃ varārohe divyādivyairniṣevitam  |
catuṣkaṃ pañcakaṃ ṣaṭkaṃ catuṣkaṃ pañcakaṃ catuḥ  || 3.112 ||

ślokadvādaśakaṃ cānyatpañcaratnaṃ satadgraham  |
ṣoḍhānyāsakramaṃ jñātvā etatsarvaṃ vidhānataḥ  || 3.113 ||

sa yogyaḥ kramiko śiṣyo anyathā nāmadhārakaḥ  |
tataḥ prabhṛti siddho'sau pūjyaḥ pūjāpakaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 3.114 ||

etadguṇaviśiṣṭo'yaṃ śiṣyaḥ sarvārthadāyakaḥ  |
mahadanyāyasamprāpto gurustaṃ na tiraskaret || 3.115 ||
evaṃ gurutvamāpnoti siddhāmnāye kujeśvari  |
anyathā jīvikārthaṃ tu ātmānaṃ ca viḍambitaḥ  || 3.116 ||

ājñāyā guṇamaiśvaryaṃ yasya jātaṃ yaśasvini  |
tasmātsampadyate sarvaṃ yadi dattā prasādataḥ  || 3.117 ||

krameṇa vihitā cājñā ājñāmoghakramaṃ viduḥ  |
te jyeṣṭhāḥ kramasantāne yadyeṣāṃ'nukramo na hi  || 3.118 ||

kiṃ tu maṇḍalayogyāste na bhavanti kuleśvarāḥ  |
candanākṣatadīpānāṃ nārhatvaṃ ca bhajanti te  || 3.119 ||

adhikārājñā prathamā prasādājñā dvitīyakā  |
sā yadi kramaśaḥ prāptā sakramānukrameṇa tu  || 3.120 ||

tatra kālaṃ samārabhya gurutvaṃ bhajate tu saḥ  |
sa yatra tiṣṭhate deśe tatra ye'nye tu kanyasāḥ  || 3.121 ||

bhrātṝṇāṃ bhrātṛputrāśca tatputrāśca gururyathā  |
pūjayantyavikalpena siddhimārge vidhirhyayam  || 3.122 ||

ārādhyastiṣṭhate yatra tatra kiñcinna kārayet |
mantratantrakriyāyogamadhikāraṃ prabhutvatā  || 3.123 ||

pañcayojanamātreṇa gatvā karma samārabhet |
tatpure dāsavattiṣṭhedājñāśravaṇatatparaḥ  || 3.124 ||

svapurasthaṃ prayatnena yadārādhyaṃ na paśyati  |
bhuñjate mohitātmānaḥ kilbiṣaṃ bhuñjate tu saḥ  || 3.125 ||

prāyaścittaṃ careddevi kubjikāyāyutadvayam  |
atha ceddarpamūḍhastu jñātvā bhuñjatyaśaṅkitaḥ  || 3.126 ||

lakṣaṃ japtvā bhavecchuddhi[r] gurupūjā tvanantaram  |
samapādena cāruhya guroragre ajānataḥ  || 3.127 ||

kubjikāyutamekaṃ tu śudhyate gurupūjayā  |
gurorāsthānasaṃsthāne cāruhya pādukaiḥ saha  || 3.128 ||
gurudṛṣṭigate pāde japate tasya pūrvavat |
jyeṣṭho bhrātā gurormātā guroḥ sthānārcakāstu ye  || 3.129 ||

trīṇyetāstatsamā jñeyā draṣṭavyā guruvadyathā  |
apamānya yadā hyetānātmasambhāvitaḥ kudhīḥ  || 3.130 ||

prāyaścittī salakṣeṇa śudhyate gurupūjayā  |
upamardya guroḥ sthānaṃ pāpātmā yatra tiṣṭhati  || 3.131 ||

tasya darśanasambhāṣātpātakino bhavanti te  |
yadā sādhuḥ prasannātmā tadā lakṣatrayeṇa vai  || 3.132 ||

maṇḍalānāṃ sahasreṇa gurupūjā tvanantaram  |
pādukopānahau chattraṃ śayyāpaṭṭo'tha bhājanam  || 3.133 ||

pādena saṃspṛśedyastu śire dhṛtvāṣṭakaṃ japet || 3.133* ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate manthānabhedapracāraratisaṅgamo nāma tṛtīyaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************


śrīkubjikā uvāca


tṛptāhaṃ devadeveśa bījamantrairanekadhā  |
cakraiśca vividhākāraiḥ sadyaḥpratyayakārakaiḥ  || 4.1 ||

adyāpi saṃśayo nātha mantrāṇāṃ nirṇayaṃ prati  |
pūrvaṃ ca kathitā mantrāḥ saptakoṭirasaṅkhyayā  || 4.2 ||

sādhanāni punasteṣāṃ  sadyaḥpratyayakārakāḥ  |
kliṣyanti manujātyantaṃ japahomaparāyaṇāḥ  || 4.3 ||

vrataiśca vividhākāraiḥ kaṣṭaiścāndrāyaṇādibhiḥ  |
kimarthaṃ te na sidhyanti japtvā koṭiśatairapi  || 4.4 ||

tvaṃ ca devo vibhuḥ kartā tvayoktaṃ satyamucyate  |
avicāreṇa tadgrāhyaṃ mithyā kiṃ deva bhāṣitam  || 4.5 ||

dhvaṃsitāśca tvayā lokā māyārūpeṇa bhairava  || 4.6 ||

śrībhairava uvāca


sādhu sādhu mahāprājñe vastucodyavikalpini  |
ye mayā kathitā mantrāḥ pūrvaṃ ye kāmasiddhidāḥ  || 4.7 ||

te gopitā mayā devi varṇarūpāḥ prakāśitāḥ  |
tena te na prasidhyanti japtvā koṭiśatairapi  || 4.8 ||

oṃkāreṇa tu te guptā namaskāreṇa bhāmini  |
tena guptena guptāste śeṣā varṇāstu kevalāḥ  || 4.9 ||

ekākṣarā dvyakṣarāśca catuḥpañcanavākṣarāḥ  |
kūṭamantrāśca ye kecitpiṇḍamantrāstathaiva ca  || 4.10 ||

ekāśītipadāścānye sahasrāntāḥ śatārdhikāḥ  |
sarve te niṣphalāḥ proktāḥ kiṃ tu jīvavivarjitāḥ  || 4.11 ||

loke prasiddhamevaṃ hi jīvahīnā mṛtāḥ kila  |
mṛtasya copacāreṇa kiṃ teṣāṃ jīvitaṃ bhavet || 4.12 ||

evaṃ mantrā varārohe akṣarārthe vyavasthitāḥ  |
vratacaryairna sidhyanti satyametadudāhṛtam  || 4.13 ||

sidhyante jīvayuktāstu kimatra pravicāryate  |
anyonyavalitāścaiva bheditā dvādaśasvaraiḥ  || 4.14 ||

rañjitāḥ śaktibījena sidhyante varavarṇini  |
uktāḥ kāmapradāḥ sarve sarve cāmoghaśaktayaḥ  || 4.15 ||

śivavaktrodbhavāḥ sarve mananatrāṇadharmiṇaḥ  |
trāṇaṃ tu rakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ tacca varṇavivarjitam  || 4.16 ||

śuddhasphaṭikasakāśaṃ cāroccāravivarjitam  |
jvalantaṃ svena tejena hṛtpadme saṃvyavasthitam  || 4.17 ||

bhāvayecchūnyamātmānamekībhūtaṃ tayā saha  |
suṣumṇācārayogena udyantaṃ ravibimbavat || 4.18 ||

o-jā-pū-kā-kramān bhittvā vidyākubjapade sthitam  |
tāvatkampatyasau yogī stobhamāyāti tatkṣaṇāt || 4.19 ||

mudrā mantraṃ tathā bhāṣā sarvaṃ jānāti tattvataḥ  |
kubjīśānapadaṃ prāptaṃ suptāvasthā prajāyate  || 4.20 ||

īṣanmātraṃ vijānāti suptāvasthāvyavasthitaḥ  |
brahmarandhragatā cājñā kāṣṭhavattiṣṭhate tadā  || 4.21 ||

yavamātrapramāṇaṃ tu trikoṇākṛtimuttamam  |
vaḍavāmaṇīndriyaṃ yadvanmīlanonmīlanāni ca  || 4.22 ||

tatra madhye gataṃ cetaḥ kāṣṭhāvasthā tu jāyate  |
bherīmṛdaṅgaśabdādyairgītavādyairanekadhā  || 4.23 ||

na śṛṇoti na paśyeta na cānyaṃ manyate prabhum  |
khaḍgacakrādibhiḥ śastraiśchidyamāno na vindati  || 4.24 ||

īṣanmātraṃ vijānāti śaktyāvasthā varānane  |
śaktyantaṃ tu yadā prāptastadā cotpatate kṣaṇāt || 4.25 ||

evaṃ krameṇa deveśi śaktyuccāraṃ samabhyaset |
satatābhyāsayogena vāgīśatvaṃ prajāyate  || 4.26 ||

māsamekaṃ yadābhyastaṃ kāvyakartā na saṃśayaḥ  |
dvibhirmāsairvapuṣmantaḥ kṣuttṛṣṇādyairna bādhyate  || 4.27 ||

vicaredakhilān lokān yāvadābhūtasamplavam  |
akṣarārthena ye mantrāsteṣāmeva vidhiḥ sphuṭam  || 4.28 ||


śrīkubjikā uvāca

akṣarārthopadeśaśca sampradāyaśca kaulikaḥ  |
yathā vijñāyate deva prasādaṃ kuru bhairava  || 4.29 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi mantrāṇāṃ nirṇayaṃ sphuṭam  |
prastārādi-r-anekaiśca ye mantrāścoditāḥ priye  || 4.30 ||

akṣarārthena te jñeyāḥ khaṇḍamantrāḥ śivoditāḥ  |
rañjakena samāyuktā upadeśaḥ surārcite  || 4.31 ||

sampradāyo bhaveddevi so'pi ṣaṭsu prabhedataḥ  |
pallavo yogarodhaśca sampuṭo grathanastathā  || 4.32 ||

vidarbhaśca ṣaḍete hi sampradāyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ  |
mālinī śabdarāśiśca kauliko vidhiruttamaḥ  || 4.33 ||

sā tu jñeyā varārohe bhedāḥ pañcāśa suvrate  |
kulaṃ tu ṣaḍvidhaṃ jñeyaṃ tasya vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam  || 4.34 ||

paraṃ bījaṃ tathā mūlamāgamo vidhireva ca  |
varṇarāśisamāyuktaḥ ṣaḍvidhastu kulakramaḥ  || 4.35 ||

sakalo niṣkalaścaiva tathā sakalaniṣkalaḥ  |
sūkṣmo bhinnakalaścaiva kalātīto varānane  || 4.36 ||

ṣaṭprakāro bhavenmantro jñātavyaḥ siddhimicchatā  |
śuddhadvandvajasaṭkīrṇa upadeśastribhedataḥ  || 4.37 ||


śrīkubjikā uvāca

sūcitā mantramārge tu ye mantrā lakṣaṇānvitāḥ  |
te jñeyāstvatprasādena dhyānadhāraṇayogataḥ  || 4.38 ||

kulamārgagatā deva yathā bhavati tatkatham  |
kathaṃ tu pallavo yoga ādi ṣaṭsu prakārataḥ  || 4.39 ||
kaulikaṃ ṣaḍvidhaṃ kiṃ tu mantrāṇāṃ ṣaḍvidhā gatiḥ  |
trividhaścopadeśaśca etadicchāmi veditum  || 4.40 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

pallavo ādideśe tu yogo madhye vijānataḥ  |
rodhastu ādimadhyānte sampuṭaścādi-r-antagaḥ  || 4.41 ||

grathanaṃ cāntare jñeyamakṣarākṣarayogataḥ  |
vidarbho mantra-m-ādau tu mantrānte vīranāyike  || 4.42 ||

mālāgrathanametaddhi jñātavyaṃ mantravādinā  |
pallavo mantrabodhe tu yogo jñeyastu sarvadā  || 4.43 ||

amalīkaraṇe caiva sandhānasya vidhau priye  |
yogastu kathito hyevaṃ rodhaścaivānukathyate  || 4.44 ||

tīvramantrapadastambhe vākstambhe sainyastambhane  |
hastyādiśakaṭayantre nāvānte ca prakīrtitaḥ  || 4.45 ||

teṣu rodhaḥ praśasyeta paśūnāmutkrameṣu ca  |
sampuṭo mantrarakṣāsu vaśyārthe caiva yojayet || 4.46 ||

amṛtīkaraṇe caiva viṣe sthāvarajaṅgame  |
śāntikādiṣu kāryeṣu sampuṭastu praśayate  || 4.47 ||

grathanaṃ rūpakāryeṣu ākṛṣṭyādiṣu karmasu  |
sandhāne tu varārohe grathanaṃ samudāhṛtam  || 4.48 ||

vidarbhaḥ sarvakāryeṣu uktānukteṣu vastuṣu  |
kartavyaṃ satataṃ devi yadi siddhiṃ samīhate  || 4.49 ||

etaddevi samākhyātaṃ sampradāyavidhiḥ śubhaḥ  |
na mayā kasyacitkhyātaṃ satyaṃ satyaṃ gaṇāmbike  || 4.50 ||

sāmprataṃ kulamārgastu yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu  |
paraṃ binduḥ samākhyāto hṛtpadme suranāyike  || 4.51 ||
grahaṇaṃ tasya copāyastaṃ jñeyaṃ kaulikaṃ param  |
bījaṃ kuṇḍalinī śaktiryā karoti gamāgamam  || 4.52 ||

tasyāntaṃ tu tato jñātvā bījaṃ kaulikamuttamam  |
mūlaṃ śaktiḥ smṛtā kubjī jagataḥ kāraṇātmikā  || 4.53 ||

tasyā jātamaśeṣaṃ tu ābrahmabhuvanāntikam  |
sṛjate yena suśroṇi kāryakāraṇayogataḥ  || 4.54 ||

jñeyā dharmiṇi dharmitvaṃ yathoṣmā kṛṣṇavartmanaḥ  |
etatkaulikamākhyātaṃ mūlasaṃjñā varānane  || 4.55 ||

āgamastatra sūtrārtho vidhistatraiva coditā  |
varṇarāśikramo jñeyo nādiphāntasvarūpataḥ  || 4.56 ||

ādikṣāntaśca deveśi śabdarāśikramo viduḥ  |
etatkaulikamākhyataṃ ṣaṭprakāraṃ varānane  || 4.57 ||

sakalādikrameṇaiva vakṣyamānaṃ nibodhata  |
brahmasthaḥ sakalo mantra aṣṭatriṃśatkalairyutaḥ  || 4.58 ||

pūryaṣṭakasamopeta udbhavastho vijānataḥ  |
kaṇṭhastho niṣkalo devi kalākālavivarjitaḥ  || 4.59 ||

rudrasthānagato bhadre mantro bhāvairdvibhiryutaḥ  |
sthūlajālakalairyukto bindvādīnāṃ ca sambhavaiḥ  || 4.60 ||

sūkṣmādhārasthito hyekaḥ sparśākhyo mantravedakaḥ  |
sakalo niṣkalaścāsau mantro jñeyastu suvrate  || 4.61 ||

vilīno bindudeve tu yonyākārasvarūpataḥ  |
śabdasparśavinirmukto mantro jñeyastu niṣkalaḥ  || 4.62 ||

sūkṣmātsūkṣmataro devi sa ca sūkṣmo nigadyate  |
kālarūpaḥ smṛto bindustaṃ bhittvā vrajate yadi  || 4.63 ||

ūrdhvapade pravṛttasya suṣumṇādhāragocaraḥ  |
pralīnaḥ śabdadeve tu cicchaktipratibodhitaḥ  || 4.64 ||
bhinnakalaḥ smṛto hyevaṃ layātītastu mokṣadaḥ  |
pañcāvasthā samākhyātā mantrāṇāmamitaujasām  || 4.65 ||

yāvadevaṃ na vindeta tāvatsiddhiḥ kuto bhavet |
hṛtkaṇṭhatālujihvauṣṭhau dantanāsodbhavākṣarāḥ  || 4.66 ||

kṣaṇapradhvaṃsino devi yathotpattistathā kṣayaḥ | |
kṛtakā hyacetanā śūnyā anityā jalpakārakāḥ  || 4.67 ||

pañcāvasthāprabhinnastu tadā mantra-mihocyate  |
evaṃ mantragatiṃ jñātvā sidhyante līlayā narāḥ  || 4.68 ||

udbhave śuddhamityukto viśleṣe dvandvajaḥ smṛtaḥ  |
saṅkīrṇe layasaṃsthā hi upadeśastridhā smṛtaḥ  || 4.69 ||

sparśanaṃ cāvalokaṃ ca sambhāṣaṃ cātmadarśanam  |
svayamāveśanaṃ caiva saṅkrāntiḥ pañcalakṣaṇā  || 4.70 ||

sparśanaṃ hṛdisaṃsthaṃ tu ālokaṃ kaṇṭhadeśataḥ  |
tālusthāne tu sambhāṣaṃ darśanaṃ bindumadhyataḥ  || 4.71 ||

svayamāveśanaṃ devi kubjirandhre na saṃśayaḥ  |
sparśane kampanaṃ jñeyamāloke dhunanaṃ bhavet || 4.72 ||

sambhāṣe tu bhavetstobhaḥ śāstrārthaṃ caiva manyate  |
darśanena guṇāvāptiraṇimādiguṇāṣṭakam  || 4.73 ||

svayamāviśane devi utpatennātra saṃśayaḥ  |
evaṃ mantragatiṃ jñātvā sidhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 4.74 ||

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi mantroddhāraṃ varānan  |
sugupte bhūsame śuddhe gomayenopalepite  || 4.75 ||

puṣpaprakaragandhāḍhye gahvaraṃ tu samālikhet |
saptatrayodaśairbhāgaiḥ ṣaḍlopyāḥ ṣaṭkrameṇa tu  || 4.76 ||

yathā caivaikapārśve tu dvitīyamevameva hi  |
ekaṃ trīṇi tathā pañca sapta nava tathaiva ca  || 4.77 ||
ekādaśa tathāpyevaṃ trayodaśāvasānataḥ  |
pañcāśadūnamekena kartavyaṃ hi yathāvidhi  || 4.78 ||

kāmarūpādakārādau likhedevaṃ krameṇa tu  |
svarāḥ sparśā yathāvṛttyā yāvanmadhyamupāgatāḥ  || 4.79 ||

oḍḍiyānagataṃ devi haṃsākhyaṃ tu mahātmanam  |
ka-ṣākhyaṃ mantrarājānaṃ saṃyogena tu jāyate  || 4.80 ||

evaṃ nyāse kṛte devi uddharenmālinīṃ śubhām  |
nādiphāntakrameṇaiva yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu  || 4.81 ||

pa-dha-madhye śikhā jñeyā adhaḥśiravyavasthitā  |
e-pūrvākṣaracatuṣkaṃ śiromālā nigadyate  || 4.82 ||

ai-śa-madhye śiro devyāḥ kārayecchubhalakṣaṇam  |
tṛtīyaṃ nayanaṃ devyā ṅa-cha-madhyagataṃ punaḥ  || 4.83 ||

na-da-madhyagataṃ jñeyaṃ dvidhābhūtaṃ varānane  |
nayanau ca smṛtau devyāḥ kramāddakṣiṇavāmagau  || 4.84 ||

ṭa-pūrve nāsikā jñeyā saṃsṛṣṭā caiva madhyagā  |
ḍha-ta-madhyagataṃ gṛhya dvirabhyāsapaderitam  || 4.85 ||

ṭha-ḍa-pūrvau yutau'dhastādbhūṣaṇau karṇayoḥ smṛtau  |
vāmadakṣiṇamārgeṇa karṇabhūṣasthitāviha  || 4.86 ||

sa-ca-madhyagataṃ vaktraṃ devyāyā vīranāyike  |
visargānta-kha-madhyasthaṃ ka-ga-madhyagataṃ punaḥ  || 4.87 ||

kha-paścimaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ paścimottarameva ca  |
gha-ca-madhyagataṃ caiva uddharedakṣaraṃ śubham  || 4.88 ||

ete pañca smṛtā varṇā devyā daśanakalpanā  |
ña-pūrve rasanā devyā jhordhvena sarasvatī  || 4.89 ||

sa-ta-madhyasthitaḥ kaṇṭhaḥ ma-cha-madhyagatoddharet |
ra-ma-madhyagataṃ tadvadakṣarau tu śubhātmakau  || 4.90 ||
śikharau tau smṛtau bhadre vāmadakṣiṇagau śubhau  |
ū-ḍha-madhyagataṃ gṛhya ḍa-ṇa-madhye dvitīyakam  || 4.91 ||

vāmadakṣiṇagau dvau tu bāhū devyāḥ surārcite  |
ṭa-ḍa-madhyagataṃ caiva dvidhābhūtaṃ tu kārayet || 4.92 ||

karatalau smṛtau devyāḥ savyāsavyau vijānataḥ  |
ja-ma-pūrvau tu aṅgulyau vāmadakṣiṇagau śubhau  || 4.93 ||

aṃ-ka-madhye karapṛṣṭhe dvidhābhūtaṃ prakalpayet |
ña-ṭha-madhyagataṃ gṛhya vāmahaste pradāpayet || 4.94 ||

ūrdhvavaktrakapālaṃ tu amṛtākhyena pūritam  |
dakṣiṇe tu kare jñeyaṃ ya-ḍha-madhye tu daṇḍakam  || 4.95 ||

śūlasya kathitaṃ bhadre uddhāreṇa samuddhṛtam  |
a-cha-madhyagataṃ śūlamuttānamūrdhvavaktragam  || 4.96 ||

jñātavyaṃ tu vipaścidbhiryathālakṣaṇalakṣitam  |
gha-na-madhye tu hṛdayaṃ devyāyāḥ sarvakāmadam  || 4.97 ||

ma-ṣa-madhyagataṃ gṛhya ātmabījaṃ śivātmakam  |
visargasahitaṃ bhadre uddhṛtaṃ mantramuttamam  || 4.98 ||

ya-sa-madhyagataṃ prāṇaṃ devyāyā vīranāyike  |
ja-ca-madhyagataṃ gṛhya ra-va-sandhigataṃ tathā  || 4.99 ||

vāmadakṣiṇagau dvau tu akṣarau tau stanātmakau  |
jha-pūrve tu payo jñeyamamṛtaṃ ca udāhṛtam  || 4.100 ||

na-sa-madhyagataṃ gṛhya udaramuddhṛtaṃ'naghe  |
ka-ṣākhyaṃ tattvarājānaṃ nābhiṃ devyāḥ prakalpayet || 4.101 ||

bha-ña-madhyagataṃ devi nitambaṃ sakalātmakam  |
va-ṣa-madhyagataṃ guhyamau-paścimasamanvitam  || 4.102 ||

ūrvākāraṃ bhavedbījaṃ ṇa-tha-madhyagataṃ'naghe  |
ṇa-ta-dakṣiṇagau bījau jānunī dve prakalpayet || 4.103 ||
savyāsavyagatau jñeyau krameṇaiva śubhekṣaṇe  |
tha-da-dakṣiṇagau dvau tu jaṅghau dve vāmadakṣiṇau  || 4.104 ||

tha-da-madhyagataṃ devi pa-ba-madhyaṃ tathaiva ca  |
dvau bījau coddhṛtau bhadre pādau jñeyau vipaścitā  || 4.105 ||

vāmadakṣiṇagau proktau lakṣaṇena samanvitau  |
evaṃ samyagvidhānena uddhṛtā mālinī priye  || 4.106 ||

sapta koṭyastu vidyānāṃ mantrāṇāmamitaujasām  |
eṣā hyekā parā yonirmālinī sarvakāmadā  || 4.107 ||

mālayitvā sthitā yena tenaiṣā mālinī smṛtā  |
ye bhūtā ye bhaviṣyanti aprameyā varānane  || 4.108 ||

rudrāṇāṃ yoginīnāṃ ca sā mātaiva nigadyate  |
avarṇā varṇasaṃyogā jñātavyā tu śubhekṣaṇe  || 4.109 ||

sarvarudrātmakā mantrā rudrāḥ śaktyātmakāḥ priye  |
śaktistu mātṛkā jñeyā sā jñeyā tu śivātmikā  || 4.110 ||

evaṃ mantrapramāṇaṃ tu kathitaṃ tava śobhane  |
etadādyaṃ samākhyātaṃ gopanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ  || 4.111 ||

ekavīravidhānaṃ tu prāguktamanya āgame  || 4.111* ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate mantranirṇayagahvaramālinyuddhāro nāma caturthaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************


śrībhairava uvāca

umāmāheśvaraṃ cakraṃ kathayāmi suniścitam  |
padaṃ ca padabhedaṃ ca yo jānāti sa sidhyati  || 5.1 ||

aiṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya padaṃ caiva daśākṣaram  |
namaścāmuṇḍe dvitīyaṃ syātpañcākṣaramudāhṛtam  || 5.2 ||
namaścākāśamātṝṇāṃ padamanyattṛtīyakam  |
aṣṭākṣaraṃ samākhyātaṃ lakṣaṇena vilakṣitam  || 5.3 ||

sarvakāmārthasādhakīnāṃ padaṃ caiva caturthakam  |
navākṣaramidaṃ devi padaṃ yatsamudāhṛtam  || 5.4 ||

ajarāmarīṇāṃ padaṃ cātra kathitaṃ pañcaguṇāvaham  |*
akṣarāṇāṃ samāsena rasasaṅkhyā udāhṛtā  || 5.5 ||*

sarvatrāpratihatagatīnāṃ padaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ varānane  |*
daśākṣaraṃ samākhyātaṃ kathitaṃ vīranāyike  || 5.6 ||*

svarūpapararūpaparivartanīnāṃ padaṃ saptamakaṃ bhavet |*
akṣarāṇāṃ samāsena daśatritayamuttamam  || 5.7 ||*

sarvasattva vaśīkaraṇocchādanonmūlanasamastakarmapravṛttīnāṃ padaṃ cāṣṭamakaṃ bhavet |
akṣarāṇāṃ samāsena viṃśaccatvārisaṅkhyayā  || 5.8 ||

sarvamātṛguhyahṛdayaparamasiddhaṃ padaṃ tu navamaṃ bhavet |*
akṣarāṇāṃ samāsena śakrasaṅkhyā varānane  || 5.9 ||*

parakarma tathā devi chedanakaraṃ prakīrtitam  |
siddhikaraṃ ca evātra padaṃ caiva dvipañcakam  || 5.10 ||

akṣarāṇāṃ tathā saṅkhyā ekatra samudāhṛtā  |
dvisaptaparimāṇena sphuṭametatkuleśvari  || 5.11 ||

śṛṇu cānyaṃ varārohe mātṝṇāṃ vacanaṃ śubham  |
akṣarāṇāṃ pramāṇena dvicatuṣkaṃ varānane  || 5.12 ||

ekādaśamametaddhi padaṃ sarvaguṇāvaham  |
tadyatheti samārabhya dvitīyaṃ śobhanaṃ priye  || 5.13 ||

brahmāṇīti padaṃ pūrvaṃ māheśvarī dvitīyakam  |
kaumārīti tṛtīyaṃ syādvaiṣṇavyā tu caturthakam  || 5.14 ||

vārāhyā pañcamaṃ jñeyamaindrī ṣaṣṭhamakaṃ bhavet |
aiśānī saptamaṃ proktamāgneyī cāṣṭamaṃ priye  || 5.15 ||
evamādyāḥ sthitā devyaḥ kathitāstava śobhane  |
aghore amoghe varade vicce vai vacanaṃ śubham  || 5.16 ||

sarvāsāṃ caiva mātṝṇāṃ svāhāpraṇavasaṃyutam  |
aiṃ cāmuṇḍe padaṃ pūrvamūrdhvakeśi dvitīyakam  || 5.17 ||

jvalitaśikhe tṛtīyaṃ tu vidyujjihve caturthakam  |
tārakākṣi tathā devi pañcamaṃ parikīrtitam  || 5.18 ||

piṅgalabhruve nāmena ṣaṣṭhamaṃ tu sulocane  |
vikṛtadaṃṣṭre padaṃ hyetatsaptamaṃ parikīrtitam  || 5.19 ||

kruddhe ti ca tathā cānyamaṣṭamaṃ śubhalakṣaṇam  |*
māṃsaśoṇitasurāsavapriye navamaṃ daśamaṃ tu hasadvayam  || 5.20 ||*

nṛtyadvayaṃ tathā coktaṃ daśa-m-ekaṃ tu suvrate  |
vijṛmbha ca tathā yugmaṃ daśadve ca prakāśitam  || 5.21 ||

māyātrailokyarūpeti daśatritayamuttamam  |
sahasraparivartanīnāṃ dvisaptamaṃ parameśvari  || 5.22 ||

nudayugmaṃ tripañcaiva kūṭayugmaṃ dviraṣṭakam  |
ciriyugmaṃ tathā bhadre daśasapta ca ekataḥ  || 5.23 ||

hiridvitayamekatra daśāṣṭa śubhekṣaṇe  |
bhiri caiva dvirabhyāsādviṃśa-m-ekonasaṅkhyayā  || 5.24 ||

trāsanidvitayaṃ caiva padaṃ viṃśamakaṃ bhavet |
bhrāmaṇiyugmametaddhi viṃśa-m-ekaṃ tu uttamam  || 5.25 ||

vidrāvaṇi dvirabhyāsādviṃśadvayaṃ tathānaghe  |
kṣobhaṇīti dvirabhyāsādviṃśatrikamudāhṛtam  || 5.26 ||

māraṇidvitayaṃ caiva viṃśacatvārisaṅkhyayā  |
sañjīvanipade dve tu pañcaviṃśapadaṃ priye  || 5.27 ||

heriyugmaṃ smṛtaṃ bhadre ṣaḍviṃśakamanuttamam  |
geriyugmaṃ tathā proktaṃ saptaviṃśatimaṃ padam  || 5.28 ||
ghuri caiva dvirabhyāsādaṣṭāviṃśa varānane  |
ghurileti tathāpyevamūnatriṃśamudāhṛtam  || 5.29 ||

namo mātṛgaṇāyeti triṃśakaṃ kathitaṃ sphuṭam  |
namo namaḥ aiṃ vicce svāhā triṃśamekottaraṃ padam  || 5.30 ||

samastapadasaṃyogātparimāṇaṃ śatārdhakam  |
varṇānāṃ ca śate dve tu dvānavatyā varānane  || 5.31 ||

adhikaṃ kathitaṃ bhadre mātṝṇāṃ nāmavarjitam  |
padabhedastu vidyāyā jñātavyaḥ sādhakena tu  || 5.32 ||

prayatnena varārohe tantrāmnāyaprapālakaḥ  |
sakṛduccāritā vidyā samayajño bhavatyasau  || 5.33 ||

pañcapraṇava-m-uddhāraṃ yathā tvaṃ gahvare śṛṇu  |
e o-madhye samuddhṛtya bindunāda-m-alaṅkṛtam  || 5.34 ||

bhagākhyaṃ prathamaṃ bījamuddhṛtaṃ paramākṣaram  |
va-ṣa-madhyagataṃ gṛhya ṇa-ṭa-madhyāsane sthitam  || 5.35 ||

yu-madhyena sambhinnamaḥ-au-madhya-m-alaṅkṛtam  |
dvitīyaṃ kathitaṃ devi tṛtīyaṃ va-ma-madhyagam  || 5.36 ||

ṭha-la-madhyāsanāsīnaṃ caturthasvarabheditam  |
bindunā mastakākrāntaṃ na-ca-madhye caturthakam  || 5.37 ||

ī-ta-madhye samāruddhamai-pūrveṇa vibheditam  |
bindunādasamākrāntaṃ caturthaṃ praṇavaṃ bhavet || 5.38 ||

pañcamaṃ ya-sa-madhyasthaṃ ba-ha-madhyāsane sthitam  |
aṃ-pūrveṇa samāyuktamau-paścimavibhūṭitam  || 5.39 ||

ardhacandrānvitaṃ kṛtvā bindunādayutaṃ kuru  |
pañcapraṇava-m-uddhāraṃ rahasyaṃ kathitaṃ tava  || 5.40 ||

mantrāṇāṃ dīpakaṃ devi yathākarmaṇi yojayet |
vidyāyāstu prasaṅgena pañcapraṇavamuddhṛtam  || 5.41 ||
sāmprataṃ śṛṇu kalyāṇi vidyāmāhātmyamuttamam  |
sevanājjapahomādvā dhyānācca kramaśo bhavet || 5.42 ||

ṣaṇmāsāccotpateddevi satyametadudāhṛtam  |
kṛtvā sāmrāṭajān doṣānuccārātkalmaṣāpaham  || 5.43 ||

devadrohe gurudrohe koṭitriṃśaiḥ sa śudhyati  |
chedane puṣpapattrāṇāmāvartācchudhyate tu saḥ  || 5.44 ||

sandhyālope kṛte devi trirāvarteṇa śudhyati  |
āhnikacchedasañjāte śatamekamudīrayet || 5.45 ||

laṅghane samayānāṃ ca abhakṣyasya tu bhakṣaṇe  |
avācyavācite devi sahasrācchuddhiriṣyate  || 5.46 ||

kākolūkakapotānāṃ pakṣiṇāṃ ghātane kṛte  |
sahasrairdvibhiḥ śudhyeta satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 5.47 ||

chāgameṣa tathānyāni mṛgajambūka ṛkṣayoḥ  |
śuddhistrisahasrāddevi yathā bhairava-m-abravīt || 5.48 ||

sarpamārjārahantāro dundubhomatsyaghātakaḥ  |
caturbhiśca sahasrairhi śīghraṃ śuddhimavāpnuyāt || 5.49 ||

śvasūkaranakulādi mūṣakaścātha vāpi vā  |
pañcabhiḥ śuddhiriṣyeta sahasraistu kulānvaye  || 5.50 ||

gavāṃ hatvā praśudhyeta daśalakṣaistu saṅkhyayā  |
brāhmaṇastu yadā devi pramādādghātito budhaiḥ  || 5.51 ||

lakṣairviṃśati śudhyeta naktāśī tu jitendriyaḥ  |
bauddhārahantahantā ca dvijāddviguṇa śudhyati  || 5.52 ||

lākulā mauṣalāścaiva ye cānye lātapāṇayaḥ  |
hatvā śuddhimavāpnoti koṭitrayajapena tu  || 5.53 ||

guruṃ hatvā pañca koṭyaḥ śudhyate tu pramādataḥ  |
striyo ghātī durācāro daśa koṭyo japetpriye  || 5.54 ||
naktāśī śuddhimāpnoti kṣetrapīṭhān bhramedyadi  |
anyeṣāṃ varṇajātīnāmadhamottamamadhyamāḥ  || 5.55 ||

lakṣaistu bhavate śuddhirdvitricatvārisaṅkhyayā  |
ekādekona kartavyaṃ varṇāṇāṃ ca krameṇa tu  || 5.56 ||

nindate yoginīṃ yastu śivabhaktāṃśca nindati  |
śāstrāṇi dūṣayedyastu striyamākoṭayeti ca  || 5.57 ||

kroṣanti kanyakā devi sahasrācchuddhiriṣyate  |
vāmadakṣiṇasiddhānte śivavratadharo hataḥ  || 5.58 ||

koṭicaturbhirdeveśi śudhyate japatatparaḥ  |
yaḥ punastattvavettā ca ṣoḍhānyāsaviśāradaḥ  || 5.59 ||

smaraṇācchuddhiriṣyeta tathyaṃ bhairava-m-abravīt |
krodhena tu yadā devi uccaiḥśabdapralāpitam  || 5.60 ||

trivārāvartayedvidyāṃ śāntimāśu prayacchati  |
kṛte karmaṇi bālānāṃ lūtācipiṭagaṇḍayoḥ  || 5.61 ||

jvaragrahaviṣādibhyaḥ oṣadhākhyāpanāya ca  |
pañcāvartādviśudhyeta anvayī yastu śāsane  || 5.62 ||

yaḥ punaḥ kramavettā ca śuddhāśuddhairna bādhyate  |
deśikaṃ putrakaṃ vāpi sādhakaṃ samayajñakam  || 5.63 ||

pramādānnindate yastu daśāvartādviśudhyati  |
aliṃ jugupsayedyastu phalguṣaṃ vā yadi priye  || 5.64 ||

ekoccāreṇa śudhyeta annaṃ vā yajjugupsate  |
kandukaṃ mallakoṣāḍhyā chippakaṃ carmakārakam  || 5.65 ||

dhvajaṃ sūnākaraṃ vāpi matsyaghātaṃ tu lubdhakam  |
koṅkaṇaṃ cīnabāhlīkaṃ vaṅgālaṃ kāmarūpakam  || 5.66 ||

māgadhaṃ saindhavaṃ vāpi gujjaraṃ lāṭasaṃjñakam  |
anye'pi deśamadhyasthā vanavāsāntyajātayaḥ  || 5.67 ||
veśyādikramaśaḥ sarve nindanācchuddhiriṣyate  |
trirāvarteṇa deveśi akāmātkāmato'pi vā  || 5.68 ||

kāmato dviguṇaṃ devi kartavyaṃ siddhimicchatā  |
trikhaṇḍā yādṛśaṃ proktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ kulānvaye  || 5.69 ||

dvātriṃśākṣarayā tadvatkartavyaṃ tattvavedibhiḥ  |
ete nirodharūpāstu sādhakānāṃ prakāśitāḥ  || 5.70 ||

tadarthe kathitā vidyā yena sidhyanti sādhakāḥ  |
śreyārthināṃ mayākhyātā madbhaktāḥ kṛtaniścayāḥ  || 5.71 ||

anyeṣāṃ na kadācitsyāllaulyārthe ye sthitānaghe  |
iyaṃ vidyā samākhyātā upayogādvarānane  || 5.72 ||

sāmprataṃ padabhedastu yathā yojyastu bhairavi  |
tathā te kathayiṣyāmi tacchṛṇuṣva samāsataḥ  || 5.73 ||

yā vidyā kathitā pūrvaṃ nādiphāntakrameṇa tu  |
taccharīragatā varṇāḥ pañcapraṇavabheditāh || 5.74 ||

pañcayonyāḥ svarūpeṇa varṇamekaikasaṅkhyayā  |
bhairavaḥ śabdarāśistu ādikṣāntakrameṇa tu  || 5.75 ||

te varṇāḥ pañcapraṇavaiḥ sampuṭe[c] ca pṛthakpṛthak |
ādikṣāntakrameṇaiva niyojanamudāhṛtam  || 5.76 ||

dvābhyāṃ tu grathanaṃ kāryaṃ samastasyāpi śobhane  |
sapta varṇān dadeccādau madhye vidyāpadaṃ dadet || 5.77 ||

punaḥ sapta padasyānte tasyānte tu padaṃ punaḥ  |
punaḥ saptakamuccārya padaṃ ca tadanantaram  || 5.78 ||

anena kramayogena nirvāhena tu yojayet |
kṣāntaṃ vai yāva deveśi tāvadeva niyojayet || 5.79 ||

padasaṅkhyā samastasya nirvāhobhayadīpite  |
mālinī dvādaśairbhedaiḥ śabdarāśistu ṣoḍaśaiḥ  || 5.80 ||
anena kramaśaḥ sarve varṇāścaiva pṛthakpṛthak |
calacakravibhāgena padavidyāṃ yadā yajet || 5.81 ||

tadā kṣobhaṃ karotyāśu divyādivyetaraṃ priye  |
yonayaḥ pañcadhā yāstu sarvāḥ klidyanti nānyathā  || 5.82 ||

drāvaṇaṃ kṣobhaṇaṃ mohaṃ jṛmbhaṇaṃ śoṣaṇaṃ tathā  |
sarvān tān kurute devi yadā śaktisamo bhavet || 5.83 ||

prasuptabhujagākārā dvādaśānte varānane  |
nābhiṣṭhā tu tathāpyevaṃ draṣṭavyā parameśvari  || 5.84 ||

dṛśyate dehamadhye tu vyomānte ca parāparā  |
tasyāgre tu tato mantraṃ hutāśakaṇikākṛtim  || 5.85 ||

uccāreta tato mantraṃ śabdarūpaṃ hṛdi priye  |
śabdānte śaktiruccāryā īṣanmandagamārutā  || 5.86 ||

padmasūtranibhākārā uccāryā sānunāsikā  |
uccārāntāvasāne tu jñātavyālātacakravat || 5.87 ||

tatra madhyagataṃ devi caitanyaṃ mantrasaṃyutam  |
prasphuratkiraṇānekaiḥ koṭiśo dikṣvavasthitaiḥ  || 5.88 ||

tasyāpyante tato devi śaktirādyā manonmanī  |
atītā tu yadā sā vai tadā bindvī udāhṛtā  || 5.89 ||

bindvante vyāpako devo māyātīto nirāmayaḥ  |
sa śivo bhāvanātīto nirguṇo guṇasambhavaḥ  || 5.90 ||

adhikārī sarvakārī ca śaktyātīto mahāprabhuḥ  |
anena kramayogena kramaḥ kāryaḥ suniścitaiḥ  || 5.91 ||

ātmā manaśca mantraśca śivaḥ śaktistathaiva ca  |
ekībhāvagato devi jñātavyaḥ siddhimīhakaiḥ  || 5.92 ||

tasmātprāṇasamaṃ jāpyaṃ mantrānte nādagocare  |
nādasyānte tato jñātvā etatsmaraṇamucyate  || 5.93 ||
smaraṇaṃ śaktiruddiṣṭā yā karoti gamāgamam  |
tasyānte tu parā sūkṣmā sā kalā amṛtātmikā  || 5.94 ||

layātītā arūpā tu svayaṃvedyāvicārataḥ  |
na tasya lakṣaṇaṃ devi na lakṣo naiva yojanā  || 5.95 ||

na kṣayo naiva vṛddhiśca śuklakṛṣṇau na caiva hi  |
na rātrirna dinaṃ caiva na sandhyā ayanaṃ tathā  || 5.96 ||

viṣuvaṃ naiva deveśi saṅkrāntirnaiva vidyate  |
sarvāvasthagatiṃ jñātvā vijñānamupajāyate  || 5.97 ||

etatkaulikamākhyātamumāmāheśvaraṃ priye  |
utpateta na sandeho lakṣajāpāccalasya tu  || 5.98 ||

calā śaktiḥ samākhyātā lakṣaṇena udāhṛtā  |
avarṇā varṇasaṃyogā mālinī sā udāhṛtā  || 5.99 ||

padabhedagatā hyekā asaṅkhyātā varānane  |
evaṃ tadgraha-m-ākhyātaḥ sadyaḥpratyayakārakaḥ  || 5.100 ||

na kasyacinmayākhyātamumāmāheśvaraṃ priye  |
satataṃ japate yastu yoginīvallabho bhavet || 5.101 ||

ṣaṇmāsācchudhyate devi brahmaghno'pi na saṃśayaḥ  |
paśyate virajāṃ śāntāṃ jyotirūpāṃ mahādyutim  || 5.102 ||

japasya lakṣaṇaṃ devi idānīṃ śṛṇu sāmpratam  |
ekoccāraśatānte tu parāvasthā tu gīyate  || 5.103 ||

śatabhedaḥ samākhyātastadguṇo daśa eva tu  |
sahasrabhedamityuktaṃ lakṣaḥ śataguṇaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 5.104 ||

koṭibhedaḥ śatānāṃ tu lakṣāṇāṃ varavarṇini  |
etajjapavidhānaṃ tu kathitaṃ tava śobhane  || 5.105 ||

muktakaḥ śatabhedena yuktaṃ śataguṇaṃ śatam  |
calacakravibhāgena lakṣabhedamudāhṛtam  || 5.106 ||
calacakraṃ yadā devi koṭibhedo varānane  |
bāhyataḥ kathito bhadre adhyātmikamataḥ śṛṇu  || 5.107 ||

udbhave śatabhedastu sahasro viśleṣake viduḥ  |
laye tu lakṣabhedo vai layātīte tu koṭayaḥ  || 5.108 ||

vāmā jyeṣṭhā tathā raudrī bindvī ca samudāhṛtā  |
icchā jñānī kriyā śāntā krameṇaiva surārcite  || 5.109 ||

ātmacāragatiṃ jñātvā japaḥ kāryaḥ sadā budhaiḥ  |
anenaivākṣasūtreṇa lakṣalakṣaṇalakṣite  || 5.110 ||

kartavyo hi japo nityaṃ  sarvaśāstraviśāradaiḥ  |
bhuvanākhye varārohe śaktyākhye tu tathaiva hi  || 5.111 ||

cāroccāravibhāgena japaḥ śreṣṭha udāhṛtaḥ  |
mālā pañcāśikā proktā sūtraṃ śaktiḥ śivātmikā  || 5.112 ||

grathanaṃ kuṇḍalī śaktirlayānte merusaṃsthitam  |
etadguptataraṃ kāryamakṣasūtraṃ śivātmakam  || 5.113 ||

prakaṭaṃ naiva kartavyaṃ na meruṃ laṅghayetkvacit |*
śaṅkhasphaṭikarudrākṣaputrañjīvakariṣṭakāḥ  || 5.114 ||*

evamādyāḥ smṛtā ye tu maṇimālā varānane  |
na tatra vidyate devo na mantro naiva cetanā  || 5.115 ||

yatra yatra sthitā mālā na doṣo vidyate priye  |
mantranyāse kṛte devi kila gopyaṃ tu kārayet || 5.116 ||

śarīraṃ kutra gopyaṃ tu kārayīta varānane  |
mantranyāse kṛte hyātmā sakalīkṛtavigrahaḥ  || 5.117 ||

yathā gopyaṃ na yuñjeta tadvaccaivākṣamālikā  |
akṣaṃ cendriyamityuktaṃ sūtraṃ kuṇḍalinī smṛtā  || 5.118 ||

lakṣaṃ tu sā parā sūkṣmā kalā hyamṛtavāhinī  |
saṃyogakāriṇī vyomni tena sūtreti kīrtitā  || 5.119 ||
saṅkhyāgrahaṇakāryeṣu sā coktā akṣamālikā  |
śaṅkhādyāstu varārohe japakarmaṇi śasyate  || 5.120 ||

śaṅkhajaṃ tu śriyākāmaḥ sphāṭikaṃ muktihetave  |
padmākṣā padmajā proktā śriyāpuṣṭikarā priye  || 5.121 ||

rudrākṣaiḥ siddhimāpnoti yaccānyaṃ khecarīpadam  |
jīvakā sarvadā jñeyā gopucchāgrathitānaghe  || 5.122 ||

vidrumā vaśyakāryeṣu mauktikā sarvakāmadā  |
anyāni tu smṛtā ye vai ratnajā parameśvari  || 5.123 ||

sarvadā te samuddiṣṭā nātra kāryavicāraṇāt |
riṣṭakākṣāsthijā mālā abhicāre praśasyate  || 5.124 ||

nāgavaṅgastathā lohā miśrāścānye'pi ye smṛtāḥ  |
māraṇe tāṃ praśasyeta stambhane mohane tathā  || 5.125 ||

kampane dhvaṃsane devi kartavyā cābhicārake  |
evamanye'pi ye proktāsteṣāṃ śreṣṭhā tu śaṅkhajā  || 5.126 ||

praśastā sarvakāryeṣu japakarmaṇi śasyate  |
śaṅkhāvartā tu yā nāḍī śikhānte tu vyavasthitā  || 5.127 ||

tena śaṅkhamayaṃ proktamakṣasūtraṃ surādhipe  |
sphuṭate mastake yā sā dvidhā caiva visarpiṇī  || 5.128 ||

sphāṭikaṃ tena coddiṣṭaṃ guruvaktre pratiṣṭhitam  |
raudrībhāva[ḥ] smṛto rudrastālvagre ca vyavasthitaḥ  || 5.129 ||

śabdasparśaraso rūpaṃ gandhatanmātrasaṃyutam  |
vikāritve pravarteta nirodhāllakṣameva ca  || 5.130 ||

tena rudrākṣamālāyā japaḥ śreṣṭha udāhṛtaḥ  |
putravadudare kṛtvā prasuptāmṛtakuṇḍalī  || 5.131 ||

tayā nīyatyasau jīva adhaścordhvena bhāvini  |
putrañjīvakasaṃjñā tu tenaiṣā samudāhṛtā  || 5.132 ||
ariṣṭāni anekāni sukhaduḥkhātmikāni tu  |
bhuñjate satataṃ devi arjitaṃ yatpurā dhanam  || 5.133 ||

ariṣṭākhyā smṛtā mālā aprameyā bhavāntare  |
hṛtpadme saṃsthitā nityamakṣarāṇāṃ prabodhikā  || 5.134 ||

padmākṣamālā sā proktā śāstre śāstre varānane  |
evamādyāḥ smṛtā ye tu paryāyā akṣamālayā  || 5.135 ||

te sarve ātmanaścaiva kathitāśca kuleśvari  |
akṣasūtravidhiḥ khyātaḥ samyakkaulikavedinām  || 5.136 ||

sāmprataṃ nyāsamākhyāmi śṛṇu tattvena śobhane  |
svābhāvikaṃ calaṃ dīptaṃ sthiraṃ dravanabhoyutam  || 5.137 ||

nyāsamātraṃ samākhyātaṃ ṣoḍhādvādaśabhedataḥ  |
ṣoḍhā śaktiḥ samākhyātā parā caivākṣarā śubhā  || 5.138 ||

kuṇḍalī nābhideśasthā parā sā vyomarūpiṇī  |
ekā eva parā sūkṣmā akṣayā tejarūpiṇī  || 5.139 ||

jñātavyā sā parā devī ṣaḍvarṇarahitā kalā  |
brahmasthānagatā sūkṣmā svābhāvikamudāhṛtā  || 5.140 ||

viṣṇusthāne calā proktā dīptā rudrapade smṛtā  |
īśvare sthirasaṃjñā tu sadākhye dravasambhavā  || 5.141 ||

śaktisthā vyomarūpā tu jñātavyā tattvavedibhiḥ  |
aṇimādiguṇādhārā ṣaḍguṇā guṇabodhanī  || 5.142 ||

vyāpinī vyomarūpā ca anantānāthanāśritā  |
saṃyoktrī ca viyoktrī ca sadbhāvaguṇasaṃsthitā  || 5.143 ||

ekā eva parā śaktiḥ  saṃsthitā kṛtyabhedataḥ  |
kṛtyabhedena bhedo'syā na bhedaḥ paramārthataḥ  || 5.144 ||

evaṃ nyāse kṛte devi antaraṅge pravartate  |
bahiraṅge varṇarūpā ca ekā caiva anekadhā  || 5.145 ||
ṣoḍhādvādaśabhedena nyāsaḥ prokto gamāgame  || 5.146 ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate bṛhatsamayoddhāraḥ śabdarāśimālinītadgrahavyāptinirṇayo nāma pañcamaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************


śrīkubjikā uvāca

japasya lakṣaṇaṃ deva pūrvaṃ hi kathitaṃ tvayā  |
ekoccāraśataṃ jñeyaṃ sahasraṃ lakṣameva ca  || 6.1 ||

koṭayastu tathā śambho pṛthaglakṣaṇalakṣitāḥ  |
na me jñātaṃ pramāṇaṃ tu japasya suranāyaka  || 6.2 ||

tadahaṃ śrotumicchāmi mandabuddhyālpacetasā  || 6.3 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

yā sā kuṇḍalinī śaktiscidrūpā ca parā kalā  |
ādyā śaktirmaheśasya aṇumātrā hṛdi sthitā  || 6.4 ||

sā aṇu[ḥ ] kathitā tantre dve aṇū truṭimāśritā  |
truṭibhūtā tu sā devī japtavyā tu parāparā  || 6.5 ||

varṇahīnā parā proktā aparā varṇarūpiṇī  |
evaṃ jñātvā varārohe japaḥ kāryaḥ sadā budhaiḥ  || 6.6 ||

truṭirūpā tu sā devī tadādhāre vyavasthitā  |
kalate prāṇagā nityaṃ daśasthāne hyanukramāt || 6.7 ||

brahmā viṣṇustathā rudra īśvaro'tha sadāśivaḥ  |
kuṇḍalī vyāpinī caiva praśāntā viṣuvattathā  || 6.8 ||

śaktisthānaṃ tu deveśi ete sthānā daśa smṛtāḥ  |
yeṣāṃ sañcarate devaḥ śivaḥ paramakāraṇaḥ  || 6.9 ||

ūrdhve vā yadi vā tiryaksvasthāne vā sureśvari  |
prasphureta kalairyuktā daśabhistu mahābalaiḥ  || 6.10 ||

daśadhā kalanaṃ tena kathitaṃ tava śobhane  |
prayatnena kṛtoccāraṃ yāvacchaktirlayaṃ gatā  || 6.11 ||

tāvaddevi śataṃ proktaṃ varṇoccāre na saṃśayaḥ  |
svābhāvikamanuccārya sūkṣmādhāro jagatpatiḥ  || 6.12 ||

śatadhā kalanaṃ tasya truṭirūpasya yoginaḥ  |
sahasraṃ tu samuddiṣṭaṃ daśadhā parameśvari  || 6.13 ||

ubhayasya parityāgādkalādhāraḥ sadāśivaḥ1 |
dṛkkriyājñānanirmuktaḥ kalate ca sahasradhā  || 6.14 ||

lakṣabhedaḥ samuddiṣṭa iti śāstre na saṃśayaḥ  |
japo hyevaṃ samuddiṣṭo yogināṃ tattvavedinām  || 6.15 ||

lakṣātīto manātīto nirmuktastattvabandhanaiḥ  |
unmanatve sadā līno aṇurūpo nakiñcanaḥ  || 6.16 ||

sattāmātrasthito dehī guṇānāṃ pratibodhakaḥ  |
viṣayabhāvanirmuktaḥ kalate lakṣadhā priye  || 6.17 ||

koṭistu bhavate hyevaṃ jñātavyaṃ mantravādibhiḥ  |
soccāropāṃśubhūtastu mānaso manavarjitaḥ  || 6.18 ||

japaḥ pūrvaṃ samākhyātaḥ śāstre śāstre surārcite  |
saśabdoccārayogena śuddhyarthe kathitaṃ sphuṭam  || 6.19 ||

siddhyarthe'pāṃśuruddiṣṭaḥ svapravṛtto hṛdi sthitaḥ  |
mānaso yogahetvarthe ubhayatra vivarjitaḥ  || 6.20 ||

manātīto bhaveddevi mokṣadastu na saṃśayaḥ  |
evaṃ devi samākhyāto japaḥ prāṇasamastava  || 6.21 ||

japaḥ prāṇasamaḥ kāryo dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaphalārthinām  |
avarṇā varṇasaṃyogā mayā te samudāhṛtā  || 6.22 ||
nirālambe mahāśūnye yattejamupajāyate  |
tadgarbhe abhyasennityaṃ bhāgyahīno'pi sidhyati  || 6.23 ||

yogamūlī viśuddhī ca sārṇave sā ca ekatā  |
ekatra saṃsthitānandaṃ kularatnaṃ tridhā priye  || 6.24 ||

śrīkubjikā uvāca
mudrā tu sūcitā nātha na me jñātā mahāprabho  |
tanmamācakṣva deveśi yena bhrāntirvinaśyati  || 6.25 ||

śrībhairava uvāca

pātālordhvagataṃ yacca śṛṅgāṭapuramadhyagam  |
golākāraṃ tato devi randhrasyordhvagataṃ priye  || 6.26 ||

cakradvayamidaṃ proktaṃ prādhānyena vyavasthitam  |
vedhaghaṭṭanirodhaṃ ca uccārākṛṣṭikārakam  || 6.27 ||

stobhastambhanamāveśo gamaṃ caivātra suvrate  |
etadvirahito mantrī hāsyatāṃ yāti niścitam  || 6.28 ||

anena jñātamātreṇa pratyayān kurute bahūn  |
vṛttirājā varārohe niveśya cakramadhyataḥ  || 6.29 ||

vṛttihīnastatastatra kāvyakartā na saṃśayaḥ  |
cakramadhye ca sañcintya suśuklāṃ ca parāparām  || 6.30 ||

pustakavyagrahastāṃ ca jñānamudrādharāṃ tathā  |
sphāṭikenākṣasūtreṇa sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām  || 6.31 ||

sragdāmalambitagalāṃ prabhāmaṇḍalamaṇḍitām  |
dvibāhu-r-ekavadanāṃ candrakoṭisamaprabhām  || 6.32 ||

udgirantī[ ṃ ] mahaughena śāstrakoṭīranekaśaḥ  |
evaṃ dhyānasamāviṣṭaḥ sākṣādvāgīśvaro bhavet || 6.33 ||

saṃskṛtaṃ prākṛtaṃ caiva vedasiddhāntagahvaram  |
granthataścārthataścaiva udgirennātra saṃśayaḥ  || 6.34 ||
pīṭhamadhyagatābhyāsātpīṭhadvāre'thavā priye  |
sampradāyamidaṃ kaulaṃ śāktaṃ śaktipadānugam  || 6.35 ||

mātrāyogena deveśi mudrābandhaṃ tu kārayet |
sā mātrā gīyate cātra uccāravaśavartinī  || 6.36 ||

uccaraṃ sahajaṃ devi dehamadhye vyavasthitam  |
śatasaṅkhyāpramāṇena yāvaduccarate parām  || 6.37 ||

tāvadāviṣṭadehastu śāstrārthaṃ vadate sudhīḥ  |
nityārūpeṇa saivātra dhyāyedraktasamaprabhām  || 6.38 ||

lākṣālaktakasaṅkāśāṃ caturvaktrāṃ caturbhujām  |
mūrtitrayasamopetāṃ tribhirbhedairvyavasthitām  || 6.39 ||

tristhāṃ trimārgagāṃ devīṃ trināḍīsamatāṃ gatām  |
nityaklinnāṃ ca deveśi tathā caiva madadravām  || 6.40 ||

devyārūpadharāṃ sarvāmekavaktrāṃ dvibāhukām  |
pāśāṅkuśadharāṃ sarvāṃ madavibhrāntalocanām  || 6.41 ||

yauvanasthāṃ madonmattāṃ madirānandananditām  |
smareddevyāḥ svarūpaṃ tu tatprayogavyavasthayā  || 6.42 ||

taḍitsahasrabandhūka- dāḍimīkusumadyutim  |
pañcaśṛṅgāṭakādhārāṃ sā parā pararūpiṇī  || 6.43 ||

mahāyogavilāsā tu śivādyavanigocaram  |
vyāpayitvā sthitā devī ravinakṣatramaṇḍalam  || 6.44 ||

śṛṅgāṭakaṃ cordhvamukhaṃ tiryagrekhāgramūlagam  |
śikhordhvakuṇḍalākāraṃ kāmaśakti-r-adhisthitam  || 6.45 ||

pañcaśṛṅgāṭakāsīnaṃ sthitaṃ tatra varānane  |
devyārūpadharaṃ cakraṃ dhyāyedevaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 6.46 ||

eṣa bandhastu mudrāyāḥ kathitaste kuleśvari  |
tritattvena tu mantreṇa vakṣyamānena kārayet || 6.47 ||
drāvaṇaṃ kṣobhaṇaṃ caiva ākarṣavaśameva ca  |
pūjāvidhānaṃ deveśi devyāyā vīravandite  || 6.48 ||


śrīkubjikā uvāca

triśikhā padmamudrā ca yonimudrā viśeṣataḥ  |
tāsāṃ lakṣaṇamākhyāhi yathāvatsphuṭato vada  || 6.49 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

mudrāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ devi kathayāmi samāsataḥ  |
hastābhyāṃ kārayedādau sampuṭaṃ cordhvadiṅmukham  || 6.50 ||

aṅgulyā grathayetsarvāḥ saṃśliṣṭamubhayeṣvapi  |
tarjanyānāmikau kuñcya saṃśliṣṭau madhyasaṃsthitau  || 6.51 ||

tābhyāṃ mūle mukhaṃ kāryaṃ tarjanyāyā varānane  |
saṃśliṣṭau sammukhau dvau tu madhyamau ūrdhvadiṅmukhau  || 6.52 ||

saṃyogena varārohe aṅguṣṭhau ca kaniṣṭhakau  |
tādṛśīva hi kartavyā triśikhā tu vidhīyate  || 6.53 ||

karābhyāṃ sampuṭaṃ kāryaṃ maṇibandhau tu saṃhatau  |
agrāṅgulyā prasāryeta aṅguṣṭhau madhyasaṃsthitau  || 6.54 ||

padmamudrā samākhyātā yonimudrāmataḥ śṛṇu  |
hastābhyāṃ sampuṭaṃ kāryaṃ kaniṣṭhā madhya yojayet || 6.55 ||

puṭākārau karau kṛtvā aṅguṣṭhau madhyasaṃsthitau  |
niḥsṛtā vāmahastasya aṅgulyā tu kanīyasī  || 6.56 ||

yonimudrā smṛtā bhadre sarveṣāṃ kṣobhakārikā  |
etā mudrāḥ samākhyātā dhyānapūjāvisarjane  || 6.57 ||

sāmprataṃ khecarīṇāṃ tu yathā mudrā khagādhipe  |
kathayāmi samāsena tvatprītyā khagagāminī  || 6.58 ||
anāmā karṇike yojyā ṣoḍaśāraṃ tu pīḍayet |
pīḍanādṛjutāṃ yāti khagamārge tu bhāvini  || 6.59 ||

phādināntagate lakṣe khagatiśca na saṃśayaḥ  |
ṣaṭpattraṃ mūrdhnitaḥ kṛtvā tarjanyāgre tu yojayet || 6.60 ||

śūnye śūnyamano bhūtvā sampīḍyeta parasparam  |
ṣaṭpattraṃ tu vikāśyeta udānapreritena tu  || 6.61 ||

bhāvayennādiphāntaṃ tu khagatīti na saṃśayaḥ  |
dvādaśārordhvanālena lambikānte niveśayet || 6.62 ||

bhedanaṃ kuñcitenaiva cārgalaṃ kūrmasaṃyutam  |
bhāvayennādiphāntaṃ tu khagatiṃ vīravandite  || 6.63 ||

padmamudrā tridhā proktā yonimudrāmataḥ śṛṇu  |
yoniṃ yonau samākramya mudgareṇa tu bhedanam  || 6.64 ||

visargadvayasaṃyogātkhagagāmī bhaveddhruvam  |
amṛtākhyā parā yonirbhāvayenmastakopari  || 6.65 ||

ākramya gandhamārgaṃ tu yojanā nādiphāntagā  |
khagatirbhavate-d-evaṃ yonimārge vicakṣaṇaḥ  || 6.66 ||

uccārya vāmaśaktiṃ tu sandhayedgranthimadhyataḥ  |
vikṣepamūrdhvataḥ kṛtvā pare yonau tu bhāvanā  || 6.67 ||

anena khagagāmitvaṃ bhavate tu na saṃśayaḥ  |
yonimudrā samākhyātā tribhedā parikīrtitā  || 6.68 ||

triśikhālakṣaṇaṃ devi kathyamānaṃ nibodhata  |
karābhyāṃ caiva tarjanyāṃ pīḍayettatprayatnataḥ  || 6.69 ||

brahmanāḍyā dvirabhyāsādāsanaṃ mandarasya tu  |
triśikhā nāma mudreyamarpaṇaṃ khagamārgayoḥ  || 6.70 ||

golakaṃ śūnyamārgasthaṃ pathatrayagataṃ priye  |
kṣepaṇaṃ bindukoṭyūrdhvamavanīṃ kramya pādayoḥ  || 6.71 ||
guhye niveśya mantrajñastriśikhaṃ khecaraṃ priye  |
anena khagagāmī'sau bhavate sādhakottamaḥ  || 6.72 ||

karaṇaṃ cordhvamūlaṃ syādbandhayetkhagamaṇḍalam  |
ākramya pañcamaṃ tatra karābhyāṃ caiva śūlini  || 6.73 ||

jānukau kurpare yojya vikṣepo guhyamadhyataḥ  |
vedhanaṃ brahmarandhrasya kathitaṃ tu tapodhane  || 6.74 ||

khagamārgagatistvevaṃ bhavate tu sulocane  |
eṣā mudrā samākhyātā navabhedairvyavasthitā  || 6.75 ||

mudrā śaktiriti khyātā mudritaṃ drāvayiṣyati  |
tena mudrā samākhyātā kathitā parameśvari  || 6.76 ||

mudritaṃ gopitaṃ proktaṃ cicchaktyā yā parāparā  |
na jñāyate varārohe sā tu mudrā udāhṛtā  || 6.77 ||

ajñānamalarūpeṇa yāvadbaddhaḥ sa pudgalaḥ  |
na jānāti parātmānaṃ tāvanmāyā pravartate  || 6.78 ||

bhinne tamasi caikatvaṃ yadā paśyati mānavaḥ  |
tadā sā tu parā proktā bandhamokṣakarī priye  || 6.79 ||

ekā sā paramā śaktiḥ saṃsthitā tu śivecchayā  |
mocayanti grahādibhyaḥ pāśaughān drāvayanti ca  || 6.80 ||

mocanāddrāvaṇādyasmānmudrākhyāḥ śaktayaḥ smṛtāḥ  |
khagatirhyūrdhvabhāvena khagamārgeṇa nityaśaḥ  || 6.81 ||

carate sarvajantūnāṃ khecarī tena sā smṛtā  |
paratv' ekā tu sā jñeyā punaścaiva tridhā smṛtā  || 6.82 ||

icchā jñānī kriyā sā tu varṇarūpamupāgatā  |
pañcāśabhedabhinnā sā ekā eva-m-udāhṛtā  || 6.83 ||

aṅgāvayavasampūrṇā mālayitvā jagatsthitā  |
nādiphāntasvarūpeṇa tena sā mālinī smṛtā  || 6.84 ||
sapta koṭyastu mantrāṇāmaprameyāstu yāḥ smṛtāḥ  |
svatantrasthāstu tāḥ sarvā mudritāḥ parameśvari  || 6.85 ||

tena mudrā samākhyātā sadyaḥpratyayakārikā  |
avayave mātṛrūpā tu svaiḥ svairaṃśairvyavasthitā  || 6.86 ||

brahmāṃśā caiva rudrāṃśā kaumāryāṃśā varānane  |
vaiṣṇavyā caiva yāmyāṃśā aindryāṃśā ca tathānaghe  || 6.87 ||

yogeśvaryā ca yogāṃśā yogayogīśanāyikā  |
ete hyaṃśāḥ smṛtāḥ sapta punaḥ saptasu saptasu  || 6.88 ||

brahmāṇyāḥ sapta-m-uddiṣṭāḥ sapta māheśvarī punaḥ  |
kaumāryāḥ sapta-m-uddiṣṭā vaiṣṇavyāḥ sapta eva ca  || 6.89 ||

vārāhī sapta-m-uddiṣṭā aindrāṇyāḥ sapta eva tu  |
cāmuṇḍā sapta-m-uddiṣṭā evaṃ vai sapta saptasu  || 6.90 ||

pañcāśaikona vai devyā bhuvanāvalisaṃsthitā[ḥ]  |
atra bhedairanekaiśca saṃsthitā bhuvanāvaliḥ  || 6.91 ||

tasya dehagatā romā[ḥ] koṭyastrīṇi prakīrtitāḥ  |
lakṣāṇi caiva pañcāśadromāṇāṃ tu tadudbhavā  || 6.92 ||

ekaikaromakūpeṣu yoginyaḥ koṭisaṃsthitāḥ  |
trikoṭikoṭikoṭīnāṃ koṭayastu anekadhā  || 6.93 ||

yathā cāmbaraparyantā pṛthivyādiṣu sambhavāḥ  |
aṇavastvapramāṇāstu tattve tattve tvanekadhā  || 6.94 ||

sūkṣmarūpāstathā rudrā eṣāṃ saṅkhyā na vidyate  |
vyāpitaṃ tu samastaṃ hi rudraiḥ sūkṣmataraiḥ priye  || 6.95 ||

evaṃ mudrā samākhyātā vyāpayitvāprameyataḥ  |
ekā eva parā mudrā yasyedaṃ tiṣṭhate jagat || 6.96 ||

yaṃ yaṃ spṛśati sā hyaṅgaṃ sā sā mudrā vidhīyate  |
nṛtyaṃ valgaṃ tathā hāsyaṃ rodanaṃ sphoṭameva ca  || 6.97 ||
yadvikāraṃ prakurvanti tatsarvaṃ mudrasaṃjñakam  |
aṅgulyā aṅganā proktā aṅge carati nityaśaḥ  || 6.98 ||

aṅgulyā tena coddiṣṭā mudrābandhe varānane  |
kaṃ śarīramiti khyātaṃ nyastā tasmin pravartate  || 6.99 ||

helāgamanamārgeṇa tena nāmā kanīyasī  |
anāmā nāmarahitā koṭibhedairvyavasthitā  || 6.100 ||

nāmaṃ na śakyate vaktumanāmā tena gīyate  |
madhye pravartate nityamāśrayā pudgalasya tu  || 6.101 ||

tatrādhārādvrajedūrdhvaṃ punarāgamanaṃ priye  |
madhyamā nāma tenātra kathitā mantravādinām  || 6.102 ||

tarjanaṃ kurute nityaṃ saṃyojanaviyojanam  |
tarjayantī mahāmohaṃ pāśajālamanantakam  || 6.103 ||

tarjanī tena sā proktā mudrā sarveṣu cottamā  |
aṅguṣṭhaścaughabhūtā tu pravāhe satataṃ priye  || 6.104 ||

uccāreṇa pravarteta aṅguṣṭhastena sa smṛtaḥ  |
aha-m-ūrdhvagatiḥ proktaḥ stau ti rātrī nigadyate  || 6.105 ||

hastau tena samākhyātau vāmadakṣiṇa ucyate  |
vāme sṛṣṭiriti proktā saṃhāraṃ dakṣiṇe viduḥ  || 6.106 ||

savyāsavyagatau tena kathitau vīranāyike  |
vāmāvyāptaṃ jagatkṛtsnaṃ saṃhārāntaṃ tadā priye  || 6.107 ||

saṃyogena varārohe ātmā kuṇḍalinī smṛtā  |
iyaṃ sā paramā yoniryonīnāmuttamā priye  || 6.108 ||

yo jānāti varārohe śaktirādyā manonmanī  |
tena jñātaṃ jagatsarvaṃ varṇāvarṇavivarjitam  || 6.109 ||

sā mudrā tu samākhyātā viśvavyāptikarī parā  |
dvau bindū cūlike dve tu visargaśaktisampuṭam  || 6.110 ||
tadūrdhvamiha nādāntaṃ vibhvī śūnyamudāhṛtam  |
janmasthānātsamudyantī yāvattadbhavamaṇḍalam  || 6.111 ||

sṛṣṭisaṃhārayogena meḍhram-ādhāramadhyagam  |
evaṃ mudrā samākhyātā vāṅmanaḥkāyakarmabhiḥ  || 6.112 ||

kimanyatpṛcchase devi tatsarvaṃ kathayāmyaham  || 6.113 ||


iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate japamudrānirṇayo nāma ṣaṣṭhaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************




śrīkubjikā uvāca

yā sā devī (deva Ed.) parā yoniḥ samayā kubjinī parā  |
tāmācakṣva prayatnena saṃsphuṭaṃ vyāptilakṣaṇam  || 7.1 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

śṛṇu devi yathātathyaṃ devyā māhātmyamuttamam  |
kārye vātha akārye vā uktānukteṣu vastuṣu  || 7.2 ||

kubjīśānīṃ japedyastu tasya pāpaṃ na vidyate  |
yaḥ punaḥ śuddhabhāvātmā samayākhyāṃ maheśvarīm  || 7.3 ||

japettasya pa tadvastu yanna sidhyati bhūtale  |
etatparamasamayaṃ devītantre prakīrtitam  || 7.4 ||

na deyaṃ yasya kasyāpi yasmātsarvaṃ prapadyate  |
tantre tantre tu samayā kathitānekadhā mayā  || 7.5 ||

anyakalpopacāreṣu niruddhā tatra śāsane  |
eṣā sā samayā devi atra sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam  || 7.6 ||
catuṣpīṭheṣu samayāste'tra sarve vinirgatāḥ  |
eṣā rājeśvarī devī samayācāranirgatā  || 7.7 ||

nirācāreṇa yogena sidhyate hyavicārataḥ  |
pañcavyāptamataḥ sarvaṃ sarvavyāptyantasaṃsthitam  || 7.8 ||

asyoccāraṇamātreṇa tannāsti yanna sādhayet |
kampate bhuvanaṃ sarvaṃ trailokyaṃ suraḍāmaram  || 7.9 ||

samayākhyaṃ japantasya kṣubhyate sacarācaram  |
vāmadakṣiṇatantreṣu sāmānyā samayā parā  || 7.10 ||

tasyā devyāḥ prabhāvo'yaṃ yā pañcāśapadātmikā  |
siddhamārge'nyathā devi dvātriṃśaguṇalakṣitā  || 7.11 ||

kubjikā nāma vikhyātā samayasthā kuleśvarī  |
yatra viśveśvaraṃ sarvaṃ samayādyaṃ vinirgatam  || 7.12 ||

mantramudrāgaṇo hyatra vidyāmaṇḍalakādikam  |
yasyāḥ kamalinī devī hṛdisthā sampravartate  || 7.13 ||

yayā sṛṣṭamidaṃ sarvamābrahmastambhagocaram  |
kuladīpā śirasthāsyāḥ ṣaḍvidhādhvaprabodhikā  || 7.14 ||

barbarākhyā śikhā hyasyāstritattvordhvavyavasthitā  |
mudrādhāragatā devī bahurūpātra nirgatā  || 7.15 ||

chādayantī samastāṃ tu śabdarāśiṃ tu mālinī  |
kavacaṃ yasyā mahādevyā mantramāyātmakaṃ mahat || 7.16 ||

kiṇkiṇiṃ taṃ pracaṇḍograṃ tejodedīpyavarcasam  |
jñeyaṃ vṛddhopamaṃ netraṃ tattvārthaguṇasaṅkulam  || 7.17 ||

saṃvartādiśivāntasthaṃ ṣaḍasraṃ piṅgalocanam  |
tadastraṃ koṅkaṇeśānyā yena vyāptaṃ ṣaḍadhvaram  || 7.18 ||


śrīkubjikā uvāca
kathaṃ tu kubjikā nātha vada mantrapadānvitam  |
sarvajñā sarvadā devī lakṣaṇena samanvitā  || 7.19 ||

uvāca bhairavo hyevaṃ kubjikāṃ śṛṇu kubjike  |
kiṃ tu tvayā na vaktavyā yāvannādeśitaḥ śiśuḥ  || 7.20 ||

ccevīti padaṃ prathamaṃ ṇiki ṇiki dvitīyakam  |
chīṃ chāṃ padaṃ tṛtīyaṃ tu khimurāgho-a caturthakam  || 7.21 ||

me na ṇa ña ṅa pañcamaṃ hrauṃ hrīṃ hrāṃ ṣaṣṭhamaṃ padam  |*
yaikābjikuśrī saptamaṃ tivagabha mona aṣṭamam  || 7.22 ||*

vilomena padānyaṣṭau dvātriṃśākṣaramālikā  |
pañcapraṇava-m-ādyantā viyuktā lakṣaṇādhikā  || 7.23 ||

ādikūṭāvasāne tu catvāriṃśaddhi mālinī  |
vilomenoddhareddevī[ṃ]  guruvaktropadeśataḥ  || 7.24 ||

rephasahamidaṃ kūṭaṃ vidyāsaptamakaṃ padam  |
śrīlope sanniyoktavyaṃ jīvitaṃ kubjike mama  || 7.25 ||

svamanīṣikāto'nyathā sa vidviṣṭo marīcibhiḥ  |
yasmādbhāṇḍāramityevaṃ sarvasvaṃ yoginīkule  || 7.26 ||

atha cetsarvapīṭheṣu māteyaṃ samayātmikā  |
asyāḥ smaraṇamātreṇa vihvalaṃ tu jagattrayam  || 7.27 ||

bhavate nātra sandeha iti mātā surakṣitā  |
