hṛdayādyastraparyantamekoccāreṇa suvrate  || 7.28 ||

siddhamārgaṃ yathā brūmi vilomena vilomataḥ  || 7.29 ||

yastrā yaivvāṇāṅkako ccevi ṇiki ṇiki  |
yayātratrane yaikārintāhama chīṃ chāṃ  |
yacāvaka yaipārūhuba khimurāgho-a me na ṇa ña ṅa |
yaikhāśi kheśirarbaba hrauṃ hrīṃ hrām |
seraśi yaipādīlaku yaikābjikuśrī |
yayādahṛ yailāmatkahṛ tivagabha mona  || 7.30 ||
pañcadaśākṣaraṃ hṛdayaṃ śiraścaiva trayodaśa  |
ekādaśākṣarā śikhā viṃśadekona kavacam  || 7.31 ||

netraṃ trayodaśaiḥ proktamastraṃ caiva caturdaśam  |
pañcapraṇava-m-ādyantā yathā vidyā tathā kuru  || 7.32 ||

etatkaulikabhāṣāyāṃ kathitaṃ tu sapratyayam  |
saṃsphuṭaṃ guruvaktrasthaṃ vilomasthaṃ na sidhyati  || 7.33 ||

kaulikedaṃ samākhyātaṃ siddhamārgasudurlabham  |
ccevi ti prathamaṃ padaṃ ṇiki ṇiki dvitīyakam  || 7.34 ||

chīṃ chāṃ caiva tṛtīyaṃ syāthrauṃ hrīṃ hrāṃ rdhvekhośitri caturthakam  |
hreṃ me na ṇa ña ṅa pañcamaṃ khimurāgho-aśrī ṣaṣṭham  || 7.35 ||

yaikābjiku mona hrīṃ saptamam  |
raphasaha eaṃ kūṭamaiṃ vilomena cāṣṭamam  || 7.36 ||

dvātriṃśadakṣarā devī niyuktā guṇaśālinī  |
ādikūṭakrameṇaiva vilomenoddhṛtā iyam  || 7.37 ||

guruvaktropadeśena saṃsphuṭaṃ kathitaṃ tava  || 7.38 ||

stram-a vvāṇāṅkako ccevi |
traṃne nijiteśvavi ṇiki ṇiki caṃvaka keghvila chīṃ chām |
yaikhāśi kerintāhama hrauṃ hrīṃ hrāṃ me na ṇa ña ṅa |
seraśi rarbaba khimurāgho-aśrī |
yaṃdahṛ yaikābjikulamaka mona hrīṃ hsphremaiṃ  || 7.39 ||


sarvasādhāraṇaṃ kaulaṃ brūmi anyopadeśataḥ  |
pañcamaṃ tu padādisthaṃ hṛdayaṃ ca daśākṣaram  || 7.40 ||

śiramaṣṭākṣaraṃ viddhi dvādaśārdhaṃ śikhā smṛtā  |
dvisaptakaṃ ca kavacaṃ netraṃ saptākṣaraṃ śubham  || 7.41 ||

astraṃ navākṣaraṃ proktaṃ jātayaśca pṛthakpṛthak |
kavacāntaṃ caturvaktraṃ pañcamaṃ tu tadagrataḥ  || 7.42 ||
paripāṭistu vaktrāṇāmūrdhvavaktrāditaḥ kramāt |
eṣā sā samayā devī kulamārge vyavasthitā  || 7.43 ||

sakalasthā tu sācārā aśeṣārthaviśodhikā  |
kaulabhāṣoditā yā tu sā tu siddhā kulānvaye  || 7.44 ||

aśeṣārthapradātārā anekārthaprabodhikā  |
yāntyanena tu dehena khecaratvaṃ tadāśritāḥ  || 7.45 ||

akṣarākṣarasantānaṃ yojayellakṣasaṅkhyayā  |
kubjīśaguṇatulyo'sau hartā kartā svayaṃ prabhuḥ  || 7.46 ||

khecarīṇāṃ padaṃ so hi paśyate hyavicārataḥ  |
nirācāreṇa yogena cintayantaḥ kuleśvarīm  || 7.47 ||

atha sāmānyarūpeṇa tadā bhūcaratāṃ vrajet |
kupitaḥ pātayecchailān śoṣayejjaladhīśvarān  || 7.48 ||

sphoṭayecchailavṛkṣāṃśca taddhyānaguṇamāśritaḥ  |
bhūcarīṇāṃ patitvaṃ ca kṣudrakarmopajīvinām  || 7.49 ||

kurute vividhāścaryaṃ pūjyate sa śivo yathā  |
yatra tiṣṭhatyasau deśe tatra vighnaṃ palāyate  || 7.50 ||

kubjikāyāśca yā dūtī kālikā nāma viśrutā  |
kālikākhye mahātantre svatantrā sā udaḥrtā  || 7.51 ||

śṛṇuṣvekamanā bhadre jñānavijñānadāyinī  |
sarvasiddhikarī devī sarvakāryaprasādhanī  || 7.52 ||

vyāghrasiṃhagajavyāla- bhūtavetālaśatravaḥ  |
smaraṇānnāśamāyānti vighnasaṅghāni yāni ca  || 7.53 ||

praśnakāle parīkṣeta kumāryāveśapūrvikā  |
śubhāśubhaṃ vadatyāśu yadbhūtaṃ yadbhaviṣyati  || 7.54 ||

asyoddhāraṃ pravakṣyāmi yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ  |
aḥ-kha-madhyagataṃ gṛhya jha-pūrveṇa samanvitam  || 7.55 ||
prathamamuddhṛtaṃ bījaṃ dvitīyaṃ ṇa-ha-sandhigam  |
bheditaṃ tu ña-pūrveṇa etadvarṇadvayaṃ punaḥ  || 7.56 ||

ā-sa-randhragataṃ gṛhya ya-sa-madhyagataṃ punaḥ  |
dvitīyena tu sambhinnaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ vai bījamuttamam  || 7.57 ||

prathamaṃ saptamaṃ jñeyaṃ dvitīyasya dvitīyakam  |
aṣṭamamuddhṛtaṃ bījaṃ navamaṃ bha-ña-madhyagam  || 7.58 ||

ñottarasamāyuktaṃ śūnyamastakabhūṣitam  |
ma-ṣa-madhyagataṃ gṛhya daśamaṃ kevalaṃ priye  || 7.59 ||

ṣa-va-madhyagatoddhṛtya au-pūrveṇa tu bheditam  |
ekādaśākṣaraṃ proktamai-ṭha-madhyagataṃ dadet || 7.60 ||

ña-pūrveṇa tu sambhinnaṃ daśadvitayamuttamam  |
e-va-randhragataṃ gṛhya kevalaṃ tridaśaṃ punaḥ  || 7.61 ||

ja-sa-madhyagataṃ gṛhya ayau-madhyena āhatam  |
caturdaśoddhṛtaṃ bījama-cha-sandhigataṃ punaḥ  || 7.62 ||

kevalaṃ kathitaṃ bījaṃ daśapañcākṣaraṃ priye  |
pa-dha-madhyagataṃ gṛhya ña-pūrveṇa tu bheditam  || 7.63 ||

ṣoḍaśamuddhṛtaṃ bījaṃ sa-ya-madhyagataṃ dadet |
la-ṭha-madhyāsanāsīnaṃ jha-pūrveṇa tu bheditam  || 7.64 ||

nādabindukalākrāntaṃ daśasaptakamuddhṛtam  |
va-ma-madhyagatoddhṛtya ṭa-ṇa-madhyāsane sthitam  || 7.65 ||

ṭa-pūrveṇa tu sambhinnaṃ śūnyayuktaṃ daśāṣṭamam  |
vahniyuktaṃ mahāprāṇamaṃ-pūrveṇa tu bheditam  || 7.66 ||

caturdaśasvarākrāntaṃ bindunādasaśaktigam  |
viṃśamaṃ nyūnamekena uddhṛtaṃ bījamuttamam  || 7.67 ||

ya-ḍha-madhyagataṃ gṛhya kevalaṃ viṃśamaṃ bhavet |
kha-pūrvavarṇamuddhṛtya tha-pūrvāsanasaṃsthitam  || 7.68 ||
viṃśamekādhikaṃ bhadre aḥ-kha-madhyagataṃ punaḥ  |
ṝ-pūrvāsanamārūḍhaṃ dvāviṃśatimudāhṛtam  || 7.69 ||

da-ca-randhragatoddhṛtya ta-pūrvāsanasaṃsthitam  |
viṃśatritayamākhyātaṃ ya-ja-madhyagataṃ punaḥ  || 7.70 ||

pañcamasvarasaṃyuktaṃ caturviṃśatimaṃ punaḥ  |
ga-pūrvaṃ tu samuddhṛtya caturthasvarasaṃyutam  || 7.71 ||

pañcaviṃśa samākhyātamo-ṣa-madhyagataṃ punaḥ  |
ai-pūrveṇa tu sambhinnaṃ ṣaḍviṃśakamudāhṛtam  || 7.72 ||

sa-ta-madhyagataṃ cānyaṃ ṭa-pareṇa samāhatam  |
saptāviṃśatimaṃ bhadre ā-sa-madhyagataṃ punaḥ  || 7.73 ||

jha-pūrveṇa samāyuktamaṣṭāviṃśa tu pārvati  |
punarevaṃ dadeddevi triṃśatyūnaṃ sabindukam  || 7.74 ||

gha-na-madhyagataṃ gṛhya kevalaṃ triṃśamaṃ bhavet |
dha-ha-randhragataṃ devi vāyvāsanasamanvitam  || 7.75 ||

triṃśamekādhikaṃ proktaṃ ka-ṣa-madhyagataṃ punaḥ  |
tha-ṇa-madhyāsanārūḍhaṃ pañcamasvarayojitam  || 7.76 ||

dvātriṃśamaṃ samākhyātaṃ kevalaṃ va-ṣa-madhyagam  |
trayastriṃśa samuddiṣṭaṃ ṇa-tha-madhyagataṃ punaḥ  || 7.77 ||

vahninā dīpitaṃ kṛtvā triṃśamaṃ caturādhikam  |
sa-ta-randhragataṃ bījaṃ kevalaṃ sṛṣṭisaṃyutam  || 7.78 ||

pañcatriṃśa smṛtā varṇāḥ pañcapraṇavasampuṭāḥ  |
yojitavyā mahāvidyā kālikā siddhikāṅkṣiṇā  || 7.79 ||

adṛśīkaraṇe hyeṣā sarvasampadadāyinī  |
na deyā duṣṭabuddhīnāṃ devīdūtyā mahābalā  || 7.80 ||

dūtī tu kathitā hyevaṃ mudrābandhamataḥ śṛṇu  |
padmāsanasthito yogī samakāya ṛjuḥśiraḥ  || 7.81 ||
recya vāyuṃ svakāddehātpunarākṛṣya dhārayet |
hṛdaye yaḥ sthito granthistasya nābhau kṣipenmanaḥ  || 7.82 ||

mantraṃ caiva tathātmānamekīkṛtya trayaṃ budhaḥ  |
daṇḍākāraṃ nayet'tāvadyāvadbrahmabilāntagam  || 7.83 ||

tatsthānātprerayettūrṇaṃ mahāyānena sundari  |
karābhyāṃ caiva tarjanyāṃ pīḍayeta punaḥ punaḥ  || 7.84 ||

lalanāghaṇṭike yojya pañcamaṃ sthānamākramet |
ākramedguhyacakraṃ tu karaṇaṃ cordhvamūlakam  || 7.85 ||

lagne granthitrayaṃ devi khagatirnātra saṃśayaḥ  |
aṅgaṣaṭkaṃ śṛṇuṣvedaṃ kubjikāyāḥ kuleśvari  || 7.86 ||

hṛdayena tu devyāyāḥ kṣobhayeccāsurīgaṇam  |
navalakṣakṛte jāpye rājikālavaṇe hute  || 7.87 ||

rājarājeśvarāṇāṃ tu martyaloke'thavā patiḥ  |
sāmānyajapahomena sadyaḥsampadadāyinaḥ  || 7.88 ||

strījanaṃ kṣobhayetsarvaṃ bālavṛddhayuvān paśūn  |
śiro [']dhiṣṭhitayogena bhūtavetālarākṣasān  || 7.89 ||

yakṣiṇīyakṣakanyāśca piśācīnāṃ ca sādhanam  |
kurute vividhāścaryaṃ phalapuṣpādikarṣaṇam  || 7.90 ||

yakṣiṇyākarṣaṇaṃ devi mṛtakotthāpanādikam  |
sākinīkulasāmānyaḥ pāśacchedaṃ paśugraham  || 7.91 ||

kurute vividhāścaryaṃ śiraḥsiddho hyanekadhā  |
asiddhasya tu karmāṇi karmayogādvadāmyaham  || 7.92 ||

aśaktahsādhane vīrastasyedaṃ dveṣaṇaṃ prati  |
śiroruhasamutpannā caṇḍālī juṣṭapūrvikā  || 7.93 ||

rakṣaṇārthaṃ tu sā dūtī śāsane sampratiṣṭhitā  |
paṭhanādeva saṃsiddhā japahomavivarjitā  || 7.94 ||
śiraso vaśagā dūtī tadājñā nigrahātmikā  || 7.95 ||

oṃ hāsvā ṭpha 2 hūṃ 2 sagra 2 yaramā kaṃmva 2 capa 2 madha 2 hada 2 naha ṇiridhāṅgaṭvākhalapāka ṇikṣabhasamāṃradhiru līṇḍācāṣṭaju tivagabha mona oṃ  || 7.96 ||

vilomena kṛtābhyāsamuddharedupadeśataḥ  |
sampūjya yoginīṣaṭkaṃ rāmaṇīśirasānvitam  || 7.97 ||

dāghamutpādayetprathamaṃ lekhya mātrā na saṃśayaḥ  |
ṣaḍasrapuramadhyasthaṃ rakāraṃ tu adhordhvataḥ  || 7.98 ||

rakāraṃ tu tadevaṃ syādbahiṣkoṇe pṛthakpṛthak |
ṣaṭprakāraṃ pradātavyaṃ jvālāsañchannalāñchitam  || 7.99 ||

kopakāle samutpanne citivastre nṛcarmaje  |
likhitavyaṃ sakruddhena viṣonmattarasena ca  || 7.100 ||

śmaśānāṅgārasaṃyuktaṃ sādhyanāmaṃ tu madhyataḥ  |
likhitvā tāpayetpaścājjvaro bhavati dāruṇaḥ  || 7.101 ||

jvaramutpādayitvā tu sadantamānayecchiraḥ  |
pūrvadravyairlikhitvā tu nāma tasya gale kṣipet || 7.102 ||

viparītamukhaṃ kṛtvā ūrdhvagrīvaṃ yathā bhavet |
tathā saṃsthāpayedbhūmau kapālaṃ mantravitsudhīḥ  || 7.103 ||

śmaśāne vā nadītīre kṛtvā vedīṃ tadūrdhvataḥ  |
paścāddhomaṃ prakurvīta ugradravyaiḥ samāhitaḥ  || 7.104 ||

viṣeṇa gandhakenaiva kunaṭyā tālakena ca  |
rājikālavaṇenaiva nimbapattraistrisaptakam  || 7.105 ||

prathame'hani chāgāntraṃ raktāktaṃ homayedbudhaḥ  |
paścāddhyānaṃ prakurvīta kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ sudāruṇam  || 7.106 ||

jvalantaṃ pādasandhīni mastakāntaṃ vicintayet |
rakāraṃ tu lalāṭasthaṃ japenmantraṃ punaḥ punaḥ  || 7.107 ||

homamaṇḍalakaṃ yacca caturasraṃ vajralāñchitam  |
evaṃ vai bhavate kālo yadi sākṣātsacīpatiḥ  || 7.108 ||

evaṃ nigrahamākhyātaṃ śirodevyāḥ samudbhavam  |
caṇḍālīti prayogo'yaṃ gopitavyaṃ prayatnataḥ  || 7.109 ||

śāsanasya ca yo dveṣṭā yo dveṣṭā guravopari  |
teṣvamoghinī cāṇḍālī yojayetparamārthataḥ  || 7.110 ||

lakṣam-eke kṛte jāpye vācāmātreṇa mārayet |
ato'rthaṃ gopayeddevi siddhājñāmoghacaṇḍikā  || 7.111 ||

śirodūtī parā hyeṣā kṣudrakarmasvanekadhā  |
sarvaṃ svacchandadeveśī kariṣyati śikho[j]jvalā  || 7.112 ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate mantroddhāre ṣaḍaṅgavidyādhikāro nāma saptamaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************



śrīkubjikā uvāca

śrutaṃ deva mayākhyātamaśeṣārthasuvistaram  |
kathaṃ devyāḥ śikhāsaṃsthā svacchandaḥ katirūpadhṛk || 8.1 ||

prayogavipulaṃ deva sarvopāyavivarjitam  |
adhunāśrotumicchāmi sārātsārataraṃ vibho  || 8.2 ||

āpyāyanaṃ śarīrasya ākāśādiprasādhanam  |
arcanaṃ caiva saṅkṣepādgrahamardakaraṃ yathā  || 8.3 ||

ripumardakaraṃ caiva bilayantraprasādhanam  |
jvaraduṣṭaviṣādīnāṃ sarvaduḥkhavimardanam  || 8.4 ||

yathā smaraṇamātreṇa vyādhito mucyate kṣaṇāt |
dharmakāmārthasaṃsiddhamarthamokṣaprasādhanam  || 8.5 ||

vaśīkaraṇakarmāṇi ākarṣaṇavidhikriyā  |
divyādivyeṣu kāryeṣu nāgakāryeṣu bhairava  || 8.6 ||

śarīrasthaṃ yathā brūhi nāḍisthaṃ mudrayā saha  |
saṅkṣepārcanakarma ca saṃsiddhaṃ bhogasādhanam  || 8.7 ||

vratayāgavihīnaṃ ca vittopāyavivarjitam  |
smaraṇātkevalo mantraḥ sukhamutpādayedyathā  || 8.8 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

sādhu kubjini bhadre tvaṃ kathayataḥ śṛṇuṣva me  |
śikhā guṇakalā yasya svacchandasyāmitadyuteḥ  || 8.9 ||

kubjikāyāḥ śikhā raudrā raudrasiddhipradāyikā  |
sārātsārataraṃ devi satyaṃ satyaṃ na cānyathā  || 8.10 ||

aghorānna paro mantraḥ kasmāccūlīgatastu saḥ  |
śamanaṃ sarvaduḥkhānāṃ vyādhīnāṃ ca nikṛntanam  || 8.11 ||

sarvānugrahakaṃ devi bhuktimuktipradāyakam  |
kālanirṇāśanaṃ devi jarāsiṃhamudāhṛtam  || 8.12 ||

dāridraśamanaṃ cedamacireṇa gaṇāmbike  |
āśāḥ saṃśodhayitvā tu devyā nyāsaṃ hi pūrvavat || 8.13 ||

hṛdayādiprabhedena astrāntaṃ yāvadāvadhim  |
svasthānanyāsavinyāsaṃ pūrvavacca yathāsthitam  || 8.14 ||

śikhāsvacchandadeveśaṃ mantredaṃ pārameśvaram  |
ṣaḍaṅgayajanādvātha nirācāreṇa sidhyati  || 8.15 ||

tato mudrāṃ parāṃ baddhvā cintayedyonimadhyagām  |
mahāmudreti vikhyātā nākhyātā kasyacinmayā  || 8.16 ||

dhyātvā pretaṃ purā devi sarvakāraṇakāraṇam  |
mahāyogī mahāsiddhaḥ sarvalokadharaḥ prabhuḥ  || 8.17 ||

sarvajñaguṇasaṃyuktaṃ padmaṃ tasyopari sthitam  |
karṇikopari dīpyantaṃ prajvalantaṃ mahaujasam  || 8.18 ||

anantaṃ tadvijānīyāttasyordhve tacchikhāśivam  |
aṣṭapattrāsanāsīnaṃ dvātriṃśārcibhirāvṛtam  || 8.19 ||

nānālaṅkārasampannaṃ nānāvarṇaṃ vicintayet |
daśabāhuṃ mahāghoraṃ caturvaktraṃ sulālasam  || 8.20 ||

sarvavarṇadharaṃ devamatha śyāmaṃ vincintayet |
kapālaṃ caiva khaṭvāṅgamanyatparaśuśūladhṛk || 8.21 ||

ḍamaruṃ cākṣamālāṃ ca phalaṃ haste niveśayet |
gajacarmadharau cobhau hastau tu parikīrtitau  || 8.22 ||

garjantaṃ bhīṣaṇaṃ nādaṃ sarvakāraṇakāraṇam  |
bhakṣantaṃ cintayedvyādhiṃ viśveśvarakujeśvaram  || 8.23 ||

khādyamānāṃ raṭantīṃ tāṃ tāḍyamānāṃ vibheditām  |
bhedayantīṃ triśūlena chedayantīṃ mahāsinā  || 8.24 ||

kruddhabhāvāddhunantīṃ tāṃ pūrvavyādhiṃ vicintayet |
tasyopari tamaikāramīśatattvāvadhisthitam  || 8.25 ||

śikhāsvacchandadeveśaṃ tatsthāpyopari pūjayet |
svakīyāṅgasamudbhūtamekavīrāṅgapañcakam  || 8.26 ||

āgneyyāṃ hṛdayaṃ nyasya śirastvīśānagocare  |
śikhāṃ śikhātmakāṃ rakṣe tanutrāṇaṃ tu vāyave  || 8.27 ||

astraṃ diśāsu vinyasya bhūyo madhye prapūjayet |
juṣṭacaṇḍeśvaraṃ kṣetre pūjādau vighnarāṭkule  || 8.28 ||

calādīnāmadhiṣṭhānaṃ jānīyādgurupaṅktivat |
dhūpacandananaivedyaṃ trayāṇāṃ prathamaṃ dadet || 8.29 ||

siddhisādhanayuktasya mārgo'yaṃ hyavicārakaḥ  |
nirācārapadasthānāṃ  kṣetracaṇḍīśavighnarāṭ || 8.30 ||
balipūjāsu naivedyaṃ trayāṇāṃ prathamaṃ dadet |
paścātkramasya kubjīśe ataḥ śāṭhyaṃ na kārayet || 8.31 ||

pāramparyakramaṃ pūjya paścānmaṇḍalakopari  |
śikhāsvacchandadeveśaṃ yāmalaṃ tu catuṣkalam  || 8.32 ||

hṛttanutrāṇaparyantaṃ yajeddevaṃ catuṣkalam  |
yogamārgāvalambānāṃ śrīsiddhākhyaṃ catuṣkalam  || 8.33 ||

pūjya svacchandadeveśaṃ kramāgre maṇḍalopari  |
tato japecchikhānāthamaghoraṃ parameśvaram  || 8.34 ||

praṇavādyaṃ namaskāramasiddhānāṃ niyuñjayet |
siddhārthayogayuktānāmaiṃpādādyantayojitam  || 8.35 ||

tato' gnipūjanaṃ kṛtvā yathā pūjā tathāhutim  |
sahasraṃ vā śataṃ vātha pañcāśatpañcaviṃśa vā  || 8.36 ||

tilairhomaṃ prakurvīta dadhimadhvājyasaṃyutaiḥ  |
ghṛtasaktvā ca madhunā sarvaduḥkhapramardakam  || 8.37 ||

vyādhinirṇāśanaṃ kubji śeṣahomaṃ tu bhūtidam  |
sahasreṇa mahābhūtiḥ śatena vyādhināśanam  || 8.38 ||

śatamaṣṭaśataṃ kubji devatulyo bhaviṣyati  |
sarvaduḥkhavinirmuktaṃ japapūjāsamanvitam  || 8.39 ||

śatato' ṣṭasahasreṇa triṣkālena tu sundari  |
ṣaṇmāsājjāyate siddhiḥ sākṣātpaśyati bhairavīm  || 8.40 ||

yatheṣṭaṃ jāyate siddhirnāstyatra-m-anṛtaṃ vacaḥ  |
sahasreṇa jvaraṃ yāti chāgasya piśitairhutaiḥ  || 8.41 ||

triṣkālaṃ māsamekaṃ tu sahasraṃ hunate tu saḥ  |
sidhyate māṃsahomena kṣaudrājyadadhisaṃyutam  || 8.42 ||

yavakṣīrānnahomena śālitaṇḍulasādhitam  |
prīyate tu śikhādevaḥ svacchando ghorarūpadhṛk || 8.43 ||
dadhihomātparā puṣṭiḥ kṣīrahomena śāntikam  |
ṣaṇmāsāttu ghṛtaṃ hutvā sarvavyādhivināśanam  || 8.44 ||

rājayakṭmaṃ tilairhomādāyuvṛddhiryavairhutaiḥ  |
kuṣṭhasyaiva sadā homāttriyuktaistaṇḍulaiḥ priye  || 8.45 ||

samasaktughṛtenāśu nāśayeta bhagandaram  |
tilairhomaṃ prakurvīta dadhimadhvājyasaṃyutam  || 8.46 ||

vyādhinirṇāśanaṃ kubji śeṣahomastu bhūtidaḥ  |
ghṛtakevalahomena sarvavyādhinikṛntanam  || 8.47 ||

prayogaṃ sampravakṣyāmi yaduktaṃ te purā mayā  |
dharmakāmārthamokṣāṇāṃ caturvargaphalodayam  || 8.48 ||

tava kubji pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvekamanādhunā  |
sarvavyādhiharaṃ dhyānaṃ paraṃ puṣṭivivardhanam  || 8.49 ||

āśāṃ saṃśodhayetpūrvaṃ nyāsaṃ kṛtvā tu pūrvakam  |
pūrvaṃ nyasya ca mantreśaṃ nāḍīvarṇaistathākṣaraiḥ  || 8.50 ||

adhaḥsrotaṃ tu vāmena dakṣiṇordhvagataṃ priye  |
nyāsaṃ kṛtvā śarīre tu mantrarājamanusmaret || 8.51 ||

pañcapraṇava-m-ādyena aghoreṇa surādhipe  |
adhyuṣṭamātrāduttīrṇaṃ jñātvā mantramanusmaret || 8.52 ||

akulāditrimadhyasthaṃ kulā[c] cādestrimadhyagam  |
madhyamāditrimadhyasthaṃ piṇḍādestu trimadhyagam  || 8.53 ||

trayārdhamātrasaṃyuktaṃ praṇavedaṃ śikhāśivam  |
trināḍīpiṇḍasambhūtaṃ mudrayā cordhvadīpitam  || 8.54 ||

tripakṣakṣayakartāraṃ tridhābaddhaṃ triśūlinam  |
trimūrtiguṇasambhūtaṃ tenāsau tridaśeśvaraḥ  || 8.55 ||

trimārgavihitaṃ śāntaṃ tripathāntasamudbhavam  |
tripathena vinā bhadre bhrājate yonimaṇḍalam  || 8.56 ||
yoniṃ vinā na niṣpattirdivyādivyeṣu vastuṣu  |
uttamottamamadhyasthā kanyasāntavyavasthitā  || 8.57 ||

bindu śaktistathā nādaṃ mātrātrayamudāhṛtam  |
trayāṇāmapi saṃyogānniṣpadyeta bhagālayam  || 8.58 ||

parārdhamātrasambhinnaṃ praṇavo'yaṃ kulāgame  |
o-ma-kārasaṃyuktaṃ praṇavedaṃ kriyātmakam  || 8.59 ||

sādākhyeśvararudrāṇāṃ brahmaviṣṇu-r-anukramāt |
ete te praṇavāḥ pañca kriyākāraṇagocare  || 8.60 ||

praṇavādisamudbhūtāḥ pañcaite guṇavattarāḥ  |
pañcapraṇava-m-ādyantaṃ tatordhve tu śikhāśivam  || 8.61 ||

evaṃ tu praṇavaṃ divyaṃ sugopyaṃ prakaṭīkṛtam  |
atra devi sphuṭaṃ tubhyaṃ bhrāntaṃ cātra jagattrayam  || 8.62 ||

jñātvevaṃ saṃsmaredyastu sannidhāno'sti tasya vai  |
sudurlabhaḥ prayogo'yaṃ guruvaktrāttu labhyate  || 8.63 ||

yatrotpannaṃ tato yāti layaṃ kṛtvā surādhipe  |
utpattipralayaṃ jñātvā tato mantramanusmaret || 8.64 ||

yatkiñcitkurute kāryaṃ sādhakaḥ sādhanātmakaḥ  |
uccarettu layāntasthaṃ tarjanyāgre vyavasthitam  || 8.65 ||

nāḍīsūtreṇa vinyastaṃ bahirante ca mātaraḥ  |
yā nāḍī sā bhavedvarṇastayā nāḍyā tu veṣṭayet || 8.66 ||

yadi candraṃ vaheccakraṃ sūryaṃ vā cakramuttamam  |
tasya madhye svayaṃ sthitvā viśvo'hamiti cintayet || 8.67 ||

ahaṃ brahmā tathā viṣṇurahaṃ devo maheśvaraḥ  |
bhairavo'hamiti devi cintayitvā tu sādhakaḥ  || 8.68 ||

hṛnmadhye cintayeccakraṃ nāḍīvarṇaistathākṣaraiḥ  |
ādyakṣaraṃ japenmantraṃ punarādyaṃ niyojayet || 8.69 ||
evaṃ saṃsmṛtya vidhivatsarvakarmāṇi sādhayet |
arcanaṃ havanaṃ dhyānaṃ japamekāntarūpiṇam  || 8.70 ||

karma kṛtvā kujeśāni kujeśāya nivedayet |
tato dhyānaṃ prakurvīta viśuddhenāntarātmanā  || 8.71 ||

sa eva mantramuccārya ādyādau yāvadantimam  |
nādena tu gatiṃ kuryātsvacchandagatibhāvitaḥ  || 8.72 ||

brahmaṃ bhittvā tato viṣṇuṃ rudramīśvarameva ca  |
setumadhyena gamanaṃ kuñcikodghāṭayedbilam  || 8.73 ||

udghāṭya paramaṃ sthānamaghoraṃ yatra saṃsthitam  |
aṣṭākapāla ghorīśaṃ tryakṣaraṃ samanusmaret || 8.74 ||

sarvamantreṣu hṛdayaṃ yatkubjīśaśikhātmakam  |
manasā smṛtamātreṇa khecaratvaṃ prajāyate  || 8.75 ||

sarvavighnopaśamanaṃ mantraṃ tryakṣaramuttamam  |
śeṣaṣaṭkaṃ tu yaddevi tadaṅgānyasya kalpayet || 8.76 ||

japtavyaṃ tu śikhāsūtraṃ sakṛtsiddhiḥ prajāyate  |
ākāśādiprasiddhyarthaṃ siddhiranyāsu kā kathā  || 8.77 ||

mantrasannaddhadehastu sarvāvastho'pi sādhakaḥ  |
tiṣṭhan jāgran svapan gacchan bhuñjāno maithune rataḥ  || 8.78 ||

caryādhārī nirācāro mantrasaṃsmaraṇācchuciḥ  |
sāmānyasmaraṇādeva vyādhibhirnābhibhūyate  || 8.79 ||

prajvalan dṛśyate bhūtairyasyedaṃ tu śarīragam  |
ataḥ kiṃ bahunoktena siṃhasyaiva yathā mṛgāḥ  || 8.80 ||

gandhena pralayaṃ yānti satyaṃ satyaṃ mahātape  |
japena sādhayetsarvaṃ vratastho yastu sādhakaḥ  || 8.81 ||

pūrvameva japellakṣaṃ sidhyate ghoramūrdhajam  |
aviditvā vidhānena kiñcitkāryaṃ na sādhayet || 8.82 ||
yaḥ kuryādvidhihīnaṃ tu sa vighnaiścābhibhūyate  |
tasmātpadārthanavakaṃ jñātavyaṃ tu kujeśvari  || 8.83 ||

kṣetrasthānāni suśroṇi jñātavyāni suniścitaiḥ  |
kṣetraṃ vratāni mantrāśca akṣasūtraṃ japaṃ tathā  || 8.84 ||

dhyānaṃ pūjā tathā dravyaṃ varṇaṃ mukhasamanvitam  |
mukhahīnā na sidhyanti agnihotravivarjitāḥ  || 8.85 ||

mukhamāhavanīyaṃ syāttasminmantrāḥ sadā sthitāḥ  |
aghoraṃ kālamityuktamaghoraṃ viṣṇurucyate  || 8.86 ||

aghorastvaṃ maheśāni aghoraścāhameva ca  |
bahurūpadharo hyagniḥ pracaṇḍaḥ kāla-m-antagaḥ  || 8.87 ||

sa śivaḥ paramo brahmā nirvāṇaḥ sa sadāśivaḥ  |
īśvaraḥ sa paro nityamasmātparataro na hi  || 8.88 ||

anena smṛtamātreṇa sarvaduḥkhaiḥ pramucyate  |
dāridrasiṃho'ghorīśo vyādhisiṃhaḥ kuleśvari  || 8.89 ||

pracaṇḍaduṣṭasiṃhaśca mahāpātakanāśanaḥ  |
sarvatīrthābhiṣekaśca saptajaptena jāyate  || 8.90 ||

śatajaptena devena sarvayajñaphalaṃ labhet |
dīkṣānirvāṇakārī syāttrisaptaparivartanāt || 8.91 ||

daśāvartena duritaṃ brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati  |
daśāvartādguropekṣī smaraṇādeva mucyate  || 8.92 ||

vidhihīne tathā pāne pañcabhiścopapātakī  |
śatena caiva triṣkālyaṃ varṣātsiddhiryathepsitā  || 8.93 ||

balavatāṃ ripūṇāṃ tu vyastamāvartayetprabhuḥ  |
dakṣiṇāsyo mahādevi sahasreṇa nipātayet || 8.94 ||

saṅgrāmakāle smartavyamasipattragataṃ hṛdi  |
veṣṭantaṃ mātṛbhiḥ sainyaṃ bhakṣa bhakṣeti bhāṣayet || 8.95 ||
hatadarpāḥ prajāyante na punaḥ saṃharanti ca  |
duḥsvapne dviguṇaṃ jāpyaṃ vraṇe caiva caturguṇam  || 8.96 ||

lūtā daśaguṇaṃ caiva viṣe vai viṃśatistathā  |
dine dine śataṃ japtvā vibhūtirvardhate' cirāt || 8.97 ||

prāṅmukho yasya nāmnā tu sādhyārūḍho hṛdi sthitaḥ  |
vaśībhavati rājānaṃ śatajāpyena dhīmatā  || 8.98 ||

saptāhātsa balopeto vaśībhavati nānyathā  |
dine dine sahasreṇa nāsti tadyanna sādhayet || 8.99 ||

ādityābhimukho bhūtvā sahasraṃ parivartayet |
yatkiñcidvihitaṃ citte saptāhātsādhayiṣyati  || 8.100 ||

nyastaṃ sarvāṅgikaṃ mantraṃ bhairavākārasaṃsthitam  |
sa tu bhojanakāle tu pātre sañcintya sādhakaḥ  || 8.101 ||

sampūrṇaśaśinaṃ dhyāyedbhuñjāno'mṛtamaśnute  |
sampūrṇacandramadhyasthamadhordhvasampuṭīkṛtam  || 8.102 ||

paryaṭetsādhako nityaṃ sarvaśreyamavāpnuyāt |
yadicchetsādhakaḥ siddhiṃ hṛdi kṛtvā kujeśvaram  || 8.103 ||

candramaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ svacchandagatibhāvitam  |
tatpraviṣṭaṃ vicinteta antyādantaṃ parāparam  || 8.104 ||

parasparaṃ tu sañcintya yāvadbrahmabilaṃ gataḥ  || 8.105 ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate svacchandaśikhādhikāro nāmāṣṭamaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************



śrībhairava uvāca

bhedayitvā paraṃ tattvaṃ hakāraṃ nāma nāmataḥ  |
so'ṣṭākapālo vijñeyastasyākāśaṃ tu tacchiraḥ  || 9.1 ||

aghoramiti vikhyātaṃ dvātriṃśākṣarabhūṣitam  |
tasmātsañjāyate sṛṣṭiḥ sā tu sṛṣṭirhṛdi sthitā  || 9.2 ||

dvātriṃśa mātarastāstu cakrārūḍhā vicintayet |
caṇḍā ghaṇṭā mahānāsā sumukhī durmukhī balā  || 9.3 ||

revatī prathamā ghorā saumyā bhīmā mahābalā  |
jayā ca vijayā caiva ajitā cāparājitā  || 9.4 ||

mahotkaṭā virūpākṣī śuṣkā cākāśamātarā  |
sehārī jātahārī ca daṃṣṭrālī śuṣkarevatī  || 9.5 ||

pipīlikā puṣpahārī aśanī sasyahārikā  |
bhadrakālī subhadrā ca bhadrabhīmā subhadrikā  || 9.6 ||

manasā pūjayettasthā bhakṣyabhojyādibhiḥ kramāt |
puṣpairnānāvidhairdevi nānālaṅkārakādibhiḥ  || 9.7 ||

sravantaṃ cintayettasthamamṛtaṃ sarvatomukham  |
tenāpyāyitadehastu tatkṣaṇādvirajo bhavet || 9.8 ||

yāgaṃ tu mānasaṃ kṛtvā kasya siddhirna jāyate  |
sampūrṇamaṇḍalaṃ dhyātvā aghoraṃ nāma nāmataḥ  || 9.9 ||

so'ṣṭākapālaḥ pravarastattvavyāpī nirakṣaraḥ  |
sa eva candrarūpī syātkarṇikāyāṃ vicintayet || 9.10 ||

tattvaṃ tatra mahānādaṃ hakāraṃ nāma nāmataḥ  |
ṣaṭpadārthayuto devi navakena prasidhyati  || 9.11 ||

sa eva līyate viṣṇorviṣṇu rudrasamāśritaḥ  |
sa eva kālo vijñeyaḥ sarvabhakṣo hutāśanaḥ  || 9.12 ||

sa eva līyate māyā sā ca viṣṇuḥ prakīrtitā  |
sā śaktirnirmalā kubji kālo vai yena bhakṣitaḥ  || 9.13 ||

sa viṣṇuḥ śivatāṃ yāti setuṃ bhittvā kuleśvari  |
sa ca turyapadaṃ prāpya unmanatvaṃ hi tatpadam  || 9.14 ||

āśrayaṃ devadevasya aghorasya mahātape  |
nirvāṇaṃ tu paraṃ vindyātsa kubjīśaḥ prakīrtitaḥ  || 9.15 ||

sa dhruvo vāsudevaśca ajātaḥ parikīrtitaḥ  |
tatra śaktiṃ sadā kuryāttatrāsaktaḥ sadā bhavet || 9.16 ||

na pāpairlipyate devi mahāpāpaiḥ sudāruṇaiḥ  |
na kālasya vaśaṃ gacchenna jarā na ca duḥkhitaḥ  || 9.17 ||

sarvatīrthaphalaṃ caiva sarvayajñeṣu dīkṣitaḥ  |
hṛnnādaṃ manasotthāpya vrajennirvāṇajaṃ padam  || 9.18 ||

cetasā tvamṛtaṃ gṛhya āgacchedghaṇṭikāśrayam  |
tadutthaṃ bhāratīmūle kṛtvāsau'mṛtamaśnute  || 9.19 ||

āpūrya vadanaṃ tena svacchandena kujeśvari  |
anangadhenavīṃ dugdh[ v]ā tattvaṃ vyāpyeśvareṇa tu  || 9.20 ||

aghoraṃ pañcamadhye tu ātmatattvaṃ vicintayet |
yo'gnirjvalati cāpena ekastiṣṭhati pañcadhā  || 9.21 ||

trailokyaṃ vyāpitaṃ tena yajante brahmavādinaḥ  |
tasyaiva yaḥ śikhāṃ vetti āhitāgniḥ sa ucyate  || 9.22 ||

so' gnirdevamukhaṃ vindyādaghoraḥ sarvatomukhaḥ  |
mukheṣu ca mukhaṃ devi trailokye'pi pragīyate  || 9.23 ||

vinā tena varārohe na homo na ca bhojanam  |
śuciragnirbhaveddevo bahurūpaḥ kujeśvari  || 9.24 ||

tadantaṃ tu japaṃ kuryātkṛtvā hṛtsthaṃ tu keśavam  |
adhastātsetumārgasya tiṣṭhate tu kujeśvaraḥ  || 9.25 ||

sa cāsanaṃ paraṃ tasya sevyate kiṃ na mantrarāṭ |
vidyārājeti vikhyāto mantrarājeti kathyate  || 9.26 ||
mudrārājeti mahatāṃ maṇḍalādhipatiḥ smṛtaḥ  |
brahmaviṣṇvīśvarādyeṣu patirdevi pracakṣyate  || 9.27 ||

nānena sadṛśo devi mantrakoṭiśatairapi  |
hṛdayaṃ sarvamantrāṇāṃ paramaṃ parikīrtitam  || 9.28 ||

anena hīnā deveśi mahānapi na sidhyati  |
grahayantreṣu sarveṣu vyādhiteṣu kuleśvari  || 9.29 ||

ripunāśe ca balavān dāridrabhayanāśanam  |
tasmādārādhya yatnena duḥkhasiṃhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ  || 9.30 ||

nānena sadṛśaḥ kaścinnānyo'sti sacarācare  |
devāsuramanuṣyānāṃ tattvarūpo maheśvari  || 9.31 ||

mūrdhnaḥ pādatalaṃ yāvattattvaṃ carati dehinām  |
niṣkalātsakalaṃ yāti sakalānniṣkalaṃ padam  || 9.32 ||

ekenāṃśena vīrāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ kimapi stutam  |
sa bhairavaḥ śivo bhāti sarvajñaḥ sarvajantuṣu  || 9.33 ||

yāvattiṣṭhatyasau gātre tāvajjīvanti jantavaḥ  |
vinā tena varārohe nāsti nāstīti kathyate  || 9.34 ||

tasya devādhidevasya sarvavyāpimayasya ca  |
sarvadevamayo devi kathaṃ bhaktyā na sidhyati  || 9.35 ||

yena vijñānamātreṇa smṛtenaiva tu sundari  |
akṣayān labhate lokānmuktisthānaṃ gamiṣyati  || 9.36 ||

sarvalakṣaṇahīno'pi smaraṇātkalmaṣāpahaḥ  |
aho mantrasya māhātmyaṃ japyamānasya nityaśaḥ  || 9.37 ||

vināpi layayogena yoginīsamatāṃ vrajet |
sādhakāya prayacchanti trailokyajñānamuttamam  || 9.38 ||

ākāśādi prayacchanti divyadṛṣṭiśrutāgamam  |
sarvabhūtā vaśaṃ yānti grahāścaiva viśeṣataḥ  || 9.39 ||
viṣaṃ ca nirviṣaṃ kuryāddarśanādeva sarvataḥ  |
na tasya tiṣṭhate gātre viṣaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamam  || 9.40 ||

kīṭalūtāstu bhūtāśca apamṛtyurna tiṣṭhati  |
garajaṃ yogajaṃ doṣaṃ pralayaṃ yānti dūrataḥ  || 9.41 ||

cūrṇalepāñjanādīni kuhakāni tu yāni vai  |
ye kariṣyanti ripavaḥ striyo vā puruṣasya vā  || 9.42 ||

tatkṣaṇātpralayaṃ yānti teṣāṃ pratyaṅgirā bhavet |
smaraṇāddevadevasya indraṃ yāti narottamaḥ  || 9.43 ||

jvalanto dṛśyate bhūtairhṛccakre vidhisaṃsthitaḥ  |
duṣṭāśca pralayaṃ yānti siṃhasyaiva yathā mṛgāḥ  || 9.44 ||

eko doṣo hi mantrasya japyamānasya jāyate  |
jarā mṛtyuśca dāridryaṃ vyādhayo vividhāḥ priye  || 9.45 ||

smaraṇātpralayaṃ yānti tuhinaṃ tu raveriva  |
japyate yeṣu rāṣṭreṣu deśe vā surasundari  || 9.46 ||

na rujā jāyate tatra svāmī tatra vivardhate  |
ekenāpi suputreṇa ghoradevāṅgapūjanāt || 9.47 ||

ghorīśaṃ tu yadā jñātaṃ sa kulaṃ tārayiṣyati  |
paśavaśca na naśyanti sadā vardhati gokulam  || 9.48 ||

vandhyā na jāyate nārī na mriyante ca bālakāḥ  |
jvararogādibhistasya kuṭumbaṃ naiva pīḍyate  || 9.49 ||

sarvalokasya sampūjyo jāyate rājavallabhaḥ  |
dhāraṇīyaṃ sadā gātre yathāvatpravadāmyaham  || 9.50 ||

puṣpeṇa guḍikāṃ kṛtvā mantraṃ bhūrje samālikhet |
kuṅkumena likheddevi rocanāyāthavā punaḥ  || 9.51 ||

akāracaturo madhye ātmanāma samālikhet |
mantreṇa chāditaṃ nāma aṅkusena tu rakṣitam  || 9.52 ||
māyayācchādayitvā tu śivaṃ mūrdhni gataṃ likhet |
yāṣṭaṃ ṣaṣṭhasamāyuktaṃ bindunādāṅkitaṃ priye  || 9.53 ||

tacchivaṃ tu varārohe caturāśramapūjitam  |
sarvaṃ kṣasthaṃ paraṃ mantraṃ sarvarakṣākaraṃ param  || 9.54 ||

nāmnā tu guḍikā hyeṣā sarvarogavimardanī  |
sāntā pūrvaṃ tu kartavyā tataḥ kṣasthāṃ tu kārayet || 9.55 ||

kṣakāraṃ kālamārūḍhamokāroparidīpitam  |
ṣaṣṭhasvarayutaṃ devi amaratvaṃ prayacchati  || 9.56 ||

yastu dhārayate divyāṃ guḍikāṃ śivapūjitām  |
tasya vakṣyāmi suśroṇi guṇānnānāvidhān śṛṇu  || 9.57 ||

sarvatīrtheṣu yaḥ snātaḥ sarvayajñeṣu dīkṣitaḥ  |
na bhayaṃ vidyate tasya dharaṇadajarāmaraḥ  || 9.58 ||

sarvavratāni cīrṇāni sarvatīrthanamaskṛtaḥ  |
avaniṃ vicaretsarvāṃ bhairavastu yathā hi saḥ  || 9.59 ||

sarve te darśanāttasya sādhakasya mahātmanaḥ  |
duṣṭāśca pralayaṃ yānti vyādhayo vidravanti ca  || 9.60 ||

abrahmacārī cārī syādasnātaḥ snānamāpnuyāt |
na bhayaṃ vidyate tasya saṅgrāme ca sadā jayaḥ  || 9.61 ||

abhakṣyabhakṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā agamyāgamanaṃ tathā  |
nāsau lipyati pāpena paṅkasthaṃ kamalaṃ yathā  || 9.62 ||

guḍikā tu sadā siddhā mahābhairavadhāritā  |
yogeśvarādimunibhiḥ sarvadevairnamaskṛtā  || 9.63 ||

bahunāpi kimuktena satyaṃ satyaṃ yaśasvini  |
jvalanto dṛśyate bhūtairyathā rudro makhāntakṛt || 9.64 ||

supto bhuktaḥ prabuddhaśca atha maithunamāgate  |
mahāhave mahādevi duṣṭasiṃhagajeṣu ca  || 9.65 ||
vidyudvajrāśaniścaiva utpāteṣvaśanīṣu ca  |
śatrunāśe ca gonāśe viṣaśaṅkāgataṃ ca yat || 9.66 ||

ārṇaveṣu ca sarveṣu dhāraṇānna bhayaṃ bhavet |
śākinyo vaśagāstasya duṣṭavetālarākṣasāh || 9.67 ||

śucirvāpyaśucirvāpi vidravanti diśo daśa  |
guḍikaiṣā samākhyātā trilohapariveṣṭitā  || 9.68 ||

dhāraṇīyā prayatnena śivalokamavāpnuyāt |
sarvāvasthagato vāpi muktiṃ yāti surādhipe  || 9.69 ||

matsamo dhāraṇāddevi satyaṃ satyaṃ yaśasvini  |
mayāpi dhāritā hyeṣā brahmaṇāpi tataḥ punaḥ  || 9.70 ||

viṣṇunā devarājena yuddhe daityāstu nirjitāḥ  |
agnivāyukubereṇa yamena varuṇena ca  || 9.71 ||

mātṛbhirguhyakaiścaiva garuḍena ca dhīmatā  |
dadhīcinā ca śukreṇa durvāsenāpi dhīmatā  || 9.72 ||

ṛṣibhiśca tathā sarvairdevadaityaiḥ kujeśvari  |
tatastvanyaiśca rājānairbalibhirnahuṣādibhiḥ  || 9.73 ||

yuddhe jayārthibhirdevi ugravyādhijayārthibhiḥ  |
prajāvaśyārthibhiścaiva guḍikā kaṇṭhadhāritā  || 9.74 ||

nānayā sadṛśī vidyā guḍikā bhuvi vidyate  |
piṇḍaṃ tu prathamaṃ mantryamaghoreṇa susaṃskṛtam  || 9.75 ||

bhuñjīyāccaiva niḥśaṅkaṃ tatastasyāmṛtāyate  |
diśo'bhimantrya gaccheta vāmaṃ cāgrapadaṃ nyaset || 9.76 ||

ubhayoścandramadhye tu paryaṭeta sadā sthitaḥ  |
bhuñjāne śayane caiva candramadhye sadā sthitaḥ  || 9.77 ||

candrārūḍhena satataṃ sthātavyaṃ varavarṇini  |
nāghorasadṛśo mantro mantrā yasmādvinirgatāḥ  || 9.78 ||
guruvaktrāttu vijñeyo madhye oṃkāramadhyagam  |
sa eva nādasaṃlīno yāvadbrahmabilaṃ gataḥ  || 9.79 ||

dhāraṇāddhāritaṃ kṛtvā tribhiḥ prāṇairalaṅkṛtam  |
svacchandasahitaṃ devaṃ varṇāntapariveṣṭitam  || 9.80 ||

mukhe'naṅgāṃ tato dugdhvā dhenavīṃ cāmbarāṃ priye  |
grāhyagrāhavimardaśca triśūlaṃ vaḍavāmukham  || 9.81 ||

kuñcikā ghaṇṭikā caiva rājadantāmṛtāgamam  |
āyuṣo jñānamutkrāntiraghorasya vaśe sthitaḥ  || 9.82 ||

nāghorasadṛśo mantro mantrakoṭiśatairapi  |
satyaṃ satyaṃ punaḥ satyaṃ bhūyaḥ satyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  || 9.83 ||

sarvajñaṃ paramaṃ mantraṃ muktidaṃ vyādhināśanam  |
jarāmṛtyuharaṃ devi vidyārājeti kīrtitam  || 9.84 ||

viṣuvaṃ ca sadā tatra yatra sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam  |
utpattisthitikartāraṃ yatra sarve layaṃ gatāḥ  || 9.85 ||

kiṃ na sevyati deveśi bahurūpaṃ kujeśvari  |
devādhidevaṃ paramaṃ yattatkāraṇamavyayam  || 9.86 ||

tattvavyāpīti paramaṃ vyomavyāpīti kathyate  |
brahmaviṣṇusurādīnāmutpattipralayāntikam  || 9.87 ||

aghoraṃ ghorarūpeti aghorīśa iti smṛtaḥ  || 9.88 ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate śikhākalpaikadeśo nāma navamaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************



śrībhairava uvāca

kavacasya tu māhātmyaṃ śṛṇu devi vadāmyaham  |
yena saṃrakṣayetsarvaṃ kruddhaḥ śatrūnnipātayet || 10.1 ||

āgataṃ rakṣayetkālaṃ kruddhaḥ kālaṃ vināśayet |
kālavatkulasiddho'sau tanutrāṇāvalambakaḥ  || 10.2 ||

śākinībhūtavetālānnāśayetsādhayeti ca  |
māyārūpadharo mantrī māhendraguṇaśālinaḥ  || 10.3 ||

kurute vividhāścaryaṃ picchakabhrāmaṇena vai  |
kavacaṃ tu samākhyātamasiddhabhedakṛdbhavet || 10.4 ||

asya dūtīṃ pravakṣyāmi sadyaḥsiddhāṃ kulodbhavām  |
yasyā lekhanamātreṇa prasrāvo'ṅghritalaṃ bhavet || 10.5 ||

ākhkhilla bheṭṭā durvasa ākhkhille usi ānnidi  |
āṭṭi vasaṃ viha pūrvasa aṭṭi masi ālitto u  || 10.6 ||

evaṃ pārampareṇaiva kaulabhāṣā samuddhṛtā  |
guruvaktrādvilomena tarjanyagreṣu siddhidā  || 10.7 ||

kavacaṃ tu samākhyātaṃ śṛṇu netraṃ yathāsthitam  |
netrasiddho mahāyogī lokālokaṃ carācaram  || 10.8 ||

paśyate nikhilaṃ sarvaṃ śivādyavanigocaram  |
kruddhaḥ saṃśoṣayetsarvaṃ sāgarāṃśca nadānadīn  || 10.9 ||

āpyāyati tadāvasthaṃ pañcavyāptyantagocaram  |
nirācārapadastho'sau tattvastho japate yadi  || 10.10 ||

asya dūtī parā devyā paradṛṣṭisamudbhavā  |
sadyaḥsiddhā mahādevi sadyaḥpratyayakārikā  || 10.11 ||

hāsvā yairīśvaṇḍemucā kterahāma ktera ktera oṃ  || 10.12 ||

guruvaktropadeśena pāramparyakrameṇa vai  |
tithisaṅkhyākalairyuktā kulabhāṣāsurakṣitā  || 10.13 ||

asyopacāraḥ kartavyaḥ kaumāryau dve samāharet |
gandhadhūpapayaḥpānaṃ śucau sthāne nayettu te  || 10.14 ||

śuklavastradharāṃ tāṃ vai devīṃ dhāyedyathā tu tām  |
bhāvanāntānusāreṇa mardayeddārikānanām  || 10.15 ||

śikhinocchiṣṭayogena śarīraṃ tasya lāñchitam  |
svastikena tu kumbhordhvaṃ sitavastrāvaguṇṭhitam  || 10.16 ||

kuryātsnānaṃ tu tailāktā bhuñjānastilapiṣṭakam  |
tṛptāḥ santaḥ prapaśyanti dārikānanamadhyataḥ  || 10.17 ||

yatkiñcidvāṅmayaṃ loke cintayitvā tu sādhakaḥ  |
bhūtabhavyārthanirdeśaṃ tatpasyati tadodare  || 10.18 ||

eṣā netragatā dūtī sadyaḥsiddhiphalapradā  |
kālavelāvinirmuktā sādhitā sati sarvadā  || 10.19 ||

astraṃ pracaṇḍadaṇḍograṃ sādhitaṃ vidhinā yadi  |
hṛdādau kramaśo vṛddhyā saṅkruddhaḥ saṃharetkhilam  || 10.20 ||

aśuddhaṃ śodhayetsarvaṃ sakṛduccāraṇāttu tam  |
tanna vastvantaraṃ kiñcidyadanena na sidhyati  || 10.21 ||

asya dūtī mahāmāyā śrīmadguhyeśvarī parā  |
guhyakālīti nāmena sarvāyudhavimardanī  || 10.22 ||

rakṣaṇī kālapāśānāṃ śatrūṇāṃ tu nikṛntanī  |
chedanī paramantrāṇāṃ yantramantrāpavādinām  || 10.23 ||

yasyeṣā tiṣṭhate kaṇṭhe mahākṛtyā sudāruṇā  |
tasya yaḥ kurute kiñcittasyaiva tu punarbhavet || 10.24 ||

aśubhe vā śubhe vātha karmavṛttau niyojayet |
sādhakendrasya yaḥ kaścittasya pratyaṅgirā bhavet || 10.25 ||

mahābhaye samutpanne sitagandhāmbarānvitaḥ  |
cintayanto niśābhāge śatroryuddhaṃ parasparam  || 10.26 ||

evamanyāni karmāṇi sādhayetparameśvarī  |
devyāḥ śastrasya dhāreṇa amoghotkaṭavarcasā  || 10.27 ||

śrūyatāṃ kula-m-īśāni kālasya kālarūpiṇī  |
amoghā śakti vikhyātā saṃvartāṅgasamudbhavā  || 10.28 ||

hāsvā yaikābjikuhyagu ṭpha hūṃ hreṃ hūṃ hrīṃ hreṃ lirākaṣṭrādaṃ naha naha rvānsa tān tiṣyarika taṃpirākā taṃkṛ naye kaṃdigāyoprarṇacūntratantramantrayavāndraparvosa mama ṭpha hūṃ kebjikuhyagu oṃ  || 10.29 ||

svāhā oṃ vai parityajya siddhavarṇāstriṣaṣṭi ca  |
khādakāstreti vikhyātā sarvārthaguṇarūpadhṛk || 10.30 ||

asya nāmnā pṛthaktantraṃ svatantraṃ siddhasāgaram  |
guhyakālīti nāmena sapādalakṣapūrvakam  || 10.31 ||

vyāvarṇitaṃ tu tatrasthamatra kiñcidudāhṛtam  |
kubjikāstrasya māhātmyaṃ kulālītantranirgatam  || 10.32 ||

pāramparyakramāyātamupadeśasamanvitam  |
vilomavihitaṃ sarvaṃ khādakāstre'pyayaṃ vidhiḥ  || 10.33 ||

khādakāstrasya lakṣeṇa nirācāreṇa yojayet |
māṃsāhārasvarūpasya parivartaṃ karoti ca  || 10.34 ||

tatkṣaṇādviṣṇupaṅkena lepanātsiṃharūpadhṛk |
jāyate nārasiṃhatvaṃ yaddhṛtaṃ viṣṇunā purā  || 10.35 ||

viṣṇunāpi purā cīrṇaṃ vrataṃ hyasyāḥ subhīṣaṇam  |
tena taṃ nārasiṃhatvaṃ tasya siddhaṃ sudāruṇam  || 10.36 ||

nāyātaṃ martyalokedaṃ kvacitsiddhaṃ krame sthitam  |
māhātmyaṃ gopitaṃ hyasyāḥ siddhairbhṛgupuraḥsaraiḥ  || 10.37 ||

astrasya dūtikā hyeṣā kubjikāmnāyanirgatā  |
siddhavidyāmahaughaiṣā āśusiddhā sugopitā  || 10.38 ||

kuleśvaryāṅgasambhūtā suvratā yā guṇojjvalā  |
gopitā anyatantreṣu pratyakṣā kubjikāmate  || 10.39 ||

etaddevyāṅgaṣaṭkaṃ tu nānānandapradāyakam  |
devyā hṛdayamāhātmyaṃ nityātantramaśeṣakam  || 10.40 ||

nityānandakarī dūtī devyā hṛdi samudbhavā  |
tena nityā samākhyātā svādhiṣṭhānaṃ samāśritā  || 10.41 ||

siddhātantraṃ śirodbhūtaṃ tatra devyā mahābalā  |
siddhayogeśvarī nāma raudraśaktirmahojjvalā  || 10.42 ||

anāhatena saṃyuktā raudradevyā mahābalā  |
siddhayogeśvarītantre asyāḥ kīrtiranekadhā  || 10.43 ||

devyāḥ śikhiśikhodbhūtā svacchandānekabhedataḥ  |
maṇibhedāntarālena svacchandādyaṃ vinirmitam  || 10.44 ||

svatantrā sahajā śāntā svacchandagatigāminī  |
maṇibhedaṃ pūrayantī svacchandārthaprabodhikā  || 10.45 ||

svacchandena svarūpeṇa śikhāsūtraṃ pravartate  |
svacchandāghorarūpasya tasyedaṃ tantramuttamam  || 10.46 ||

tanutrāṇasamudbhūtaṃ tantraṃ sammohanādikam  |
viśuddhibhāvanāsīnaṃ dūtyanekasusaṅkulam  || 10.47 ||

anekāścaryakartāraṃ sammohadhvaṃsakārakam  |
sammohanaṃ tu tenedaṃ māhātmyaṃ tatra tasya vai  || 10.48 ||

devyā netrasamudbhūtaṃ jyotiḥśāstraṃ svarodayam  |
ājñādhāragataṃ hyetatsāmarthyānekasaṅkulam  || 10.49 ||

kaivalyādyaṃ ca yatkiñcittannetrāṅgasamudbhavam  |
asyāṅgasya tu māhātmyaṃ jyotiṣeśvarasāgare  || 10.50 ||

paramāstrasya madhye tu khādakāstraṃ mahābalam  |
tasya vyāvarṇitaṃ pūrvaṃ tantraṃ svābhāvalakṣaṇam  || 10.51 ||

abhiṣekaṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  |
paramāstraprayogena sarvaṃ tatra na saṃśayaḥ  || 10.52 ||
śūladaṇḍaṃ samuddhṛtya nābhisthaṃ varṇamuddharet |
śūladaṇḍāsanasthaṃ tu karṇabhūṣaṇavāmakam  || 10.53 ||

vāmajaṅghāsamāyuktaṃ nitambālaṅkṛtaṃ priye  |
etaddevyāstraparamaṃ nāpuṇyo labhate sphuṭam  || 10.54 ||

kramapūjāvidhānena yathāvibhavavistaram  |
dīpamālābhiruddyotaṃ kṛtvā dhūpādhivāsitam  || 10.55 ||

śaṅkhaṃ vā kalaśaṃ vāpi abhimantrya svavidyayā  |
uttamādhamamadhyasya karmasevānusārataḥ  || 10.56 ||

tayā vidyābhiṣekaṃ tu nyastavyā kalaśe tu sā  |
śiṣyahaste tu taṃ dattvā idaṃ kūṭaṃ tu yojayet || 10.57 ||

yāvatkṣubhyatyasau hastaḥ svayameva calatyasau  |
dhāraṇādiva saṃyātaṃ yadā patati mastake  || 10.58 ||

tadā tu jāyate'sau vai sādhyalakṣaṇasādhakaḥ  |
dagdhapāpaḥ prajāyeta nātra kāryavicāraṇāt || 10.59 ||

nāśiṣyāya pradātavyaṃ na dhūrtāya na nindake  |
bhaktāya śraddadhānāya gurubhaktāya sundari  || 10.60 ||

tasya deyamidaṃ devi abhiṣekaṃ varānane  |
tadā tu sādhayetkarma yaduktaṃ karmasantatau  || 10.61 ||

3 ṭpha 3 hūṃ 2 yataghāvi 2 yataghā 2 mava 2 haka 2 ṭacapra 2 ṭaca parūnuta rataragho 2 rasphupra hrauṃ hrīṃ hrāṃ  || 10.62 ||

lakṣaṃ vai pūrvasevāyāṃ sidhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
ṣaḍaṅgaṃ ṣaṭprakāraṃ ca ṣaḍyoginyaḥ ṣaḍadhvaram  || 10.63 ||

ṣaṭprakārāṇi ṣaṭsiddhā jñātvaitān bhinnadṛṣṭinā  |
sa jānāti varārohe samastāmnāyapaddhatim  || 10.64 ||

anyathā na bhavetsiddhiḥ kiñcijjñaḥ paścimānvaye  |
śrutvā savismayaṃ vākyamānandapraṇayānvitam  || 10.65 ||

uvācedaṃ punaḥ kubjī ṣaḍadhvaṃ vada me prabho  || 10.66 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

yuktamuktaṃ ca deveśi śrūyatāṃ paramārthataḥ  |
saṅkṣepātkathayiṣyāmi śeṣānyatpurataḥ punaḥ  || 10.67 ||

bhūtaṃ bhāvaṃ tathā śāktaṃ māntraṃ raudraṃ ca śāmbhavam  |
ājñātaḥ sampravarteta ṣaḍadhvedaṃ kulānvaye  || 10.68 ||

bhūtaṃ bhuvanāvaraṇaṃ padaṃ bhāvaṃ prayujyate  |
śāktaṃ varṇāḥ samākhyātā māntraṃ dvādaśa kīrtitāḥ  || 10.69 ||

raudraṃ kalādhvaraṃ proktaṃ śāmbhavaṃ tattvalakṣaṇam  |
ājñānalavatī dīkṣā mantrāṇāṃ sādhane hitā  || 10.70 ||

sā cājñā pūrvikā siddhā anyathā tilaghātakī  |
sā ca tattvavatāṃ caiva tattvaṃ vai śāmbhavaṃ padam  || 10.71 ||

tatpadaṃ vidyate yasya sāmarthajñaḥ sa sarvaśaḥ  |
jñānamārgaprasiddhyarthaṃ dīkṣā vedhavatī śubhā  || 10.72 ||

yogyatātaḥ pradātavyā subhaktasya kulādhvare  |
sarvāsāmeva dīkṣānāṃ cottamā parikīrtitā  || 10.73 ||

tena vedho na kartavyo na jñātaṃ yāva niścayam  |
śāmbhavājñābhimānena lobhamohaḥ prakīrtitaḥ  || 10.74 ||

sāmarthyo'nyo na me tulyo ya evaṃ manyate kudhīḥ  |
ājñātaḥ sampravarteta kiṃ tu bhūtavatī bhavet || 10.75 ||

atha cetparipakvasya ṣaḍvidho hyalpasvalpavat |
pṛthivyādīni bhūtāni cāviśanti ca yasya vai  || 10.76 ||

bhūtāveśaṃ tu tadviddhi bhāvāveśamataḥ śṛṇu  |
śabdaḥ sparśastathā rūpaṃ raso gandhaśca bhāvajam  || 10.77 ||

śrotraṃ tvakcakṣuṣī jihvā ghrāṇaṃ śaktimano viduḥ  |
vācā pāṇistathā pādaṃ pāyūpasthaṃ tu māntrajam  || 10.78 ||

mano buddhistathā garvaḥ prakṛtau guṇa raudrajam  |
puruṣādinivṛt[t]yantamunmanatvaṃ parāntikam  || 10.79 ||

etatte śāmbhavaṃ jñānaṃ bhuvanādyaṃ mahāhradam  || 10.80 ||


śrīkubjikā uvāca

bhūtādiśāmbhavāntasya bhedopāyaṃ pṛthakpṛthak |
kathitaṃ tu yathā nātha tathā tatpratyayaṃ vada  || 10.81 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

sādhu devi mahāprājñe kathayāmi sapratyayam  |
anyathā tatkathaṃ tasya bhrāntijñānaṃ vinaśyati  || 10.82 ||

kampate bhramate rodeccotpatennipatedvadet |
anibaddharavonmādī sasaṃjño bhūtavadyathā  || 10.83 ||

bhūtāveśasya cihnedaṃ bhāvāveśamataḥ śṛṇu  |
yāni cihnāni jāyante bhāvaviddhasya bhāvini  || 10.84 ||

ghūrmaṇaṃ svedaromāñca aśrupātāṅgamoṭanam  |
ārādhya smaraṇādevaṃ sampadyante svabhāvadhṛk || 10.85 ||

bhramate cakravatpātaḥ kāṣṭhavatkṣubhitekṣaṇaḥ  |
paśyate vibhramāpannaḥ śaktivedhopalakṣayet || 10.86 ||

kampate bhramate caiva jalpate vadate'khilam  |
mantrāveśasya cihnedaṃ kathitaṃ tava śobhane  || 10.87 ||

raudraṃ caivamato brūmi pañcāvasthā[ s] tu raudrajāḥ  |
anādhītāni śāstrāṇi granthataścārthataḥ sudhīḥ  || 10.88 ||

atītānāgataṃ sarvaṃ vartamānasya yatphalam  |
raudraśaktisamāveśātsarvameva prapadyate  || 10.89 ||

yasyedaṃ vartate cihnaṃ raudrāveśaṃ taducyate  |
śāmbhavena tu vedhena sarvāṇyetāni suvrate  || 10.90 ||

śuddhaśāmbhavavedhasya sāmprataṃ nirṇayaṃ śṛṇu  |
yena viddhasya loke'smin sarvajñatvaṃ prapadyate  || 10.91 ||

pūrvoktena tu kālena śodhitastu yadā śiśuḥ  |
tadā sampadyate tasya śāmbhavaṃ guṇadāyakam  || 10.92 ||

kubjīśo yaṃ yadāyātaḥ puṃso janmanyapaścime  |
tadā sampadyate tasya śāmbhavaṃ kubjike tanau  || 10.93 ||

bahvarthakāle'pi viśodhitātmā ātmaiva sau paśyati sarvabhūtān  |
na me samāno bhuvanāntarāle viśuddhabhāvo bhavate hyakāle  || 10.94 ||

ekaikaṃ bhuvanaṃ paśyetpuṃsādau conmanāvadhim  |
viśuddhatanujo hyevaṃ dehenānena cotpatet || 10.95 ||

na kampadhunane tasya īśadghūrmiḥ pravartate  |
viṣonmūrchāgatastvevaṃ tiṣṭhate bhūtakumbhavat || 10.96 ||

paśyate cāgrataḥ sarvaṃ tattvavrātaṃ sadoditam  |
tatkṣaṇādviṣayānmucyejjīrṇakañcuryathoragaḥ  || 10.97 ||

sadānandamadonmattaḥ sarvajñaguṇabhūṣitaḥ  |
śāmbhavena tu viddhasya cihnedaṃ sampravartate  || 10.98 ||

bhūtabhāvanaśaktīnāṃ mantrāveśa[ ṃ ] saraudrajam  |
krameṇa śāmbhavasteṣāṃ viśuddhatvaṃ yathā yathā  || 10.99 ||

jhalajhaleti yadvedhaṃ sampūrṇaghaṭavadyathā  |
bhūtāntaśaktimantrādau tathedaṃ sampracakṣyate  || 10.100 ||

gurubhaktivihīnānāṃ vañcakānāṃ yaśasvini  |
pūrvaṃ śāmbhavaviddhasya bhūtādyaṃ sampravartate  || 10.101 ||

śrīkubjikā uvāca

vedhadīkṣāparaṃ nāsti kathaṃ sā pratyayātmikā  |
pratyaye sati sañjāte kathaṃ tanmokṣalakṣaṇam  || 10.102 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

pratyaye sati mokṣo' sti piṇḍapātena sarvathā  |
viṣayeṣu na mucyeta siddhabhāvaṃ na gacchati  || 10.103 ||

śāmbhavena tu vedhena tatkṣaṇādviṣayojjhitaḥ  |
viṣayojjhitātmā vai dehenānena cotpatet || 10.104 ||

yena vedhena viddhasya sukhāsvādo na vidyate  |
sa kathaṃ svārthanirmukto viṣayeṣu virajyate  || 10.105 ||

śāmbhave na hi samprāpte darpeṇākulitekṣaṇaḥ  |
nāyakaiḥ so'bhibhūyeta na sidhyatyadhikārakṛt || 10.106 ||

ājñānande samutpanne na gantavyaṃ guroḥ kulāt |
kasmātsāmarthyahetvarthaṃ yāvannotpādayedguṇān  || 10.107 ||

divā preṣaṇatanniṣṭho rātrau jñānaparigrahaḥ  |
evaṃ sampādayetsarvaṃ sāmarthyaṃ tu guroḥ kule  || 10.108 ||

apreṣite na gantavyaṃ na kuryāccolbaṇādikam  |
ye na kopavaśādājñāṃ dāsyanti gamanaṃ prati  || 10.109 ||

śāmbhavājñāsamutpanne ya evaṃ kurute kudhīḥ  |
tasya pīṭhādhipāḥ pālāścābhibhūyantyanekadhā  || 10.110 ||

anuṣṭhānatapopāyairyadānandabhṛtastanuḥ  |
tadādhikāraḥ kartavyo yasyājñā tasya tatpade  || 10.111 ||

anujñāto'bhiṣiktasya nāmamālāṃ prakāśayet |
navapañcavidhaṃ dravyaṃ pūjārthe sampradarśitam  || 10.112 ||
śubhe'hani muhūrte ca caturdaśyāṣṭamīṣu ca  |
darpaṇodarabhūbhāge vastre vātha suśobhane  || 10.113 ||

tatopari yajetsiddhān sarvajñaguṇaśālinān  |
caturviṃśa ṣoḍaśaivamaṣṭau caiva tripaṅktiṣu  || 10.114 ||

dvau siddhau madhyadeśe tu kuṅkumena tu cākṣataiḥ  |
trihastaṃ maṇḍalaṃ kuryādūrdhvādau pūrvapaścimam  || 10.115 ||

śṛṅgātakākṛti hyevaṃ tatra pūjāṃ samārabhet |
pūjayitvā vidhānena dravyaiḥ pañcanavādibhiḥ  || 10.116 ||

pṛthagdīpaiḥ pūjayitvā phalguṣālisugandhibhiḥ  |
supraṇītaṃ subhaktaṃ ca ājñāguṇavidhāyinam  || 10.117 ||

tataḥ praveśayecchiṣyaṃ puṣpaṃ mocāpayediti  |
yasminmārge patetpuṣpaṃ tannāma tasya dāpayet || 10.118 ||

prakaṭaṃ śiva vijñeyaṃ guptamānanda-m-ucyate  |
akārādikṣakārāntaṃ pañcāśaguṇalakṣitam  || 10.119 ||

akṣare akṣare siddhaṃ  puṣpapātādvilakṣayet |
śrīkaṇṭhānantasūkṣmeśaṃ trimūrtiramaro'rghinaḥ  || 10.120 ||

tithīśo bhārabhūtiśca sthāṇunāmo harastathā  |
jhaṇṭīśo bhauktikaścaiva sadyojātastvanugrahī  || 10.121 ||

krūrasenastathānyo vai mahāsenastataḥ paraḥ  |
prathamādau sthitā hyete upariṣṭādvilakṣayet || 10.122 ||

krodhaścaṇḍaḥ pracaṇḍaśca śivaikarudra eva ca  |
kūrmaścaivaikanetraśca caturāsyo'vasānugaḥ  || 10.123 ||

prathamā yā sthitā paṅktiḥ pīṭhatrayavibhūṣitā  |
ajeśaḥ śarma somaśca lāṅgulīśo'tha dārukaḥ  || 10.124 ||

ardhanāryo hyumākānto āṣāḍhī diṇḍireva ca  |
dhātrīśaśca tathā mīno meṣo lohita-m-eva ca  || 10.125 ||
śikhīśaśchagalaṇḍaśca dviraṇḍo madhyapaṅktigāḥ  |
mahākālaśca vālākhyo bhujaṅgākhyaḥ pinākinaḥ  || 10.126 ||

khaḍgānando bakānandaḥ śvetānandastathaiva ca  |
bhṛguścaivāntime cakre aṣṭau tāṃśca prapūjayet || 10.127 ||

lākulānanda madhyasthaṃ saṃvartānandasaṃyutam  |
tayormadhyagatāṃ devīṃ kubjikāṃ parameśvarīm  || 10.128 ||

pūjayetpīṭhasaṃyuktāṃ pāramparyeṇa saṃyutām  |
yathā siddhāstathā devyāḥ saṃhāryādi prapūjayet || 10.129 ||

vāgeśyantāḥ krameṇaiva guruvaktraprasādataḥ  |
ṣaḍāre ḍādiṣaṭkaṃ tu krameṇaiva prapūjayet || 10.130 ||

kulāṣṭakaṃ tato bāhye aṣṭāre paṅkaje kramāt |
pañcadravyabhṛtaṃ pātraṃ tadagre sanniveśayet || 10.131 ||

kramāmnāyaṃ punaḥ pātre kuryāttenābhiṣecanam  |
mukhena vātha kartavyaṃ yasyopari subhāvanā  || 10.132 ||

tataścādeśayettaṃ tu kuru kāryaṃ yadṛcchayā  |
adhikārapadaṃ sarvaṃ mokṣitaṃ te prasādataḥ  || 10.133 ||

tataḥ prabhṛti deveśi yogyo bhavati śāsane  |
śāsanaṃ bhūṣayennityaṃ guptācāravidhau sthitaḥ  || 10.134 ||

avyaktena tu liṅgena vyaktaliṅgena vā punaḥ  |
yena liṅgena yasyedaṃ talliṅgaṃ na parityajet || 10.135 ||

ākāśātpatitaṃ toyaṃ yathā gacchati sāgaram  |
gartānadyopacāreṇa tathā sarvaṃ kulānvaye  || 10.136 ||

yāsyanti liṅginaḥ sarve niścayārtho'nyathā na hi  |
kasmātpratyakṣarūpeṇa tatrājñā vartate yataḥ  || 10.137 ||

samudravatkulānandaṃ yasmāttatsarvatomukham  |
kulaṃ tadeva vijñeyaṃ sarvānugrahakārakam  || 10.138 ||
brāhmaṇaṃ kṣatriyaṃ vaiśyaṃ śūdraṃ prākṛtamantyajam  |
mātaṅgamlecchajātyutthaṃ bauddhasāṅkhyadigambaram  || 10.139 ||

tridaṇḍamuṇḍakhaṭvāṅga- muṣalānyakriyānvitam  |
yāsyanti paramaṃ śaivaṃ śaivo yāti na kutracit || 10.140 ||

tacca kaulabhṛtānandaṃ  netaraṃ tu kriyākulam  |
sarvajñamārgavihitaṃ sarvācāraprapālakam  || 10.141 ||

kaulikācāramārgeṇa bhāvādvaitena sarvathā  |
tattvādvaitena mārgeṇa sarvathā yatra saṃsthitaḥ  || 10.142 ||

pālayellaukikācāramadvaitaṃ samanuṣṭhayet |
gopayedguptaliṅgāni tatpraviṣṭāni sarvathā  || 10.143 ||

adhamāduttamaṃ jñānaṃ yadyarthī uttamo bhavet |
liṅgino vā dvijanmā vā ājñārthī tu na vañcayet || 10.144 ||

ekānte vihitaṃ sarvaṃ kurvīta na janākule  |
anyathā sthitibhaṅgaḥ syānnaśyate śāsanaṃ priye  || 10.145 ||

varjayetkaulikān bauddhān tathā mīmāṃsakāsthitān  |
kasmādbhraṣṭakriyā teṣāṃ na mokṣo naiva sādhanam  || 10.146 ||

jihvopasthanimittārthamadvaitaṃ teṣu sarvathā  |
kaulikācāranirmuktāḥ śvānavadvicaranti te  || 10.147 ||

nirācāraṃ prakurvanti nirācāravivarjitāḥ  |
viṣaṃ bhakṣanti te mūḍhā yathājñāmantravarjitāḥ  || 10.148 ||

yadyapi te trikālajñāstrailokyākarṣaṇakṣamāḥ  |
tathāpi saṃvṛtācārāḥ pālayanti kulasthitim  || 10.149 ||

nirācāreṇa yogena paśyanti viṣayojjhitāḥ  |
viṣayastho'pahāsitvaṃ nirācāreṇa yātyasau  || 10.150 ||

bhraṣṭanaṣṭakulaṃ tyajya kulakaulaṃ samāśrayet |
tatra yogigurūṇāṃ ca pūjyate caraṇāmbujam  || 10.151 ||
samayena vinā devi samartho bhavate katham  |
sāmarthyena vinā caryā nirācārātmikā bhavet || 10.152 ||

jñātvāmnāyapadaṃ sarvaṃ yathāvasthaṃ kuleśvaram  |
ṣaṭprakāravidhānena nirgatācārarūpiṇam  || 10.153 ||

sāmarthyaguṇayuktātmā viṣayātīto jitendriyaḥ  |
virajo rañjitātmā vai nirācāro bhavettu saḥ  || 10.154 ||

etatte kathitaṃ devi sarahasyaṃ sugopitam  |
anyadyatte manasthaṃ tu tatpṛccha vadato mama  || 10.155 ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate devyāsamayo nāma mantroddhāre daśamaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************



śrīkubjikā uvāca

mantratantraistvayā deva bhrāmitāhaṃ kriyādibhiḥ  |
dhyānadhāraṇayogaiśca idānīṃ kathaya sphuṭam  || 11.1 ||

pūrvatantre tvayā deva sūcitaṃ na prakāśitam  |
adhunā śrotumicchāmi ṣaṭpadārthavinirṇayam  || 11.2 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

mahānandakaraṃ vākyaṃ mahāścaryakaraṃ param  |
gopitaṃ sarvadevānāṃ tathā te kathayāmyaham  || 11.3 ||

anādinidhaneśānācchivātparamakāraṇāt |
divyājñāyāḥ kramo jātaḥ pāramparyaughasantatiḥ  || 11.4 ||

akulaṃ ca kulaṃ caiva kulākulavinirṇayam  |
adhunā kathayiṣyāmi navadhā nirṇayo yathā  || 11.5 ||

parasya paramāṃ viddhi yonimādyāṃ mahāmbike  |
rūpātītādiyogena pareccheyaṃ caturvidhā  || 11.6 ||

rūpātītaṃ tu kāmākhyaṃ rūpaṃ pūrṇagirirmahān  |
padaṃ jālandharākhyaṃ tu piṇḍamoḍraṃ prakīrtitam  || 11.7 ||

antimāmṛta sūkṣmā ca susūkṣmādyaṃ catuṣṭayam  |
akuleśvaradevasya sambandhaḥ prathamaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 11.8 ||

rūpātītātparo hinduḥ śaktyādhiṣṭhita bhāsvaraḥ  |
tato nādo nirodhaśca ardhacandramanukramāt || 11.9 ||

etattatpañcakaṃ proktaṃ jñānaratnamahodayam  |
sā yoniḥ paramā jñeyā kriyādhvānamahodadhiḥ  || 11.10 ||

bindutattvātparo bindurmakārokāra-m-eva ca  |
akārastu samākhyātaḥ ṣaṭpadārthavibhedakaḥ  || 11.11 ||

rūpātpadaṃ samutpannaṃ kālarūpaṃ ṣaḍānanam  |
ṣaḍvidhādhvānayogena sṛjate saṃharanti ca  || 11.12 ||

ādhārādheyayogena ṣaṭpadārthapadena ca  |
kurute vividhāṃ sṛṣṭiṃ yena tatkathyate' dhunā  || 11.13 ||

ātmā dhārayate śaktimātmā haṃsoparisthitaḥ  |
haṃsaḥ samīraṇāntasthaḥ sa ca nāḍīpathe sthitaḥ  || 11.14 ||

nāḍyaḥ piṇḍe sakarmādyaḥ paśurmāyāmalānvitāḥ  |
etatṣaṭkaṃ samākhyātaṃ kulamārgaprabodhakam  || 11.15 ||

atra jātaṃ jagatsarvaṃ kriyākāraṇagocaram  |
parācca śāmbhavaṃ jñānaṃ vijñānānekasaṅkulam  || 11.16 ||

viśuddhirbodhajananī ṣoḍaśānta-m-adhordhvataḥ  |
maṇipūraka śabdasthaṃ daśapañcāvatārakam  || 11.17 ||

sā tu māyā parā jñeyā caturyonirmahāmbike  |
śabdasūtreṇa yenaitāḥ pañcāśa maṇayo mahān  || 11.18 ||
āpūritāśca mahatā tenedaṃ maṇipūrakam  |
asyādhāraṃ tu vijñeyaṃ karṇakubjaṃ mahāpuram  || 11.19 ||

vijñānaiḥ pañcadaśabhiḥ pūritaṃ bhuktimuktidam  |
maṇipūrakamālāyāṃ granthirjātā caturvidhā  || 11.20 ||

maṇḍalaṃ mantravidyāśca mudrā granthiścaturvidhā  |
māyāyantrodare cānyā puṃsāṃ sṛṣṭiranāhatā  || 11.21 ||

nadate daśadhā sā tu divyānandapradāyikā  |
ciṇīti prathamaṃ śabdaṃ ciñcinī tu dvitīyakam  || 11.22 ||

cīravākī tṛtīyaṃ tu śaṅkhaśabdaṃ caturthakam  |
pañcamaṃ tantrinirghoṣaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ vaṃśaravastathā  || 11.23 ||

saptamaṃ kaṃsatālaṃ tu meghaśabdaṃ tu cāṣṭamam  |
navamaṃ dāghanirghoṣaṃ daśamaṃ dundubhisvanaḥ  || 11.24 ||

navaśabdaṃ parityajya daśamaṃ mokṣadaṃ param  |
hananena vinā yena vyāhareddaśadhā ravam  || 11.25 ||

tenaivānāhataṃ jātaṃ karṇakubjādvinirgatam  |
daśadhā ravate-d-evamaṣṭapattroparisthitam  || 11.26 ||

daśadhā guṇadātāraṃ ciccetāhṛdayātmakam  |
pramāṇapadayogena kṣobhayitvā navān bahūn  || 11.27 ||

kalākarmasamāyogātsvādhiṣṭhānaṃ vinirmitam  |
śatakoṭisuvistīrṇaṃ bhuvanānekasaṅkulam  || 11.28 ||

māyākālakalākīrṇamādhāraṃ brahmaṇastu tat |
catuṣkalasamopetaṃ śivaśaktisamanvitam  || 11.29 ||

ṣaṭprakāramidaṃ kubji svādhiṣṭhānaṃ pṛthakpṛthak |
ṣaṭpadārthavibhāgo'yaṃ durlabhaḥ prakaṭīkṛtaḥ  || 11.30 ||

kriyātattvārthanirdeśaṃ kubjike'nyatra gopitam  |
kulākulamidaṃ ṣaṭkamuttaraṃ te prakāśitam  || 11.31 ||
dakṣiṇasyāpi ṣaṭkasya sāmprataṃ nirṇayaṃ śṛṇu  |
maṇipūrakadevasya tattejo bhāsvarastu yaḥ  || 11.32 ||

tatra taddakṣiṇaṃ ṣaṭkamājñāpūrvaṃ kulodbhavam  |
sṛṣṭimārgakramāyātaṃ śivaśakteḥ kulākulam  || 11.33 ||

saṃhārapadaṣaṭkasya kulaṃ śaktyāntadakṣiṇam  |
gudamādhāramityuktaṃ svādhiṣṭhānaṃ tu liṅgajam  || 11.34 ||

maṇipūraka nābhisthaṃ hṛdisthaṃ ca anāhatam  |
viśuddhiḥ kaṇṭhadeśe tu ājñā netradvayāntare  || 11.35 ||

viśuddhiḥ ṣoḍaśairbhedairdaśadhā tu anāhatam  |
maṇipūraka vijñeyaṃ bhedairdvādaśabhiḥ sthitam  || 11.36 ||

anekārthaguṇādhāraṃ  svādhiṣṭhānaṃ tu ṣaṭkalam  |
catuṣkalaṃ tu ādhāramājñābhedadvayaṃ viduḥ  || 11.37 ||


śrīkubjikā uvāca

ājñābhedadvayaṃ nātha kathaṃ tatparameśvara  |
ācacakṣva prayatnena yena bhrāntirvinaśyati  || 11.38 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

lakṣavārasahasraistu vāraṃ vāraṃ punaḥ punaḥ  |
eṣa sāṅketiko hyarthaḥ kathyamānaṃ na budhyasi  || 11.39 ||

śāmbhavaṃ kathitaṃ jñānaṃ sṛṣṭimārgeṇa śaktigam  |
icchāśaktisamāyuktamuttaraṃ te prakāśitam  || 11.40 ||

icchājñānaṃ parityajya śambhuratrāpi dakṣiṇam  |
kriyāśaktiradhobhāge saṃyogātpratyayāyate  || 11.41 ||

ūrdhvaśaktinipātena adhaḥśaktinikuñcanāt |
kurute vividhāṃ sṛṣṭimanekākārarūpiṇīm  || 11.42 ||
na śivena vinā śaktirna śivaḥ śaktivarjitaḥ  |
kriyātattvasya mārgo'yaṃ parecchādhvaṃ tu kevalam  || 11.43 ||

uttarasya tu mārgasya yacchatuṣkaṃ susūkṣmagam  |
kṣobhitaṃ tena cātmānaṃ punaḥ ṣoḍaśadhā kṛtam  || 11.44 ||

viśuddhaṃ paratattvāntaṃ tenātmānaṃ visarpitam  |
catustriṃśatibhedena tasmān'nekavidhākṛtiḥ  || 11.45 ||

sapratyayaguṇādhāramavasthāguṇadāyakam  |
lakṣyate yena suśroṇi tacchṛṇuṣva yathārthataḥ  || 11.46 ||

muktāphalanibhākāraṃ kvacijjvālācalācalam  |
kvacinmarkaṭijālābhaṃ mṛgatṛṣṇeva cāpalam  || 11.47 ||

rūpātītaṃ ca rūpaṃ ca padapiṇḍaṃ caturvidham  |
viśuddhatanudevasya ādyabhedaṃ catuṣṭayam  || 11.48 ||

sarahasyaṃ prabuddhānāṃ kṣubdhānāṃ tu kriyādhvare  |
tasmātpīṭhacatuṣkaṃ tu sañjātaṃ tu kulākulam  || 11.49 ||

kalābhṛttanudevasya kailāsoparisaṃsthitam  |
madhyadeśe tu randhrasthaṃ śrīmadoḍrakuleśvaram  || 11.50 ||

prathamaṃ pītavarṇaṃ tu saśailavanakānanam  |
vanopavanasaṃyuktaṃ hemaprākāramaṇḍitam  || 11.51 ||

nadīnadasamākīrṇamanekārthasamākulam  |
sarvabījasamākīrṇaṃ caturasraṃ samantataḥ  || 11.52 ||

vajrārgalasamopetaṃ vajrahastā tu mālinī  |
tatrādhipatyayogena pīṭhapīṭheśvarīyutam  || 11.53 ||

tasyaiva dakṣiṇe koṇe candrābhaṃ candravarcasam  |
ardhacandra purākāraṃ  sari tsarasamākulam  || 11.54 ||

jalakallolagambhīraṃ ṣaḍrasārṇavasaṅkulam  |
vīcītaraṅgakallolaistaṭāsphālanabhīṣaṇaiḥ  || 11.55 ||
tattvanāthoparisthaṃ tu puraṃ tatpārameśvaram  |
himacandraśilābhiśca samantānnicitaṃ tu tam  || 11.56 ||

prākāreṇa vicitreṇa gopurāṭṭālaśobhitam  |
anekaguṇasañchannamanekāścaryasaṅkulam  || 11.57 ||

tatra tattveśvaraṃ devaṃ devyādhiṣṭhitavigraham  |
śyāmavarṇaṃ sutejāḍhyaṃ pāśahastaṃ sulocanam  || 11.58 ||

ādhāraṃ sarvasṛṣṭestu mahāpīṭhoparisthitam  |
kailāsadakṣiṇe śṛṅge anekaguṇasaṅkulam  || 11.59 ||

śrīmajjālandharaṃ pīṭhaṃ tatrasthaṃ lakṣayetpriye  |
kailāsasyottare śṛṅge anekārcisamākulam  || 11.60 ||

grasantamiva trailokyaṃ sūryakoṭisamaprabham  |
piṅgalaṃ dahanāvasthaṃ lelihānaṃ sudāruṇam  || 11.61 ||

mamāpi devi duṣprekṣyaṃ kiṃ punastvitarairjanaiḥ  |
trikoṇapuramadhyasthaṃ vajraprākāramaṇḍitam  || 11.62 ||

vajrastambhamayaṃ divyaṃ puraṃ vai pārameśvaram  |
kālāgnigopurāṭṭālaṃ samantātpariveṣṭitam  || 11.63 ||

bahurūpasamākīrṇaṃ vidyāguṇavibhūṣitam  |
anekāścaryasampannaṃ jīvabhūtaṃ jagattraye  || 11.64 ||

āpūritamidaṃ yena tena tatpūrṇasaṃjñitam  |
saptajihvāsamopetaṃ kālarūpaṃ ṣaḍānanam  || 11.65 ||

pūrṇamāyā samāyuktaṃ sāñjanaṃ cārurūpiṇam  |
śaktihastaṃ mahāvīryaṃ sṛṣṭisaṃhārakārakam  || 11.66 ||

napuṃsakaguṇāntasthaṃ vyāptibhūtaṃ vinirgatam  |
madhyapīṭhasya pūrveṇa cāgraśṛṅge vyavasthitam  || 11.67 ||

padminīdalasaṅkāśaṃ dhūmravattāmravarcasam  |
mahāpracaṇḍadaṇḍaughaiḥ sphālanollālalālasaiḥ  || 11.68 ||
dhūyamānaṃ samantāttu śoṣayantaṃ carācaram  |
ṣaḍasramaṇḍalāntasthaṃ sarvavyāpikuleśvaram  || 11.69 ||

na tena rahitaṃ kiñcitsṛṣṭisaṃhāragocare  |
indranīlanibhaiḥ stambhaiḥ samantānnicitaṃ puram  || 11.70 ||

prākāragopurāṭṭālaṃ dhvajāekuśadhanurdharam  |
pañcabāṇadharaṃ devaṃ kāmadevyā samanvitam  || 11.71 ||

drāvayantaṃ jagatsarvaṃ śrutirūpaṃ tanūjjhitam  |
caturdaśavidhasyāpi nāyako daṇḍadhārakaḥ  || 11.72 ||

tasyecchāpreritaṃ sarvaṃ kāmādyaṃ sampravartate  |
tenedaṃ cāgrakoṭisthaṃ manonmanyordhvasaṃsthitam  || 11.73 ||

strīpuṃnapuṃsake dve tu pīṭhavyāptau pare viduḥ l |
kāmena kṣubhitaṃ tattvaṃ sthāṇusaṃjñā manonmanam  || 11.74 ||

manonmanena samanaṃ dvāvetau tu napuṃsakau  |
puṭarūpau samākhyātau tasmān'nyo vyāpinaḥ paraḥ  || 11.75 ||

sā tu māyā parā devī durbhedyā cākṣayāvyayā  |
vyāpinī sarvatattvānāmātmādau tvapare'dhvani  || 11.76 ||

māyaiva sā ṣaḍadhvasya ṣaṭtriṃśānāṃ viśeṣataḥ  |
yayā vibhajya cātmānaṃ svarūpe cādhvanirmitam  || 11.77 ||

ardhakoṭyā adhaḥsthāne nādāntaṃ sanniveśitam  |
unmanaḥ samanaścaiva vyāpino dhvanireva ca  || 11.78 ||

pīṭhacatuṣkametattu sa evānyonyataḥ kramāt |
dhvanernādaḥ samutpannaḥ sa cānekavidhaḥ sthitaḥ  || 11.79 ||

sūkṣmaścaiva susūkṣmaśca vyaktāvyakto'tha kṛtrimaḥ  |
ātmano'pyardhakoṭyante adhaḥsthāne niveśitaḥ  || 11.80 ||

tasmātsa kurute sṛṣṭimanekākārarūpiṇīm  |
sūkṣmanādo guhāvāsī kālāgnau tu susūkṣmagaḥ  || 11.81 ||
svasthānasthastu avyaktaḥ padānte vyakta-m-āśritaḥ  |
kṛtrimaścaiva saṃyogātsa cākāśe vyavasthitaḥ  || 11.82 ||

tasmādakṣarasantānaṃ vāgvilāsaṃ pravartate  |
tena saṅkṣobhya cātmānamavyaktāvyaktarūpiṇam  || 11.83 ||

nirodhitaṃ tu tenedaṃ sūkṣmabhāvasya sambhavaḥ  |
tena nairodhikaṃ nāma golākāraṃ vyavasthitam  || 11.84 ||

ātmalagnasvarūpeṇa pratimūrti dvitīyakam  |
tena saṅkṣobhya cātmānamardhacandravinirmitam  || 11.85 ||

sravantamamṛtaṃ divyaṃ sarvasya jagataḥ sthitam  |
tasya samplāvanātyarthaṃ visargābhiratastu yaḥ  || 11.86 ||

tatrādityaṃ samutpannaṃ varṇānāṃ prabhumīśvaram  |
bindurūpaṃ jagannāthaṃ kriyākālaguṇottaram  || 11.87 ||

varṇasṛṣṭestu kartāraṃ dedīpyantaṃ suvarcasam  |
unmanādicatuṣkasya sañjātedaṃ catuṣkalam  || 11.88 ||

kṣubhitaṃ kramayogena viśuddhatanu śāmbhavam  |
sthitaṃ ṣoḍaśabhedena catuṣkena pṛthakpṛthak || 11.89 ||

kulātītaśarīrasya piṇḍamādyaṃ catuṣkalam  |
dvādaśāṅgaṃ kuleśasya mastake saṃvyavasthitam  || 11.90 ||

catuṣkalaṃ dvitīyaṃ tu pīṭharūpaṃ jagāmbike  |
nādāntordhvaṃ tu māyādyaṃ vijñeyaṃ tu puṭatrayam  || 11.91 ||

lalāṭordhvaṃ kuleśasya jñātavyaṃ tu kuleśvari  |
tadadhaḥ pañcadhā nādaṃ kṛtrimaṃ mukhamaṇḍale  || 11.92 ||

nirodhaṃ tatsamaṃ jñeyaṃ candrasūryaṃ tatodare  |
evaṃ viśuddhadevena ṣoḍaśāvayavaṃ tanum  || 11.93 ||

akuleśakuleśānaṃ vibhajya ca niveśitam  |
atra yogābhipannānāmavasthāṃ śṛṇu bhāvini  || 11.94 ||
romāñcaścāśrupātaśca viṣuvaṃ candradarśanam  |
pipīlikāparaḥ sparśaḥ sūryaṃ rātrau ca paśyati  || 11.95 ||

utpatedgaganāmbhobhiḥ śabdānmuñcati dāruṇān  |
vāgīśatvaṃ prapadyeta kiṃ tvabaddhapralāpinaḥ  || 11.96 ||

kṣobhaḥ kṣudhājayo nidrā unmanatvaṃ kṣanātkṣanāt |
sugandhaśca sudīptaśca vācāsiddhiḥ pravartate  || 11.97 ||

ṣoḍaśaite mahāvasthāḥ pratyakṣānubhavedyadi  |
tadā tena tu dehena khecarīkulanandanaḥ  || 11.98 ||

etatte sarahasyaṃ tu viśuddhaṃ kathitaṃ mayā  |
idānīṃ śṛṇu kalyāṇi yathāvasthamanāhatam  || 11.99 ||

kaṇṭhādhastātkuleśasya udarordhvamavasthitam  |
krodhaśarmādibhiḥ siddhaiścakravartidaśānvitaḥ  || 11.100 ||

ekarudraḥ suśarmā ca granthau nāle vyavasthitaḥ  |
krodhājeśādayaḥ siddhāścakravartidale sthitāḥ  || 11.101 ||

pūrveśagocarāntāste madhye devaḥ sadāśivaḥ  |
cāroccāravicāraiśca ebhiḥ sārdhaṃ ramettu saḥ  || 11.102 ||

rājyakrīḍāmathordhve ca saṃhārātmā jagattrayam  |
māṃsādapiśunatvena hyabhilāṣo' dhunā punaḥ  || 11.103 ||

āpyāyitamano hṛṣṭastuṣṭacittastu vatsalaḥ  |
pṛthvīṃ bhramāmi nikhilāṃ vrajāmo girigahvaram  || 11.104 ||

dravyamāvarjayāmāsa vilasāmo dadāmyaham  |
paraṃ vairāgyamāpanno mokṣānveṣaṇatatparaḥ  || 11.105 ||

gurumanveṣayiṣyāmi yena bhūyo na sambhavaḥ  |
sandhinālāntarastho'sau pātālamanukāṅkṣati  || 11.106 ||

divyasiddho bhaviṣyāmaḥ krīḍāmaḥ kāminījanaiḥ  |
madhyadeśāntarastho'sau na kiñcidapi cintayet || 11.107 ||
sukhāvastho jitakrodhaḥ sattvāvastho jitendriyaḥ  |
tiṣṭhate'nāhato devaścakravartyaṣṭakairvṛtaḥ  || 11.108 ||

daśadhāvasthite cakre bhāvābhāvasamanvitaḥ  |
guruvaktragato devaścakravartisamanvitaḥ  || 11.109 ||

svabhāvaguṇasaṃyuktaṃ cintayantopadeśataḥ  |
abhyasantasya deveśi avasthāḥ sambhavanti hi  || 11.110 ||

puṃso bhedena jāyante sāttvarājasatāmasāḥ  |
uttamo madhyamaśceti kanyasastu tṛtīyakaḥ  || 11.111 ||

kanyase tāmasāvasthā rājasā sāttvikā punaḥ  |
madhyame rajasā yuktaṃ sattvāvasthāditottamaḥ  || 11.112 ||

yāśca tāḥ śṛṇu kalyāṇi yeṣu yogasya sādhanam  |
tamo moho rajaḥ śokaścatuṣkaṃ kanyasādikam  || 11.113 ||

lolupā rāgavatyā ca kāmukā cāpalāyinī  |
madhyamādiṣvavasthaitāḥ kanyase tu dvitīyakā  || 11.114 ||

prabhāvatī sutārā ca bimbā bimbakhageśvarī  |
jyeṣṭhādimadhyame dvisthā tristhā kanyasagocare  || 11.115 ||

udayanti kramā hyetāḥ samādhiviṣaye sthitāḥ  |
antimaikā dvimadhyasthā tridhāvasthā tu kanyase  || 11.116 ||

kiṃ tu jyeṣṭhacatuṣkasya dve'vasthā na bhavanti hi  |

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate ṣaṭprakāranirṇayo nāma ekādaśamaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************



śrīkubjikā uvāca

kuleśānāmavasthānāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ vada bhairava  |
yena vai'nāhataṃ devaṃ jānīmaḥ parameśvara  || 12.1 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

kathayāmi varārohe pratyayaṃ tu salakṣaṇam  |
tāmavijñāya bhraṣṭatvamavaśyaṃ hitakāriṇi  || 12.2 ||

akramājñā bhavedyeṣāṃ rabhasājñā prakāśitā  |
sāmarthyato'tha dayayā uktakālādavāntare  || 12.3 ||

tāmasāste samākhyātāstamo'vasthāntarānvitāḥ  |
samayāni na manyante gurvājñālopakārakāḥ  || 12.4 ||

kalidvandvapriyā nityaṃ chidrānveṣaṇatatparāḥ  |
guropavādaniratā nirapekṣā muhurmuhuḥ  || 12.5 ||

apavādaṃ ruṣitvā tu guroryānti parāṅmukhāḥ  |
yenāsau nidhanaṃ yāti tatkaroti tamo'nvitaḥ  || 12.6 ||

mohāviṣṭo na jānāti ātmasambhāvitaḥ kudhīḥ  |
ahaṅkāratamolubdhaḥ pūrvajātimanusmaret || 12.7 ||

guruṃ vicārayitvā tu śokenāntaritātmanaḥ  |
prayāti gṛhasāyojyaṃ tamenākulitekṣaṇaḥ  || 12.8 ||

tenādhamapadaṃ yāti jīvanneva mṛtastu saḥ  |
buddhimanto mahāprājñaḥ svāgamārthaviśāradaḥ  || 12.9 ||

tataḥ kṣamāpayennāthaṃ tadvidāmnāyapūjanam  |
trisaptakaṃ tu maunena sarvopaskaraṇaiḥ saha  || 12.10 ||

avasthāścopaśāmyante tamo'vasthācatuṣṭayam  |
lolupādau tu catvāri kramāddhyevaṃ vyapohayet || 12.11 ||

māyayā bhṛtacittastu dāsatvena tu rañjayet |
uktakālārdhamānena rañjito'nugrahedguruḥ  || 12.12 ||

tīvratve'pi hi sañjāte mandatvaṃ sampravartate  |
upadeśopacāreṇa avasthālakṣaṇaṃ bhavet || 12.13 ||

rājaso'yaṃ samākhyātaścāhaṅkāraguṇānvitaḥ  |
paṇḍito'haṃ subhakto'haṃ vaktāhaṃ bodhako hyaham  || 12.14 ||

jñānino'haṃ samartho'haṃ vayaṃ sarvaguṇeśvarāḥ  |
karoti guruṇā sārdhaṃ vādamajñānacetasaḥ  || 12.15 ||

idaṃ tattvamidaṃ tattvamāgamoktaṃ na jānatha  |
evaṃ'sau rajasālipto yadyātmānaṃ na saṃsmaret || 12.16 ||

tadāvasthācatuṣkeṇa lolupādyena gṛhyate  |
parastriyaṃ hasennityaṃ dhāvayitvā vilagyate  || 12.17 ||

sa śṛṅgārī madasrāvī nityamevaṃ gajo yathā  |
ātmānaṃ vikrayitvā tu madyamāṃsaṃ samācaret || 12.18 ||

viveko yadi cittasthastadārādhyaṃ samāśrayet |
atha cetpūrvavihitāṃ kramapūjāṃ samācaret || 12.19 ||

madhyamasya tataḥ paścādavasthā śubhadāyikā  |
uttamaṃ parayā bhaktyā āviṣṭastu sadā guroḥ  || 12.20 ||

uktakālena cādeśā- nugrahaḥ samprapāditaḥ  |
triśuddhāntarabhāvena yasya bhāvo na cānyathā  || 12.21 ||

tasya caivottare mārge dakṣiṇāmnāyapūrvakam  |
vindate nikhilaṃ jñānaṃ nirahaṅkārī dṛḍhavrataḥ  || 12.22 ||

udayanti śubhāvasthāḥ prabhāvatyāditaḥ kramāt |
ṣaṭkamārgeti yāḥ proktāḥ śubhāstāścodayanti vai  || 12.23 ||

prabhābhirañjitātmā vai paśyate bhuvanatrayam  |
tārakāntasthamātmānaṃ dedīpyantaṃ suvarcasam  || 12.24 ||

candrarūpaṃ yadā paśyettārāmaṇḍalamadhyataḥ  |
tārāvatī tu sā proktā avasthā siddhidāyikā  || 12.25 ||
abhyasyantaḥ svarūpeṇa samādhisthaḥ prapaśyati  |
ātmabimbapurasthaṃ tu bimbā sāvaśyasiddhidā  || 12.26 ||

samādhisthaḥ svabimbaṃ tu āsanena samanvitam  |
utpatantaṃ yadā paśyettadā sā bimbakhecarī  || 12.27 ||

dṛṣṭvaitāṃ tu mahāvasthāṃ siddhe[r] bhrāntiṃ na kārayet |
avaśyaṃ yāti khecakre hyuktakālaṃ kuleśvari  || 12.28 ||

eṣāvasthā samāsādya daśāvasthā[s] tyajetpunaḥ  |
guṇānutpādayitvā tu anāhatapadaṃ vrajet || 12.29 ||

athānyatparamaṃ vakṣẏe maṇipūraṃ yathā sthitam  |
tathā tvaṃ śṛṇu kalyāṇi kalyāṇānandavardhanam  || 12.30 ||

sthitaṃ dvādaśabhedena someśādau śikhāntikam  |
nābhyudaranitamboru- jaṅghāṅghrīmanukramāt || 12.31 ||

kulanāthamaheśasya saṃsthito maṇipūrakaḥ  |
tanucakre samāvṛtya yathāvasthaṃ tathā śṛṇu  || 12.32 ||

someśodarasaṃsthaṃ tu dvādaśārcisamanvitam  |
dvīpakṣetrasamāyuktaṃ tadevānyān vilakṣayet || 12.33 ||

lāṅgalī dakṣiṇe kukṣau vāme dārukajaṃ vibhum  |
ardhanārīśvaraṃ nābhau svacakraparivāritam  || 12.34 ||

dakṣiṇena hyumākāntaṃ nitambe vāmato'ṣaḍhim  |
ḍiṇḍitriyugalorubhyāṃ jānubhyāṃ mīnameṣakau  || 12.35 ||

lohitākhyaṃ śikhīnāthaṃ dakṣādau vāmamāśritau  |
pīṭhanāthaṃ tathā kṣetraṃ dvīpaṃ dvīpādhipaiḥ saha  || 12.36 ||

maṇivaddyotayantaṃ tu pūrayantaṃ diśo daśa  |
sūryakāntimaṇiprakhyaṃ bhāskareva prapaśyate  || 12.37 ||

kālasaṅkhyākaraṃ devaṃ kalairdvādaśabhiryutam  |
pīṭhanāthaṃ tu dvīpasthaṃ māsamāsāditaḥ kramāt || 12.38 ||
pūrayedvarṣasantānaṃ yugamanvantarāṇi ca  |
kalpaṃ ceti mahākalpaṃ maṇidvādaśabhiḥ khilam  || 12.39 ||

yataḥ pūrayedviśvātmā tenedaṃ maṇipūrakam  |
śaktimārgaprapannānāṃ bhuktimuktiphalapradam  || 12.40 ||

ekaikaṃ cintayeccakraṃ nāthājñā hyupadeśataḥ  |
bhavanti sarvasiddhīni uttamādhamamadhyamāḥ  || 12.41 ||

maṇipūraka pādasthaṃ pīṭheśvarasamanvitam  |
dvīpadvīpādhipairyuktaṃ māsamekaṃ yadābhyaset || 12.42 ||

pādacāri jagatsarvaṃ kṣobhayedavicārataḥ  |
pūjādhyānasamādhisthaḥ śaktimārgeṇa yogavit || 12.43 ||

ṣaṇmāsena avaśyaṃ hi vatsarāntaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  |
anyacchīghragatistasya ātmanaḥ sampravartate  || 12.44 ||

pāduke pādalepaṃ vā manovegaḥ prajāyate  |
evaṃ jānuni abhyāsādbhūtavetālanayakaḥ  || 12.45 ||

kurute vividhāścaryaṃ kalpasthāyī bhavettu saḥ  |
ātmavanto mahotsāha ūrubhyāmurageśvaraḥ  || 12.46 ||

kiṃ tu taddviguṇenaiva kālena prathamāditaḥ  |
krameṇa sidhyate sarvamādyantena vilakṣayet || 12.47 ||

nitambābhyāsayogena guhyakānāṃ patirbhavet |
yakṣavidyādharāṇaṃ ca pretapaiśācarākṣasām  || 12.48 ||

krīḍate nāyako bhūtvā pūrvamārgavidhau sthitaḥ  |
kukṣimārgagate cakre abhyasantaḥ śriyaṃ labhet || 12.49 ||

kinnarendra sagandharvo lokālokeṣu pūjyate  |
vāyuvadbhramate so hi sarvatraivamaśaṅkitaḥ  || 12.50 ||

madhyanābhigate cakre mūlameḍhre yadābhyaset |
śāntipuṣṭivaśākarṣaṃ sarvajñatvaṃ pṛthuśriyam  || 12.51 ||
sakṛtsaṃsmaraṇādevamabhyasantaḥ khageśvaraḥ  |
brahmāṇḍāntaraniḥśeṣaṃ bhramate kāmarūpiṇaḥ  || 12.52 ||

sāṅkhyajñānavido bhūtvā vicaretsvapuraṃ punah |
atha spaṣṭataraṃ devi śaktityāgaṃ śṛṇuṣva me  || 12.53 ||

yadetatparamaṃ bījaṃ haṃsākhyaṃ hṛdi saṃsthitam  |
vinā tenopalabdhiṃ ca na jānāti kadācana  || 12.54 ||

tasya rūpatrayaṃ bhadre nādaṃ saṃyogameva ca  |
viyogaṃ ceti suśroṇi lakṣaṇīyaṃ prayatnataḥ  || 12.55 ||

caitanyatritayaṃ cātra ātmaśaktiśivātmakam  |
avinābhāvayogena caitanyatritayasthitam  || 12.56 ||

tenopacaryate bhadre haṃsadevaḥ parāparaḥ  |
saṅkoce tu parā śaktirvikāse bhairavaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 12.57 ||

madhye ātmā sadā tiṣṭhetpūryaṣṭakasamanvitaḥ  |
vikāsaścordhvanāḍistu saṅkoco'dhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ  || 12.58 ||

madhye nābhiriti proktastrayametatsudurlabham  |
ūrdhvanāḍīnirodhena adhonāḍīnikuñcanāt || 12.59 ||

madhye cittaṃ samādāya mathanaṃ tatra kārayet |
yonimadhyagataṃ liṅgaṃ yonyodarapuṭīkṛtam  || 12.60 ||

tanmadhye cātmano rūpaṃ lakṣayeta punaḥ punaḥ  |
mathanaṃ hyetadākhyātamajñānamalanāśanam  || 12.61 ||

madhyamanthānayogena jñānāgnirjvalate kila  |
jvalite tu tadā vahnau jyotirevaṃ pravardhate  || 12.62 ||

pravardhanānmahājyoterānandamupajāyate  |
mathanādbhagaliṅgābhyāṃ yathānandaḥ prajāyate  || 12.63 ||

mathanācchivaśaktyostu tathānandaḥ prajāyate  |
niścayatvaṃ bhaveddevi śivaśaktyorabhedataḥ  || 12.64 ||
mathanaṃ hyetadevoktamamṛtotpādakaṃ priye  |
tenāmṛtena cātmānaṃ plāvyamānaṃ vicintayet || 12.65 ||

eṣā sā paramā vṛttiḥ paratattvamidaṃ smṛtam  |
etattatparamaṃ brahma paramānandalakṣaṇam  || 12.66 ||

tadānandaparānandaṃ śaktityāgamiti smṛtam  |
eṣa te maṇipūrastu sarahasyaṃ prakāśitam  || 12.67 ||

gopitaṃ pūrvatantreṣu kubji tubhyaṃ prakāśitam  |
dvīpamārgavibhāgena pīṭhanāthakrameṇa tu  || 12.68 ||

durlabhaṃ siddhamārgasya kiṃ punastvitareṣu ca  |
uktakālena sidhyanti avaśyaṃ nānṛtaṃ vacaḥ  || 12.69 ||

śṛṇu devi yathāvasthaṃ svādhiṣṭhānaṃ vadāmi te  |
kalākalitadehasya yathāsthānaṃ nigadyate  || 12.70 ||

pūrvamekārṇave ghore tamobhūte jagattraye  |
liṅgarūpadharaścāhaṃ parecchāvaśavartinaḥ  || 12.71 ||

ṣaṇmukhaḥ kālarūpo'haṃ liṅgākāro vyavasthitaḥ  |
ṣaṭkalābhirvṛto nityaṃ viśvamadhye ramāmyaham  || 12.72 ||

ṣaṭkauṣikena yukto'haṃ piṇḍo'haṃ'naṅgavarcasaḥ  |
tataḥ pravartitā sṛṣṭirmamecchā tu punaḥ priye  || 12.73 ||

brahmaviṣṇvādibhiḥ siddhaiḥ pūjitārādhita[ḥ] stutaḥ  |
tato'haṃ varamāpannasteṣu bhāvānuvartinām  || 12.74 ||

ṣaḍasraṃ caturasraṃ tu ātmānaṃ ca samarpitam  |
tena te kāraṇatvena sṛṣṭikṛtkāraṇeśvarāḥ  || 12.75 ||

hartā kartā svatantrāste madrūpaguṇacetasaḥ  |
punaḥ stotraṃ samārabdhaṃ taistu nāthaiḥ punarhyaham  || 12.76 ||

yāvan'nekavidhānena tāvatteṣāṃ varapradaḥ  |
punaḥ santoṣito'tīva varaṃ prārthaya puṣkalam  || 12.77 ||
tairuktaṃ devadeveśa liṅgedaṃ sarvatomukham  |
yena pūjyo bhavāmīha tadvaraṃ dada me prabho  || 12.78 ||

asya liṅgasya māhātmyaṃ vyāptibhūtaṃ yathāsthitam  |
tathā kuru maheśāna jānīmo niścayaṃ yathā  || 12.79 ||

tatasteṣāṃ mahādevi vyāptimārga[ḥ] pradarśitaḥ  |
vyaktaliṅgaṃ kṛtaṃ paścātṣaḍadhvaguṇagocaram  || 12.80 ||

ṣaḍadhvaropadeśena tanusteṣāṃ pradarśitā  |
dviraṇḍena kṛtaṃ dehaṃ śeṣā vaktrāṇi cordhvataḥ  || 12.81 ||

vāmādikramayogena sañjātāni vidurbudhāḥ  |
chagalaṇḍottaraṃ vaktraṃ mahākālordhvataḥ sthitaḥ  || 12.82 ||

vālivaktraṃ bhavetpūrvaṃ puruṣaṃ jīvarūpiṇam  |
bhujaṅgaṃ dakṣine krūraṃ nāgarūpaṃ mahadbhutam  || 12.83 ||

paścimaṃ tu pinākākhyaṃ nivṛttisthaṃ niyāmakam  |
avidyākhyaṃ purā proktaṃ kṣaṇadhvaṃsīvināśakam  || 12.84 ||

atra madhye trayaṃ śreṣṭhamavināśākṣayāvyayam  |
māyā śambhuśca puruṣaṃ kṣīẏate na kadācana  || 12.85 ||

pañcavaktratanūdbhūtaṃ ṣaṭkauṣakulasambhavam  |
teṣāṃ pradarśitaṃ rūpaṃ kalādhvaṃ kulanāyakam  || 12.86 ||

sādhito'haṃ tvayā viṣṇo niścalenāntarātmanā  |
bhāvādhiṣṭhānayogena tenedaṃ darśitaṃ mayā  || 12.87 ||

svādhiṣṭhānaṃ paraṃ yogaṃ praviśya mama sarvathā  |
liṅgaṃ praviśya medhāvī yena pūjyo bhaviṣyasi  || 12.88 ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate ṣaṭprakārādhikārārṇavo nāma dvādaśamaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************


śrībhairava uvāca

evaṃ devi mayāsau tu pūrvaṃ cakradharaḥ sudhīḥ  |
liṅge svādhiṣṭhito yena svādhiṣṭhānaṃ tu tena vai  || 13.1 ||

māyāśāmbhavasaṃsthānaṃ kalādhiṣṭhānaśāsanam  |
puruṣāṇusamāyuktaṃ svādhiṣṭhānamato'rthataḥ  || 13.2 ||

rāgeṇa rañjitātmā vai niyatyā yo niyāmitaḥ  |
avidyāprerito gacchetsvargaṃ vā svabhrameva vā  || 13.3 ||

tritayaṃ śubhamuddiṣṭamaśubhaṃ tu tathā trikam  |
ṣaṭkauṣikamidaṃ sthānaṃ vyāptibhūtaṃ mayā tava  || 13.4 ||

śakte yaṃ tu samākhyātaṃ śāmbhavaṃ paratottare  |
kathayiṣyāmi suśroṇi idānīṃ pratyayaṃ śṛṇu  || 13.5 ||

sādhanaṃ lokavikhyātaṃ ṣaṭsiddhādhiṣṭhitaṃ tu tat |
svādhiṣṭhānaṃ tu liṅgasthaṃ yathā sthānagataṃ śṛṇu  || 13.6 ||

dviraṇḍena tanustasya chagalaṇḍāditaḥ kramāt |
yatra sthāne sthitā māyā mahākāla mukhāgrataḥ  || 13.7 ||

vālīśvaraṃ tu randhrasthaṃ bhujaṅga maṇimastake  |
pinākinaṃ tu sīmanyāṃ saṃsthitaṃ tu niyāmakam  || 13.8 ||

atra yogaṃ pravakṣyāmi yogināṃ śubhadāyakam  |
yena paśyanti taṃ liṅgaṃ pūrvoktaṃ guṇaśālinam  || 13.9 ||

dvīpadvīpeśvaraṃ nāthaṃ dvādaśārcisamanvitam  |
māsamāsāvadhī'kaikamabhyasanto guṇān labhet || 13.10 ||

yuñjantaḥ śriyamāpnoti ṣaḍrasāsvādanaṃ kramāt |
kaṭutiktakaṣāyāmlaṃ kṣāraśca madhurāvadhi  || 13.11 ||

nāthaṃ dvīpastu dvīpārci dvīpādikramasaṃyutam  |
dhyānasthānasamāyogāttannāsti yanna sādhayet || 13.12 ||

ṣaḍvaktraṃ cintyamātmānaṃ devīṃ ca guṇalālasām  |
mukhena mukhamālagnaṃ hyātmaliṅgoparisthitam  || 13.13 ||

bhāvānandarasālāḍhyaṃ helādolairvyavasthitam  |
liṅgarandhraṃ tu randhrasthaṃ tena mārgeṇa cābhyaset || 13.14 ||

vidyujjyotilatākāraṃ vaktramaṇḍalaniḥsṛtam  |
tasya vai hyātmanaḥ paścātnityameva samabhyaset || 13.15 ||

ṣaṇmāsena varārohe sphoṭayetparvatānapi  |
dvitīye'naṅgarūpo'sau kṣobhayeta varāṅganām  || 13.16 ||

tatsthāne tiryagālokātkiṃ tu raktāruṇena tu  |
martyajān khecarān yakṣān rakṣaḥpaiśācagocarān  || 13.17 ||

kṣobhayeddhāṭakīśasya puraṃ sādhakapuṅgavaḥ  |
tatraiva brahmayogena cakrāvartena cakṣuṣā  || 13.18 ||

karṣayennikhilān sarvān phalapuṣpāditaḥ kramāt |
martyalokāditaḥ kṛtvā pātālasvargasaṃsthitān  || 13.19 ||

tṛtīyena tu yogena caturthaṃ stambhane kṣamaḥ  |
kiṃ tu pītena tattvākṣaścakṣuṣā paripūrṇadhīḥ  || 13.20 ||

stambhayedgaganāmbhobhirvimānapavanau mahān  |
nāvāgati gajānāṃ ca vājicaurāripannagān  || 13.21 ||

pañcamena tu yogena tatrasthaḥ kṛṣṇamaṇḍale  |
mārayedyasya kruddho'sau yaḥ kruddho mriyate tu saḥ  || 13.22 ||

sa devāsuratrailokyaṃ dvipadaṃ vā catuṣpadam  |
caturdaśavidhasyāpi kruddhaḥ saṃharaṇe kṣamaḥ  || 13.23 ||

ṣaṣṭhamūrdhvaparaṃ sthānaṃ brahmadvāreti kīrtitam  |
aprasiddhena mārgeṇa helādolaikatatparaḥ  || 13.24 ||

vidyullatāchaṭāṭopaṃ vāraṃ vāraṃ muhurmuhuḥ  |
abhyasedyāva yogeśi tāvadānandatāṃ vrajet || 13.25 ||
tyajetsvābhāvikaṃ sarvaṃ saṃsārapathagocaram  |
niḥsaṃjño mṛtavadyogī kāṣṭhavadupalakṣyate  || 13.26 ||

sāttvikaṃ rājasaṃ bhāvaṃ tāmasaṃ tu yadā bhavet |
trayāvasthagato yogī pūrvaliṅgasamo bhavet || 13.27 ||

pūjyate sa suraiḥ sarvaiḥ khecarasthairna cāparaiḥ  |
ṣaṭprakāramidaṃ liṅgaṃ yo jānāti sa tattvavit || 13.28 ||

etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ sarahasyaṃ sugopitam  |
na deyaṃ duṣṭabuddhīnāṃ jñānacaureṣu śāsanam  || 13.29 ||

yāvanna sarvabhāvena kāyakleśasahā narāḥ  |
tataścedaṃ pradātavyamanyāyānnarakaṃ vrajet || 13.30 ||

etatkuleśvaraṃ liṅgaṃ pralayotpattikārakam  |
yo jānāti varārohe sa siddho hyatra śāsane  || 13.31 ||

tasmālliṅgaṃ na nindeta yāvattāvattanau sthitam  |
sarveṣāṃ vidyate hyetatkalpanā hyatra kāraṇam  || 13.32 ||

dvipadaṃ martyajaṃ liṅgaṃ raupyahemamaṇirmayam  |
mantramūrtikuleśānamāvāhyāpyatra ropitam  || 13.33 ||

svādhiṣṭhānaṃ tu tattasya pūjanāttatpadaṃ labhet |
prathamaṃ na hi sarvasya sarvajñatvaṃ prapadyate  || 13.34 ||

tasmānna nindayelliṅgaṃ tanmūrtiguṇaśālinam  |
sarvajñatve'pi samprāpte samayān samprapālayet || 13.35 ||

tamorajaḥpraviṣṭānāmahaṅkāravaśānugām  |
na teṣāṃ sādhanaṃ siddhirjāyate patanaṃ punaḥ  || 13.36 ||

śrīkuleśvaradevasya liṅgādhāraṃ śṛṇu priye  |
vṛttākāraṃ sarandhraṃ tu caturasraṃ prakīrtitam  || 13.37 ||

trirandhravalayākāraṃ śṛṅgāṭākṛtivarcasam  |
piṇḍikopariliṅgasya jagadyonirmahāmbike  || 13.38 ||
catuṣkalasamopetaṃ catuṣpīṭhasamanvitam  |
catuḥsiddhasamāyuktaṃ jñātvā siddhiphalapradam  || 13.39 ||

khaḍgīśaḥ prathame vṛtte jalapaṭṭe niveśitaḥ  |
bakaścāṅkurarūpeṇa randhrasandhau vyavasthitaḥ  || 13.40 ||

śvetaḥ praṇālake dvisthaḥ pravāhe saṃvyavasthitaḥ  |
bhṛgurmekhalarūpeṇa samantātparimaṇḍalam  || 13.41 ||

śṛṅgāṭake tu pīṭhāni khātasyāgre vilakṣayet |
o-jā-pū-kā-matatvaṃ tu madhyadakṣiṇavāmataḥ  || 13.42 ||

agradeśe tu koṭisthaṃ śṛṅgāṭaṃ caturasrakam  |
kṛ-tre-dvā-ka-kramādevamādhāraṃ caturaṅgulam  || 13.43 ||

tatrābhyāsaṃ prakurvīta abhiṣekaguṇānvitaḥ  |
ājñālabdhaparo bhaktaścaturmāsātphalaṃ labhet || 13.44 ||

jalapaṭṭagataṃ devamādipīṭhasamanvitam  |
śuklavarṇaṃ yadā dhyāyecchāntipuṣṭiparaṃ vrajet || 13.45 ||

taṃ tyajya bakanāthākhyaṃ dakṣapīṭhagataṃ yadā  |
tadā puṣṭiśriyārogyaṃ pūrvābhyāsaphalaṃ labhet || 13.46 ||

śvetaṃ praṇālarandhrasthaṃ vāmapīṭhagataṃ yadā  |
abhyasetkramayogena vaśyākarṣaṇamāraṇam  || 13.47 ||

rogavyādhijayaḥ puṣṭiḥ kramātkhecaratāṃ vrajet |
bhṛgu[ṃ] kāmasamāyogādabhyasanto guṇān labhet || 13.48 ||

śāntipuṣṭivaśākarṣaṃ purakṣobhaṃ pṛthuśriyam  |
valīpalitanāśastu vāgīśatvaṃ pravartate  || 13.49 ||

sañjīvanaṃ mṛtānāṃ ca drumākṛṣṭi jalaplavam  |
vātameghanadīnāṃ ca stambhakṛdvācahāriṇaḥ  || 13.50 ||

vācāsiddhiḥ prabhutvaṃ ca stobhakṛtparvatādiṣu  |
stambhayetsarvasainyāni ādhāragatacetasaḥ  || 13.51 ||
ādhāraṃ kramamityuktaṃ tadvinā sādhanaṃ na hi  |
na mokṣo na ca bhuktiśca yāvāmnāyo na veditaḥ  || 13.52 ||

etadādhāramityuktamājñābhedamataḥ śṛṇu  |
yena vijñātamātreṇa sarvajñatvaṃ prapadyate  || 13.53 ||

kramaṃ śāmbhavamityāhuryasmātsambhavate'khilam  |
vācāsiddhestu ādhāraṃ vācayājñā pravartate  || 13.54 ||

śāmbhavābhyāsamātraṃ tu yatkramātsampravartate  |
athāṇurudraśaktisthā bhāvabhūteṣu śāmbhavā  || 13.55 ||

adhikārātmikā hyeṣā viśuddhiguṇadāyikā  |
na mokṣo vidyate teṣāṃ prasādājñā vivarjitā  || 13.56 ||

prasādaṃ kramamityuktaṃ kramājjñānaṃ tu śāmbhavam |
śāmbhavena samastārthān vetti paśyati cāgrataḥ  || 13.57 ||

yadā dṛṣṭaḥ samastārtho gurutaḥ śāstrataḥ svataḥ  |
tadāsau kramikaḥ proktaḥ kramatulyo'thavā hi saḥ  || 13.58 ||

ājñābhyāse na muktistu yāvāmnayo na veditaḥ  |
sabāhyābhyantaraṃ bhadre ato'rthaṃ toṣayedgurum  || 13.59 ||

sarvāṅgabhaktiyuktastu triśuddhenāntarātmanā  |
bhaktyā cārādhayennāthaṃ tasya sarvaṃ prapadyate  || 13.60 ||

yā bhaktiḥ sā bhavecchaktiḥ śaktyā sambhavate kramaḥ  |
kramātsambhavate vācā vācayājñā pravartate  || 13.61 ||

yādṛśena tu bhāvena gurudevamupāsayet |
tādṛgbhāvena tasyājñā kiñciccāṃśena saṅkramet || 13.62 ||

uparodhaprasaṅgena uktakālādavāntare  |
kiñciccājñā bhavettasya bhūtormiguṇasaṅkulā  || 13.63 ||

paripakvaphalaṃ yadvatsusvādaguṇasaṃyutam  |
tadvacchiṣyo'pi kālena samastārthavido bhavet || 13.64 ||
rasojjhitaṃ na susvādaṃ yathāmlaphalabhakṣaṇam  |
tathā hyapakvaśiṣyāṇāṃ vṛthājñānapariśramaḥ  || 13.65 ||

sāmarthyenāpi dattājñā bhūtāṃśena samāviśet |
ūrmigrasto hyahaṅkārī ahaṅkārādvinaśyati  || 13.66 ||

ekapakṣaḥ samākhyātaḥ sāmprataṃ vai'dhikaṃ śṛṇu  |
sthūlamārgeṇa sūkṣmatvaṃ kramādevaṃ prajāyate  || 13.67 ||

bhedo randhraṃ tathā chidramekā saṃjñā yaśasvini  |
sabījāśceti nirbījāḥ sthitibhedo dvidhā sthitaḥ  || 13.68 ||

kūrmānandaṃ ca painākaṃ mahākālaṃ tṛtīyakam  |
krodhīśamarghiṇā yuktaṃ vidyā caiva dvitīyakam  || 13.69 ||

jhaṇṭīśena samāyuktaṃ kulavāgeśvarī smṛtā  |
praṇavaṃ kaulikaṃ gṛhya lakulīśādanantaram  || 13.70 ||

śrīkaṇṭhaṃ coṣmaṇā yuktaṃ lakulīśādimaṃ punaḥ  |
upadeśasamāyuktaṃ sarvadaṃ bhṛgu-r-āvadhim  || 13.71 ||

vajrarandhrāntare yojya kodaṇḍāntaṃ vicintayet |
ślokadvādaśakopetaṃ cakradvādaśakānvitam  || 13.72 ||

guruvaktrasamopetaṃ dhyātvā vācāṃ prasādhayet |
smaraṇamātrayogena kālakṣepo na cātra vai  || 13.73 ||

atha cedabhyasedevaṃ vajrakodaṇḍakāntaram  |
sarvajñatvaṃ bhavettasya kriyākhyaṃ yāva sundari  || 13.74 ||

kriyāto' ghaṃ pravarteta vācājñāmoghaśālinī  |
vāgīśatvaṃ punaḥ paścādvāgīśaḥ sṛjate'khilam  || 13.75 ||

jvalantaṃ svena tejena lakulī vāmamārgataḥ  |
sa jyeṣṭhaḥ kulasantāne raudraśaktibhirāvṛtaḥ  || 13.76 ||

trayastriṃśatime tattve hyadhikāro layaḥ pare  |
saṃvartaḥ kevalo nāthaḥ sabījo bījavarjitaḥ  || 13.77 ||
asya randhrāntarasthānamājñādhyānaṃ tu śāmbhavam  |
na mantroccāraṇaṃ jñānaṃ na mudrā dhyāna cintanam  || 13.78 ||

nāyāmo na nirodhaśca granthibhedo na dhāraṇā  |
sarvopāyavihīno'sau kiṃ tu sthānavikalpanā  || 13.79 ||

adhordhvaromasaṃsthāne tatra bhāvaṃ vinikṣipet |
ūrdhvagranthiradhaḥkando madhye kiñcinna vidyate  || 13.80 ||

tatsthānaṃ śāmbhavaṃ viddhi śambhurandhropalakṣitam  |
na kiñciccintayettatra īṣadāropaṇaṃ citau  || 13.81 ||

evaṃ saṃsmaraṇādeva jñānānandaṃ pravartate  |
vācāmātreṇa cānyeṣāṃ kurute pratyayān bahūn  || 13.82 ||

sakṛtsaṃsmaraṇādevamabhyasantaḥ śriyaṃ labhet |
vijñānāni ca sarvāṇi ṣaṇmāsābhyāsayogataḥ  || 13.83 ||

catustriṃśapadeśānaṃ vindate vatsarāṣṭakān  |
tatsthānaṃ sahajaṃ tasya saṃyogaṃ yadi tasya vai  || 13.84 ||

bhujaṅgānugrahīśena mantrayuktena tatpriye  |
uccaranto hanetsṛṣṭiṃ lakulīśāntakārakaḥ  || 13.85 ||

bhogaścāsya hi nādānte layaḥ syādvyāpinīpade  |
ājñābhedadvayaṃ nāthe hyetattatparamārthataḥ  || 13.86 ||

śaktimārgagataṃ viddhi śeṣo'nyaccottare punaḥ  |
etatṣaṭkaṃ paraṃ śāktaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ parikīrtitam  || 13.87 ||

yogiṣaṭkasamāyuktaṃ sadyomelakadāyakam  |
tvayā mahyaṃ mayā tubhyaṃ tvayāhaṃ tvaṃ mayā punaḥ  || 13.88 ||

kathitaṃ tava suśroṇi tvatsaṅgānyeṣu mokṣadam  |
paśu pakṣi tathā vṛkṣāstṛṇagulmasarīsṛpam  || 13.89 ||

vyākhyānaṃ yatra mārgasya mu cyante tānyavaśyataḥ  |
yena varṣasahasrāṇi bhaktyā ārādhito hyaham  || 13.90 ||
janmanyapaścime puṃsāṃ jāyate'daṃ sudurlabham  |
cetaścittavihīnānāṃ prasaṅgānmuktidaṃ priye  || 13.91 ||

kiṃ punaścittayuktānāṃ saṅgādeva na muktidam  |
ato'rthaṃ saha saṃyogaṃ khānapānaṃ sahāsanam  || 13.92 ||

vastramālyopahārāṇi svajuṣṭānyaṃ na dāpayet |
asatsaṅgaṃ na kartavyaṃ satsaṅgaṃ na vivarjayet || 13.93 ||

śuddhāśayasamācāraṃ jñānādhāraṃ prapūjayet |
viśuddhaṃ kāñcanaṃ yadvannāgasaṅgādvinaśyati  || 13.94 ||

evaṃ viśuddhatattvo'pi asatsaṅgādvinaśyati  |
yoginīkulagarbhasthaḥ kulavīrāṅgasambhavaḥ  || 13.95 ||

siddho'sau siddhasantāne ṣaṭpādārthaṃ sa vindati  |
etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ dakṣiṇedaṃ salakṣaṇam  || 13.96 ||

yogaṣaṭkaṃ kulādhāraṃ pṛcchasvānyadyadicchasi  || 13.97 ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate dakṣiṇaṣaṭkaparijñāno nāma trayodaśamaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************



śrīkubjikā uvāca

dayā ca paramā mahyaṃ maṇḍalīśakulākulam  |
ṣaṭpadārtho mayā jñātaḥ ṣaḍyoginyo vada prabho  || 14.1 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

uvāca bhagavānnāthaḥ kubjīśāni vadāmyaham  |
sadyaḥpratyayakartāraṃ sadyomelakadāyakam  || 14.2 ||

kam-ba-mā-lam-vi-kā devyaḥ kramātṣaṭkaṃ kulākule  |
mokṣabhuktipradātāraḥ ṣaḍetāḥ ṣaṭkanāyikāḥ  || 14.3 ||

ḍa-ra-la-ka-sa-ha-jotthāḥ saṃsthitāḥ kulagocare  |
kulaṣaṭkanivāsinyo nigrahānugrahe'pi vā  || 14.4 ||

ekaikānugrahantyetā nigrahantyanulomakṛt |
ṣaṭpattre pūjitāḥ santyaḥ ṣaṭpadārthaphalapradāḥ  || 14.5 ||


śrīkubjikā uvāca

purataḥ pṛcchayiṣyāmi prārabdhaṃ kathayasva me  |
catuṣkapañcakānāṃ ca caturṇāṃ ca phalodayam  || 14.6 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

ṣaṭpadārthopadeśena samastaṃ kathitaṃ mayā  |
tathāpi kathayiṣyāmi adhikāro yathā sthitaḥ  || 14.7 ||

o-jā-pū-kāmuko bhedo dṛṣṭvākṣaraviniścitam  |
mudrācatuṣṭayopetaṃ saṅketātkathitaṃ priye  || 14.8 ||
