caturyugaṃ catuṣpīṭhaṃ yonyaścatvāri yāḥ priye  |
sabāhyābhyantare sarvaṃ kathayāmi yathārthataḥ  || 14.9 ||

nābhyadhodarahṛtkaṇṭhe kṛ-tre-dvā-kamanukramāt |
o-jā-pū-kāmarūpiṇyaścatvāryevaṃ vyavasthitāḥ  || 14.10 ||

sabāhyābhyantareṇaiva catuṣkaṃ parikīrtitam  |
gurūpadeśasaṃyuktaṃ pañcakaṃ kathayāmi te  || 14.11 ||

devyo dūtyastathā mātryo yoginyaḥ khecarīgaṇaḥ  |
pañcadhā hyadhikāro'yaṃ kurvantyetāḥ kulākule  || 14.12 ||

devīcatuṣṭayādhāraṃ svādhiṣṭhānaṃ ṣaḍāśrayam  |
randhrakāmaśikhirgolaṃ dhvajakandāntakāvadhim  || 14.13 ||

saptamaṃ tattvamuddiṣṭaṃ brahmaṇaḥ padamuttamam  |
atra sṛṣṭiḥ samutpannā ṣaṭkauṣakulasambhavā  || 14.14 ||

devyādhiṣṭhitamīsānaṃ svādhiṣṭhānaguṇāśrayam  |
randhradvādaśakopetaṃ  kāmadvādaśakānvitam  || 14.15 ||

vahnīśvare tathāpyevaṃ dvādaśaṃ dhvajakandayoḥ  |
piṇḍadvādaśakopetaṃ caturāśītyanekaśaḥ  || 14.16 ||

śāmbhavādhiṣṭhite yoge svādhiṣṭhāne nirāmaye  |
caturāśītiguṇānāṃ vijñānapadavīṃ labhet || 14.17 ||

atra madhye maheśāni sarvakāraṇakāraṇam  |
yathā niṣpadyate piṇḍaṃ tattathā cāvadhārayet || 14.18 ||

ādhāraśaktimādau tu brahmaśaktimatordhvataḥ  |
etadbrahmāṇḍamityuktaṃ saptalokasamanvitam  || 14.19 ||

ādhāraṃ caiva bhūrlokaṃ bhuvarlokaṃ tu kāmagam  |
svarlokaṃ śikhimityuktaṃ maharlokaṃ tu golakam  || 14.20 ||

dhvajasthaṃ janamityuktaṃ tapolokaṃ tu kandagam  |
satyalokaṃ tu tattvasthaṃ brahmagranthyāvadhisthitam  || 14.21 ||

satyalokādadhaḥ sṛṣṭiḥ kalādyā piṇḍasambhavā  |
ājñātaḥ sampravartante trayāntaṃ yāva mānasī  || 14.22 ||

caturṇāṃ tu punaḥ sṛṣṭiradhastādyonisambhavā  |
madhyamanthānayogena śubhāśubhanibandhanam  || 14.23 ||

bhuvarlokādadholoke vividhā sṛṣṭiḥ pravartate  |
jarāyujā ca sā jñeyā bahuduḥkhasamākulā  || 14.24 ||

kandātsañjāyate sṛṣṭiḥ kandaṃ vai saptalaukikam  |
randhrādau granthiparyantaṃ vijñeyaṃ saptadhātukam  || 14.25 ||

kandātsañjāyate'ṅkuraḥ aṅkurānmūlasambhavaḥ  |
mūlātparṇalatāśākhā[s]  tataḥ puṣpaphalādikam  || 14.26 ||
phalaṃ śarīramityuktaṃ dhātuvṛkṣasamudbhavam  |
piṇḍaṃ kandodbhavaṃ tacca śubhāśubhajalāntagam  || 14.27 ||

tvagraktamāṃsa randhrādau aśubhaṃ kāmavahnigam  |
śubhaṃ medo'sthimajjāntaṃ golakandadhvajānvitam  || 14.28 ||

aśubhaṃ tu rajaḥ sākṣāttriśaktiguṇa mātṛjam  |
paitṛkaṃ śubhamuddiṣṭaṃ reto hyātmādi-m-īśvaraḥ  || 14.29 ||

piṇḍaṃ sarvatra sāmānyamubhayorapi kubjike  |
saṅgame śivaśaktīnāṃ piṇḍabandho bhavettadā  || 14.30 ||

yatkiñciccintayenmātā yatkiñciccintayetpitā  |
ubhau bhāvasamāyogāttadbhāvaḥ sahajo bhavet || 14.31 ||

viśvarūpo maṇiryadvadupādhiviṣayo yathā  |
tatkālopādhicintāyāṃ sa rāgaḥ sahajo bhavet || 14.32 ||

etadantaramāsādya piṇḍaḥ kāraṇarūpadhṛk |
bandhate pañcadhātmānaṃ pañcapañcādibhiḥ kramāt || 14.33 ||

puruṣaṃ prakṛtiścaiva guṇo'haṅkāra dhīrmanaḥ  |
ṣaṇmukhastu paro hyātmā catuṣkapariveṣṭitaḥ  || 14.34 ||

adhordhvaṃ nīyate jīvaḥ koṣakīṭa-m-iva sthitaḥ  |
prakāśayati cātmānaṃ badhnāti ca punaḥ punaḥ  || 14.35 ||

niyāmikācatuṣkeṇa sannaddho bhramate hyaṇuḥ  |
ekaikaṃ taṃ caturdhā tu devīcakraṃ prakīrtitam  || 14.36 ||

niyāmikā bhavetpṛthvī pratiṣṭhā śabdapūrvikā  |
śrotrapūrṇā bhavedvidyā śāntirvāgeśvarī smṛtā  || 14.37 ||

kṣoṇī tu prathamā jñeyā śabdadevī dvitīyakā  |
tṛtīyā śrotrikā nāma vācādevī caturthikā  || 14.38 ||

devīcatuṣṭayaṃ hyetadekaikaṃ tu catuṣṭayam  |
etaccatuṣṭayaṃ devi saṃsārapathavartmani  || 14.39 ||
catuṣṭayaṃ tu bhūtānāṃ tanmātrāṇāṃ catuṣṭayam  |
buddhīndriyacatuṣkaṃ tu catuṣkaṃ karmayājinām  || 14.40 ||

pañcakaṃ tattu vijñeyaṃ puṃsaḥ ṣaḍguṇasaṃyutam  |
evaṃ niṣpadyate piṇḍaṃ pañcadhā pañcaviṃśakam  || 14.41 ||

ṣaṭkauśikaṃ tu mārgo'yamādidevīcatuṣṭayam  |
kathitaṃ sarahasyaṃ tu ṣaṭsiddhapuraniścayam  || 14.42 ||

uvāca kubjikā nāthaṃ ṣaṭsiddhapuraniścayam  |
na me jñātaṃ kuleśāna saṃsphuṭaṃ kathayasva me  || 14.43 ||

uvāca bhagavānnāthaḥ kubjīśāni mayā tava  |
kathitā saptadhā sṛṣṭiḥ siddhān sapta vadāmyaham  || 14.44 ||

navatattveśvaro nātho navacakreśvareśvaraḥ  |
brahmāṇḍaśivasiddho' sau  hartā kartāvatārakaḥ  || 14.45 ||

sa nāthaḥ sarvasiddhānāṃ patitve saṃvyavasthitaḥ  |
kandabhūto'ṅkuro'sau vai ṣaṭpurādhipatiḥ prabhuḥ  || 14.46 ||

pumpuraṃ prathamaṃ kandaṃ prākṛtaṃ cāparaṃ puram  |
guṇānandaṃ tu golākhyaṃ garvaṃ jālandharātmakam  || 14.47 ||

dhīpuraṃ kāmarūpākhyamādhāraṃ tu manaḥpuram  |
pumpure śrīmatkhaḍgīśaḥ khagīśaḥ prākṛte pure  || 14.48 ||

viśvanātho guṇānande jhaṇṭīśo'hammahāpure  |
dhīpure'nugrahīśāno mitreśāno manaḥpure  || 14.49 ||

ṣaṭpurādhipatirnāthāḥ kaulīśāḥ kulanāyakāḥ  |
kulasiddhāḥ samākhyātāḥ ṣaṭkramaughaprakāśakāḥ  || 14.50 ||

bhaviṣyanti purā kalpe martyalokamupāgatāḥ  |
prabhurānanda yogākhyamāvalī pādamantimam  || 14.51 ||

bhaviṣyantyapare kalpe kulasiddhāḥ kulotthitāḥ  |
kulasiddhādhipo deva ājñāmoghakuleśvaraḥ  || 14.52 ||
ṣaṭkulānāṃ tvasau nāthastasmātsarvaṃ kulānvayam  |
navānāṃ cakravartīnāṃ cakravartistvasau prabhuḥ  || 14.53 ||

tasmātpravartate sṛṣṭirbrahmādyā kulasambhavā  |
ṣaṭpurāṇāṃ tamādhāraṃ kartāraṃ kulapaddhatau  || 14.54 ||

śāstāraṃ brahmajantūnāṃ devīnāṃ tu catuṣṭayam  |
apare brahmaṇaḥ sṛṣṭau yatkiñcidvāṅmayākhilam  || 14.55 ||

tatsarvaṃ devibhirvyāptaṃ tvayādhārāntakāvadhim  |
caturmukheśvarasyānte kandaḥ saptavidhaśca yaḥ  || 14.56 ||

tatra jātaṃ jagatsarvaṃ sadevāsuramānuṣam  |
devīcatuṣṭayānāṃ tu mārgo'yaṃ kathito'khilam  || 14.57 ||

adhunā kathayiṣyāmi dūtīnāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ yathā  |
brahmādhāramiti proktaṃ saptādhārasamanvitam  || 14.58 ||

prathamaiṣā parā sṛṣṭiḥ śāmbhavī yā kulādhvare  |
navatattveśvareśasya nābhyadhastāttu maṇḍalam  || 14.59 ||

śatakoṭisuvistīrṇaṃ devīkulasamāśrayam  |
trikoṇaṃ caiva ṣaṭkoṇaṃ vṛkṣavallīkramastathā  || 14.60 ||

dvividhājñādhikāro'yaṃ nigrahānugrahaṃ prati  || 14.61 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

brahmaṇo'ṇḍakaṭāhasya samantātparimaṇḍalam  |
sahasrakoṭivistīrṇamapsu viṣṇoḥ puraṃ mahat || 14.62 ||

ardhenduśikharākāraṃ potanāvākulaṃ tu tat |
anekatattvasaṅkīrṇaṃ navanālopaśobhitam  || 14.63 ||

padmapattramanaupamyaṃ ṣoḍaśāraṃ sakarṇikam  |
yatra dūtyaḥ svabhāvinyaḥ krīḍante vividhaiḥ sukhaiḥ  || 14.64 ||

yatrāsau ramate nityamuttamaḥ puruṣottamaḥ  |
tatsthānaṃ paramaṃ proktaṃ yatra dūtyo'mṛtodbhavāḥ  || 14.65 ||

tāstu kṣubdhā yadā kāle'mṛtaṃ muñcanti bhāvitāḥ  |
tadā caturvidhā sṛṣṭirbrahmacakre tu nānyathā  || 14.66 ||

ṣoḍaśāre mahāpadme divyāmṛtapariplute  |
tatrastho dūtibhiḥ sārdhaṃ poṣayedbrahmaṇaḥ padam  || 14.67 ||

brahmakandāntabījānāmūrdhvarandhrāṅkuratrayam  |
tatra granthīśvaro'nantaḥ svaśaktikiraṇojjvalaḥ  || 14.68 ||

sthito mahāmbhasi madhye navadūtīsamanvitaḥ  |
sṛṣṭikṛdbhagavānantaḥ padārthapada-m-īśvaraḥ  || 14.69 ||

kapālaṃ caṇḍalokeśaṃ yogeśaṃ tu manonmanam  |
hāṭakeśvara kravyādaṃ mudreśaṃ diṅmaheśvaram  || 14.70 ||

śrī anantīśa nāthānto navaite bhāsvareśvarāḥ  |
vibhajya navadhātmānaṃ padasṛṣṭiṃ vinirmite  || 14.71 ||

ekaikā navadhātmānaṃ punaścaivaṃ sṛjanti te  |
navanava padāni syurdūtīnāṃ kāraṇātmakam  || 14.72 ||

padabhuktigatānāṃ tu dūtīnāṃ ca pṛthakpṛthak |
nāmāni kīrtayiṣyāmi yā yasyāṅgasamudbhavāḥ  || 14.73 ||

śrī anantāṅgasambhūtāḥ sarve yāstu navaiva hi  |
adhikārapadaṃ teṣāṃ tatpravakṣyāmyaśeṣataḥ  || 14.74 ||

bindukā bindugarbhā ca nādinī nādagarbhajā  |
śaktī ca garbhiṇī cānyā parā garbhārthacāriṇī  || 14.75 ||

nirācārapadāvasthā madhyasthānantavarcasaḥ  |
adhikāraṃ prakurvanti kulākulasamāśritāḥ  || 14.76 ||

caṇḍā caṇḍamukhī caiva caṇḍavegā manojavā  |
caṇḍākṣī caṇḍanirghoṣā bhṛkuṭī caṇḍanāyikā  || 14.77 ||
caṇḍīśanāyakopetā hyakuleśapade sthitāḥ  |
tasmātpadātparā sṛṣṭirmanonmanyādisambhavā  || 14.78 ||

manojavā mano'dhyakṣā mānasī mananāyikā  |
manohārī manohlādī manaḥprītirmaneśvarī  || 14.79 ||

manonmanyā samāyuktā unmanaḥpadamāśritāḥ  |
navaiva paramā dūtyo manaśconmanakārikāḥ  || 14.80 ||

aindrī hutāśanī yāmyā nairṛtī vāruṇī tathā  |
vāyavī caiva kauberī aiśānī kaulikeśvarī  || 14.81 ||

samanaughapadāntasthā[ḥ] parākāśe vyavasthitāḥ  |
janayantyaparāṃ sṛṣṭiṃ yogākhyā vyāpinīpade  || 14.82 ||

hiraṇyā ca suvarṇā ca kāñcanī hāṭakā tathā  |
rukmiṇī ca manasvī ca subhadrā jambuhāṭakī  || 14.83 ||

vyāpinīpadamāpannā yogadūtyo mahābalāḥ  |
vyāpyavyāpakabhāvena vyāpayanti carācaram  || 14.84 ||

vāgvatī vāktathā vāṇī bhīmā citrarathā sudhī  |
devamātā hiraṇyā ca yogeśī navamā smṛtā  || 14.85 ||

vāgeśvarapadāntasthā vāgīśvaryasamanvitāḥ  |
mantravidyāṅgasambhūtāḥ sarvārthapratipādikāḥ  || 14.86 ||

vajriṇī śakti daṇḍī ca khaḍginī pāsinī dhvajī  |
gadī ca śūlinī padmī mudreśapadasambhavāḥ  || 14.87 ||

piṅgadūtyo mahāvīryāḥ kalākālavidhāyikāḥ  |
tejorūpā mahādevyo anantaguṇasambhavāḥ  || 14.88 ||

lambā lambastanī suṣkā pūtivaktrā mahānanā  |
gajavaktrā mahānāsā vidyutkravyādanāyikā  || 14.89 ||

kālānalāntare dūtyaḥ saṃhārapadasaṃsthitāḥ  |
anantaguṇavīryāstāḥ saṃharanti carācaram  || 14.90 ||
suprabuddhā prabuddhā ca caṇḍī muṇḍī kapālinī  |
mṛtyuhantā virūpākṣī kapardī kalanātmikā  || 14.91 ||

niyāmikāpadāntasthāḥ śubhāśubhaniyāmikāḥ  |
ekāśītivibhāgena dūtyo hyevaṃ mahābalāḥ  || 14.92 ||

navakeśvaradevasya udaredaṃ prakīrtitam  |
ekāśītipadairvyāptamanekāścaryasaṅkulam  || 14.93 ||

padarūpasamāyuktaṃ rūpātītādisaṃyutam  |
padmamārgavidhāyinyastritattvapadavīṃ labhet || 14.94 ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate devīdūtīnirṇayo nāma caturdaśamaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************



śrībhairava uvāca

devīdūtīmataṃ kubji kathitaṃ tu suvistaram  |
idānīṃ mātarāṇāṃ ca śṛṇu tvaṃ vyāptilakṣaṇam  || 15.1 ||

padapattrordhvagaṃ padmaṃ tejorūpaṃ subhāsvaram  |
lakṣakoṭisuvistīrṇamambhodhiparimaṇḍalam  || 15.2 ||

tatra madhye maheśānaṃ piṅgeśaṃ piṅgarūpiṇam  |
trikoṇapuramadhyasthaṃ tejorāśimanāmayam  || 15.3 ||

kāle hyaharmukhe prāpte kṣobhayitvā svakāṃ tanum  |
vijñānakevalānyaṣṭau bodhayāmāsa pudgalān  || 15.4 ||

aṣṭau mudrā mahāmātryo jagadyonirmahāmbike  |
tāsu jātaṃ jagatsarvaṃ yatkiñcidvāṅmayaṃ'khilam  || 15.5 ||

prathamā khecarīmudrā ātmī nāma dvitīyakā  |
tṛtīyā śaśinī jñeyā vahnināmā caturthikā  || 15.6 ||

pañcamī calanī nāma ṣaṣṭhī bhānumatī smṛtā  |
saptamī mahimā nāma aṣṭamī sukṛtālayā  || 15.7 ||

etā aṣṭau mahāmātryaḥ śrīmanmitrāṅgajodbhavāḥ  |
kurvanti vividhāṃ sṛṣṭiṃ sthūlasūkṣmaparāparām  || 15.8 ||

vibhājayanti cātmānamekaikā cāṣṭadhāṣṭadhā  |
teṣvanyāḥ ṣoḍaśādhārāścatuḥṣaṣṭyānta-m-antikāḥ  || 15.9 ||

khecarītanusambhūtāścāṣṭau mātryo diśātmikāḥ  |
aindrādīśāna-m-antasthāḥ sarvādhārāḥ parāparāḥ  || 15.10 ||

rudrāṇyaṃśāḥ samākhyātā devīkoṭāntasaṃsthitāḥ  |
saṃvartavīrasaṃyuktāḥ śambhukalpāvatārakāḥ  || 15.11 ||

ātmamātrodbhavā hyevaṃ sakalā niṣkalāśca ye  |
vijñānapralayāntānye dharmādharme niyojayet || 15.12 ||

tatrāṇavo'tha māyāyā aṣṭau mātryo'ṇusambhavāḥ  |
prayāgapuramadhyasthāścaṇḍakaulīśasaṃyutāḥ  || 15.13 ||

brāhmīcakraṃ samuddiṣṭamādikalpasya madhyagam  |
ātmamātryaṣṭakaṃ proktamindramātryaṣṭakaṃ vadet || 15.14 ||

chāyā tu śīkarā jyotsnā ṛturatnā suśītalā  |
payoghṛtavatī cānyā indramātryo'ṣṭa vaiṣṇavī  || 15.15 ||

vārāṇasīpurāntasthā amṛtādhāraśītalā  |
āpyāyanti jagatsarvaṃ pālayanti jagāmbikāḥ  || 15.16 ||

kalpāvāntaramāsādya krodhakaulīśasaṃyutāḥ  |
kurvanti vividhāṃ sṛṣṭimāpadāṃ mocayanti tāḥ  || 15.17 ||

tṛṣṇā rāgavatī mohā kāmā kopā tamotkaṭā  |
īrṣā śokavatītyaṣṭau vahnimātryaḥ prakīrtitāḥ  || 15.18 ||

kaulīśonmattasaṃyuktāḥ kollādrau saṃvyavasthitāḥ  |
mahāntakalpamadhyasthāḥ krīḍantyamitatejasā  || 15.19 ||
tvacī sparśavatī gandhā prāṇāpānī samānanī  |
udānī vyāni kṛkarā marunmātryo'ṣṭa kīrtitāḥ  || 15.20 ||

asitāṅgakuleśānamaṭṭahāsapurāntagāḥ  |
divyakalpe purā mātryaḥ krīḍantyamitatejasā  || 15.21 ||

tamohantā prabhā mohā tejinī dahanī dinā  |
jvalanī śoṣaṇītyaṣṭau arkamātryaḥ prakīrtitāḥ  || 15.22 ||

divyādivyapare kalpe jayantīpuramadhyagāḥ  |
rurukauleśasaṃyuktāstena sārdhaṃ ramanti tāḥ  || 15.23 ||

nivṛttiśca pratiṣṭhā ca vidyā śāntistathaiva ca  |
śāntātītā ca pṛthivī vajriṇī kāmadhenavī  || 15.24 ||

mahimeśānadevasya aṣṭau mātryaścaritragāḥ  |
adivyakalpamadhyasthā jhaṇṭhakauleśvarānvitāḥ  || 15.25 ||

aindryādhiṣṭhitacakrasthāḥ krīḍantyamitatejasā  |
pūryaṣṭakasya madhyasthā vajrahastā mahābalāḥ  || 15.26 ||

payoṣṇī vāruṇī śāntā amṛtā vyāpinī dravā  |
plavanī jalamātā ca payomātryo'ṣṭa viśrutāḥ  || 15.27 ||

vartamānikakalpe tu ekāmrakavanāntagāḥ  |
kapālīśakuleśānaṃ cāmuṇḍācakramadhyagāḥ  || 15.28 ||

śrīkuleśvaradevasya hṛtpadme' ṣṭadale sthitāḥ  |
īśānakramayogena sṛṣṭimārgāvalambikāḥ  || 15.29 ||

karṇikāyāṃ sthito devaścatuṣkaparivāritaḥ  |
raktākarālācaṇḍākṣī- mahocchuṣmāsamanvitaḥ  || 15.30 ||

mahāraktavanāntasthastejomaṇḍalamadhyagaḥ  |
navatattveśvaraṃ devaṃ pūryaṣṭakasamanvitaṃ li  || 15.31 ||

antardehasthito yasmātpūrayetsa carācaram  |
tena pūryaṣṭakaṃ proktamaṣṭadhā tu prapūrakam  || 15.32 ||
sa śivaḥ sarvasattvānāṃ hṛdisthaḥ parameśvaraḥ  |
bhrāmayeta jagatsarvaṃ yantrārūḍhastu māyayā  || 15.33 ||

dvāsaptatisahasrāṇāmuparistho'ntare sthitaḥ  |
akṣārūḍho'kṣagamyo'yaṃ manīśānāṃ piśācavat || 15.34 ||

guruvaktraṃ tu tatproktaṃ guruvaktrāttu labhyate  |
gurutvaṃ yātyasau yogī guruvaktrāvalambakaḥ  || 15.35 ||

tejastattvaṃ tu taṃ devi rudraśaktibhirāvṛtam  |
atordhvaṃ yoginīnāṃ tu ghaṭasthānaṃ nigadyate  || 15.36 ||

guruvaktre gurorvaktraṃ guruvaktre tu saṃsthitam  |
guruvaktrāttu labhyeta tasmātsantoṣayedgurum  || 15.37 ||

stutiṃ kṛtvā uvācedaṃ kubjikā parameśvaram  |
pṛcchāmi nātha yatnena ghaṭasthānaṃ suvistaram  || 15.38 ||

uvāca bhagavān devastvatpṛcchā rahitaṃ'naghe  |
nikhilaṃ kathayiṣyāmi yathā tvaṃ kubji cetasā  || 15.39 ||

koṭikoṭisuvistīrṇaṃ ghaṭādhāraṃ tatordhvataḥ  |
vajrapadmāṅkitaṃ divyaṃ piṅganāthāvadhisthitam  || 15.40 ||

anantaguṇadātāraṃ sarvārthapratipādakam  |
tasmātsampadyate sarvamaihiṃ pāratrikaṃ ca yat || 15.41 ||

yatra bhāṇḍāni sarvāṇi labhyante ca sahasradhā  |
layaṃ yānti punastatra ghaṭasthānaṃ taducyate  || 15.42 ||

yasmātsarvaṃ yathā yāti yasmādyānti truṭanti ca  |
yatra nirbhedyatāṃ yānti tatsthānaṃ ghaṭikātmakam  || 15.43 ||

bhuvanāṣṭottaraṃ bhāṇḍaṃ padabhāṇḍaṃ tu tatra vai  |
varṇabhāṇḍaṃ tu tatrasthaṃ mantratattvakalātmakam  || 15.44 ||

bhāṇḍāriṇo amīṣāṃ ca nivṛttyādyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ  |
yasyādhāreṇa vartante bhogānte tatpadaṃ punaḥ  || 15.45 ||
śrīkuleśvaradevasya hṛdyordhvaghaṭa-m-antare  |
tatkuleśvaradevasya durbhedyaṃ ṣaṭpuraṃ mahat || 15.46 ||

vibhajya svatanuṃ devaḥ ṣaṭpadārthapadena ca  |
ṣaḍyoginyo mahātejāḥ ṣaṭpure sanniveśitāḥ  || 15.47 ||

ḍāmarī rāmaṇī caiva lambakarṇī ca kākinī  |
sākinī yakṣiṇī cānyā kusumbhodasamudbhavāḥ  || 15.48 ||

vajrapadmāsanārūḍhāḥ kusumbhaguṇaśālinī[ḥ]  |
ṣaṭpurādhipatīnāṃ ca patitve samvyavasthitāḥ  || 15.49 ||

o-jā-pū-kāmabhedena kā-pū-jau-vyatikramāt |
etatkramaṃ samākhyātaṃ sṛṣṭisaṃhāragocare  || 15.50 ||

parāparavibhāgena sthūlasūkṣmaparāntagam  |
yathādhipati devatvaṃ yoginīnāṃ tathā śṛṇu  || 15.51 ||

duḥśīlā ḍamarī bhīmā ādhārasthā tu ḍāmarī  |
svādhiṣṭhānapurāntasthā rāmaṇī ramaṇātmikā  || 15.52 ||

maṇipūrapurāntasthā lambakarṇī mahadbhutā  |
dhvanidevapure kākī viśuddhau sākinī smṛtā  || 15.53 ||

ājñāpurasya madhyasthā yakṣiṇīti nigadyate  |
kubjikodarasambhūtāḥ ṣaḍyoginyaḥ parāparāḥ  || 15.54 ||

atra jātaṃ jagatsarvaṃ rudrāntaṃ brahmaṇo'vadhim  |
saṃharanti punastāstu vilomena prapūjitāḥ  || 15.55 ||

upadeśapragamyāstāḥ pāramparyakrameṇa vai  |
jñātavyaṃ ṣaḍvidhādhvānaṃ ṣaṭprakāraṃ gurormukhāt || 15.56 ||

śravaṇe cakṣuṣī nāsā mukhe caiva tathaiva hi  |
cibuke kaṇṭhadeśe tu guruvaktrāttu labhyate  || 15.57 ||

praṇayāviṣṭacetaskā uvācedaṃ kujeśvarī  |
vyāptisthānaṃ kathaṃ teṣāṃ kā kasya pathayāyinī  || 15.58 ||
bhaktyā pṛṣṭavatī matvā prahasya parameśvaraḥ  |
uvāca kubjike tubhyaṃ kathayāmyanupūrvaśaḥ  || 15.59 ||

ghaṭanti sarvavastūni yasyāṅge tu varānane  |
ghaṭasthānaṃ tu tenoktaṃ  sandohaguṇalakṣaṇam  || 15.60 ||

rudrapañcāśakopetaṃ śaktipañcāśakānvitam  |
cakravartyaṣṭakopetaṃ bhuvanāṣṭottaraṃ śatam  || 15.61 ||

manojanapadākīrṇamādhāragṛhasaṅkulam  |
oṃkāradalamadhyasthamaghorīhṛdayānvitam  || 15.62 ||

tatra sā ḍāmarī devī jvalatpiṅgogralocanā  |
manorasādhipatyasthā duḥsādhyā bhuvanātmikā  || 15.63 ||

punarjapattramadhyasthā ekāśītipadāvṛtā  |
śirasādhiṣṭhitā yogī svādhiṣṭhānagṛhākulā  || 15.64 ||

buddhijanapadākīrṇā padādhve rāmaṇī ramet |
duṣprekṣā duḥsahā bhīmā buddhyāsṛglolavigrahā  || 15.65 ||

maṇipūragṛhāntasthā kāmapattrāntare gatā  |
taḍitsahasravarṇābhā śikhārūpā maheśvarī  || 15.66 ||

ahaṅkārajanānandā prāleyāvalisannibhā  |
varṇeśvarī mahādevī kriyārūpā parāparā  || 15.67 ||

tasyāṅgasambhavā mantrāḥ sarvajñāste prakīrtitāḥ  |
lambikā sā samākhyātā māṃsāhārā ca lampaṭā  || 15.68 ||

pūrṇakadalamadhye tu nīlāñjanasamaprabhā  |
tanutrāṇakṛtāṭopā mantrādhvā-s-tu vibhūṣitā  || 15.69 ||

anāhatakamadhyasthā guṇānekajanāvṛtā  |
kākī medavasālubdhā guṇānnāśayate kṣaṇāt || 15.70 ||

kruddhā tamotkaṭā nityaṃ pracaṇḍogrā bhayānakā  |
mantrādhvānagatā yogī layabhogādhikārikā  || 15.71 ||
punaḥ puṃdalamadhyasthā viśuddhigṛhamadhyagā  |
saṃvartānalasaṅkāśā netrādhiṣṭhitabhāsvarā  || 15.72 ||

prākṛtajanasaṅkīrṇā kalādhvānasamāvṛtā  |
rudraśaktisamāviṣṭā raudrabhāvapradāyikā  || 15.73 ||

asthibhaṅgapriyā nityaṃ prākṛtārthavināśanī  |
sākinīyaṃ mahāghorā sthūlasūkṣmaparāntagā  || 15.74 ||

dakṣe kāmeśvarīpattre prāleyāvalisannibhā  |
kadācin [']nekarūpābhā upādhiguṇagocarā  || 15.75 ||

tattvādhvapuramadhyasthā ājñāmandiraśobhitā  |
puṃjanākṛtasampūrṇā mahāstraughasamāvṛtā  || 15.76 ||

majjabījāśinī yogī yakṣiṇī śakti śāmbhavī  |
ṣaḍyoginyo ghaṭādhāre ṣaḍadhvānavidhāyikāḥ  || 15.77 ||

tatra madhye sthitā kanyā viśvarūpā parāparā  |
sā patiḥ sarvayogīnāṃ yogeśī [']nantavigrahā  || 15.78 ||

kam-ba-mā-lam-vi-kāntābhirāvṛtā madhyasaṃsthitā  |
'nugrahanti punastāstu ṣaḍadhvānaprayogataḥ  || 15.79 ||

bhūtaṃ bhāvaṃ tathā śāktamāṇavaṃ raudra śāmbhavam  |
kramādanugrahantyetāstattvādau bhuvanāditaḥ  || 15.80 ||

dakṣiṇādhvānasaṃsthāstāḥ kṛntayanti mahāmbikāḥ  |
uttarasthāḥ prakurvanti śreyaṃ cāmṛtasambhavam  || 15.81 ||

vajrapadmāsanāsīnā ghaṭāmbodadhimadhyagāḥ  |
amṛtaughataraṅgaughaiḥ plāvayanti carācaram  || 15.82 ||

asyā rūpaṃ ca māhātmyaṃ sādhanaṃ siddhilakṣaṇam  |
purataḥ kathayiṣyāmi idānīṃ khecarīṃ śṛṇu  || 15.83 ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate ṣaṭprakāre yoginīnirṇayo nāma pañcadaśamaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************



śrībhairava uvāca

bhuvanāṅkurasaṃyuktaṃ padapattravibhūṣitam  |
varṇakaṇṭakasaṅkīrṇaṃ mantracchidrasamanvitam  || 16.1 ||

kalāsūtracitaṃ divyaṃ tattvagranthyuparisthitam  |
koṭikoṭiśatāyāmaṃ caturviṃśadalāyatam  || 16.2 ||

vyomodārṇavamadhyasthaṃ nīlāñjanasamaprabham  |
sahasrādityasaṅkāśaṃ kālāgniriva varcasam  || 16.3 ||

tatrordhve maṇḍalānyāhuścaturviṃśamanukramāt |
kṣetropakṣetrasandohān pūrvādau pārthivāditaḥ  || 16.4 ||

kṣetre dve copakṣetre dve sandohe dve vijānataḥ  |
dalopari virājante catuḥṣaṭkaṃ diśāditaḥ  || 16.5 ||

aṭṭahāsāditaḥ kṛtvā rājagṛhamapaścimam  |
pārthivādiprakṛtyantaṃ saṃyogānmaṇḍalāyate  || 16.6 ||

saumyādibhagnanāsāntāḥ sūryamaṇḍalasaṃsthitāḥ  |
pṛthagmaṇḍalacakrasthāḥ khecaryaḥ kulanāyikāḥ  || 16.7 ||

somamaṇḍalamadhye tu dvātriṃśānye mahābalāḥ  |
kubjikāṅgasamudbhūtāḥ pracaṇḍogrā guṇotkaṭāḥ  || 16.8 ||

caṇḍā ghaṇṭā mahānāsā sumukhī durmukhī balā  |
revatī prathamā ghorā saumyā bhīmā mahābalā  || 16.9 ||

jayā ca vijayā caiva ajitā cāparājitā  |
mahotkaṭā virūpākṣī śuṣkā cākāśamātarā  || 16.10 ||

sehārī jātahārī ca daṃṣṭrālī śuṣkarevatī  |
pipīlikā puṣpahārī aśanī sasyahārikā  || 16.11 ||
bhadrakālī subhadrā ca bhadrabhīmā subhadrikā  |
dvātriṃśadguṇaśālinyaścakreśvaryā mahāmbikāḥ  || 16.12 ||

somamaṇḍalamadhyasthā vahnisthāstu tataḥ śṛṇu  |
khageśvarapatīnāṃ tu patimātryo'tra saṃsthitāḥ  || 16.13 ||

asitāṅgatanūdbhūtāḥ kriyārūpāḥ parāparāḥ  |
kurvanti vividhāṃ sṛṣṭiṃ tvaritaṃ lāghave sthitāḥ  || 16.14 ||


śrīkubjikā uvāca

tvaritāśabdaṃ kathaṃ deva khañjīśabdaṃ kimucyate  |
kubjāśabdaṃ kathaṃ proktaṃ kathaṃ tanmaṇḍalāgaṇaḥ  || 16.15 ||

kathaṃ rūpaṃ maheśānyāḥ sarvametadyathākramam  |
ācacakṣva prayatnena yena bhrāntirvinaśyati  || 16.16 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

sādhu devi mahādurge kiṃ na budhyasi pārvati  |
yadasmāttvamihāyātā tatkiṃ te veditaṃ na hi  || 16.17 ||

vālāgraśatabhāgasya vibhinnasya sahasradhā  |
asya kālavibhāgasya tvarātsañcarase yathā  || 16.18 ||

tathā tvaṃ tvaritā nāma aśeṣārtivināśinī  |
khañjinī kathitā tubhyaṃ vāraṃ vāraṃ punaḥ punaḥ  || 16.19 ||

yasyā madhyagataṃ viśvaṃ viśvamadhyagatā tu yā  |
khañjikā tena sā proktā sūkṣme vastuni sūkṣmagā  || 16.20 ||

eṣā te khañjikā khyātā kubjinī[ṃ] śṛṇu sāmpratam  |
anvarthasaṃjñikā nāma ekā tvaṃ tu kuleśvari  || 16.21 ||

sthūlasūkṣme pare tattve vyaktāvyakte nirāmaye  |
sarvaṃ vyāptamidaṃ devyā sā ca tvaṃ kiṃ na budhyasi  || 16.22 ||
bṛhatkāyo yadā kaścitsvalpe vyācarate gṛhe  |
kuñcitāṅgo viśedyasmāttadvadeṣā maheśvarī  || 16.23 ||

caturdvādaśadhādhāraṃ granthau ṣoḍaśakānvitam  |
vaḍavākhyaṃ triśūlordhvaṃ trittattvaṃ tu kalāntagam  || 16.24 ||

nididhyāsyaṃ śrutaṃ deśyametatkauleśvaraṃ tanum  |
yasyodaragataṃ tacca tasya kimaparaṃ param  || 16.25 ||

kūṭabhūtaṃ tu tanmadhye yasmātsā kuṭilītayā  |
gatānekakulodbhinnā iccheyaṃ pārameśvarī  || 16.26 ||

tena tvaṃ kubjikā proktā parā sarveṣu vastuṣu  |
tatredaṃ durlabhaṃ devi sugopyaṃ prakaṭīkṛtam  || 16.27 ||

vedasiddhāḥ paśuscordhve ūrdhvaṃ vāme paśutvatā  |
vāmaṃ dakṣiṇamārgasya dakṣiṇaṃ kulaśāsane  || 16.28 ||

taṃ tu yonyārṇave līnaṃ yoniḥ srīkubjikāmate  |
ato'rthaṃ gopitaṃ tantraṃ na kasyacinmayoditam  || 16.29 ||

rabhasāviṣṭabhāvena tavādya prakaṭīkṛtam  || 16.30 ||

śrīkubjikā uvāca

sūryasomasthitiḥ proktā vahnisthānāvadhāritā  |
kathaṃ sā kurute sṛṣṭiṃ ko'sitāṅgaḥ kuleśvaraḥ  || 16.31 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

kubjeśi śrūyatāṃ sṛṣṭiryathāvasthā prapadyate  |
asitāṅgo maheśānastatordhve maṇḍalopari  || 16.32 ||

somamadhye ravisthānaṃ sūryamadhye śikhi[ḥ] sthitaḥ  |
tatra madhye'ṅkuraṃ divyamasitāṅgasamudbhavam  || 16.33 ||

tato niṣpadyate sṛṣṭirvicitrānekarūpiṇī  |
tattvāni ca kalā varṇā mantravidyā padaḥ purā  || 16.34 ||

visṛjanti mahānandaṃ śaktibhairavamaṇḍalam  |
pañcaviṃśatimadhyādau ṣoḍaśaivāṣṭa cāntimāḥ  || 16.35 ||

bhairavānandaśaktisthamasitāṅgakuleśvaram  |
ādimaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ siddhaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhirvṛtam  || 16.36 ||

ādiyonipurasthaṃ tu maṇḍalaṃ khecarātmakam  |
asya pūjāvidhānena ājñāyoniphalaṃ labhet || 16.37 ||

ādimaṇḍalakaṃ hyetatpravaraṃ hyuttamottamam  |
atrotpannāni sarvāṇi maṇḍalāni hyanekadhā  || 16.38 ||

pañcaviṃśātmakaṃ madhye maṇḍalānāṃ tadādimam  |
catuḥsiddhānvitaikaikaṃ vijñeyaṃ pañcaviṃśakam  || 16.39 ||

vālādau khaḍgaparyantaṃ maṇḍale maṇḍale tu tam  |
eṣānyatpañcakaṃ devi kulavidyā kulādhvare  || 16.40 ||

bhujaṅgakrūrasaṃyuktā trimūrtiguṇadhāraṇā  |
sāmānyā sarvasiddhānāṃ pañcaviṃśatimaṇḍale  || 16.41 ||

maṇḍalo[ d]bhṛtadehā sā kriyākālaguṇottarā  |
caturbhiḥ sahitā devī sṛjate varṇasāgaram  || 16.42 ||

kakārādau maparyantaṃ yakārādau ha-m-antimam  |
atra mantrāḥ samutpannā vidyāmudrāgaṇo mahān  || 16.43 ||

devīdehātsamutpannā sā devī maṇḍalodbhavā  |
caturviṃśakamadhyasthā ṣaṭcatuṣkavibhūṣitā  || 16.44 ||

vahnimaṇḍalamadhyasthā bahurūpā arūpiṇī  |
barbaroruha piṅgākṣī danturā bṛhadodarā  || 16.45 ||

nīlameghaprabhā bhīmā gambhīrābharaṇojjvalā  |
vedaiḥ kṛtaśiromālā saṣaḍaṅgapadakramāt || 16.46 ||

brahmasūtraṃ maheśānyāḥ purāṇodbaddhamekhalā  |
jyotiḥśāstrāñjitākṣī sā dhvanikarṇāvataṃsakā  || 16.47 ||

kalālambitahāraughā vijñānakaṭakojjvalā  |
śabdapaṅkāmṛtodbhinnā maṇḍitaṃ mukhamaṇḍalam  || 16.48 ||

vicitravasanānekā śāstrapaṭṭāṃśukomalā  |
ābaddhāṃśukaparyaṅkā prameyāsanasaṃsthitā  || 16.49 ||

īdṛgrūpadharāṃ devīṃ pañcaviṃsāntamadhyagām  |
aparāṃ sṛṣṭikartārāṃ parāṃ ṣaḍviṃśa-m-ādimām  || 16.50 ||

ādimaṇḍalamadhyasthāmasitotsaṅgagāminīm  |
dvibhujābharaṇopetāmekavaktrāṃ trilocanām  || 16.51 ||

cārubimboṣṭhavadanāmanekaguṇaśālinīm  |
arūpāṃ rūpasampannāṃ tasyānte rūpasambhavām  || 16.52 ||

icchārūpadharāṃ devīṃ navātmānena labhyate  || 16.53 ||

śrīkubjikā uvāca

navātmānamayaṃ sarvaṃ tasyaitatparamā parā  |
sā parā labhyate yena sa navātmā vada prabho  || 16.54 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

sādhu bhairavi yatnena pṛcchitaṃ nirmalārthataḥ  |
na tena rahitaṃ kiñcitsatyedaṃ paramārthataḥ  || 16.55 ||

prasahyaṃ pūjyate yatra tatra siddhakramo na hi  |
yatra siddhakramo bhadre tatredaṃ gopitaṃ mayā  || 16.56 ||

ājñālabdharasāsvādāstyajantīdaṃ sudurlabham  |
viśuddhamaṇḍalordhvedaṃ maṇḍalaṃ na tadojjhitam  || 16.57 ||

pañcaviṃśakabhedasya pūrvaṃ vidyā samuddhṛtā  |
tasyaivādyaṃ dvikaṃ tyajya śeṣānyatkevalākṣarāḥ  || 16.58 ||

bhṛgulākulasaṃvartāstrīṇyetāni anukramāt |
tatra lākulabhṛgveśaṃ bhujaṅgāsanasaṃsthitam  || 16.59 ||

saṃvartakamahākālaṃ pinākīguṇasaṃyutam  |
khaḍgavālāsanāsīnamarghīśānandanopari  || 16.60 ||

'nugrahānandamūrdhnisthaṃ krūrānandasamanvitam  |
parānandasamāyuktaṃ kūṭedaṃ maṇḍaleśvaram  || 16.61 ||

yasya garbhagataṃ sarvaṃ vāṅmayaṃ sacarācaram  |
tasyopāyamidaṃ devi upeyasya mahātmanaḥ  || 16.62 ||

etatkauleśvaraṃ nāma kūṭarūpaṃ kuleśvaram  |
nānena rahitā siddhiḥ sādhanaṃ khecarīpade  || 16.63 ||

maṇḍalāntargataṃ pūjya maṇḍalaṃ kāmadaṃ smṛtam  |
yena pūjitamātreṇa sarvavyāptipadaṃ labhet || 16.64 ||

mahataḥ kulavṛkṣasya ḍālāḥ syuḥ pañcaviṃśati  |
ājñāpuṣpopaśobhāḍhyaṃ vijñānaphalamālitam  || 16.65 ||

paripakvarasānandaṃ mokṣatṛptikaraṃ phalam  |
prāpyate yena yajñena helayā maṇḍalaṃ tu tam  || 16.66 ||

khecarīcakramadhyasthaṃ tritattvaguṇaśālinam  |
maṇḍalodbhṛtadīpyantaṃ maṇḍalaṃ vaḍavāmukham  || 16.67 ||

catuḥśaktisamāyuktamekaikaṃ pañcaviṃśakam  |
ambikā raudriṇī jyeṣṭhā vāmādau sṛṣṭisāgare  || 16.68 ||

etaccatuṣṭayāntasthaṃ naveśānaṃ kuleśvaram  |
vaḍavānala-m-āsīnamājñāśūladharaṃ vibhum  || 16.69 ||


śrīkubjikā uvāca

maṇḍalānāṃ pṛthakpūjā siddhyarthaṃ sādhakeśvara  |
vyāptisthaṃ tu yathā sarvaṃ tathā vadata bhairava  || 16.70 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

kathayāmi varārohe devyā dehagataṃ yathā  |
vyāptināmavibhedena jñāsyante jñānino yathā  || 16.71 ||

kāmamaṇḍalakaṃ skandhe khecaraṃ tadadhaḥsthitam  |
gurumaṇḍalakaṃ sandhau pāṇimadhye ghanojjvalam  || 16.72 ||

rudramaṇḍalakaṃ dakṣe pāṇau tattu nakhāgratah |
candramaṇḍalakaṃ vāme chāyāmaṇḍalakaṃ tvadhaḥ  || 16.73 ||

jayantamaṇḍalaṃ sandhau jhaṅkāraṃ karamadhyataḥ  |
jñānamaṇḍalakaṃ vāme aṅgulyāgre vyavasthitam  || 16.74 ||

varāṅgordhvanitambādho dakṣiṇe'mṛtamaṇḍalam  |
somamaṇḍalakorubhyāṃ sandhau ḍāmaramaṇḍalam  || 16.75 ||

kanyāmaṇḍalakaṃ padbhyāmumāmaṇḍalakaṃ nakhe  |
tārāmaṇḍalakaṃ vāme kuladivyorumadhyataḥ  || 16.76 ||

anantamaṇḍalaṃ sandhau pādānte mitramaṇḍalam  |
aṅgulyāgre samākhyātaṃ maṇḍalaṃ merupūrvakam  || 16.77 ||

raktamaṇḍalakaṃ kukṣau dakṣiṇe vāmataḥ śikhī  |
kulamaṇḍalakaṃ pṛṣṭhau vajrasaṅkhyāta madhyagam  || 16.78 ||

maṇḍalaiścaikaviṃśābhirāvṛtaḥ sa kuleśvaraḥ  |
ṣaṣṭhamaṇḍalakaṃ nābhau kālamaṇḍalakaṃ hṛdi  || 16.79 ||

śrīmannāthāditaḥ kṛtvā trayaitānukrameṇa tu  |
ekaikaṃ caikaviṃśānāṃ maṇḍalānāṃ patīśvarāḥ  || 16.80 ||

pañcaviṃśakayogasya catuṣkaṃ patirūpiṇam  |
samudāyapatīnāṃ ca patireko visuddhirāṭ || 16.81 ||
randhramaṇḍalakaṃ vṛtte romakoṭyordhvasaṃsthitam  |
sarvāṅgasundaraṃ devyāḥ śarīraṃ maṇḍalodbhavam  || 16.82 ||

śāmbhavīyaṃ parā mūrtiḥ svayaṃsambhṛtamaṇḍalam  |
maṇḍalodbhṛtadehā sā sā ca maṇḍalamadhyagā  || 16.83 ||

svayaṅkartā svayaṃhartā maṇḍalānāṃ kuleśvarī  |
vaḍavānalarūpeṇa triśūlāsanasaṃsthitā  || 16.84 ||

kaṅkāleśvaramūrdhnisthā ṣaṭpadārthoparisthitā  |
caturbhujaikavadanā cākṣasūtrakarābhayā  || 16.85 ||

sarvajñānāvabodhena pustakānyavarapradā  |
pañcamordhvakramo devyā maṇḍalodbhṛtavigrahā  || 16.86 ||

caturāśītipramāṇena koṭīnāṃ mūlatordhvataḥ  |
śarīraṃ śrīkuleśasya tasya kumbho'bjamaṇḍale  || 16.87 ||

sthitā sañjanate sarvaṃ tena kubjeśvarī parā  |
maṇḍalodbhṛtadehā sā maṇḍaloparisaṃsthitā  || 16.88 ||

maṇḍalāntargatā devī dhyātvā maṇḍaladāyikām  |
śrīmahānandavṛkṣo'yaṃ ḍālānekacitaṃ tu tam  || 16.89 ||

śāstrapallavasaṃyuktaṃ vijñānāṅkuraśobhitam  |
akhaṇḍajñānapuṣpāḍhyaṃ siddhodayaphalānvitam  || 16.90 ||

pakvānandarasālāḍhyaṃ mokṣatṛptyādisatphalam  |
eṣa maṇḍalavṛkṣo'yaṃ yasmātsarvaṃ prapadyate  || 16.91 ||

sarvathā tadyajennityaṃ vyākulena-m-anena kim  |
nirācāreṇa yogena sācāreṇa na tadyajet || 16.92 ||

vyāptibhāvamato matvā bhuktvā cāṇḍālajāṃ tanum  |
sa paśyati paraṃ vṛkṣaṃ khecaraṃ maṇḍalodbhavam  || 16.93 ||

tadbhāvabhāvanāṃ kṛtvā guruṃ matvāvadhārayet |
yatkiñcitpuratastasya tatsarvaṃ maṇḍalaṃ viduḥ  || 16.94 ||
yadi syānmaṇḍalo dehaḥ pūjayenmaṇḍalādibhiḥ  |
vaḍavānalayogena ekaikaṃ māsakāvadhim  || 16.95 ||

kulavidyāsamāyuktaṃ catuṣkalasamanvitam  |
kauleśānasamāyuktaṃ svasthānasthopadeśagam  || 16.96 ||

evaṃ sañcintya manasā bhaktiyukto jitendriyaḥ  |
pañcaviṃśatimāsena prākṛtān labhate guṇān  || 16.97 ||

dviguṇena tu kālena paiśācaguṇakṛdbhavet |
triguṇena tu kālena daivatyaṃ bhajate tu saḥ  || 16.98 ||

caturguṇena kāmitvaṃ sāmānyatvāmarālaye  |
pañcamāvasthayogena satyalokāvadhiṃ vrajet || 16.99 ||

ṣaṣṭhamena tu yogena viṣṇutvaṃ jāyate dhruvam  |
saptamena tu yogena brahmāṇḍāntamanuvrajet || 16.100 ||

aṣṭamena tu piṅgo'sau navamānteśvaraḥ prabhuḥ  |
maṇḍalīśo daśāvasthaḥ khecaraḥ khecarādhipaḥ  || 16.101 ||

maṇḍalābhyāsayogena nirācāreṇa yoginaḥ  |
vaḍavānalamadhye tu vaḍavānalapūritaḥ  || 16.102 ||

vaḍavānalarūpeṇa nirācāravrataṃ caret |
vaḍavānalamārūḍho vāḍavīyaṃ padaṃ labhet || 16.103 ||

yataḥ sarvamayaṃ tacca jagedaṃ vaḍavodaram  |
ājñeyaṃ sakalā devī divyājñāto'sya sambhavaḥ  || 16.104 ||

ṣaṭpadārthasya cānyasya pradhānaṃ vaḍavānalam  |
mahāvṛkṣavaṭo yasya sūkṣmabījavaṭo yathā  || 16.105 ||

tathā tu hṛdayasyāsya sarvamevodare jagat |
khecarādhipatirdevyā vaṭamālāvalambinī  || 16.106 ||

ājñāsūtraprayoktā sā caturāśītiguṇojjvalā  |
guruvaktrāttu labhyeta māleyaṃ vaḍavānalī  || 16.107 ||
svamanīṣikato'nyathā paśyanto'pi na paśyati  |
etatte pañcakaṃ proktaṃ sarvavyāptibhṛtodaram  || 16.108 ||

khecarāntapadaṃ divyaṃ catuṣkānyaṃ punaḥ śṛṇu  || 16.109 ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate ṣaṭprakāre mahānandapañcake ṣoḍaśamaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************


śrīkubjikā uvāca


catuṣkaṃ pañcakaṃ nātha ṣaṭkaṃ tu pañcakaṃ tathā  |
jñātaṃ vyāptibhṛtaṃ sarvaṃ catuṣkaṃ kīdṛśaṃ punaḥ  || 17.1 ||

pṛcchāmi tvāṃ na śaknomi svata eva prasādataḥ  |
vada nātha guṇānandaṃ yena jānīmahe'khilam  || 17.2 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

kubjike'timahāprājñe kiṃ na budhyasi mūḍhadhīḥ  |
yadyevaṃ lapitaṃ sarvaṃ kathayāmyavaśeṣakam  || 17.3 ||

sthitibhogalayāntasthaṃ pūjāvyāptipadaṃ yathā  |
tathā taṃ nikhilaṃ sarvamuktānuktaṃ vadāmi te  || 17.4 ||

devyāpīṭhacatuṣkaṃ tu siddhapiṇḍacatuṣṭayam  |
yugaiścaturbhistadvyāptaṃ parāparavibhāgaśaḥ  || 17.5 ||

akuleśvaradevasya yathā tiṣṭhati vigrahe  |
tathā śṛṇu maheśāni nirācārapadaṃ yathā  || 17.6 ||

o-jā-pū-kā-kramaṃ madhyādvāmadakṣāgratordhvataḥ  |
vyāptibhūtaṃ yajetsarvaṃ nābhyadhaḥ siddhapūrvakam  || 17.7 ||

o-jā-pū-kā-kramāddhṛtsthaṃ dakṣādau vāma nābhigam  |
upadeśena jānīyādguruvaktrāttu śāmbhavam  || 17.8 ||

evaṃ karṇamukhe nāsā nāsordhvaṃ pūrvavadyajet |
pūrvoktārcisamāyuktaṃ siddhavṛndasapālakaiḥ  || 17.9 ||

balakaumāravṛddhasthaṃ triśuddhisthaṃ tritattvagam  |
vaḍavānalayogena gahvarādupadeśataḥ  || 17.10 ||

kulākule pare sthāne sarvajñāṃ śāmbhavaṃ kramam  |
sarvavyāptisamopetamājñāpuṣpaiḥ prapūjayet || 17.11 ||

rūpātītādiyogena maṇḍalodbhṛtavigrahā  |
tatrasthā paramā devī madhyasthā pararūpiṇī  || 17.12 ||

kālamūrdhni sthitā śāntā kalātītā kalākalā  |
kālahantā kalātītā kambalīyaṃ kuleśvarī  || 17.13 ||

vaḍavānalamuttīrṇā dedīpyantaguṇojjvalā  |
sahasrādityasaṅkāśā rūpātītā kuleśvarī  || 17.14 ||

rūpaṃ pīṭhakramaṃ devyā nīlameghāñjanaprabhā  |
bhujairdvādaśakopetā ṣaḍvaktrā barbarālakā  || 17.15 ||

bṛhodarā ca lamboṣṭhī stabdhākṣī viraladvijā  |
bālakramasya madhyasthā rūpasthā nagnakubjikā  || 17.16 ||

vaḍavānalasandīptā atiraudrā subhīṣaṇā  |
gurūpadeśagamyā sā divyājñārthapradāyikā  || 17.17 ||

kaumārakramamadhyasthā ekavaktrā caturbhujā  |
pustakamaṇḍaludharā akṣasūtravarapradā  || 17.18 ||

vaḍavānalamadhyasthā saumyarūpā sulālasā  |
mahānandamahāviṣṭā āveśantī jagattrayam  || 17.19 ||

mahāsaṃsāra-m-ambhodhestārayantī vyavasthitā  |
sā mahāntārikāpyatra kaumāre mahatāṃ gatā  || 17.20 ||

pāśaughakṣayakartā sā vidyāśāstrāvalambinī  |
yasyoccārātsphuṭantyāśu parvatān vajramausalān  || 17.21 ||

pañca pañca ca vidyāstraṃ mahāntārī sa eva hi  |
akṣaraughena siddhā sā uccārāveśinī parā  || 17.22 ||

abhaktaṃ vā dviṣantaṃ vā vādasthaṃ vā tathetaram  |
kupitaḥ pātayetsarvaṃ yasyaiṣā hṛdi śālinī  || 17.23 ||

padasthena tu yogena siddhā sā parameśvarī  |
piṇḍamāveśayecchīghraṃ piṇḍasthāṃ śṛṇu kubjini  || 17.24 ||

vṛddhakramasya madhyasthāṃ laghurūpāṃ sutejasām  |
dvibhujaikavadanāṃ tāṃ piṇḍasthāṃ patirūpiṇīm  || 17.25 ||

paśuprāṇaharāṃ devīṃ pāśajālanikṛntanīm  |
damanīṃ sarvapāpānāṃ vijñānakaraṇīṃ parām  || 17.26 ||

pūrvaṃ vyāvarṇitaṃ yacca rūpānte guṇaśālinīm  |
vidyāṅgābharaṇopetāṃ piṇḍasthāṃ tāṃ vijānatha  || 17.27 ||

dvātriṃśākṣarasaṃyuktāṃ ṣaḍaṅgapariveṣṭitām  |
yaduktaṃ karmasantānaṃ tadatra phaladaṃ kramāt || 17.28 ||

mantrasiddhikarī devī piṇḍasiddhikarī parā  |
vaḍavānalayogena sarvajñatvaṃ labhettu saḥ  || 17.29 ||

vaḍavānalakubjasthā kūjate'nandarūpadhṛk |
tena sā kubjikā nāma maṇirūpā1 akhañjikā  || 17.30 ||

asyā devyāḥ padaṃ rūpaṃ rūpātītaṃ pravartate  |
piṇḍeśinī parā mātā caturāśītiguṇojjvalā  || 17.31 ||

sampūjya mānasaiḥ puṣpairaliphalgvādibhiḥ kramāt |
catuḥsiddhakramāmnāyaṃ svāmipādamanukramāt || 17.32 ||

piṇḍayogasthitāṃ cājñāṃ saṃsmarenmadhyatiryagām  |
kubjinīkulamārūḍhāmabhyasantaḥ śriyaṃ labhet || 17.33 ||
śāntipuṣṭivaśākṛṣṭi vāgvilāsaṃ jvarāpaham  |
mṛtyunāśaṃ purakṣobhaṃ sainyastambhāmbhaśoṣaṇam  || 17.34 ||

paśupāśagrahastobhaṃ dantakāṣṭhāñjalīghaṭam  |
nirbījīkaraṇādyaṃ ca vṛkṣasphoṭaṃ jalaplavam  || 17.35 ||

mudrāsphoṭaṃ śilācchedaṃ vṛkṣāṇāṃ labhanaṃ mahat |
nadīpravartanastambho nāvādiśakaṭasya ca  || 17.36 ||

anagnijvalanaṃ pātaḥ sainyastambhordhvaropaṇam  |
jvālāstambhaṃ jalastambhaṃ ghaṭasastragirācalam  || 17.37 ||

stambhayedvajrapātaṃ tu aśanyaughaṃ nivārayet |
mārayeṭṭālayecchailān dārayeddharaṇītalam  || 17.38 ||

hastavṛddhirmanaḥsiddhirdūrāśravaṇadarśanam  |
vartamānamatītārthaṃ bhaviṣyaṃ ca laghutvatā  || 17.39 ||

vācāsiddhiśca māhendramindrajālapravartakam  |
kāmarūpāntaradhyānaṃ jñānaṃ mātṛkulasya ca  || 17.40 ||

jihvāprasāraṇaṃ cānyamaṅgaikaikavivardhanam  |
sphoṭanaṃ śuṣkakāṣṭhānāṃ troṭanaṃ phalapuṣpayoḥ  || 17.41 ||

mṛtakotthāpanaṃ śīghraṃ dagdhasañjīvanaṃ mahat |
akāle vṛkṣaphalanaṃ puṣpadhānyāvarohaṇam  || 17.42 ||

parakāyapraveśaṃ ca anyajanmāvabodhanam  |
parokṣamṛtakānayanaṃ dṛṣṭijvālāprasāraṇam  || 17.43 ||

dantavṛddhikaraṃ jñānaṃ jvālāvijñānamuttamam  |
śarīre phalapuṣpāni pratimājalpakarṣaṇam  || 17.44 ||

jalpāyanaṃ kumārīṇāmantardhānordhvadarśanam  |
nirālambordhvaruhaṇaṃ pararūpāpakarṣaṇam  || 17.45 ||

citranṛtyāpanaṃ yuddhaṃ śatrūṇāṃ ca parasparam  |
haraṇaṃ śabdadṛṣṭīnāṃ vardhataikaikahelayā  || 17.46 ||
parasāmarthyaharaṇaṃ puṃstriyopakaraṇaṃ param  |
aṅgasaṅkocanānayanaṃ bhūtānāṃ bhūtasādhanam  || 17.47 ||

piṇḍakramasya pūjāyāṃ svādhiṣṭhānaphalaṃ labhet |
kubjānalena yogena kubjipiṇḍaṃ caturvidham  || 17.48 ||

aśvatthapattravatkubjaṃ śukacañcunibhaṃ param  |
maṇikubjaṃ paraṃ cānyaṃ randhrakubjaṃ tato'param  || 17.49 ||

etatte saṃsphuṭaṃ sarvaṃ kubjāmbīnāṃ catuṣṭayam  |
piṇḍo'tha pada rūpaṃ ca rūpātītāditaḥ kramāt || 17.50 ||

ādyaṃ piṇḍasthitā kubjī kubjeśīti kuleśvarī  |
padasthā kubjikā cānyā mahāntārī mahadbhutā  || 17.51 ||

rūpasthā kubjinī cānyā bindusthā barbarā parā  |
rūpātītā tu randhrasthā kubjinī kamalānanā  || 17.52 ||

vijñānānekaviśliṣṭā ekaikā phaladāyinī  |
piṇḍayogakrameṇaitāḥ kubjidehaphalapradāḥ  || 17.53 ||

mitreśānasamāyuktā kaṇṭhoṣṭhāliṅganānvitā  |
golāntapaścimāntasthā śaṅkhabhedādvinirgatā  || 17.54 ||

vidyudanyonyatārebhyo viśatyekā punardvidhā  |
evamabhyasate yāvattāvatkāmaḥ svayaṃ kṣubhet || 17.55 ||

svādhiṣṭhānagate yoge śākte śaktiṃ samabhyaset |
abdaikena jagatsarvaṃ kṣobhayettridaśeśvaram  || 17.56 ||

durbhagānāmabhāgyānāmabdātsarvaṃ bhaviṣyati  |
yaḥ punaḥ sarvathā siddhaḥ sa sidhyatyacirātpriye  || 17.57 ||

sāttvikena tu rūpeṇa tyaktamāyāsukhojjhitaḥ  |
pādaprakṣālanaṃ juṣṭaṃ yo na dadyātsa sidhyati  || 17.58 ||

etatkulālikāmnāye piṇḍakubjicatuṣṭayam  |
avijñāya na dātavyaṃ yāvannādeśitaḥ śiśuḥ  || 17.59 ||
anādivimalamātaṅgī sarvajñā ca pulindikā  |
yoge ca śabarī proktā siddhasaṃjñeti campakā  || 17.60 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

adhunā śṛṇu kubjīśe padagranthivibhañjakam  |
kramaṃ vakṣyāmi divyaughaṃ sāmarthyādinirākulam  || 17.61 ||

ājñāmoghapadaṃ khañji dvitīyaṃ kubjinīpadam  |
śaktividyā tṛtīyaṃ tu caturthaughapadakramam  || 17.62 ||

catuṣpīṭhavibhedena punaḥ pīṭhacatuṣpadam  |
ṣoḍaśaiva padānyāhurgranthibhūtāḥ pṛthakpṛthak || 17.63 ||

kalanti sakalaṃ sarvaṃ sthūlasūkṣmavibhāgataḥ  |
nadanti kālarūpasthā[ḥ]  sthūlasūkṣmān tathāntimān  || 17.64 ||

tithyādyāntapadaṃ yānti vāmāntaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ punah |
candrasūryavibhāgena jīvitaṃ maraṇaṃ padam  || 17.65 ||

padaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ sarvaṃ granthiṃ baddhvādhvaraṃ'khilam  |
kālarūpāstu tāḥ kālaṃ hanantyuccārayogataḥ  || 17.66 ||

o-jā-pū-kā-ḍi-lam-rṇa-mā-dha-gi-rū-ṇa-ra-ri-pū  |
evaṃ jñāte hanetkālamuccaranto'nupūrvaśaḥ  || 17.67 ||

akṣare akṣare granthiḥ pīṭhaṃ granthicatuṣṭayam  |
catuṣpīṭhamayā yoniścaturyonimayaṃ'khilam  || 17.68 ||

pañcamī yā parā yonistasyā granthiḥ pade pade  |
tayā vyāptamidaṃ sarvaṃ kāraṇānalamadhyagam  || 17.69 ||

tatpadaṃ paramaṃ proktaṃ yatra sarve padā gatāḥ  |
ṣoḍaśākṣarabhedena tatpadaṃ labhate sphuṭam  || 17.70 ||

prathamādhāranantākhyā madhyamāṅghryāṅgulīgatā  |
kālagranthistu gulphādho raudrīgranthirnalāntare  || 17.71 ||
jyeṣṭhāgranthirnitambādho vāme vāmādhasaṃsthitā  |
kāmagranthirgudādhāre piṅgagranthistatordhvataḥ  || 17.72 ||

adhordhvaromamadhye tu brahmagranthirudāhṛtā  |
somagranthistataścordhve sūryagranthistatordhvataḥ  || 17.73 ||

prāṇagranthiḥ punaścordhve jīvagranthistatordhvataḥ  |
yena jīvanti bhūtāni tadviyogānmriyanti ca  || 17.74 ||

viṣṇugranthistu sā jñeyā kaṇṭhasthā tāluke'nyathā  |
rudragranthirmahāraudrā īśagranthistatordhvataḥ  || 17.75 ||

sādākhyastu parā granthistrikoṭyordhvavyavasthitā  |
māyā śaktistataścordhve icchānandāmṛtāplutā  || 17.76 ||

ṣoḍaśāvayavā devī khecarī tu khageśvarī  |
padmasthā padmamadhyasthā haṃsasthā haṃsavāhinī  || 17.77 ||

niṣkalā sakalā devī vajradehā manonmanī  |
padakramasya madhyasthā padāṅgābharaṇojjvalā  || 17.78 ||

kramamantrapadālabdhā helayā cāṇimāṣṭakam  |
sādhayenmahatā devī ṣoḍaśākṣarasambhavā  || 17.79 ||

mālinī siddhadehā sā tritattvārcighanojjvalā  |
aghoryāṣṭakasaṃyuktā dvādaśāṅgaprapūritā  || 17.80 ||

ṣaḍaṅgāvayavopetā divyadehā mahābalā  |
asitāṅgatanūdbhūtā mantradehā maheśvarī  || 17.81 ||

mālinī śabdarāśiśca trividyāghorikāṣṭakam  |
dvādaśāṅgaṣaḍaṅgaṃ ca etaddehaṃ kulātmakam  || 17.82 ||

lalāṭakaṇṭhavakṣasthaṃ guhyāṅghrau ratnapañcakam  |
ślokadvādaśabhirmālā pādādau cūlikāvadhim  || 17.83 ||

brahmasūtrojjvalā devyāḥ skandhobhau tadgrahānvitau  |
pañcabījairmukhakoṣaṃ pañcauṃkāraiḥ khilaṃ nyaset || 17.84 ||

śrīkubjikā uvāca

paramaṃ vada kauleśa padamantrā yathā sthitāḥ  |
yatra sthāne niyoktavyāḥ sphuṭaikaikaṃ pṛthakpṛthak || 17.85 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

kathayāmi varārohe padārthārthapadaṃ yathā  |
siddhamantropadeśo'yaṃ prakaṭārthaṃ vadāmi te  || 17.86 ||

mūrdhni vaktrākṣiṇau karṇau nāsāgaṇḍau dvijauṣṭhakau  |
bhāratīśaṅkhinīdvāre śrīkaṇṭhātsenakāvadhim  || 17.87 ||

krameṇa ṣoḍaśaivaitān dakṣiṇādau padā nyaset |
dantauṣṭhādimadho nyasya bhāratīśaṅkhikāvadhim  || 17.88 ||

krodhādāvekarudrāntaṃ skandhādau cāṅgulāvadhim  |
dakṣiṇe vāmato'pyevaṃ kūrmādau śarmakāvadhim  || 17.89 ||

someśvarādyumākāntaṃ sphicādau'ṅghryānta dakṣiṇam  |
vāmato'ṣāḍhimeṣāntaṃ pārśvau lohiśikhānvitau  || 17.90 ||

chagalaṇḍaṃ tu vaṃśasthaṃ dviraṇḍaṃ nābhimaṇḍale  |
hṛdaye tu mahākālamaṣṭakaṃ purato'nyathā  || 17.91 ||

vālibhaujaṅgapaināka- khaḍgīśabakaśvetakāḥ  |
bhṛgulākulasaṃvartāḥ kālaprāṇasaśukragāḥ  || 17.92 ||

majjāsthisnāyumāṃsasthā raktatvagvālimāditaḥ  |
anulomavilomena kālavelāditaḥ kramāt || 17.93 ||

nādinī tu śikhāgrasthā nakārākṣarasambhavā  |
ṛ-ṝ-ḻa-ḹ nivṛttyādyā mālikā śirasi sthitā  || 17.94 ||

tha śiro grasanī devī dha netre priyadarśanā  |
ī guhyaśakti nādasthā nāsāyāṃ netramadhyataḥ  || 17.95 ||
vyāpayitvā sthitā devī ca tṛtīyaṃ tu locanam  |
cāmuṇḍā parameśānī lalāṭasthā virājate  || 17.96 ||

bakāraṃ vadanaṃ devyā vajriṇī śaktiravyayā  |
kavarge daśanāstīkṣṇāḥ kaṅkaṭā kālikā śivā  || 17.97 ||

ghoraghoṣā mukhīvīrā kavarge dasanā[ḥ ] śubhāḥ  |
māyādevī i jihvā tu a vāgvāgeśvarī matā  || 17.98 ||

nārāyaṇī ṇa karṇau tu tayorbhūṣaṇamī-parau  |
mohanī ca tathā prajñā va kaṇṭhe śikhivāhinī  || 17.99 ||

lāmā vināyakī devī ḍaḍhau bāhudvayaṃ matam  |
paurṇimā hastadeśasthā ṭhakārākhyaṃ vibhormatam  || 17.100 ||

jhaṅkārī kurdanī caiva jhañau cāṅgulayaḥ kramāt |
kapālinī vāmakare ṭakāraḥ parameśvarī  || 17.101 ||

dīpanī śūladaṇḍaṃ ca rephaḥ samyagudāhṛtam  |
jayantī ja bhavecchūlamevaṃ devī virājate  || 17.102 ||

bhīṣaṇā vāyuvegā ca skandhayorubhayorapi  |
pāvanī tu pa hṛllagnā ṣodaraṃ lambikā sthitā  || 17.103 ||

saṃhārikā kṣakāro'yaṃ nābhirdevyā[ś] ca bhairavi  |
chagalī pūtanā caiva stanau chalau paristhitau  || 17.104 ||

āmoṭī tadgataṃ kṣīramāvarṇaḥ parikīrtitaḥ  |
paramātmā sakāro'yaṃ ha prāṇe śaktirambikā  || 17.105 ||

icchāśaktirvisargākhyā vyāpyabhāvena saṃsthitā  |
ma nitambaṃ mahākālī śa guhyaṃ kusumāyudhā  || 17.106 ||

śukrā devī tvanusvāraṃ śukraṃ devyāstu bhairavi  |
tārā takāramūrusthā e ai jñānīkriyāvubhau  || 17.107 ||

jānunī saṃsthitau devi bhairavyāśca mahātmanaḥ  |
gāyatrī caiva sāvitrī o au jaṅghau prakīrtitau  || 17.108 ||
dahanī dakṣapādasthā vāme phetkārikā matā  |
nādiphāntā varārohe dehaṃ śaktimayaṃ śubham  || 17.109 ||

siddhapañcāśakopetaṃ mālinyārdhaśatānvitam  |
evaṃ śataṃ samākhyātaṃ yojyamānaṃ tanau bhṛtam  || 17.110 ||

padadvayaṃ samākhyātaṃ tatra haṃso vyavasthitaḥ  |
yāvaccarati tau dvau tu tāvadātmā samāpyate  || 17.111 ||

padamānamaśeṣaṃ tu atra sarvaṃ samāpyate  |
ājñāto bhuñjate kālaṃ padaṃ jñātvājarāmaram  || 17.112 ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate padadvayahaṃsanirṇayo nāma saptadaśamaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************



śrībhairava uvāca

padagranthisamālabdhastattvagranthisamāśritaḥ  |
kubjigranthipadāntastho haṃsabhedapadaṃ vrajet || 18.1 ||

haṃsabhede parā śaktiḥ sahajā śivatattvagā  |
sā vidyā prathamā jñeyā dvitīyā tu parāparā  || 18.2 ||

catvāriṃśatpadā jñeyā vidyātattve niveśitā  |
haṃsagranthisamārūḍhāṃ prakaṭārthaṃ vadāmi te  || 18.3 ||

someśvaraṃ samuddhṛtya patitaṃ suranāyike  |
śikhīśaṃ kevalaṃ paścāllākulaṃ tadanantaram  || 18.4 ||

arghīśāsanasaṃsthaṃ hi bindunā mastake hatam  |
lākulaṃ punaruddhṛtya bhujaṅgāsanasaṃsthitam  || 18.5 ||

trimūrtinā ca cākrāntaṃ śūnyamastakabhūṣitam  |
śikhīśaṃ vahnisaṃyuktaṃ jhaṇṭīśena samanvitam  || 18.6 ||

krūrānandena sambhinnaṃ lākulī tadanantaram  |
bhujaṅgena tu sandīptamarghīśāsanasaṃsthitam  || 18.7 ||

tadevaṃ lākulīśaṃ tu bhujaṅgādhāramīśvaram  |
trimūrtinā tu cākrāntamaṃ krūraṃ śirasi sthitam  || 18.8 ||

bhujaṅgaṃ tu caturdhā vai kartavyaṃ tu kuleśvari  |
kevalaṃ dvitayaṃ devi amarīśadvitayānvitam  || 18.9 ||

lākulaṃ tu tato deyaṃ jhaṇṭīśena tu bheditam  |
mahāsenāhataṃ devi lākulīśaṃ samuddharet || 18.10 ||

khaḍgīśaṃ kevalaṃ gṛhya lohitaṃ tadanantaram  |
sūkṣmānandena sambhinnaṃ mahākālaṃ tu kevalam  || 18.11 ||

khaḍgīśaṃ kevalaṃ paścādumākāntaṃ tataḥ punaḥ  |
jhaṇṭānandena sambhinnaṃ śvetānandaṃ kulādhipe  || 18.12 ||

kevalaṃ tu smṛtaṃ bhadre dviraṇḍaṃ tadanantaram  |
trimūrtinā tu cākrāntaṃ mahākālaṃ tataḥ punaḥ  || 18.13 ||

dviraṇḍaṃ tu punardevi trimūrtyālaṅkṛtaṃ kuru  |
sūkṣmānandena sambhinnaṃ caṇḍānandaṃ yaśasvini  || 18.14 ||

mahākālaṃ punaḥ paścādamarīśāsanasthitam  |
bhujaṅgaṃ kevalaṃ devi śiveśaṃ sadyasaṃyutam  || 18.15 ||

lākulī bhṛgusaṃyuktaṃ nugrahīśānvitaṃ priye  |
krūradevaṃ śirasthaṃ hi lohitaṃ jhaṇṭināhatam  || 18.16 ||

raktaṃ caivārghinā yuktaṃ bhujaṅgaṃ kevalaṃ punaḥ  |
śivānandaṃ tu deveśi sadyojātena bheditam  || 18.17 ||

lākulamarghinā yuktaṃ śūnyamastakabhūṣitam  |
bhujaṅgaṃ jhaṇṭidevena bheditaṃ kuru pārvati  || 18.18 ||

śivottamaṃ tataḥ paścātsadyojātena bhūṣitam  |
kevalaṃ tu mahākālaṃ bhujaṅgaṃ kevalaṃ punaḥ  || 18.19 ||
lohitaṃ tu punaḥ paścānmahāsenaṃ tataḥ punaḥ  |
āsanasthaṃ bhṛgordevi lākulīśaṃ samuddharet || 18.20 ||

lākulīśaṃ punarbhadre bhujaṅgāsanasaṃsthitam  |
trimūrtinā tu cākrāntaṃ krūrānandasamanvitam  || 18.21 ||

jhaṇṭinā bheditaṃ devi bhujaṅgaṃ kārayettataḥ  |
śivānandaṃ tatoddhṛtya sadyānandānvitaṃ kuru  || 18.22 ||

śrīkaṇṭhaṃ kevalaṃ paścādbhauktikaṃ bindunā yutam  |
eṣā parāparā devī uddhṛtā tu vilomataḥ  || 18.23 ||

catvāriṃśaddvayo varṇā ardhavarṇānvitā priye  |
eṣā vidyā tathā proktā vidyātattve niveśayet || 18.24 ||

devyā caivātmatattvasthā kriyāśaktyāparā punaḥ  |
ardhasaptākṣarā devī vilomena tataḥ śṛṇu  || 18.25 ||

patitamīśa somaṃ hi śikhīśaṃ kevalaṃ tataḥ  |
lākulamarghinā yuktaṃ śikhīśaṃ bhujagānvitam  || 18.26 ||

jhaṇṭīśena samāyuktaṃ lākulaṃ raktasaṃsthitam  |
dvidhā bhūtaṃ tu kartavyaṃ bhedaṃ cātra vadāmi te  || 18.27 ||

arghinā pūrva sambhinnamaparaṃ tu trimūrtinā  |
bhauktikaṃ kevalaṃ devi uddhṛtaṃ paramākṣaram  || 18.28 ||

bindunādānvitāḥ pañca kartavyāstu yaśasvini  |
apareyamimā vidyā sarvasvaṃ yoginīkule  || 18.29 ||

parāṃ devīṃ tato vakṣye śivatattvānusāriṇīm  |
lākulaṃ bhṛgusaṃsthaṃ hi bhujaṅgena samanvitam  || 18.30 ||

arghīśāsanamārūḍhaṃ'nugrahīśena bheditam  |
bindunādakalākrāntamuddhṛtaṃ paramaṃ priye  || 18.31 ||

praṇavoccārasaṃyuktā vidyā tattvatrayātmikā  |
śabdamālinimūrtisthā vidyādehaguṇojjvalā  || 18.32 ||
puryāṣṭakamaghoristhaṃ yathāvasthāṃ vadāmi te  || 18.33 ||

hrīṃ ru aghore hrīṃ shaumaghoryāyai prathamaṃ śiraḥ  || 18.34,1 ||
hrīṃ ru paramaghore hūṃ shauṃ paramaghorāyai mukham  || 18.34,2 ||
hrīṃ ru shauṃ ghorarūpe shauṃ ghorarūpāyai hṛdi  || 18.34,3 ||
hrīṃ ru shauṃ ghoramukhi shauṃ ghoramukhyai guhye  || 18.34,4 ||
hrīṃ phaṭbhīmanāme shauṃ bhīmāyai dakṣiṇabhuje  || 18.34,5 ||
hrīṃ hūṃ bhīṣaṇe shauṃ bhīṣaṇāyai vāmato bhuje  || 18.34,6 ||
shauṃ hrīṃ haḥ vama shauṃ vamanyai dakṣiṇoru  || 18.34,7 ||
hrīṃ hūṃ phaṭpiba he shauṃ pibanyai vāmatoru  || 18.34,8 ||

etatpuryāṣṭakaṃ devyā aghoryāṣṭakasaṃyutam  |
nyasedaṣṭavidhāṅgaṃ tu aṣṭapattreṣu sādhakaḥ  || 18.35 ||

atordhvaṃ dvādaśāṅgaṃ tu dehaniṣpattikāraṇam  |
pādādau śiraso yāvannyasedaṅge yathoditam  || 18.36 ||

siddhāīti sahaṃ padbhyāmṛddhāī jānunī sahāṃ  |
vidyutāyai sahimūrubhyāṃ sahīṃ lakṣmīti guhyagā  || 18.37ā ||

dīptāikā sahuṃ nābhau sahūṃ nālāikā hṛdi |
saheṃ śivāikā kaṇṭhe sahaiṃ vasumukhī mukhe  || 18.37B ||

sahoṃ nāsā vamanyāyai karṇe nandinikā sahauṃ  |
harikeśā sahaṃ tryakṣī mahāmukhyai sahaḥ śire  || 18.37C ||

anena dṛḍhito hyātmā jīvabhūtaḥ sthirīkṛtaḥ  |
ṣaḍaṅganyāsayogena vyaktatvaṃ bhajate tu saḥ  || 18.38 ||

shāṃ sarvajña hṛdayāya namaḥ  || 18.39,1 ||
amṛte tejomālini tṛpti śirase shīṃ svāhā  || 18.39,2 ||
vedavedini hūṃ phaṭshūmanādibodhāya śikhāyai vauṣaṭ || 18.39,3 ||
shaiṃ vajriṇe vajradharāya svatantra kavacāya hūṃ  || 18.39,4 ||
shauṃ nityamaluptaśakti shauṃ vauṃ dhauṃ sahaje trinetrarūpiṇe namastubhyamanantaśakti  || 18.39,5 ||
shaḥ ślīṃ paśu hūṃ phaṭpāśupatāstrāya sahasrākṣāya hūṃ phaṭ || 18.39,6 ||

ṣaḍaṅganyāsayogena parādehaṃ parādhvaram  |
evaṃ niṣpannadehasya slokadvādaśamekhalā  || 18.40 ||
tadgraheṇa tu yogena brahmasūtraṃ vilambayet |
śiraḥprabhṛti pādāntaṃ pañcaratnavibhūṣitam  || 18.41 ||

yatra yatpadavinyāsaṃ tatkubjīśe-m-ataḥ śṛṇu  || 18.42 ||

aiṃ haḥ a paramānande haḥ ā siddhidānandane haḥ i parāpare hrūṃ phreṃ phaṭkṣaḥ ī śrīkulāmbike  || 18.43,1 ||
aiṃ haḥ u kālarudrasthe haḥ ū ṛddhibalānvite haḥ ṛ śirohāre hrūṃ  phreṃ phaṭkṣaḥ ṝ śrīkulāmbike  || 18.43,2 ||
aiṃ haḥ ḻa narakāntasthe haḥ ḹ guhyamahāmbike haḥ e sṛṣṭigate hrūṃ phreṃ phaṭkṣaḥ ai śrīkulāmbike  || 18.43,3 ||
aiṃ haḥ o kuṇḍalaguhyānte haḥ au kuṇḍalalakṣmike haḥ aṃ kuṇḍalinī hrūṃ phreṃ phaṭkṣaḥ aḥ kulamālinī  || 18.43,4 ||
aiṃ haḥ ka kamaladīpte haḥ kha kuṇḍalanābhige haḥ ga kālahare hrūṃ phreṃ phaṭkṣaḥ gha svatejaśive  || 18.43,5 ||
aiṃ haḥ ṅa kamalamāle haḥ ca kramasauhṛdi haḥ cha pāpahane hrūṃ phreṃ phaṭkṣaḥ ja kāmarūpiṇī  || 18.43,6 ||
aiṃ haḥ jha kaṇṭhakūpasthe haḥ ña śaivāmṛtātmike haḥ ṭa candrātmike hrūṃ phreṃ phaṭkṣaḥ ṭha sukhadeśvarī  || 18.43,7 ||
aiṃ haḥ ḍa kuharāntasthe haḥ ḍha saumukhyatāmane haḥ ṇa svānandane hrūṃ phreṃ phaṭkṣaḥ ta kālanāśanī  || 18.43,8 ||
aiṃ haḥ tha kālavamani haḥ da cogrānunāsike haḥ dha karoti sā hrūṃ phreṃ phaṭkṣaḥ na kālarodanī  || 18.43,9 ||
aiṃ haḥ pa śrāvaṇāntasthe haḥ pha siddhimāśrite haḥ ba karoti sā hrūṃ phreṃ phaṭkṣaḥ bha pārameśvarī  || 18.43,10 ||
aiṃ haḥ ma kālakalātīte haḥ ya śrībindunetrage haḥ ra śrīhārike hrūṃ phreṃ phaṭkṣaḥ la sṛṣṭibinduge  || 18.43,11 ||
aiṃ haḥ va prakaṭagupte haḥ śa mahāmukhe pare haḥ ṣa svākāśage hrūṃ phreṃ phaṭkṣaḥ sa śrīkujāmbike  || 18.43,12 ||

ajacakreśvarī rekhā dvitīyā kādibhāntagā  |
mādisāntā tṛtīyā tu tribhi rekhaiḥ svareśvarī  || 18.44 ||

ambikā śūladaṇḍasthā guhyaśaktyā vibhūṣitā  |
bindunādasamāyuktā uddhṛtaṃ paramākṣaram  || 18.45 ||

śrīkaṇṭha ambikā caiva dvidhārūpā tu kārayet |
punarambā ca phetkārī dīpanyāsanasaṃsthitā  || 18.46 ||

prajñābhūṣitamekaṃ hi jñānadevyā dvitīyakam  |
bindunādayute dve tu saṃhāryā icchayānvitā  || 18.47 ||

śrīkaṇṭhādibhṛgo'ntā vai saṃsthitā kulapaddhatiḥ  |
anena vidhinā devi bhinnā tu kulapaddhatiḥ  || 18.48 ||

parāmṛtapadaṃ hyetatślokānāṃ daśakaṃ dvikam  |
etasmin paṭhite devi kṣubhyante mātaraḥ sadā  || 18.49 ||

sadyāveśaḥ prajāyeta kavitvaṃ tasya jāyate  |
ājñāvedhādikā siddhiḥ paṭhite' smin prajāyate  || 18.50 ||
tvayā na kathyamabhakteṣvityājñā pārameśvarī  |
ślokadvādaśakaṃ hyetadvaṭamālāvibhūṣitam  || 18.51 ||

vaḍavānala mantavyaṃ pādādau mastakāvadhim  |
mekhaleyaṃ nyaseddevi vilomenopadeśataḥ  || 18.52 ||

uccāraṃ tasya cāveśaṃ sthāne sthāne pravartate  |
gopanīyaṃ prayatnena na deyaṃ yasya kasyacit || 18.53 ||

jīvasya rakṣaṇaṃ hyetadanyathā hānikṛdbhavet |
tadgraho'nyaṃ nyaseddevi brahmasūtravibhūṣaṇam  || 18.54 ||
dedīpyantaṃ pracaṇḍograṃ yathāvasthaṃ tathā śṛṇu  || 18.55 ||

ana aim | ā tha hrīm | i ṛ a | ī ṛ gho | u ḻa re | ū ḹ hrīm |
ṛ ca hsaḥ | ṛ dha pa | ḻa ṇa ra | ḹ u ma |
e ū gho | ai ī re | o ba hūm | au ka gho | aṃ kha ra | aḥ ga rū |
ka gha pe | kha ṅa hsaum | ga i gho | gha a ra | ṅa va mu |
ca bha khi | cha ya bhī | ja ḍa ma | jha ḍha bhī | ña ṭha ṣa |
ṭa jha ṇe | ṭha ña va | ḍa aḥ ma | ḍha ṭa va | ṇa ra ma |
ta ja pi | tha pa va | da sa pi | dha ha va | na cha ha |
pa la he | pha ā ru | ba ṣa ru | bha kṣa ru | ma ma ru |
ya śa ra  | ra aṃ ra | la ta ra | va e ra |
śa ai hrīm | ṣa o hūm | sa au phrem |
ha da hsaum | kṣa pha aiṃ  || 18.55 ||

rudraśaktitrayaṃ hyetadānandapadasaṃsthitam  |
anyonyavalayākāraṃ pañcāśatpadabhūṣitam  || 18.57 ||

vāmaraudrāntadakṣasthaṃ dakṣaraudrāntavāmagam  |
raudrāraudrasamāyuktaṃ triraudraṃ tadgraho'pyayam  || 18.58 ||

vāgeśvarī tathā māyā mohanī ca tṛtīyakā  |
jñānadevī ca gāyatrī ratnāḥ pañca ime smṛtāḥ  || 18.59 ||

nyāsamātreṇa cāveśamuccārādbhavate sphuṭam  |
piṇḍasya bandhanaṃ hyetadduṣṭasiṃhavināśanam  || 18.60 ||

tadgrahābaddhamūlaṃ tu pañcaratnopaśobhitam  |
ratnāṅgī ratnadehā tu ratnānāṃ nirṇayaṃ śṛṇu  || 18.61 ||

ratnānyamṛtamityuktaṃ pañcadhā tvamṛtaṃ priye  |
piṇḍabandhaṃ vinā tena jīvitaṃ tu na vidyate  || 18.62 ||

gaganāmṛtaratnaṃ tu svargaratnāmṛtaṃ tathā  |
pātālamartyaratnaṃ ca nārakaṃ ratnapañcakam  || 18.63 ||

devīdehātsamudbhūtaṃ devīdehādvinirgatam  |
jñānavīryaḥ savīryastu ajñāte vīryahānikṛt || 18.64 ||

tṛtīyaṃ daśanaṃ devyā ātmahṛtsthaṃ nitambagam  |
śikhāntasahitaṃ hyetatstanavāmoparisthitam  || 18.65 ||

karṇabhūṣaṇavāmasthaṃ bījenāhatamastakam  |
pañcaratnādiyogasya padoddhāraḥ prakīrtitaḥ  || 18.66 ||

prāṇaṃ vahnisamārūḍhaṃ guhyaśaktyā vibhūṣitam  |
bindumastaka cākrāntaṃ pañcaratnavibhedakam  || 18.67 ||

ekaikaṃ rakṣitaṃ ratnaṃ yoginībhiryathā yathā  |
tattathā śṛṇu kalyāṇi vyāptibhedo yathā sthitaḥ  || 18.68 ||

catuḥṣaṣṭigaṇaṃ vyomni dvātriṃsatsvargacāriṇaḥ  |
pātālaṃ ṣoḍaśairvyāptaṃ martyaṃ caivāṣṭakānvitam  || 18.69 ||

nirayasthāstu catvāri ratnānāmadhidevatāḥ  |
taddīptibhāsakā jñātā ajñātā dīptihārikāḥ  || 18.70 ||

ratnaprabhāvamatulaṃ dedīpyārcisamujjvalam  |
tatprabhāvādvarārohe yoginyo balavattarāḥ  || 18.71 ||

ato'rthaṃ gopayantyetāstadvīryaguṇavattarāḥ  |
rakṣanti svāminoddiṣṭā anādiṣṭaṃ haranti tāḥ  || 18.72 ||

amīṣāṃ darśanātsparśātpadārthapadayogataḥ  |
divyadehatvamāpnoti uccārātkṣobhakṛdbhavet || 18.73 ||

ṣoḍaśāvayavaṃ piṇḍaṃ ṣoḍhānyāsasuyantritam  |
ślokamālānvitaṃ divyaṃ tadgrahāvalibhūṣitam  || 18.74 ||

pañcaratnakṛtāṭopaṃ parāsyamakulānvitam  |
evaṃ kṛtvā śarīrasthaṃ nyāsamaṇḍalabhṛttanum  || 18.75 ||

devyā dehaṃ paraṃ hyetacchāmbhavaṃ padapūrvakam  |
vaḍavānalayogasthaṃ padadehaṃ padodbhavam  || 18.76 ||

padadehopadeśena yogīnyāsaparāyaṇaḥ  |
kṛtvā nyāsamaśeṣaṃ tu yastiṣṭhati subhāvitaḥ  || 18.77 ||

tasya duṣṭānyanekāni vighnāni prabhavanti na  |
śākinībhūtavetālāḥ piśācoragarākṣasāḥ  || 18.78 ||

siṃhavyāghragajā ṛkṣā duṣṭacittānyanekadhā  |
ye hiṃsanti yadālabdhaṃ teṣāṃ pratyaṅgirā bhavet || 18.79 ||

āpado rakṣayetsarvā ātmanaśca parasya ca  |
pracaṇḍayoginīghrāto nīto vā yamaśāsanam  || 18.80 ||

nyastvā ṣoḍaśavāreyaṃ satyedaṃ na mriyettu saḥ  |
sakṛnnyāse kṛte devi brahmahatyādipātakaiḥ  || 18.81 ||

samparke'pi na lipyo'sau sādhayeditarāṃstu saḥ  |
satyaṃ satyaṃ punaḥ satyaṃ pratyakṣedaṃ parādhvaram  || 18.82 ||

kṛtanyāsaḥ patetpadbhyāṃ yasyāsau mriyate dhruvam  |
gurostu na patetpāde yāvedaṃ dehasaṃsthitam  || 18.83 ||

vyādhiduḥkhaṃ bhavettasya yadyākrośenmriyettu saḥ  |
jñāte sati na kartavyaṃ yāvadgurukule vaset || 18.84 ||

guruhānikṛte śiṣyo na nandatyavaśaṃ priye  |
ājñāniṣṭho guṇaśreṣṭhaḥ kramajñaughaviśāradaḥ  || 18.85 ||

svādhikārī tu nānyo vai vācājyeṣṭho'bhivādayet |
pūrvasiddheṣu liṅgeṣu susiddhapratimāsu ca  || 18.86 ||
kṛtanyāsaḥ patetpādau sphuṭatyāśu na saṃśayaḥ  |
sadā pravartate yastu nyāsaṃ dehasya bhāvini  || 18.87 ||

anuṣṭhānādṛte tasya ūrdhvenotkramaṇaṃ bhavet |
trikālanyāsayogena ardharātre tathā punaḥ  || 18.88 ||

anena vidhinā kālaṃ kṣapayanti mahāmbike  |
anyaṃ ca paramaṃ devi granthibhedaṃ sudurlabham  || 18.89 ||

haṃsabhedaprayogena nyāsaṃ vakṣyāmi durlabham  |
o-jā-pū-kāma-madhyasthaṃ hṛnnābhau liṅgamadhyagam  || 18.90 ||

pīṭhaṃ vā padasaṃyuktamādyagranthicatuṣṭayam  |
oghānandaṃ jayānandaṃ purānandaṃ tṛtīyakam  || 18.91 ||

kamalānandasaṃyuktamādyabhedacatuṣṭayam  |
ḍikkariyāṇa lampārṇaṃ mahānandapuraṃ tathā  || 18.92 ||

karṇau mukhe tu nāsādyaṃ pīṭhaṃ vā padasaṃyutam  |
dvitīyaṃ padagranthīnāṃ nyāso'yaṃ parikīrtitaḥ  || 18.93 ||

aṣṭakoṭisuvistīrṇaṃ trikoṭyardhamataḥ śṛṇu  |
āmardakaṃ dharāpīṭhaṃ girāṅkaṃ rūpiṇīpuram  || 18.94 ||

dvau śaṅkhāvūrdhvamāyāntaṃ madhyakoṭigataṃ nyaset |
pīṭhaṃ vā padasaṃyuktaṃ tristhaṃ granthicatuṣṭayam  || 18.95 ||

māyānirodhimadhyasthamanyagranthicatuṣṭayam  |
jñānaśṛṅgaṃ ramāśṛṅgamṛṣiśṛṅgaṃ tṛtīyakam  || 18.96 ||

pūrṇaśṛṅgasamāyuktaṃ pīṭhaṃ vā padasaṃyutam  |
siddhakramasamāyuktaṃ gurupaṅktisamanvitam  || 18.97 ||

jñānadṛṣṭyā nyasettaṃ tu catuṣkedaṃ kulākulam  |
etatkulākulaṃ divyaṃ sarvasādhāraṇaṃ param  || 18.98 ||

padabhuktimatānāṃ ca padedaṃ parikīrtitam  |
anyatparataro dehaḥ koṭidvādaśamāśritaḥ  || 18.99 ||
akulīnapadādhvānaṃ nirādhāraṃ khagālayam  |
akulavyāptirityeṣā kṣetrapīṭhasamākulā  || 18.100 ||

sācārakulayogīnāṃ ṣaṇnavatyāpadānugā  |
caturāśītipadairvyāptiḥ sā cānyatra prakāśitā  || 18.101 ||

yeṣāṃ te tu punastatra vyāvṛtante punaḥ punaḥ  |
ṣaṇnavatipado haṃsaḥ sa cāmnāyavidāṃ viduḥ  || 18.102 ||

siddhakaulābhipannānāmitareṣāṃ na darśitaḥ  |
tatra divyakramaḥ pūjyaḥ padacāreṇa yoginā  || 18.103 ||

yena gacchennirācāraṃ tatpadaṃ paramaṃ śṛṇu  |
dvādaśādhāramūrdhnisthaṃ catuṣpīṭhasamucchritam  || 18.104 ||

kṣetrāṣṭakaṃ tato'dhastātsandohāni tato'pyadhaḥ  |
ṣoḍaśaiva pramāṇena caturdvāraṃ tatastvadhaḥ  || 18.105 ||

upaśabdasamopetamupadvāravivarjitam  |
akulīnaśarīredaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭipadānvitam  || 18.106 ||

tasmātsañjāyate sarvaṃ sarvaṃ tatraiva līyate  |
tatra dhyānaṃ japaṃ yogaṃ tatra pūjākriyādhvaram  || 18.107 ||

atra sthito na kenāpi vastunā bādhyate tu saḥ  |
paro hyātmā parā vidyā paraḥ śaivaḥ sanātanaḥ  || 18.108 ||

akulīnatanurbaddhaḥ paratattvatrayeṇa tu  |
ātmatattvagataṃ piṇḍaṃ satataṃ yogamabhyaset || 18.109 ||

vidyātattvagatā mantrāḥ padayogasamanvitāḥ  |
śivatattvagato yogaṃ rūpābhyāsaṃ samabhyaset || 18.110 ||

śivatattvagato yogaṃ rūpātītaṃ tu tatra vai  |
piṇḍaṃ kuṇḍalinī śaktiḥ padaṃ haṃsaḥ prakīrtitaḥ  || 18.111 ||

rūpaṃ binduḥ samākhyātaṃ rūpātītamanāmayam  |
kuladehaṃ parityajya akulīnavapuḥsthitaḥ  || 18.112 ||
sa ca vai sakalaḥ piṇḍaḥ kaulikānāṃ kujīmate  |
aparaṃ pāśavaṃ sarvaṃ tritattvaguṇalakṣaṇam  || 18.113 ||

śaivamārgavihīnānāṃ śaivānāmanyadharmiṇām  |
prasiddhena tu mārgeṇa prasiddhyarthaṃ bhajanti te  || 18.114 ||

aprasiddhojjhite siddhā na paśyantyakulāṃ tanum  |
akulena vinā siddhiraihikā pātrikā na hi  || 18.115 ||

prasiddhavihite mārge mokṣaścātra na saṃśayaḥ  |
aprasiddhagatā ṛjvī ṣoḍaśāntāmṛtāhradam  || 18.116 ||

āpūrya pūrayetsarvaṃ jīvāntaṃ jīvarūpiṇī  |
suprasiddhākṣabhūtā tu kālasaṅkhyākarī tu sā  || 18.117 ||

saṃsāre tu gatistasyā mokṣamārganiyāmikā  |
viṃśatyekasahasrāṇi ṣaṭśataiśca samanvitā  || 18.118 ||

saṃsārī kurute saṅkhyā hyahorātropadeśataḥ  |
apareṇa tu mārgeṇa saṃsārapathalakṣaṇam  || 18.119 ||

ahorātreṇa lakṣaikaṃ kālasaṅkhyāṃ karoti saḥ  |
saptādaśāni lakṣāṇi koṭirekā tvahamiśi  || 18.120 ||

caturāśīti padetyevaṃ kālaḥ kalati sarvathā  |
padamārgavidānāṃ tu sakalādajarāmaraḥ  || 18.121 ||

śivamārgavidānāṃ tu siddhamārge'nyathā śṛṇu  |
koṭidvādaśakopetaṃ koṭilakṣacatuṣṭayam  || 18.122 ||

ahorātrākṣasūtrasya saṅkhyeyaṃ hyakule tanau  |
akuleśatanuṃ yāvatsācāraṃ kulayoginām  || 18.123 ||

taccharīrabhṛtānando nirācārapadaṃ vrajet |
kulādhvarapadaṃ hṛtsthaṃ tatrasthaṃ paramaṃ kramam  || 18.124 ||

pūjayeddhṛtstanau nābhiṃ siddhāvvāpīṭhapādukau  |
śrīmadbarbaramoḍḍīśaṃ padasthaughamahārṇavam  || 18.125 ||
pūjayitvā smarettasthāmabdenoktaphalaṃ labhet |
dvibhistu adhamā siddhistribhirmadhyamatāṃ vrajet || 18.126 ||

ṣaḍbhirdvādaśakābdena khecara madhyamottamaḥ  |
adhamaṃ bhūcaraṃ karma madhyamaṃ bilasādhanam  || 18.127 ||

uttamottamasiddhībhiḥ khecaraḥ khecarordhvagaḥ  |
evaṃ devi samastedaṃ padayogakriyādhvaram  || 18.128 ||

kathitaṃ sarahasyaṃ tu padamārgaṃ sudurlabham  |
padasyāpi hi rūpo'sti rūpātītaṃ tu saṅkramaḥ  || 18.129 ||

haṃsajñānapadaṃ proktaṃ rūpasthaṃ śṛṇu sāmpratam  || 18.130 ||


iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate catuṣkasya padabhedamaṣṭādaśamaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************



śrībhairava uvāca

rūpaṃ tu dvividhaṃ proktaṃ sthūlasūkṣmaṃ prakīrtitam  |
sthūlamekavidhaṃ bhadre sūkṣmarūpamanantagam  || 19.1 ||

kaṇṭhakūpāditaḥ kṛtvā nirodhyāntamapaścimam  |
rūpopalabdhisaṃsthānaṃ vijñānānandapūritam  || 19.2 ||

pramāṇaṃ rūpamārgasya viṃśatkoṭyekasaṃsthitam  |
māyāvadhistu kūpādau atra vijñānasambhavaḥ  || 19.3 ||

etadrūpapadairvyāptaṃ rūpacakrasamanvitam  |
koṭiśaḥ koṭiśaścakraṃ cakre cakre catuṣṭayam  || 19.4 ||

vijñānānāṃ varārohe prabhāvo'syānuśīlaya  |
caturāśīti-m-ekatra kramātsthūlaṃ pṛthakpṛthak || 19.5 ||

kathayāmi yathānyāyaṃ citrabhānvāditaḥ priye  |
dvitīyaṃ vāruṇaṃ rūpaṃ tṛtīyaṃ daṇḍapāṇikam  || 19.6 ||

prāṇarūpaṃ caturthaṃ tu haṃsarūpaṃ tu pañcamam  |
ṣaṣṭhamātmavataṃ rūpaṃ saptamaṃ śaktipūrvakam  || 19.7 ||

aṣṭamaṃ brahmaṇo rūpaṃ navamaṃ keśavātmakam  |
dasamaṃ tu bhavedrudraṃ candramekādaśaṃ vidhuḥ  || 19.8 ||

dvādaśaṃ bhāskaraṃ rūpamīśvarākhyaṃ trayodaśam  |
kodaṇḍadvayamadhyasthaṃ dedīpyantaṃ suvarcasam  || 19.9 ||

kodaṇḍatiryagau dvau tu vāmanau kubjikātmakau  |
kodaṇḍāntargatau cānyau karṇakubjāntare sthitau  || 19.10 ||

śaṅkharūpadharau dvau tu sāṅkhyayogasya dāyakau  |
aṣṭādaśamanantākhyaṃ piṅgalaikonaviṃśamam  || 19.11 ||

viṃśamaṃ sakalīśānaṃ nirodhī caikaviṃśamam  |
vaḍavānalamāsīnamekaikaṃ cakrarūpiṇam  || 19.12 ||

cintayantaḥ svabhāvena abhāvapadamāśritaḥ  |
akuleśvararūpeṇa vijñānaprabhavo bhavet || 19.13 ||

yasya yadyādṛśaṃ rūpaṃ tadrūpaṃ dharate tu saḥ  |
avāntarapadasthasya pāramparyojjhitasya ca  || 19.14 ||

tasya cābhyāsayogena na sarvajñapadānugam  |
yasya yadyādṛśī vyāptistatrasthastatphalaṃ labhet || 19.15 ||

kāraṇānte mahādevo vibhāti kiraṇojjvalaḥ  |
satatābhyāsayogena trirabdāttatpadaṃ vrajet || 19.16 ||

nirodhinīpadārthānāṃ mahāgranthipade sthitā  |
tārayedviditā santī avijñātā prapātayet || 19.17 ||

mahāmāyārṇavaṃ ghoraṃ tārayedviditā satī  |
mahāntārīti sā proktā sarvarūpoparisthitā  || 19.18 ||
tasyā rūpamajānantaḥ sthūlasūkṣmasusūkṣmagam  |
na paśyanti guṇaṃ rūpaṃ yāvadeṣāṃ na saṅkramet || 19.19 ||

pūjitā dhyāyitā mātā pūrvokte kramamaṇḍale  |
aṣṭakoṭisuvistīrṇe trikoṭyordhvaguṇojjvale  || 19.20 ||

tatrasthoccāritā dhyātā pūjitā tu phalapradā  |
sādhayetsarvarūpāṇi vaṭendīkusumārcitā  || 19.21 ||

rūpaṃ devyāstu pūrvoktamaśeṣaguṇaśālinam  |
kiṃ tu noccāritaṃ tasya sthūladehaṃ yathā sthitam  || 19.22 ||

tasya coccāraṇāddevi prabuddhakiraṇojjvalā  |
yaṣṭīhatā bhujaṅgīva pātayedavalokanāt || 19.23 ||

tadvadeṣā mahāvīryā mahāntārī mahābalā  |
vidyāyaṣṭihatā santī sṛṣṭicakre hyanekadhā  || 19.24 ||

ṣaṭprakāroparisthā sā ṣaḍvaktrā bṛhadodarā  |
bhujairdvādaśakopetā koṭarākṣā subhīṣaṇā  || 19.25 ||

vajrahastā tu vajrasthā ṣaḍyogikulamadhyagā  |
ṣaṣṭḥīśānasamāyuktā siddhapaṅktau niveśitā  || 19.26 ||

vidyādaṇḍasamāyuktā tasyoccāraṃ śṛṇuṣva me  |
yoginīnāṃ kuleśā tu gopitānyatra śāsane  || 19.27 ||

hā svā yai prathamaṃ padaṃ ṇḍā ka ḍu ku dvitīyakam  |
ḍu ku ṭī ṅga tṛtīyaṃ tu pi ṭī ṅga ri caturthakam  || 19.28 ||

pañca pañca tathā pañca svarūpākṣaramālikā  |
eṣā sāṅketikā proktā saṃsphuṭā guruvānane  || 19.29 ||

eṣopāyo mahāntāryā durlabhaḥ prakaṭīkṛtaḥ  |
tasyaivoccāraṇātsarvaṃ kampate ḍāmarīgaṇam  || 19.30 ||

saṃhārakramaṣaṭkasya vṛddhājñeyaṃ prakīrtitā  |
ājñā kramati bhaktānāmabhaktānāṃ na saṅkramet || 19.31 ||
kiṃ tu cārādhitā kiñcitpāramparyaughamāgatā  |
uccaranto hanecchailān kruddhasyānyeṣu kā kathā  || 19.32 ||

asyā devyārcane dhyāne jape havanatatparaḥ  |
sthūlaṃ saṃsādhayetsarvaṃ mahāmāyāntakāvadhim  || 19.33 ||

yatsañcintayate rūpaṃ tatsarvamicchayā bhavet |
mahāmāyāvinā yogī māyaiva guṇakṛdbhavet || 19.34 ||

bhūguṇo bhūcare mārge jalarūpo jaleśvaraḥ  |
tejasvī tejaso mārge vāyorvāyubhṛteśvaraḥ  || 19.35 ||

vyomni vyomādhipo yogī pañcāntaguṇayogataḥ  |
tripañcavarṣādūrdhvaṃ ca sarvajño guṇa-m-īśvaraḥ  || 19.36 ||

vaṭendīvaramālābhiḥ pūjayantaughasantatim  |
sādhayennikhilaṃ rūpaṃ sthūlasūkṣmamatīndriyam  || 19.37 ||

tatprasādena māyordhvaṃ bhittvā śaktitrayaṃ vrajet |
tatraiva sā mahāmāyā sūkṣmarūpā susūkṣmagā  || 19.38 ||

khecare'nekarūpā sā sūkṣmasūkṣmatarā parā  |
dṛśyate mṛgatṛṣṇeva gurvājñātopadeśataḥ  || 19.39 ||

akuleśvaradevasya padāntamanuvartinī  |
viśuddhamālinī hyeṣā tadābhyāsena sarvavit || 19.40 ||

abhyāso'pyasya kartavyaḥ pṛṣṭhe dattvā tu bhāskaram  |
prāsādagṛhavṛkṣāṇāṃ sandhyākālāntare sthitaḥ  || 19.41 ||

atha cedvṛkṣamūlādho madhyāhne samupasthite  |
pasyate rūpabhṛtsarvaṃ sūkṣmasūkṣmāṇavo hradam  || 19.42 ||

rūpamanyadvarārohe śṛṇuṣva karaṇātmakam  |
yena sādhayate rūpaṃ khecarādimanukramāt || 19.43 ||

sarvasādhāraṇaṃ devi na bhavatyaphalapradam  |
yāvanna tatprasādena gurvājñātaḥ pravartate  || 19.44 ||
śubhe'hani muhūrte vā śiṣyamekāntato nayet |
ājñāṃ dattvā prapūjitvā kṛtvā maṇḍalakādikam  || 19.45 ||

tatopari ca saṃsthāpya nirmale gaganāntare  |
chāyāṃ nirīkṣayitvā tu kaṇṭhakūpopadeśataḥ  || 19.46 ||

tato nirīkṣayedvyomaṃ sākāraṃ rūpadarśanam  |
paśyate bhāskaraṃ bimbaṃ śivarūpaṃ sadāśivam  || 19.47 ||

taṃ dṛṣṭvā pātakānāṃ ca avasānaṃ bhaviṣyati  |
ṣaṇmāsābhyāsayogena bhūcarīṇāṃ patirbhavet || 19.48 ||

trirabdena tu bhūnātho hartā kartā svayaṃ prabhuḥ  |
avasthāṃ tyajate sarvāṃ pañcāvasthāparaṃ vrajet || 19.49 ||

nirācāreṇa yogena tannāsti yanna sādhayet |
uktānuktaṃ tu deveśi sarvamasmātprasādhayet || 19.50 ||

sakṛdabhyāsayogena māse vā tvayane'pi vā  |
vindate hyāgataṃ kālamāpado vātmanaḥ pare  || 19.51 ||

kṛṣṇavarṇena devena ṣaṇmāsānmriyate dhruvam  |
vaktramūrdhni bhayaṃ vindyānmūrdhni pātānmriyeddhruvam  || 19.52 ||

lohite brahmahatyā tu pīte vyādhibhayaṃ bhavet |
pādau yatra na dṛśyete videśagamanaṃ bhavet || 19.53 ||

ūrumārge bhavedrogaṃ guhye vai naśyate priyā  |
udare arthanāśaṃ tu hṛdaye mṛtyubhāgbhavet || 19.54 ||

bhujahīne patedbandhurvāme bhāryābhayaṃ bhavet |
ṣaṇmāsāllakṣayetsarvamātmanaśca parasya vā  || 19.55 ||

upadeśena deveśi śeṣaṃ ca guravānanāt |
rūpapūrṇahradāntastho rūpastho nirapekṣadhīḥ  || 19.56 ||

sūkṣmasūkṣmāntarūpeṇa rūpātītapadaṃ vrajet |
yogasiddhā mahādevi dṛśyante vyomagāgaṇāḥ  || 19.57 ||
bindurūpāstu te sarve kvaciddṛśyanti na kvacit |
ghaṭādhāragataṃ prāṇaṃ kūrmayantreṇa pīḍayet || 19.58 ||

nocchvasenmāsamekaṃ tu tathyaṃ bhairava-m-abravīt |
bhairavovāca kalyāṇi kularūpaṃ prakāśitam  || 19.59 ||

akulaṃ vyāpakaṃ rūpaṃ susūkṣmaṃ śṛṇu sāmpratam  |
ekānekavibhāgena saṃsthitā vyomamālinī  || 19.60 ||

amṛtāmbhodhimadhyasthā cārasthā cāravāhinī  |
icchārūpadharā devī kubjinīti kujāmbikā  || 19.61 ||

dvibhujaikamukhī devī athānekabhujānanā  |
cārasthā cāramadhyasthā cāradehā caleśvarī  || 19.62 ||

candragarbhasya caryeyaṃ cāravī caṇḍacaṇḍikā  |
pīṭhamadhyagatā pūjyā candragarbhasamanvitā  || 19.63 ||

ṣoḍaśārakamadhyasthā caturvargaphalodayā  |
pīṭhapīṭhādhipairyuktā sarvajñā sarvadāyikā  || 19.64 ||

ājñāvabodhajananī divyarūpaprakāśinī  |
asyāḥ pragopitaṃ rūpaṃ yoginībhirvarānane  || 19.65 ||

tena rūpavatānāṃ tu rūpavyāptirna sidhyati  |
dedīpyantī mahānandā sahasrādityavarcasā  || 19.66 ||

sphurantī mālikā divyā ājñātaḥ sampravartate  |
sadoditaṃ sadānandaṃ parānandapradāyakam  || 19.67 ||

kalātītaṃ tu kālāntamājñārūpojjvalaṃ param  |
anantaṃ sakalaṃ jñānaṃ divyājñāparamojjvalam  || 19.68 ||

uttarasya ca ṣaṭkasya rūpedaṃ parasambhavam  |
dakṣiṇasyāpi ṣaṭkasya śaktiyuktasya varṇitam  || 19.69 ||

sthūlarūpaṃ varārohe sarvatraiva prakāśitam  |
uttaraṃ gopitaṃ rūpaṃ devatābhiḥ susiddhidam  || 19.70 ||
kasmātsidhyati śīghredamanyatra kṣapaṇākulam  |
bhairaveṇa tu rūpeṇa bhairavatvaṃ prasādhayet || 19.71 ||

vighnajālojjhitaṃ hyetattenedaṃ śīghrasiddhidam  |
atra rūpasamālabdhaḥ pūrvoktaṃ labhate phalam  || 19.72 ||

akulakramamārgeṇa ājñāyogena sarvathā  |
akulīnakramāntasthaḥ kubjīśapadamāśritaḥ  || 19.73 ||

prāpyate bhairavānandaṃ samastānandapūrvakam  |
toṣito'haṃ tvayā devi tenedaṃ saṃsphuṭaṃ mayā  || 19.74 ||

kīrtitaṃ tava kalyāṇi sugopyaṃ rūpasādhanam  |
sarvatantreṣu luptedamīṣadyogimate sphuṭam  || 19.75 ||

mauktikāvalisādṛśyaṃ sitaraktaṃ tu pītagam  |
grīvā kuṇḍalinī tasya viyogaṃ tu tadā bhavet || 19.76 ||

cañcuprasāraṇe varṣaṃ durbhikṣaṃ cañcusampuṭe  |
kṛṣṇavarṇe bhavenmṛtyuḥ ṣaṇmāsāttu na saṃśayaḥ  || 19.77 ||

sarvameva na paśyeta sadyameva vinaśyati  |
bhrūmadhyagatamātmānaṃ ṣaḍaṅgena mahāmate  || 19.78 ||

dṛśyate sūryavadbimbaṃ pratyakṣaṃ cāgrataḥ sthitam  |
hrasve nīle bhayaṃ vindyāddīrghe sthūle hyarogatā  || 19.79 ||

dhūmre uccāṭanaṃ proktaṃ rakte rogaṃ varānane  |
kṛṣṇe brahmavināśaṃ vā mṛtyurevābhijāyate  || 19.80 ||

samale tu tathā hānirnīlamāle tathāpadaḥ  |
vāyavyāṃ tu yadā dhūmrāṃ mālāṃ pasyati yogavit || 19.81 ||

tadā uccāṭanaṃ devi nairṛtyāṃ daṃṣṭriṇo bhayam  |
āgneyyāṃ tu yadā bhinnāṃ mālāṃ paśyati yogavit || 19.82 ||

deśabhraṃśo' gnidāhaśca rājā caiva vinaśyati  |
madhye tasya yadā chidraṃ paśyate yogacintakaḥ  || 19.83 ||
mṛtyustasya varārohe divasairdaśabhirbhavet |
īśāne sthāvarabhayaṃ kauberyāmarthasiddhidam  || 19.84 ||

aindryāṃ vai sthānalābhaṃ ca vāruṇyāṃ sukhamedhate  |
yāmyāyāṃ mriyate devi nātra kāryavicāraṇāt || 19.85 ||

sampūrṇaṃ susamaṃ pītaṃ snigdhaṃ rūkṣatvavarjitam  |
sāmalaṃ siddhidaṃ proktaṃ jīvādityaṃ varānane  || 19.86 ||

śrīmatkubjimate sarvaṃ saṃsphuṭaṃ kathitaṃ tava  |
mālinī vyomasaṃsthā ca bindurvyome tathaiva ca  || 19.87 ||

kulākhyaṃ puruṣaṃ vyome rūpātītamataḥ śṛṇu  || 19.88 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

ūcustvevaṃ punarbhadre rūpātītasya nirṇayam  |
śṛṇuṣva sarvabhāvena avajñārahitā satī  || 19.89 ||

amanaskaṃ mano'tītaṃ bhāvābhāvavivarjitam  |
layoccāravinirmuktaṃ hetutarkavivarjitam  || 19.90 ||

heyopāyavinirmuktaṃ śrutidṛṣṭāntavarjitam  |
nāstikyabhāvasampannaṃ śūnyabhūtamanāmayam  || 19.91 ||

prameyāvaliyogasya atītaṃ kāraṇeśvaram  |
atīndriyamanābhāṣaṃ parākāśaṃ tu tadviduḥ  || 19.92 ||

tasyopāyamidaṃ sarvaṃ yogamārgakriyādhvaram  |
sādhyate yena mārgeṇa rūpātītaṃ tu tacchṛṇu  || 19.93 ||

vyomaṃ kṛtvā samākāśe sa saṃsmṛtya vilāpayet |
asmin taṃ tu cidākāśe bāhyākāśe sa eva hi  || 19.94 ||

parākāśe pare sthāne yānākāśamatordhvataḥ  |
rūpātītaṃ tataścordhve niḥsandigdhaṃ padaṃ pare  || 19.95 ||

bahunoktena kiṃ devi pūrvaṃ vyāvarṇitaṃ maya  |
gurorasya prasādena labhyate paramaṃ padam  || 19.96 ||

pūrvaṃ vyāvarṇitaṃ tubhyamadṛṣṭaguṇalakṣaṇam  |
etatsarvaṃ samākhyātaṃ śāmbhavasya guṇāspadam  || 19.97 ||

nirācāreṇa mārgeṇa śāmbhavaṃ tu samabhyaset |
kimabhyāsaḥ punastasya yasya sarvaṃ puraḥsaram  || 19.98 ||

yasya sambhavitaṃ śambhumanantaguṇadāyakam  |
yogātmā vai sa sarvatra pūjyate yoginīkule  || 19.99 ||

yadi śambhuvidherbhaktaḥ saṃsāre viratātmanaḥ  |
sa sādhayati sarvajño dehenānena sarvagaḥ  || 19.100 ||

na dhyānaṃ na japaḥ pūjā maṇḍalādiprapūjanam  |
nirācāravidhānena dehenānena bhairavi  || 19.101 ||

ātmānaṃ pūjayennityaṃ yathālabdhopajīvakaḥ  |
agnivatsarvavarṇeṣu sa śīghraṃ phalabhāgbhavet || 19.102 ||

prākṛtāmadhamāṃ siddhiṃ madhyamāṃ cottamāṃ ca yām  |
uttamottamatāṃ yānti ṣaḍbhirmāsaiḥ kramātkramāt || 19.103 ||

adhikārapadasthena kartavyaṃ vidhipūrvakam  |
gurumaṇḍalakādyaṃ ca pūrvāmnāyaprapūjanam  || 19.104 ||


śrīkubjikā uvāca

śrutaṃ sarvaṃ ca deveśa padārthānāṃ ca nirṇayam  |
kimāmnāyaṃ kathaṃ pūjā etadācakṣva bhairava  || 19.105 ||

devyuktaṃ ca vacaḥ śrutvā bhairavo hasitānanaḥ  |
pūjāmnāyamidaṃ sarvaṃ kathyamānaṃ na budhyasi  || 19.106 ||

dvīpāmnāyastu prathamo devyāmnāyo dvitīyakaḥ  |
pīṭhāmnāyastṛtīyastu siddhāmnāyaścaturthakaḥ  || 19.107 ||
asyoddhāraṇamekatra pūjanaṃ tatprakīrtirtam  |
guptadeśe sugandhāḍhye viviktopadravojjhite  || 19.108 ||

pīṭhāḥ pīṭhādhipāḥ siddhāḥ pīṭhāmbāstatsamīpataḥ  |
pīṭhamadhyagatāṃ devīṃ catuḥsiddhasamanvitām  || 19.109 ||

maṇḍalottaradigbhāge gurupaṅktiṃ prapūjayet |
etadāmnāyamākhyātaṃ kiṃ tu maṇḍalakānvitam  || 19.110 ||

talahastapramāṇena yonyagre maṇḍalādikam  |
pūjyo'haṃ maṇḍale tatra navātmānapadākṣaraiḥ  || 19.111 ||

ānandapadasaṃyuktaṃ śaktibhairavapūrvakam  |
bhairaveti padaṃ paścādvīrādhipatayeti ca  || 19.112 ||

saṣoḍaśapadairyuktaḥ pūjanīyo'tra maṇḍale  |
āmnāyamaṇḍalaṃ hyetatsamekhalacatuṣkalam  || 19.113 ||

sarvametatkramāmnāyaṃ maṇḍalopari maṇḍalam  |
pūjitena bhavatyāsu tatsarvamuditaṃ mayā  || 19.114 ||

alinā pūritaṃ pātraṃ samayālabdhodakaṃ pṛthak |
karmakāle prakartavyaṃ pūjānte'rghanivedanam  || 19.115 ||

candanairdhūpanaivedyairdadyādācamanaṃ pṛthak |
tasmātkriyākalāpena ārādhanavidhiṃ yajet || 19.116 ||

dīpotsavaṃ sanaivedyamalipātraṃ saphalguṣam  |
cakrapūjāvidhirhyevaṃ kuryādārādhane vidhau  || 19.117 ||

athavāmnāyamādhāraṃ divyaughāgamapaddhatim  |
pūjayetsarvabhāvena sarvāmnāyaṃ sa gopayet || 19.118 ||

athādyamaṇḍalaṃ yonestadvadasya dine dine  |
kurvantasya parā vyāptiḥ kramoghaṃ sampravartate  || 19.119 ||

oghādhāramidaṃ divyamāgamaṃ yaḥ paṭhedidam  |
pādukau pūjayitvā tu caturdaśyāṣṭamīṣu ca  || 19.120 ||
puṣpāvaraṇake divye vastramālyopaśobhite  |
divyagandhasugandhāḍhye dīpamālopaśobhite  || 19.121 ||

sauvarṇarajatādībhistāmralohaśilāmṛdā  |
bhaktyā-devaṃ svaśaktyā ca piṣṭadīpān ghṛtānvitān  || 19.122 ||

naivedyaphalguṣālibhyāṃ puṣpadhūpairanekadhā  |
evaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ paścādvyākhyāne vācane'pi vā  || 19.123 ||

gurumaṇḍalakaṃ kuryāttriṣkālaṃ pustakāgrataḥ  |
ahaṃ vai guravastasya yatrāste cāgamaḥ svayam  || 19.124 ||

guruvacca pramantavyaṃ vidyābodhaparaṃ gurum  |
na vinā ca gurorvidyā na vidyārahito guruḥ  || 19.125 ||

yathā gurustathā vidyā yathā vidyā tathā guruḥ  |
prāptavidyā guroḥ pārśve vidyāprāpte gurutvatā  || 19.126 ||

evaṃ cāmnāyiko mārgaḥ sarvathā granthato'rthataḥ  |
vetti siddhaḥ sa me tulyaḥ sāmānyastatsamo na hi  || 19.127 ||

eṣa te kauliko mārgaḥ paramārthopadeśataḥ  |
kulaṃ ca kulavidyāṃ ca kulamārgaṃ kulakramam  || 19.128 ||

catuṣkaṃ yo vijānāti sa bhavetkulanandanaḥ  |
catuṣṭayaṃ samākhyātaṃ pṛccha-m-anyaṃ yathāruci  || 19.129 ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate catuṣkanirṇayo nāma ekonaviṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************



śrīkubjikā uvāca

purā mahyaṃ tvayā deva dvīpāmnāyaḥ pracoditaḥ  |
paramārthopadeśena yathā tvevaṃ vada prabho  || 20.1 ||

śrībhairava uvāca

satyedaṃ sādhu deveśi yattvayā paripṛcchitam  |
dvīpāmnāyāvatāraṃ tu sugopyaṃ prakaṭāmi te  || 20.2 ||

ādyakalpāvatāre tu udyānārṇavamadhyataḥ  |
kṛṣṇaraktajanākīrṇaṃ dedīpyārcisamaprabham  || 20.3 ||

oghasṛṣṭestu saṃsthānaṃ mātaṅgadvīpamuttamam  |
dvitīye'tra pare kalpe sindūrāruṇasaprabham  || 20.4 ||

pītaraktajanākīrṇaṃ brahmaghnaṃ dvīpanāyakam  |
tejaḥsṛṣṭestu saṃsthānaṃ karālāgnisamaprabham  || 20.5 ||

tṛtīye dvāpare kalpe kusumbhodakasannibham  |
pītāruṇajanākīrṇaṃ rajasā dvīpamujjvalam  || 20.6 ||

krīḍāsṛṣṭestu saṃsthānaṃ caṇḍogrākṣisamaprabham  |
udyānabhairavāmbhobhiḥ kallolālīsamākulam  || 20.7 ||

mahākalpe caturthe tu padmarāgasamaprabham  |
śvetaraktajanākīrṇaṃ viśuddhāmoghasampadam  || 20.8 ||

icchāsṛṣṭestu saṃsthānamatrodyānaṃ mahāvanam  |
gandhamālyasupuṣpāḍhyaṃ mahocchuṣmopaśobhitam  || 20.9 ||

pañcame divyakalpe tu candrakoṭisamaprabham  |
candrakāntimayaṃ divyaṃ viśuddhodadhimadhyagam  || 20.10 ||

kāmānandajanākīrṇaṃ catuḥsṛṣṭipravartakam  |
anekānandasampannaṃ candradvīpaguṇāvṛtam  || 20.11 ||

caturvarṇaguṇānandaṃ caturvargaphalodayam  |
caturmāyājanātītaṃ caturthāntamṛtātmakam  || 20.12 ||

jñānakriyāmadhiṣṭhānamavyaktāvyaktarūpiṇam  |
sṛṣṭisandohamānandaṃ candradvīpaguṇāspadam  || 20.13 ||

pañcadvīpopacāro'yamupadvīpānyataḥ śṛṇu  |
catvāryeva sabījāni vyaktiṃ yānti kulādhvare  || 20.14 ||

upadvīpāruṇaṃ cādyaṃ vāruṇaṃ tu dvitīyakam  |
narasiṃhaṃ tṛtīyaṃ tu lokālokaṃ caturthakam  || 20.15 ||

dvīpopadvīpasambhūtaṃ sarvametaccarācaram  |
vyaktāvyaktaṃ tu taṃ yasmātkāraṇaṃ taṃ nigadyate  || 20.16 ||

pare catvāri dvīpāni catvāryevaṃ parāṇi ca  |
candradvīpaṃ paraṃ tebhyo madhyasthaṃ vyaktikāraṇam  || 20.17 ||

dvīpasṛṣṭiparānandamudyānārṇavamadhyagam  |
lakṣitavyopadeśena śeṣānyadvistṛtaṃ purā  || 20.18 ||


śrīkubjikā uvāca

dvīpānandaṃ kathaṃ deva kathitaṃ tu mayā śrutam  |
tathāpi me manoglāniḥ kathayasva yathā sphuṭam  || 20.19 ||

vyāpyavyāpakabhāvena yatsthāne saṃsthitāni tu  |
yasmādutpattisaṃsthānametatsarvaṃ vada prabho  || 20.20 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

sādhu sādhu mahābhāge sādhu mālini sarvathā  |
pṛcchitaṃ śṛṇu kalyāṇi niravadyaṃ vadāmi te  || 20.21 ||

ādau ṣoḍaśa pīṭhāni pīṭhe dvīpasamudbhavaḥ  |
tāni dvādaśadhā viddhi ekaikaṃ ca pṛthakpṛthak || 20.22 ||

kulacakrasamāyuktaṃ triḥprakāraṃ vilakṣayet |
pīṭhopapīṭhasaṃyuktaṃ kṣetrasandohalakṣitam  || 20.23 ||

upakṣetropasandohe dve dve pīṭhasamāvṛte  |
lakṣitavyāni yatnena upāsya guravaṃ priye  || 20.24 ||
catustriṃśati dvīpāni dvīpasthaṃ tricatuṣṭayam  |
mātarāṇāṃ varārohe ekaikasmin vyavasthitam  || 20.25 ||

dūrasthāni purasthāni dehasthāni vilakṣayet |
tairvinā sādhanaṃ siddhiryatnenāpi na jāyate  || 20.26 ||

itarasya bahisthāni kṣetrasthāni tu sādhake  |
dehasthāni tu tasyaiva kiṃ tvevaṃ hi sa muktibhāk || 20.27 ||

kurute yatra saṃsthānaṃ kvacitsādhakapuṅgavaḥ  |
sādhanaṃ mantrayogasya liṅgasaṃsthāpane'pi vā  || 20.28 ||

pratimā cādhikārārthaṃ jñātvā sthānaṃ samāśrayet |
anyathā naiva bhuktistu dvandvadveṣo rujānvitaḥ  || 20.29 ||

dvīpaṃ dvīpādhipaṃ devyā dvīpanāthasamanvitam  |
pīṭhabhinnakramaṃ jñātvā sidhyate hyavicārataḥ  || 20.30 ||

kṣetragrāmapurasyaiva pīṭhasya nagarasya vā  |
jñātvā pañcasu saṃsthānaṃ saṃsthānaṃ kārayettadā  || 20.31 ||

pañca pañca tathā pañca pañcamāntaṃ kulāntikam  |
calasaumye catuṣkaṃ tu īśvaraikaṃ diśāditaḥ  || 20.32 ||

pīṭhavyūhavaraṃ madhye dvīpavyūhaṃ bahisthitam  |
puraṃ nāma bhavedyatra tāṃ diśaṃ tu samāśrayet || 20.33 ||

asthigūthāvṛtaṃ cāpi doṣairdviṣṭaṃ yathā bhavet |
tathāpi bhogamāpnoti tatsthānanyāsayogataḥ  || 20.34 ||

nākṣareṇa bhavenmantraṃ yogaścaiva guṇānvitaḥ  |
akṣareṇāpi mantrasya kiṃ tu tatsthānayogataḥ  || 20.35 ||

mantrasthāpitaliṅgāni nisphurāṇi yaśasvini  |
dṛśyante sthānahīnāni siddhaiḥ saṃsthāpitāni tu  || 20.36 ||

sthānavaikalyabhāvena yasyāścaryaṃ kuleśvari  |
svatejodīpitaṃ śambhuṃ kvaciddṛśyati nisphuram  || 20.37 ||
sarvajñaṃ sarvadaṃ mantramajasraṃ bhāvapūrvakam  |
sarvadaṃ sarvakālasthaṃ kālarūpāmṛtātmakam  || 20.38 ||

gopitaṃ sarvatantreṣu dvīpāmnāyena gopitam  |
dvīpākṣaraṃ tathā vāraṃ tithinakṣatrasaṃyutam  || 20.39 ||

sādhakākṣarasaṃyuktaṃ mantrametatsurārcitam  |
pīṭhayuktaṃ prameyena bhidya pīṭhena cetaram  || 20.40 ||

daśa-m-ekādaśenaiva kūṭasthaṃ vā samekataḥ  |
purasyādyakṣaraṃ vāpi svasthāne kṣobhakṛdbhavet || 20.41 ||

sarvasyāpi hi kṣetrasya praveśe japamārabhet |
svasthānātmakamantreṇa svasthānena puraṃ viśet || 20.42 ||

diśāmālokya japtavyaṃ saptavārāvadhi priye  |
tāvatkṣubhyati tatkṣetraṃ bālavṛddhayuvānapi  || 20.43 ||

sthitirvai yatra mantavyā tatraiva vidhimācaret |
sakṛdanyatra coccāraṃ japamānaṃ puraṃ viśet || 20.44 ||

tatrānnapānaśayanaṃ kiñcidduḥkhaṃ na jāyate  |
yaḥ punaḥ sarvabhāvena bhaktiyuktaḥ samabhyaset || 20.45 ||

dvīpasthānaṃ samāsthāya sveṣṭamantrasya sādhayet |
tatrāpi tasya siddhīni bhavantyaṣṭavidhā priye  || 20.46 ||

dvīpādhipamajānanto varṣapūrṇaśatena vā  |
tathāpi na hi sidhyanti yogāddhyānācca mantriṇaḥ  || 20.47 ||

pīṭhādhipatayaḥ proktāḥ ṣoḍaśaiva varānane  |
taistu vyāptamidaṃ sarvaṃ catustriṃśāntagocaram  || 20.48 ||

dvīpādhipatayaḥ proktāścatustriṃśati kevalāḥ  |
pīṭhādhipatibhiryuktāḥ pañcāśa patayastu te  || 20.49 ||

ādyantasaṃsthitaṃ bhadre madhye liṅgasya lakṣayet |
pīṭhagrāmapurasyāpi lakṣayitvā nirākulam  || 20.50 ||
pālakasyākṣaraṃ yatra yadidaṃ na tadādimam  |
kasmātpīṭheṣu adhipāḥ pīṭhabhinnaṃ na pūjayet || 20.51 ||

na guruṃ nādimaṃ cāntaṃ na madhyaṃ pīṭhasaṃyutam  |
kevalaṃ yadi labhyeta tadādyaṃ tu surārcite  || 20.52 ||

liṅgasaṃjñā tu nāmasya sarvato adhipāvṛtam  |
tasmādekatamaṃ gṛhya liṅgamūlaṃ yadakṣaram  || 20.53 ||

taṃ tu gṛhya vikalpena madhyāntaṃ varjayetpriye  |
evaṃ jñātvā tataḥ siddhirjāyate nirvikalpataḥ  || 20.54 ||

avijñāya na pūjyetāṃ yastu kurvīta sādhanam  |
mama tulyāstu kurvanti vighnaṃ vai pālakāḥ priye  || 20.55 ||

atra sārataraṃ proktaṃ niścayamadhipān prati  |
śrutaṃ devi tvayā sarvaṃ nāma pañcāśakeṣvapi  || 20.56 ||

aghoryāḍāmare tantre sūcito'pyasya nirṇayaḥ  |
saṃsphuṭaṃ sarvabhāvena nirṇītaṃ kubjinīmate  || 20.57 ||


śrīkubjikā uvāca

kathaṃ deva sthitā dehe pīṭhadvīpādhipāśrayam  |
kva sthāne saṃsthitā deva etadācakṣva niścayam  || 20.58 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

śṛṇu devi yathā dehe pīṭhaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ śiraḥ  |
āvṛtaṃ vaṃśaguhyāntaṃ dvīpaiḥ kodaṇḍakāvadhim  || 20.59 ||

grīvādho vāṃśamārgeṇa kandordhvaṃ yāva saṃsthitam  |
pañca dvīpāni deveśi brahmaṇyādhiṣṭhitāni tu  || 20.60 ||

pañca nābhigatā bhadre māheśyālaṅkṛtāstu te  |
jaṭhare pañca vaiṣṇavyā kaumāryeva hṛdi sthitā  || 20.61 ||
pañcadvīpānvitā kālī kaṇṭhānte saṃvyavasthitā  |
aindryākāśapadasthā tu catuṣkaparivāritā  || 20.62 ||

caturdvīpasamāyuktā cāmuṇḍā tu bhruvottare  |
mahākālī tu kopasthā saṃhārapathavartiṇī  || 20.63 ||

devyādhiṣṭhānadvīpeṣu yo yatrāntavyavasthitaḥ  |
daṇḍadhārī pracaṇḍaśca daṃṣṭrālī vajratuṇḍakaḥ  || 20.64 ||

trijaṭī śaṅkhatuṇḍaśca kapālī triśirastathā  |
ete vargādhipāḥ proktā aṣṭau vasumahābalāḥ  || 20.65 ||

yāṃ diśaṃ saṃsthitāste vai tanmukhastu prapūjayet |
sabāhyābhyantaraṃ matvā tato'sau siddhibhājanaḥ  || 20.66 ||

eṣa devi samāsena dvīpāmnāyaḥ prakāśitaḥ  |
śeṣo'nyo vistaro'pyasya kulasāre vadāmyaham  || 20.67 ||

vijñāna ṛddhisampannaṃ jñānamaṇḍalapūritam  |
tenedaṃ śrīmataṃ proktaṃ bhuktimuktipradāyakam  || 20.68 ||

jñātena tantrasāreṇa anuṣṭhānaṃ vinā priye  |
bhājano bhuktimuktīnāṃ yadyevaṃ gopayetsudhīḥ  || 20.69 ||

śrīmatena vinā yuktāḥ khaṇḍajñānavimohitāḥ  |
hastyandhavadvibhajyante dṛṣtihīnā yatastu te  || 20.70 ||

āgataṃ tu gajaṃ śrutvā andhavṛndena sau vṛtaḥ  |
pucchakarṇāṅghrihastābhyāṃ ড়ṛṣṭhakukṣodareṣu ca  || 20.71 ||

yena yatra gajaḥ spṛṣṭastadbhāvastena mantritaḥ  |
pucchahastā vadantyevaṃ gajo'yaṃ cāmarākṛtiḥ  || 20.72 ||

karṇalagnāstu sūrpeva pādalagnokhalaṃ yathā  |
bhittirūpaṃ tu kukṣisthā pṛṣṭhasthā gṛharūpiṇaḥ  || 20.73 ||

stambhobhau hastalagnau tu muṣalau dantidantagau  |
evamandhaganā mūḍhā anyonyaṃ spardhayanti te  || 20.74 ||
anyaiścakṣuryutaistvevaṃ yudhyamānāḥ parasparam  |
