tān dṛṣṭvā hāsyamārabdhaṃ taṃ śrutvā vismitāstu te  || 20.75 ||

atha śrutvā mahāhāsyaṃ kimarthaṃ hasitā vayam  |
ūcustvevākṣiyuktena mā yudhyaivaṃ vimohitāḥ  || 20.76 ||

dṛṣṭihīnāstvaho tubhyaṃ hastirūpo'nyathā sthitaḥ  |
hastino'ṅgāni sarvāṇi yāni spṛṣṭāni tatparaiḥ  || 20.77 ||

paṭalāntaritā dṛṣṭirgatvā vaidyamupāśrayet |
yena paśyasi sarvāṅgaṃ śrīkubjaughamahāgajam  || 20.78 ||

gajo yathāndhavṛndasya tathā jñānaṃ pravartate  |
ājñākramaṃ vinā lokastatkramaṃ kubjinīmate  || 20.79 ||

kathitaṃ niravadyaṃ te gajasyāvayavo yathā  |
gajāṅganyāyato yatra dakṣavāmordhvakaulike  || 20.80 ||

sarvaṃ sampāditaṃ tubhyamājñānandakulārṇavam  |
idānīṃ śṛṇu kalyāṇi kālacakraṃ yathāsthitam  || 20.81 ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate candradvīpāvatāro nāma viṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************



śrībhairava uvāca

lakṣācāramanorūpāḥ śaktayo vīryasaṃsthitāḥ  |
rudraśaktisamāveśāstābhirātmanibṛṃhaṇam  || 21.1 ||

śivacaitanyayogena śakticaitanyabṛṃhaṇam  |
śakticaitanyayogena jīvacaitanyabṛṃhaṇam  || 21.2 ||

jīvacaitanyayogena mantracaitanyabṛṃhaṇam  |
mantracaitanyayogena piṇḍacaitanyabṛṃhaṇam  || 21.3 ||

piṇḍacaitanyayogena bāhyācārasya bṛṃhaṇam  |
caitanyena vinā sarvamasvatantraṃ śilādivat || 21.4 ||

niṣkriyaṃ cetanāhīnaṃ mṛtadehopamaṃ priye  |
agnicaitanyayogena jalamapyatra dāhakam  || 21.5 ||

tasmātsarvaprayatnena vidyāvīryacidātmakam  |
dharāmaṇḍalagarbhe tu dvīpadeśāntaraṃ yajet || 21.6 ||

dvādaśaivātra yoginyo dvādaśāre prapūjayet |
karṇikāyāṃ yajeddevaṃ śabdarāśiṃ salakṣaṇam  || 21.7 ||

kumārī siṃhaladvīpaṃ suvarṇaṃ ca tṛtīyakam  |
karṇaprāvaraṇaṃ cānyaṃ svāmukhaṃ deśamuttamam  || 21.8 ||

kulūtamoḍḍiyānaṃ ca ebhirdeśairyajetsudhīḥ  |
jālandharaṃ ca vikhyātamekapādaṃ tathāparam  || 21.9 ||

pārasaukulavikhyātaṃ kuśadvīpaṃ ca śālmalī  |
parṇadvīpaṃ kumārākhyaṃ yavadvīpaṃ tathāparam  || 21.10 ||

deśaṃ tu kāmarūpākhyaṃ puṣkaradvīpameva ca  |
aparaṃ kaṭāhadvīpaṃ cīnadeśamataḥ param  || 21.11 ||

candradvīpaṃ janadvīpaṃ ratnadvīpaṃ suśobhanam  |
rasadvīpaṃ ca gomedaṃ garbhodaṃ sūryadvīpakam  || 21.12 ||

āsavadvīpaṃ vikhyātaṃ marudeśasamanvitam  |
vasantaṃ mahāsaradvīpamamṛtadvīpameva ca  || 21.13 ||

dvīpamānandagandharvau agnidvīpaṃ mahāvanam  |
aṅgāradvīpaparyantaṃ nagnadvīpāvasānagam  || 21.14 ||

eṣāṃ dvīpādhipānāṃ ca nāmaṃ vakṣyāmi tacchṛṇu  |
kṣetrapālā mahāraudrā rakṣāṃ kurvanti sādhake  || 21.15 ||

vidrumo gastinaścaṇḍastathā yakṣo gaṇādhipaḥ  |
mahābhṛgurjayo nāma mahājihvastu vikramaḥ  || 21.16 ||
dhvāṅkṣaśca jayabhadraśca pīṭhe pīṭhe samāsate  |
mahādivyo dadhīciśca kumārīśastathāparaḥ  || 21.17 ||

mahādhaṃṣṭraḥ karālīśaḥ śrutīdharo nigadyate  |
mahādhvāṅkṣo mahānandī sugandhī ca gopālakaḥ  || 21.18 ||

puṣpadanto dhanāḍhyaśca vipulo nandakārakaḥ  |
śukro viḍālo dvāvetau śukāruṇaśubhānanau  || 21.19 ||

ratipriyasurapriyau dvau citrāṅgasudurjayau  |
rasano viḍālaḥ pradyumnaḥ kṣetrapālāḥ kulakrame  || 21.20 ||

manoharā rūpiṇī devī citrā citrarathā tathā  |
citrāṅgī citrarekhā ca vicitrā citranā śubhā  || 21.21 ||

citrākṣī citrarūpā ca subhadrā kāmadā śubhā  |
kakārasya imā devyaḥ kanyadvīpādhikariṇī[ḥ]  || 21.22 ||

kṣetrapālo mahāviṣṇuścakrahasto mahābalaḥ  |
krūrā ca piṅgalā caiva khaḍgikā lampaṭā satī  || 21.23 ||

daṃṣṭrālī rākṣasī dhvāṅkṣī lolupā lohitāmukhī  |
bahvāśī ca virūpā ca lampaṭā āmiṣapriyā  || 21.24 ||

khakārasya imā devyaḥ siṃhaladvīpamāśritāḥ  |
kṣetrapālo mahāyogī agastiśca maharṣiḥ  || 21.25 ||

suprakīrṇā prakīrṇā ca lambā lambamukhī tathā  |
lamboṣṭhī dīrghadaṃṣṭrā ca lambajā prāṇahāmukhī  || 21.26 ||

gajakarṇā sukarṇā ca mahākālī subhīṣaṇā  |
vātavegā ravā ghorā gakāre devatāḥ sthitāḥ  || 21.27 ||

svarṇadvīpādhikāriṇyaścaṇḍanātho mahābalaḥ  |
ghanaravā ghoraghoṣā mahāghoṣātighoṣikā  || 21.28 ||

ghaṇṭā ghaṇṭeśvarī ghorā mahāghaṇṭā sughaṇṭikā  |
atighaṇṭātighorā ca kalakalāraveti ca  || 21.29 ||
ghakāre devatā hyetāḥ karṇāprāvṛtamaṇḍale  |
yakṣarājā mahādevaḥ kṣetrapālo mahābalaḥ  || 21.30 ||

vibhūtirbhogadā kāntiḥ khaḍginī padminī tathā  |
gāndhāri yogamātā ca sudhārā paramojjvalā  || 21.31 ||

sehārī māṃsahārī ca prāṇahārī balāpahā  |
ṛcchikā gṛdhratuṇḍī ca revatī raṅgisaṃjñikā  || 21.32 ||

ṅakāre devatā hyetāḥ svāmukhe maṇḍale sthitāḥ  |
rājyaṃ pālayate deśe kavarge uttarāpathe  || 21.33 ||

kṣetrapālo gaṇapati rakṣāṃ kurvanti sādhake  |
caṇḍā caṇḍamukhī caṇḍā caṇḍavegā mahāravā  || 21.34 ||

bhṛkuṭī caṇḍavīryā ca caṇḍabhrū caṇḍanāyikā  |
cañcalā calavegā ca calajihvā caleśvarī  || 21.35 ||

cakāre devatā hyetāḥ kṣetrapālo mahājayaḥ  |
kulūtadeśavāsinyo rakṣāṃ kurvanti sādhake  || 21.36 ||

kālarātrī ca vetālī kaṅkālī ca karaṅkiṇī  |
kiṅkiṇī caṇḍaghoṣā ca aṭṭahāsā mahāravā  || 21.37 ||

caṇḍamātaṅgī caṇḍālī sūkarī kukkuṭī tathā  |
gandhārī ḍombī campākṣī chakāre devatāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 21.38 ||

nāyikā oḍḍiyāne tu kṣetrapālo mahābhṛguḥ  |
jvalinī jvālinī caiva mahājvālāvatī prabhā  || 21.39 ||

tejā tejavatī vahniḥ sutejā nirmalojjvalā  |
jvālāvatī karālī ca visphuliṅgā śikhāśikhī  || 21.40 ||

jakāre devatā rājñaḥ sarvasattvavaśaṅkarī[ḥ]  |
jālandhare ca deveśe kṣetrapālo mahājihvaḥ  || 21.41 ||

subhadrā bhīmabhadrā ca bhadrā caiva śubhānanā  |
bhīmā bhīmavatī kāntī kaṅkālī ca karālinī  || 21.42 ||
bhadrakālī sukālī ca vikaṭā kaṅkaṭeti ca  |
cārvākī lampaṭī caiva jhakāre devatāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 21.43 ||

maṇḍale ekapāde tu mahāmāyā balotkaṭāḥ  |
citraseno mahāvīraḥ kṣetrapālo mahābhayaḥ  || 21.44 ||

subhaṭodbhaṭā vikaṭā kuṭilā caiva kaṅkaṭā  |
vīramātā suvīrā ca khaḍginī śūlinī kharā  || 21.45 ||

chucchundarī viḍālī ca ñakāre devatāgaṇāḥ  |
pārase tu mahādevyo adhikāraṃ prakurvate  || 21.46 ||

dhvāṅkṣo nāmeti vikhyātaḥ kṣetrapālo bhayānakaḥ  |
rājā dakṣiṇadeśe tu cavarge ca kramīśvarī  || 21.47 ||

mṛgā ca śaśirekhā ca hariṇī rohiṇī tathā  |
amṛtodbhavā parṇajīvī jīvarakṣā sujīvikā  || 21.48 ||

hariṇākṣī sujīvā ca candrodayāmṛtodbhavā  |
ṭakāre devatā hyetāḥ kuśadvīpe vyavasthitāḥ  || 21.49 ||

kṣetrapālo jayabhadraḥ kuśadvīpaprapālakaḥ  |
vyomanī vyomarūpā ca vyomavyāpī śubhodayā  || 21.50 ||

grahacārī sucārī ca viṣahārī viṣāntikā  |
jṛmbhodyānā ca phetkārī devakī durjayā mahā  || 21.51 ||

ṭhakāre devatāḥ pūjyāḥ śālmalīdvīpamāśritāḥ  |
kṣetrapālo mahādivyaḥ kapālahasto mahābalaḥ  || 21.52 ||

cañcalā capalā caṇḍā ḍamarī ḍāmarī śubhā  |
ḍiṇḍinī muṇḍinī muṇḍā śākinī ḍākinīti ca  || 21.53 ||

kartanī kākinī devī haṭṭakī ḍākinī mahā  |
ḍakāre devatā hyetāścīnadvīpe vyavasthitāḥ  || 21.54 ||

dadhīciḥ kṣetrapālastu tatra deśe prapūjayet |
yamadaṃṣṭrā mahādaṃṣṭrā antramālā karālikā  || 21.55 ||
vikarālā karālinyā tālajaṅghā sujaṅghikā  |
lohajaṅghātijaṅghā ca mahāvegātivegagā  || 21.56 ||

vajraśaṅkhī naṭī caiva balā caiva tathāparā  |
ḍhakāre devatā nāma kumārīdvīpamāśritāḥ  || 21.57 ||

kṣetrapālaḥ kumārīśo rakṣapālastathaiva ca  |
balā cātibalā caiva ajitā cāparājitā  || 21.58 ||

jayā ca vijayā devī jṛmbhanī stambhanī tathā  |
andhanī mohanī māyā nigaḍā kīlanī tathā  || 21.59 ||

yavadvīpe sthitā devya adhikāraṃ prakurvate  |
mahādaṃṣṭrastu vikhyātaḥ kṣetrapālo mahābalaḥ  || 21.60 ||

ṇakāre devatā hyetāḥ kāmarūpanivāsitāḥ  |
danturā raudrabhāṣā ca abhramāla kulāsubhā  || 21.61 ||

calajihvāgraṇetrā ca ruru[r] hūṃkārikā tathā  |
khādakā rūpanāma ca saṃhārī ca kṣayāntikā  || 21.62 ||

kaṇḍanī peṣaṇī caiva mahāgrāsī kṛtāntikā  |
takāre devatāḥ khyātāḥ puṣkaradvīpamāśritāḥ  || 21.63 ||

nāyakā devatā nāma kṣetrapālaḥ śrutīdharaḥ  |
ḍambhakī ḍimbhiḍimbhā ca kaivartarajalehikā  || 21.64 ||

dravaṇī drāvaṇī kṣobhā plavanī plāvanīti ca  |
madotkaṭā madakṣobhā madavāhā mahābalā  || 21.65 ||

kāmasandīpanī devī atirūpā manoharā  |
thakāre devatā nāma saṃsthitā[ḥ ] paratīrake  || 21.66 ||

kṣetrapālo mahādhvāṅkṣaḥ khaḍgahasto mahābalaḥ  |
aruṇā ghoṣadevī ca revatī ghoradāyikā  || 21.67 ||

stambhanī ghorarakṣā ca ghorarūpā ca ghoriṇī  |
ghoraghoratarāghorā ghorā vikaṭanāyikā  || 21.68 ||
(em.; ghorā ghoratarāghorā'tighorā vikaṭanāyikā Ed.)

vānarī kroṣṭakī caiva surāsavamadhupriyā  |
dakāre devatā rājāścīnadeśe suvāsitāḥ  || 21.69 ||

kṣetrapālo mahānandī śūlahasto mahābalaḥ  |
bhīmarāvā surāvā ca saṃstārī savarākṣikā  || 21.70 ||

stambhanī roṣaṇī raudrā rudravatyā chalāpahā  |
mahāśaktiḥ kṣāntiśīlā vajratuṇḍī vṛkodarī  || 21.71 ||

dhakāre devatā hyetāḥ pūjanīyāḥ sadā budhaiḥ  |
kṣetrapālaḥ sugandhī ca gandharvo vīṇahastakaḥ  || 21.72 ||

candradvīpe suvāsinyo ārtānāmārtināśanī[ḥ]  |
kalanī kṛntanī kālī kālasaṃvartanī kalā  || 21.73 ||

anteṣṭhī ca pratiṣṭhā ca śāntipuṣṭikarī tathā  |
jayā dhṛtikarī saumyā kāmadā śubhadānanā  || 21.74 ||

sutejā kāmamatikā nakāre devatāḥ śubhāḥ  |
janadvīparatā nityaṃ sādhakānāṃ tu vatsalāḥ  || 21.75 ||

kṣetrapālastu gopālo dharmajñaḥ satyavādinaḥ  |
dharmā dharmavatī śīlā pāpahā dharmavardhanī  || 21.76 ||

dharmarakṣitavārtā ca dharmādharmavatīti ca  |
dharmakartā dharmapriyā dharmasandīpanīti ca  || 21.77 ||

pakāre devatā rājā ratnadvīpārṇave sthitāḥ  |
kṣetrapālo mahākāyastasmin deśe' dhipo mahān  || 21.78 ||

sumatirdurmatirmedhā vimalā manavikāśinī  |
śuddhirbuddhirmatiḥ kāntirbalotsāhanavardhanī  || 21.79 ||

balā cātibalā caiva prāṇavṛddhikarī parā  |
nirlepā nirghṛṇā māyā sarvapāpakṣayaṅkarī  || 21.80 ||

phakāre devatā rājā saradvīpe suvāsinaḥ  |
puṣpadantastu vikhyātaḥ kṣetrapālo mahābalaḥ  || 21.81 ||

raktā caiva viraktā ca udvegā śokavardhanī  |
kāmatṛṣṇā kṣudhā mohā nidrālasabhayā jarā  || 21.82 ||

sukṛṣṇā rodanī kuṣmā malāṅgī śiśunāśanī  |
bakāre devatā rājā hyetā gomedamaṇḍale  || 21.83 ||

dhanado nāma vikhyātaḥ kṣetrapālo mahāyaśaḥ  |
tṛṣṇā ca kāmadā bhogā nirduḥkhā sukhadā tathā  || 21.84 ||

ānandā ca sunandā ca mahānandā śubhaṅkarī  |
vītarāgā mahotsāhā jitarāgā manoramā  || 21.85 ||

bhakāre devatā hyetā madhye garbhodamaṇḍale  |
vipulo nāma vikhyātaḥ kṣetrapālo mahābalaḥ  || 21.86 ||

manonmanī manaḥkṣobhā madonmattā madākulā  |
madā gajamadā nāma kāmānandasuvihvalā  || 21.87 ||

mahāvegā suvegā ca mahāvegā kṣaṇāpahā  |
kramaṇī caiva nāmā ca krāmaṇī ca tathāparā  || 21.88 ||

sūryadvīpe mahāyogī[ḥ] sarvāḥ kanakapiṅgalāḥ  |
makārasya imā devyo rājā adhipatirmahān  || 21.89 ||

ānando nāma vikhyātaḥ kṣetrapālaḥ sadā sthitaḥ  |
hayavegā suvegā ca ativegavatī mahā  || 21.90 ||

cakravegā viruddhā ca calacittavatī matī  |
rodanī kṣodanī bālā'tiroṣā kalahapriyā  || 21.91 ||

vidrutā trāsanī devī manovegā ca cañcalā  |
yakāre devatā rājā āsavadvīpasaṃsthitāḥ  || 21.92 ||

śukro nāmeti vikhyātaḥ kṣetrapālo mahābalaḥ  |
vidyujjihvā mahājihvā śṛṅgāṭā kuṭilā sphuṭā  || 21.93 ||
jvālā caiva sujvālā ca mahājvālā tathaiva ca  |
jvālāvatī visphuliṅgā jvālābhasmakṣayāntakā  || 21.94 ||

rakāramadhyagā devyo marudeśādhipo mahān  |
viḍālaḥ kṣetrapālaśca mahābalaparākramaḥ  || 21.95 ||

ullekhā ca patākā ca bhogā bhogavatī mahā  |
mahābhogātibhogā ca bhogāḍhyā bhogapāragā  || 21.96 ||

ṛddhirvṛddhirdhṛtiḥ kāntirlakāre devatāḥ śubhāḥ  |
vasantadvīpavāsinyaḥ kṣetrapālaśca kāruṇī  || 21.97 ||

variṣṭhā ca parā divyā amṛtā tu phalāśinī  |
hariṇākṣī suvarṇā ca kanakareṇupiñjarā  || 21.98 ||

ratnā ca ratnadvīpā ca sudvīpā ratnamālinī  |
ratnaśobhā mahāśobhā romaśobhā parādyutiḥ  || 21.99 ||

vakāre devatā hyetāḥ saradvīpādhivāsitā[ḥ]  |
kṣetrapālastu vikhyātaḥ śubhānano balotkaṭaḥ  || 21.100 ||

savarī barbarī gṛdhrī ghaṇṭakarṇā kharānanā  |
hayagrīvā ca jaṅghā ca sarvagrāsā kṛtāntakā  || 21.101 ||

sarvāśī ca mahābhakṣā mahādaṃṣṭrātirauravā  |
śakāre devatā nāma kathitāśca mahāyaśāḥ  || 21.102 ||

amṛtāsavadvīpe ca kṣetrapālo ratipriyaḥ  |
rāgā rāgavatī krodhā mahābhogā ca rauravā  || 21.103 ||

kruddhanī roṣaṇī kalahā kalakālī kalāntikā  |
durbhedyā durbhaṭā caiva dumirīkṣā subhīṣaṇā  || 21.104 ||

yamāntakā kalī nāma ṣakāre devatāḥ śubhāḥ  |
ānandadvīpavāsinyo devyo akṣayayauvanāḥ  || 21.105 ||

devaśca kṣetrapālo'tra surāsavavarapriyaḥ  |
naṭī nāṭī kunāṭī ca vāṭakī hāṭakī viṭī  || 21.106 ||
kaṅkaṭā vikaṭā caiva subhaṭā ca bhaṭodbhavā  |
sakāre devatā nāma gāndharvadvīpavāsinaḥ  || 21.107 ||

vīṇāvaṃśaratā devī nodaṃ gandharvakinnaraiḥ  |
citrāṅgaḥ kṣetrapālaśca merūrdhvavalaye sthitaḥ  || 21.108 ||

nādākṣī nādarūpā ca sarvākārī gamāgamā  |
antacārī sucārī ca ūrdhvanādī suvāhinī  || 21.109 ||

saṃyogā ca viyogā ca haṃsākhyā ca visālinī  |
aṅgāradvīpavāsinyo hakārākṣarasambhavāḥ  || 21.110 ||

viḍālo nāma vikhyātaḥ kṣetrapālo mahābalaḥ  |
sarvagrāsī kṛtāntī ca pavanī pāvanī tathā  || 21.111 ||

bhedanī chedanī caiva sarvakārī kṣudhāśanī  |
ucchuṣmā devagāndhārī bhasmāntā vaḍavānalā  || 21.112 ||

bahvāśī agnidvīpā ca kṣamā kṣemakarī parā  |
kṣakāre devatā hyetā nagnadvīparatāḥ priye  || 21.113 ||

pradyumnaḥ kṣetrapālaśca mahābalaparākramaḥ  || 21.114 ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate dvīpāmnāyo nāma ekaviṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************



śrīkubjikā uvāca

śrutā deva mahāvyāptiḥ samastavyastavistarāt |
idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi tvatsakāśānmanodbhavām  || 22.1 ||

aparāṃ dehajairbhinnāṃ śabdarāśiṃ sabhairavam  || 22.2 ||

śrībhairava uvāca

sādhu bhadre punaḥ sādhu mahāvastuprabodhake  |
tadahaṃ sampravakṣyāmi siddhakaulaṃ mahātape  || 22.3 ||
mahākalpāntasaṃhāre mahāpralayamadbhutam

sūryakoṭikarābhāsaṃ vidyucchaṭalatākulam  |
kālāgniśikharāṭopaṃ śūlajvālormisaṅkulam  || 22.4 ||

visphuliṅgajvalantaughairmahājvālārcivartulam  |
jvālāmālākulojjvālaiḥ karālairbhīmabhīṣaṇaiḥ  || 22.5 ||

svargapātālabhūrlokairantarīkṣairdiśo daśa  |
maccharīre samutpannā mahākrodhasamudbhavā  || 22.6 ||

akārādikṣakārāntā ekaikākṣarabheditā  |
bhairavāvalinī devī vilomena samuddharet || 22.7 ||

tadahaṃ sampravakṣyāmi śṛṇu kubji mahādhipe  |
kṣa ha sa āditaḥ kṛtvā akārāntena sampuṭam  || 22.8 ||


kṣa krodhāvali a ananta ajara bhairava
ha haṃsāvali ā mahānanda āpakumbha bhairava
sa ānandāvali i  pracaṇḍa iḍācāra bhairava
ṣa garbhāvali ī ḍāmara indramūrti bhairava
śa saṃhārāvali u udyāna ulkāsya bhairava
va amṛtāvali ū vasanta ūṣmāda bhairava
la vipulāvali ṛ ṛpudarpa ṛpusūdana bhairava
ra kālāntakāvali ṝ kṛtānta ṝmukta bhairava
ya pracaṇḍāvali ḻa prasanna ḻaptakāya bhairava
ma mahāmohāvali ḹ mahāmāyā ḹpāda  bhairava
bha bhūtabhayāvali e raudra ekadaṃṣṭra bhairava
ba vaḍavāmukhāvali ai mahādaṃṣṭra airāvata bhairava
pha lampaṭāvali o kaṅkāla oghāmbu bhairava
pa pavanāvali au anaṅga auṣadhīghna bhairava
na kusumāvali aṃ svacchanda aṃjana bhairava
dha vipulāvali aḥ manmatha aḥhvakāyabhairava
da suratāvali ka unmatta kambala bhairava
tha kūrmāvali kha meghanāda kharuṣānana bhairava
ta tvaritāvali ga karāla gomukha bhairava
ṇa mandarāvali gha vikarāla ghaṇṭāla bhairava
ḍha khaṭvāṅgāvali ṅa mahābala ṅaṇanāntabhairava
ḍa candrāvali ca asitāṅga caṇḍadhāra bhairava
ṭha vajrāvali cha ulka chaṭāṭopa bhairava
ṭa manthāvali ja ekapāda jaṭālākṣa bhairava
ña kāntāvali jha kapāla jhaṅkīśa bhairava
jha ambikāvali ña vyoma ñabhaṭeśvara bhairava
ja bhedakāvali ṭa vetāla ṭaṅkapāṇi bhairava
cha kṛtāntāvali ṭha āmardaka ṭhānubandha bhairava
ca caturbhujāvali ḍa mahāsāha ḍāmara bhairava
ṅa yogāvali ḍha ruru ḍhaḍhṛkarṇa bhairava
gha bhūtāvali ṇa bhuvana ṇatīkānta bhairava
ga abhayāvali ta vibhūti taḍidbhāsvara bhairava
kha carcakāvali tha ūrdhvasepha thavira bhairava
ka bhasmāntakāvali da krūra dantura bhairava
aḥ sṛṣṭikāvali dha lohita dhanada bhairava
aṃ vijayāvali na lampaṭa nāgakarṇa bhairava
au brahmāvali pa carcaka pracaṇḍa bhairava
o sadyojātāvali pha ....... phetkāra bhairava
ai phetkārāvali ba acintya vīrasiṃha bhairava
e karṇamoṭāvali bha mahādevāṅga bhṛkuṭi bhairava
ḹ mahāmohāvali ma mahākālāgni meghabhāsura bhairava
ḻa mahāmāyāvali ya mārtaṇḍa yugānta bhairava
ṝ gāndhārāvali ra prāṇa raurava bhairava
ṛ puṣpāvali la ananta lamboṣṭha bhairava
ū śabdāvali va ucchuṣma vasala bhairava
u mahāghoṣāvali śa mahāyaśa śukatuṇḍa bhairava
ī sūkṣmāvali ṣa mahodyāna ṣaṭālākṣa bhairava
vidyāvali sa amṛta sunāsa bhairava
ā vyāpakāvali ha śubhaṅkara hūhūka bhairava
a mahāmāyāvali kṣa pralayāntaka kṣayānta bhairava || 22.8 ||


eṣa krodho mahādevi mama hṛdayaniḥsṛtaḥ  |
pañcāśabhairavopetā mālinyā saha saṃyutā  || 22.9 ||

anena nyāsamātreṇa sakalīkṛtavigrahaḥ  |
cintayeddehamātmānamuktalakṣaṇasaṃyutam  || 22.10 ||

saṃharantaṃ jagatsarvaṃ chindantaṃ pāśapañjaram  |
nirdahantaṃ jagatsarvaṃ bhasmakūṭaṃ vicintayet || 22.11 ||

akārādikṣakārāntaṃ paśudehe vicintayet |
adhomukhordhvapādāntaṃ saṃhārānalamadhyagam  || 22.12 ||

saṃvartānaladahyantaṃ caturdaśaviyojitam  |
tadbījaṃ sampravakṣyāmi mahākrodhasya bhairavi  || 22.13 ||

mantrasāraṃ varārohe śṛṇu tvaṃ bhāvitātmanā  |

oṃ hūṃ kṣaḥ hūṃ phaṭhrīṃ chrīṃ kṣaḥ ślīṃ phreṃ hrūṃ hūṃ hūṃ phaṭ || 22.14ā ||

krodhahṛdayānujā devī mahābhairavamabravīt || 22.14 ||

kujākhyamantramuccārya trailokyamapi saṃharet |
kailāsamapi pātayedvācāsiddhiḥ pravartate  || 22.15 ||

ekaikākṣarasambhinnāmuddharāmi parāparām  |
akārādikṣakārāntaṃ nāmaṃ vakṣyāmi pārvati  || 22.16 ||
22.16ā a anantāvali kṣa gaganavīra
ā pralayāntakāvali ha bhuvanavīra
anantaśaktyāvali sa vijayavīra
ī haṃsāvali ṣa ajayavīra
u mohāvali śa mahājayavīra
ū kriyāśaktyāvali va aṅkuravīra
ṛ bṛhodarāvali la saṃhāravīra
ṝ ripumardakāvali ra kumāravīra
ḻa vidyāśaktyāvali ya mahāyaśavīra
ḹ icchāvali ma mahāsāhasavīra
e ratnāvali bha pracaṇḍavīra
ai vijñānaśaktyāvali ba mahārṇavavīra
o lohitāvali pha mahāmaravīra
au ulkāvali pa mahādambhakavīra
aṃ lolupāvali na carcikavīra
aḥ barbarāvali dha pramathavīra
ka pavanāvali da kanakavīra
kha lampaṭāvali tha kharodakavīra
ga mātāvali ta garuḍavīra
gha raudrāvali ṇa meghanādavīra
ṅa sarvabhakṣāvali ḍha mahāgarjanavīra
ca jyeṣṭhāvali ḍa carvakavīra
cha ambikāvali ṭha chedakavīra
ja vedāvali ṭa triśikhavīra
jha krodhāvali ña mārīcivīra
ña brahmaghoṣāvali jha vidhānavīra
ṭa sarvavīrāvali ja vikaṭavīra
ṭha vajrakāvali cha vasantakavīra
ḍa kanyāvali ca abhayavīra
ḍha prasannāvali ṅa vipakṣavīra
ṇa mahākrodhāvali gha mahābalivīra
ta ḍāmarāvali ga kaṭaṅkaṭavīra
tha mahādaṃṣṭrāvali kha ḍamarukavīra
da ādhārāvali ka dharmavīra
dha āsannāvali aḥ mahātibalavīra
na ujjvalāvali aṃ sarvavīra
pa sāgarāvali au mahākāyavīra
pha tribhuvanāvali o varalāmukhavīra
ba valayāvali ai bhasmāntakavīra
bha nidrāvali e durjayavīra
ma sandhyāvali ḹ mahāvetālavīra
ya candravīṇāvali ḻa mahārauravavīra
ra manmathāvali ṝ mahādurdharavīra
la vyomāvali ṛ mahābhogavīra
va ūṣmāvali ū vajravīra
śa sitāvali u kālāgnivīra
ṣa mahāmāyāvali ī sarvalokavīra
sa sarvadevatāvali i mahānādavīra
ha yogāvali ā parāparavīra
kṣa anantākhyāvali a mahākṣayāntavīra

athāparā parā khyātā mahāmāyā parāparā  |
mantragarbhā mahādevī viśrutā bhuvanatraye  || 22.17 ||

yryauṃ rryauṃ lryauṃ vryauṃ śryauṃ ṣryauṃ sryauṃ hryauṃ kṣryauṃ kāmākhyasya hsryauṃ pūrṇagiri smryaumoḍḍiyāna kṣmryauṃ jālandhara  |
śrī hā ka ḍo ka śrī kāmarivāḍī śrī devavāḍī śrīṃ hrīmamṛtavidye putraṃ dehi āyuṃ dehi yaśaṃ dehi  || 22.18 ||


śrīkubjikā uvāca

pramādāllopamāyāte siddhe samayamaṇḍale  |
sādhakasya bhavedglāniḥ kliṣṭo vighnaiḥ prabādhyate  || 22.18 ||

kā gatistasya deveśa kathaṃ śuddhimavāpnuyāt |
tamācakṣasva sarvajña samayaghnaḥ śudhyate yathā  || 22.19 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

aṭṭahāsāditaḥ kṛtvā rājagṛhamapaścimam  |
āyudhaiḥ sahitāṃ devīṃ kṣetrapālasamanvitām  || 22.20 ||

kṣetropakṣetrasandohaiḥ sevanānnirmalo bhavet |
athāśaktaḥ pramādī vā pīṭhasaṅkīrtanātpriye  || 22.21 ||

samyakśuddhimavāpnoti prātarutthāya yaḥ paṭhet |
tadahaṃ sampravakṣyāmi samayānāṃ viśuddhaye  || 22.22 ||

aṭṭahāse kadambasthāṃ saumyāsyāṃ vajradhāriṇīm  |
mahāghaṇṭasamopetāṃ praṇamāmi sivaṅkarīm  || 22.23 ||

caritrāyāṃ karañjasthāṃ kṛṣṇākhyāṃ śaktidhāriṇīm  |
mahābalasamopetāṃ praṇamāmi susiddhidām  || 22.24 ||

agnikena samopetāṃ daṇḍahastāṃ nagaukasām  |
kolāgirye mahālakṣmīṃ naumi lakṣmīvivardhanīm  || 22.25 ||

jvālāmukhīṃ śrījayantyāṃ nimbasthāṃ khaḍgadhāriṇīm  |
mahāpretasamopetāṃ naumi sarvārthasiddhidām  || 22.26 ||

aśvatthasthāṃ mahāmāyāmujjainyāṃ pāśadhāriṇīm  |
mahākālasamopetāṃ naumi sarvārthasiddhidām  || 22.27 ||
uḍumbaratalāvasthāṃ vāyuvegāṃ dhvajāyudhām  |
prayāge pavanopetāṃ naumi śatruvināśanīm  || 22.28 ||

vārāṇasyāṃ tu tālasthāmūrdhvakeśīṃ gadāyudhām  |
praṇamya śirasā devīṃ śāṅkarīṃ śāṅkarānvitām  || 22.29 ||

karṇamoṭīṃ vaṭasthāṃ tu saśūlāṃ hetukānvitām  |
śrīkoṭe śrīpadāṃ naumi rājyasampadadāyinīm  || 22.30 ||

virajāyāmbikadevīṃ mudrāpaṭṭiśadhāriṇīm  |
analena samopetāṃ praṇamāmi jayāvahām  || 22.31 ||

airuḍyāmagnivaktrāṃ tu vajraśaktidharāṃ śubhām  |
ghaṇṭāravasamopetāṃ namāmi ripunāśanīm  || 22.32 ||

muṣalāyudhahastāṃ tu mahājaṅghasamanvitām  |
namāmi śatrubhaṅgārthe piṅgākṣīṃ hastināpure  || 22.33 ||

elāpure kharāsyāṃ tu pāśahastāṃ mahābalām  |
gajakarṇasamopetāṃ naumi duṣṭapramardanīm  || 22.34 ||

kāśmaryāṃ caiva gokarṇāṃ mudrālakuṭadhāriṇīm  |
taḍijjaṅghasamopetāṃ namāmi ripumardanīm  || 22.35 ||

karālena samopetāṃ namāmyaṅkuśadhāriṇīm  |
kramaṇīṃ marudeśe tu trailokyākṛṣṭikārikām  || 22.36 ||

romajaṅghasamopetāṃ nagare tu halāyudhām  |
caitrakacchanivāsāṃ tu namāmi dhanasiddhaye  || 22.37 ||

kumbhakena samopetāṃ khaṭvāṅgakarabhūṣitām  |
namāmi pāpaśuddhyarthaṃ cāmuṇḍāṃ puṇḍravardhane  || 22.38 ||

parastīre prasannāsyāṃ vajraśṛṅkhaladhāriṇīm  |
namāmi trijaṭopetāṃ bhedastambhanakārikām  || 22.39 ||

pṛṣṭhāpure vidyunmukhīṃ daṇḍaśaktyāyudhodyatām  |
namāmi ghanaravopetāṃ bhedajṛmbhanakārikām  || 22.40 ||
ulkāmukhasamopetāṃ kuhudyāṃ tu mahābalām  |
mudrālakuṭadhāriṇyāṃ naumi duṣṭāṅgabhañjanīm  || 22.41 ||

piśitāśasamopetāṃ naumi kaṭṭārikodyatām  |
sopāre agnivaktrāṃ tu amitrapaśudāriṇīm  || 22.42 ||

kṣīrike lokamātāṃ tu khaḍgahastāṃ namāmyaham  |
mahāmerusamopetāṃ mahatārtinikṛntanīm  || 22.43 ||

vajrāyudhadharāṃ saumyāṃ bhīmānanasamanvitām  |
stambhākṛṣṭikarīṃ devīṃ māyāpuryāṃ tu kampinīm  || 22.44 ||

mahākrodhasamopetāṃ pūtanāmrātikeśvare  |
gadāhastāyudhāṃ naumi tāḍanākṛṣṭikārikām  || 22.45 ||

rājagṛhe bhagnanāsāṃ mahākarṇasamanvitām  |
vajraśaktidharāṃ naumi aśeṣaphaladāyikām  || 22.46 ||

kṣetropakṣetrasandohe sthitabhūcakramātarām  |
kṣetrapālasamopetāṃ kīrtayedyaḥ samāhitaḥ  || 22.47 ||

prātarutthāya mantrajñaḥ svapnakāle'thavā sudhīḥ  |
yukto'pi pātakairghorairmātṝṇāṃ sammato bhavet || 22.48 ||

mātṛhā pitṛhā caiva brahmaghna goghna eva ca  |
vīradravyāpahārī ca pramādātsamayacyutaḥ  || 22.49 ||

mantrācāravilupto'pi pīṭhasaṅkīrtanātpriye  |
pāpakañcukamutsṛjya naiva paśyati durgatim  || 22.50 ||

yaḥ punaḥ śuddhabhāvātmā triṣkālaṃ parivartayet |
prāpnoti cintitān kāmān strīṇāṃ bhavati vallabhaḥ  || 22.51 ||

kuṇḍe'tha maṇḍale vātha pratimāyāṃ paṭe'pi vā  |
liṅge dakṣiṇamūrtau vā jalamadhye gato'pi vā  || 22.52 ||

triṣkālamekakālaṃ vā yaḥ paṭhedyastu bhāvitaḥ  |
viṣaśastraja1āgnibhyo vyādhibhūtagrahairapi  || 22.53 ||
ajitaḥ suciraṃ kālaṃ jāyate nirupadravaḥ  |
mahābhaye samutpanne kapilāgomayena tu  || 22.54 ||

caturdikṣu caturviṃśa kārayenmaṇḍalāni tu  |
pūrvamuttarataścaiva vāruṇyāṃ dakṣiṇena tu  || 22.55 ||

ṣaṭkaṃ ṣaṭkaṃ tu kartavyaṃ tatra pūjya krameṇa tu  |
śmaśānakalpavṛkṣe tu yoginyaḥ kṣetrapāstathā  || 22.56 ||

pūrve tu śvetapuṣpaistu dakṣiṇe pītapuṣpakaiḥ  |
paścime raktapuṣpaistu uttare kṛṣṇapuṣpakaiḥ  || 22.57 ||

sāyudhān śvetapuṣpaistu gandhairdhūpairmanoramaiḥ  |
madhye tu kalaśaṃ sthāpya divyatoyapariplutam  || 22.58 ||

caturviṃśati dīpāṃśca sthāne sthāne pradāpayet |
caturviṃśati pīṭhāṃśca krameṇa parivartayet || 22.59 ||

ahorātroṣito bhūtvā niśāmekāṃ suyantritaḥ  |
prabhāte vimale mantrī vīrabhojyaṃ tu kārayet || 22.60 ||

rājagṛhe bhagnanāsāṃ mahākarṇasamanvitām  |
vajraśaktidharāṃ naumi aśeṣaphaladāyikām  || 22.46 ||

kṣetropakṣetrasandohe sthitabhūcakramātarām  |
kṣetrapālasamopetāṃ kīrtayedyaḥ samāhitaḥ  || 22.47 ||

prātarutthāya mantrajñaḥ svapnakāle'thavā sudhīḥ  |
yukto'pi pātakairghorairmātṝṇāṃ sammato bhavet || 22.48 ||

mātṛhā pitṛhā caiva brahmaghna goghna eva ca  |
vīradravyāpahārī ca pramādātsamayacyutaḥ  || 22.49 ||

mantrācāravilupto'pi pīṭhasaṅkīrtanātpriye  |
pāpakañcukamutsṛjya naiva paśyati durgatim  || 22.50 ||

yaḥ punaḥ śuddhabhāvātmā triṣkālaṃ parivartayet |
prāpnoti cintitān kāmān strīṇāṃ bhavati vallabhaḥ  || 22.51 ||
kuṇḍe'tha maṇḍale vātha pratimāyāṃ paṭe'pi vā  |
liṅge dakṣiṇamūrtau vā jalamadhye gato'pi vā  || 22.52 ||

triṣkālamekakālaṃ vā yaḥ paṭhedyastu bhāvitaḥ  |
viṣaśastraja1āgnibhyo vyādhibhūtagrahairapi  || 22.53 ||

ajitaḥ suciraṃ kālaṃ jāyate nirupadravaḥ  |
mahābhaye samutpanne kapilāgomayena tu  || 22.54 ||

caturdikṣu caturviṃśa kārayenmaṇḍalāni tu  |
pūrvamuttarataścaiva vāruṇyāṃ dakṣiṇena tu  || 22.55 ||

ṣaṭkaṃ ṣaṭkaṃ tu kartavyaṃ tatra pūjya krameṇa tu  |
śmaśānakalpavṛkṣe tu yoginyaḥ kṣetrapāstathā  || 22.56 ||

pūrve tu śvetapuṣpaistu dakṣiṇe pītapuṣpakaiḥ  |
paścime raktapuṣpaistu uttare kṛṣṇapuṣpakaiḥ  || 22.57 ||

sāyudhān śvetapuṣpaistu gandhairdhūpairmanoramaiḥ  |
madhye tu kalaśaṃ sthāpya divyatoyapariplutam  || 22.58 ||

caturviṃśati dīpāṃśca sthāne sthāne pradāpayet |
caturviṃśati pīṭhāṃśca krameṇa parivartayet || 22.59 ||

ahorātroṣito bhūtvā niśāmekāṃ suyantritaḥ  |
prabhāte vimale mantrī vīrabhojyaṃ tu kārayet || 22.60 ||

tataḥ kṣamāpayetpīṭhān praṇipatya punaḥ punaḥ  |
nirvighnastu tato mantrī kṣipraṃ bhavati siddhibhāk || 22.61 ||

upasargagrahādibhyaḥ kṣayakuṣṭhajvarādibhiḥ  |
mucyate sarvarogaiśca dhanavānapi jāyate  || 22.62 ||

kanyā manepsitān kāmān labhate cābhiṣekataḥ  |
putrārthī labhate putrān kāmukaḥ subhago bhavet || 22.63 ||

vidyārthī labhate vidyāṃ vaṇigvai lābhamaśnute  |
mantrārādhanaśīlaśca jāyate nirupadravaḥ  || 22.64 ||
yogābhyāsarato nityaṃ prāpya siddhiṃ paraṃ yayau  |
dvīpāmnāyaprasaṅgena sarvametatprakāśitam  || 22.65 ||

samastavyastavyāptistu kṣetropakṣetrasaṃyutam  |
yattvayā pṛcchitaṃ sarvaṃ kālajñānaṃ kujeśvari  || 22.66 ||

tadahaṃ sampravakṣyāmi bhaktānāṃ bhaktivatsale  |
sarvaṃ sampāditaṃ tubhyamājñānandakramārṇavam  || 22.67 ||

idānīṃ śṛṇu kalyāṇi kālacakraṃ yathā sthitam  || 22.68 ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate samastavyastavyāptirnāma dvāviṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************



śrībhairava uvāca

kālacakraṃ varārohe ātmanaśca parasya ca  |
jñātvā vyapohayetkālamutkrāntiṃ vā salakṣaṇam  || 23.1 ||

yena jānāti deveśi sādhako niścayātmakaḥ  |
śṛṇu tvaṃ paramānandaṃ sugopyaṃ prakaṭāmi te  || 23.2 ||

kālaṃ tu trividhaṃ proktaṃ paraṃ caiva parāparam  |
aparaṃ tu kalādhāraṃ kālasya vaśasaṃsthitam  || 23.3 ||

truṭilavātparaḥ kālaḥ kālonmeṣātparāparaḥ  |
manvantarādikāṣṭhādau sa kālaḥ kalate tanum  || 23.4 ||

kalpe parāpare kāle sthitvā kālasya vañcanam  |
parātparatare kāle sthitau kālasya kālakṛt || 23.5 ||

iti matvā paraṃ kālaṃ mānabhūtaṃ gurormukhāt |
tataḥ kurvanti sarvatra jñānakrīḍāmaśaṅkitām  || 23.6 ||

paraṃ tu ṣaṇṇavatyordhvaṃ ṣaṇṇavatyā parāparam  |
caturāśītipramāṇena aparaṃ kalanātmakam  || 23.7 ||

pañcakena nibaddhaṃ tu pañcatvaṃ yāti śīghrataḥ  |
nandābhadrādiyogena jayāriktādipaurṇimā  || 23.8 ||

pañca pañca tathā pañca māsartvayanāvadhim  |
saṃvatsaraṃ yugaṃ ceti manvantarasakalpakam  || 23.9 ||

evaṃ kalanti taṃ kālaṃ pañcapañcāntakāvadhim  |
kālāvadhisthitān dvīpān dvīpaiḥ pīṭhān vidurbudhāḥ  || 23.10 ||

pīṭhāntasthāni tattvāni pañca bhūtāni teṣu vai  |
ekaikaṃ pañcakāvṛttaṃ pañcaviṃśāntakāvidhim  || 23.11 ||

svarapañcakayuktāni saṃsaranti kalārṇave  |
padmanāḍīnibaddhetāḥ kālayantropariṣṭhitāḥ  || 23.12 ||

jīvatoyaṃ haratyāśu śoṣayanti kṣaṇe kṣaṇe  |
candrasūryakaraiḥ kṛtvā kṛtānto vāhane kṣamaḥ  || 23.13 ||

jīvārṇavaṃ samastedamākṛṣya ca punaḥ punaḥ  |
siñcayetkṣayavṛkṣāṇi jarāmṛtyuphalārthinaḥ  || 23.14 ||

evaṃ te trividhaḥ kālaḥ kathitastu suvistaraḥ  |
lakṣyate yai rutaiḥ so hi tannibodhayataḥ śṛṇu  || 23.15 ||

bahiraṅgāntaraṅgāni jñātvā vañcayate yathā  |
vañcituṃ yo na śakyeta sa yāti gurusannidhau  || 23.16 ||

svapne vā yadi pratyakṣaṃ samādhiguṇayogataḥ  |
vivarṇāṃ paśyate chāyāṃ jīvedvarṣatrayaṃ tu saḥ  || 23.17 ||

uttarābhimukho bhūtvā paśyate dakṣiṇādiśam  |
vivarṇaṃ pūrvamākhyātaṃ māsaikaṃ tridinojjhitam  || 23.18 ||

śuddhanirmalamādityaṃ viraśmiṃ yadi paśyati  |
varṣadvayena mantavyaṃ mṛtyumātmani vindati  || 23.19 ||

arundhatīṃ dhruvaṃ caiva somacchāyāṃ mahāpatham  |
yo na paśyati deveśi na jīvedvatsarātparam  || 23.20 ||

madhye chidraṃ candramasi yastu paśyati bhāmini  |
mṛtyuṃ tasya vijānīyānmāse caikādaśe tathā  || 23.21 ||

bhagnaśākhādrumaṃ paśyedgandharvanagaraṃ tathā  |
paśyetpretapiśācāṃśca daśa māsān sa jīvati  || 23.22 ||

yasya vai snātamātrasya hṛdi pādau ca śuṣyati  |
dhūmo vā mastake vāsti aṣṭamāsān sa jīvati  || 23.23 ||

agrataḥ ড়ṛṣṭhato vāpi khaṇḍaṃ yasya padaṃ bhavet |
pāṃsau vā kardame vāpi sapta māsān sa jīvati  || 23.24 ||

raktamālyānulepāni raktakṛṣṇaṃ ca vāsasam  |
labhate svapnayogena ṣaṇmāsāṃstu sa jīvati  || 23.25 ||

āruhya mastake yasya kṛkalāsaḥ sthirībhavet |
dhārayettrīṇi rūpāṇi pañcamāsān sa jīvati  || 23.26 ||

puruṣo lohadaṇḍena kṛṣṇo yasya paricchadaḥ  |
paśyate svapnayogena caturmāsān sa jīvati  || 23.27 ||

candrabimbapratīkāśamudayantaṃ divākaram  |
viraśmimaṇḍalaṃ paśyettrīṇi māsān sa jīvati  || 23.28 ||

dīpamāraktatāmrābhamākāśe ravimaṇḍalam  |
mañjiṣṭhāṃ medinīṃ paśyejjīvenmāsadvayaṃ tu saḥ  || 23.29 ||

apsu vā yadi vādarśe yadyātmānaṃ na paśyati  |
viśirāṃ paśyate chāyāṃ māsamekaṃ sa jīvati  || 23.30 ||

yadi netraṃ sravedekaṃ  kaṇṭhasthānaṃ viśuṣyati  |
vācādyaṃ kampate yasya svāṅgaṃ vahnisamaprabham  || 23.31 ||

vedanā bhavate tīvrā abdamekaṃ sa jīvati  |
lalāṭaṃ calate yasya vivarṇaṃ jāyate mukham  || 23.32 ||
dhruvasthāne tu prasvedaṃ jāyate yasya sarvadā  |
ekādaśa sa māsāni jīvatetyavicārataḥ  || 23.33 ||

hṛdaye yasya santāpaṃ svakaṃ kāyaṃ na paśyati  |
vācā ca calate yasya dantāśca pariśuṣyati  || 23.34 ||

vismṛtirnitya cittasya daśa māsān sa jīvati  |
hṛdayaṃ śuṣyate yasya svakaṃ kāryaṃ na jānati  || 23.35 ||

guhyaṃ ca śuṣyate śīghraṃ nava māsān sa jīvati  |
śuṣyate dakṣiṇāṅgaṃ tu vāmāṅgaṃ caiva śuṣyati  || 23.36 ||

ghūrmate mahatā nityamaṣṭa māsān sa jīvati  |
akasmājjāyate sthūlaḥ sthūlo'pi kṛṣatāṃ gataḥ  || 23.37 ||

dhūsaro dhūmravarṇaśca sapta māsān sa jīvati  |
pūrve tu udite sūrye chāyāṃ paśyaiva dakṣiṇām  || 23.38 ||

muhūrtaṃ jīvate so vai satyedaṃ kulanandini  |
vakranāsā bhavedyasya māsādūrdhvaṃ na jīvati  || 23.39 ||

chāgagandhaṃ bhavedgātraṃ dantāściṭiciṭāyate  |
chāyātmāṃ vikṛtāṃ paśyetsaptarātraṃ sa jīvati  || 23.40 ||

yasya kṛṣṇā bhavejjihvā padmavarṇaṃ mukhaṃ bhavet |
gaṇḍapṛṣṭhau suraktābhau trirātraṃ ca sa jīvati  || 23.41 ||

śyāmadantaṃ mukhaṃ caiva prakṛtiryasya dṛśyate  |
viparītendriyagrāmamahorātraṃ sa jīvati  || 23.42 ||

ghoṣaṃ na śṛṇute yastu dīpavartiṃ na paśyati  |
viśirāṃ paśyate chāyāṃ kṣaṇamekaṃ sa jīvati  || 23.43 ||

anyacca paramopāyaṃ śṛṇuṣva varavarṇini  |
yena vijñātamātreṇa kālaṃ jānāti tattvataḥ  || 23.44 ||

ṣoḍaśadvādaśārābhyāṃ yā gatistvarkasomayoḥ  |
tasminnirīkṣayejjyotiṃ dīpyamāne hutāśane  || 23.45 ||
ṣoḍaśāntargataṃ yacca pūrvoktaṃ yaccaturdalam  |
tasya madhye vijānīyātkālajñaḥ kālalakṣaṇam  || 23.46 ||

somādhastāddale naṣṭe ṣaṇmāsānmriyate dhruvam  |
trīṇi māsāṃstathā cordhve dvau māsau dhvanisannidhau  || 23.47 ||

māsaikaṃ vāyusāmīpye tacca pūṣodayaṃ viduḥ  |
somacakramidaṃ proktaṃ sṛṇu sūryaṃ ca sāmpratam  || 23.48 ||

yadā na dṛsyate jyotirdvādaśāre caturdale  |
pakṣaikaṃ tasya deveśi dināni daśa pañcakaiḥ  || 23.49 ||

tatraiva tena mārgeṇa yadā jyotirna dṛśyate  |
daśa pañca tathā trīṇi ekāhaṃ tasya jīvitam  || 23.50 ||

athānyatparamaṃ vakṣye niścitaṃ kālalakṣaṇam  |
jīvanti ca tadabhyāsāttadabhāvānmriyanti te  || 23.51 ||

niścitaṃ tadvarārohe kālayogaḥ sa eva hi  |
vismṛtirjāyate yasya sā vārā mṛtyukāṅkṣiṇī  || 23.52 ||

dehamadhyagataṃ sarvaṃ mriyate kālacoditam  |
parāpareṇa kālena bhedayitvā samabhyaset || 23.53 ||

vāmāvartādiyogena dakṣiṇāntamanukramāt |
śuklakṛṣṇaprayogeṇa kadahāntamapaścimam  || 23.54 ||

pūrṇamāvāsyamadhyasthaṃ kālacakraṃ samabhyaset |
pañca pañca tathā pañca pratipavchuklamāditaḥ  || 23.55 ||

svaravarṇasamāyogaṃ śuklādau kṛṣṇakāvadhim  |
pudgalātmā samāśritya abhyasedamaharahaḥ  || 23.56 ||

jarāmṛtyuvināśārthe śīghredaṃ piṇḍasādhanam  |
kathayanti mahāvidyāḥ kālasya kālalakṣaṇam  || 23.57 ||

kathamapyeṣa tanniṣṭho yadi siddhiṃ na gacchati  |
ākṛṣṭo yoginīcakre tadā vismṛtikārikā  || 23.58 ||
vismṛtirvā tithiryāti abhyasanto muhurmuhuḥ  |
sā vārā sā tithirdevi niścitedaṃ mayoditam  || 23.59 ||

etatte paramaṃ kālaṃ paramārthaṃ prakīrtitam  |
savismayakaraṃ devi abhedyaṃ samprakāśitam  || 23.60 ||

yadīcchasi ciraṃ devi jīvitaṃ paramārthataḥ  |
dehamadhyaṃ parityajya tiṣṭhasvānyatra bhāvitā  || 23.61 ||

dehāmṛtaṃ paraṃ yogaṃ na deyamaparīkṣite  |
yāvajjñānavirāgābhyāṃ pūritaṃ syāttanurna hi  || 23.62 ||

parāparasya kālasya jñātṛtvaṃ bhavate yathā  |
lekhanādiprayogeṇa vidhiyogena bhāvini  || 23.63 ||

mārgaśīrṣasya māsasya kṛṣṇāyāṃ pañcamī bhavet |
tasyāṃ sambhārasampanno rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ yajet || 23.64 ||

āharennirvraṇaṃ bhūrjaṃ rocanāsṛksakuṅkumam  |
likhetpūrvamukho bhūtvā dvādaśaiva svarān śubhān  || 23.65 ||

mātrābindususampannān rakṣayitvā punaḥ punaḥ  |
saṃvarecchuklasūtreṇa japtavidyaḥ samālabhet || 23.66 ||

sitacandananaivedyairjātīpuṣpairmanoramaiḥ  |
pūjayitvā kramāmnāyaṃ dīpamantrasusaṃyutam  || 23.67 ||

śarāvasampuṭasthaṃ tu jātīkusumamadhyataḥ  |
sthāpayitvā japenmantraṃ yāvadrātrikṣayaṃ gataḥ  || 23.68 ||

tataḥ prabhātasamaye pūjayitvā punaḥ kramam  |
kumāryo vai pratarpeta vidyā labdhā tathā śṛṇu  || 23.69 ||

hrīṃ hūṃ svleṃ svāhāpataye rakṣa rakṣāmṛtodbhave  |
svleṃ hūṃ hrīṃ ca punarjāpyaṃ sampuṭīkṛtya mantrayet || 23.70 ||

japtavidyāstu stubhyante kathayanti śubhāśubham  |
na stubhyanti yadā devyo japtavidyāsya sampuṭam  || 23.71 ||
darśayanti mahāhāniṃ bhraṣṭatvaṃ yoginīkule  |
sāmarthyato na mṛtyuḥ syādbhraṣṭasiddhiṃ na yāsyati  || 23.72 ||

evaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ paścādbhūrjapattre sthitākṣarān  |
vācayan sannirūpeta samaṃ hīnaṃ suvṛddhidam  || 23.73 ||

akṣarābhyadhike yatra tatra rājyaṃ vinirdiśet |
mātrayābhyadhike lābhaṃ same cārogyavatsalam  || 23.74 ||

binduhīnaṃ yadā paśyedhānimarthasya tatra vai  |
mātrāhīne bhavedvyādhirmṛtyuḥ syādakṣaraṃ vinā  || 23.75 ||

vāmādikramayogena lakṣayedupadeśataḥ  |
vidyākumbhaṃ savardhanyā tatkāle pūjitaṃ tu yat || 23.76 ||

tataḥ punaḥ samālabdhaṃ grāmasya ca purasya ca  |
bhrāmayetṣoḍaśavāraṃ dahyate na tadambhasā  || 23.77 ||

etatte kathitaṃ devi śubhāśubhavilakṣaṇam  |
na deyaṃ duṣṭabuddhīnāmāgamaṃ gopayetsadā  || 23.78 ||

kālāvabodhanaṃ devi pūṣākālopalakṣitam  |
samasaptagate sūrye janmarkṣe ca candramāḥ  || 23.79 ||

makarodayavelāyāṃ pūṣākālastu kubjike  |
ariṣṭadarśanaṃ nāthe japahomopaśāmyati  || 23.80 ||

mṛtyuñjayena yogena tacchṛṇuṣva parisphuṭam  |
juṃ saḥ sampuṭanāmādyaṃ saḥ jumante niyojayet || 23.81 ||

candrodayāmṛtāntasthaṃ pudgalātmā vicintayet |
japenmṛtuñjayaṃ devi parāparatanau sthitaḥ  || 23.82 ||

akṣasūtreṇa divyena netareṇa praśasyate  || 23.83 ||


śrīkubjikovāca

savismayakaraṃ vākyamatyadbhutamakāraṇam  |
akṣasūtraṃ purā jñātaṃ divyākṣaṃ vada sāmpratam  || 23.84 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi divyākṣasūtranirṇayam  |
yanna kasyacidākhyātaṃ siddhidaṃ paramaṃ padam  || 23.85 ||

yanna bhidyati cakreṇa yanna dahyati cāgninā  |
yanna protāpare sūtre paṭṭakārpāsike'pi vā  || 23.86 ||

yasya madhye sthito merurgranthayaśca na tatra vai  |
pañcāśākṣamayā tantu[r] yasmātsarvaṃ carācaram  || 23.87 ||

chinnabhinneṣu mantreṣu lubdhakruddheṣu suptake  |
japtānena tu sūtreṇa asiddhaṃ sādhayeddhruvam  || 23.88 ||

akṣasūtramidaṃ siddhaṃ sarvamārgaprabodhakam  |
sarvamārgeṣu guptedam'nuṣṭheyaṃ parameśvari  || 23.89 ||

prastutāyātamārgeṇa varṇitaṃ sūtranirṇayam  || 23.90 ||


śrīkubjikā uvāca

ḍākinī rākṣasī lāmā kākinī śākinī tathā  |
yakṣiṇī bhrāmaṇī caiva vada mantraṃ surādhipa  || 23.91 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

ū-ḍha-madhyagataṃ gṛhya ṇa-ṭa-madhyagataṃ tathā  |
va-kha-pūrvadvayoddhṛtya dha-ha-madhyagataṃ punaḥ  || 23.92 ||

ya-sa-madhyagataṃ gṛhya etatṣaṭkaṃ samuddhṛtam  |
ñapaścimaṃ samuddhṛtya dīrghasvarayutaṃ kuru  || 23.93 ||

ṣaṭprakāravidhānena ṣaṭkaṃ ṣaṭkaṃ niyojayet |
prabhurvai bhrāmaṇī proktā ṣaṭsvarādhiṣṭhitā tu sā  || 23.94 ||

sarvakārye niyoktavyā nigrahānugrahaṃ prati  |
anyadvai hṛdayaṃ vakṣye śākinīnāṃ yaśasvini  || 23.95 ||

ū-paścimaṃ samuddhṛtya ha-pūrva-m-āsane sthitam  |
rephākrāntaṃ tu kartavyaṃ dottarayutaṃ tathā  || 23.96 ||

jha-pūrveṇa samāyuktaṃ kūṭaṃ bindusamanvitam  |
prastārāyātamārgeṇa uddhṛtaṃ ṣaṭkanirṇayam  || 23.97 ||

prastutaṃ śṛṇu kalyāṇi ucyamānaṃ nigadyate  |
ariṣṭadarśanādyevamabhyasyanto'nyathā yadi  || 23.98 ||

śuṣyate ghaṇṭikāsthānaṃ tadā dhyānaṃ parityajet |
japadhyānārcanādeva sañjātopaśamaṃ na hi  || 23.99 ||

tadātra niścitaṃ jātaṃ pañcāhānmṛtyulakṣaṇam  |
niścayena tadā kāle gurudevaṃ samāśrayet || 23.100 ||

putradārādibandhūnāṃ vyāharitvā vadedidam  |
pañcāhāvāntare kāle kuryādutkrāntikāraṇam  || 23.101 ||

anyathā kurute yastu sa pāpī hyātmabhedakaḥ  |
na duḥkhito na kopena kuryādutkrāntikāraṇam  || 23.102 ||

kīrtihetoḥ śarīrasya yadi śakto na rakṣaṇe  |
guruṇāpi hi dātavyaṃ jñātvā śiṣyaṃ salakṣaṇam  || 23.103 ||

anyathā dadate yastu liṅgabhedī gurustu saḥ  |
pañcaprakārako hyātmā yena jñātaḥ svadehataḥ  || 23.104 ||

sarvatīrthamayaḥ so hi tīrthāni kṛtakānyapi  |
susiddhapumbhiḥ sarvaistu yatra baddhāspadaṃ kvacit || 23.105 ||

tatprabhāvādbhavettīrthaṃ na tīrthaṃ jalapūritam  |
jñānāvabodhasampannā jñānasampādane kṣamāḥ  || 23.106 ||
yatra tiṣṭhanti te sthāne tattīrthaṃ paramārthataḥ  |
vārāṇasī kurukṣetraṃ naimiṣaṃ bhairavaṃ tathā  || 23.107 ||

sannidhāno gururyatra sarvatīrthāni tatra vai  |
tīrthāni toyapūrṇāni devāḥ pāṣāṇamṛṇmayāḥ  || 23.108 ||

ātmavido na manyante tattīrthamitare janāḥ  |
balinopadrute sthāne gurormānamupāgate  || 23.109 ||

jñānino'pi na doṣo'sti ātmano hanane kṛte  |
tīrthaṅkaro gururyasmāttatkāryojjhitajīvite  || 23.110 ||

tiryagyoniṃ hyasau yāti duḥkhāntaṃ phalamaśnute  |
anyatkāle na kartavyamutkrāntyutkramaṇaṃ priye  || 23.111 ||

kīrtihetoḥ prakartavyā sā yathā kathyate' dhunā  |
dvāreṣvargalasaṃyogaṃ kuryāccodghāṭanaṃ kvacit || 23.112 ||

jīvādhāraṃ chinedgranthimetadutkrāntilakṣaṇam  |
gudaṃ liṅgaṃ tathā nābhirmukhaṃ nāsā śrutīkṣaṇau  || 23.113 ||

eṣu sthāne'rgalaṃ yojya kuñcikordhvaṃ niyojayet |
argalānyupadeśena śṛṇu tvaṃ karaṇaṃ yathā  || 23.114 ||

gudādhāropari sthitvā kṛtvā vai kukkuṭāsanam  |
samapādorujaṅghastu kurparau tu tadūrdhvagau  || 23.115 ||

bhagnapṛṣṭhaśiraḥskandho hyuttānordhvamukhaḥ sthitaḥ  |
muṣṭibhyāṃ pīḍayetskandhau kaṇṭhasthau cānunāsikau  || 23.116 ||

uccaretkṣurikāmūle granthicchedaṃ bhavetkṣaṇāt |
ghāṭayitvā tu dvārāṇi nityameva samabhyaset || 23.117 ||

ghaṇṭikāyāṃ tu deveśi ṣaṇmāsāvadhipūrvakam  |
nityamevābhyasantasya pratyayāni bhavanti hi  || 23.118 ||

ghaṭādhāragataṃ prāṇaṃ kūrmayantreṇa pīḍayet |
abhyasanmāsamekaṃ tu sadyamutkrāntilakṣaṇam  || 23.118ā ||
brahmarandhraṃ sphurantīva nirjīvaṃ kaṇṭhakāvadhim  |
evamabhyāsayennityaṃ yatra bandhatanu[ḥ] sthitaḥ  || 23.119 ||

gudādhāre mṛduṃ dattvā pṛṣṭhādhāraṃ suśobhanam  |
jānūrubhyāṃ tu pārśve tu kīlakau dvau nidhāpayet || 23.120 ||

tatpramāṇau samau bhūmyāṃ yantrayenmṛduyantraṇāt |
[[incorrḷoc. shouldreadbhūmau]]
evaṃ saṃśodhayitvā tu pūrvasiddhi[r] yathā yathā  || 23.121 ||

tadā saṅkurute kīrtimityājñā pārameśvarī  |
satatābhyāsayogena sadyamutkramaṇe kṣamaḥ  || 23.122 ||

kruddhaḥ saṃharate kṣipraṃ ghaṭikābhyantareṇa vai  |
tṛṇa vṛkṣalatādīnāṃ ṣaṭpadākāśagāminām  || 23.123 ||

phalapuṣpaprapātena tadā siddhiṃ vilakṣayet |
akālenāpi kālastu ṣaḍghaṭikābhyantareṇa vai  || 23.124 ||

ṣaṇmāsābhyāsayogena ātmanaḥ kurute dhruvam  |
evaṃ te kathitaṃ sarvaṃ sarahasyaṃ mahāmatam  || 23.125 ||

kṣurikādyargalābhyāsaṃ kathayāmyupadeśataḥ  |
etadvijñānasāro'yaṃ vijñānānekasaṅkulam  || 23.126 ||

anādeśānna taddeyaṃ datte syālliṅgabhedakṛt |
lubdhakruddheṣu duṣṭeṣu gopayedaṃ surakṣitam  || 23.127 ||

paraṃ cājñāpahāro'sti yasya hānirna vidyate  |
evaṃ surakṣitā devi vāritāsi punaḥ punaḥ  || 23.128 ||

anādiṣṭasya śiṣyedaṃ dāsyase narakārthinī  |
pañcātmānaṃ yadā jñātaṃ yadā jñātaṃ ṣaḍadhvaram  || 23.129 ||

tadā tvapaścimamidaṃ kathyamutkrāntikāraṇam  |
anyadvā paścimaṃ vakṣye duḥkhākrāntasya yoginaḥ  || 23.130 ||

sarvajñavihite mārge na doṣastatprasādhane  |
ātmanaśca parasyaiva kruddhaceto'vadhāraṇam  || 23.131 ||

kartavyaṃ bhīvane gatvā raktamaṇḍalakaṃ subham  |
maṇḍalānte tu ṣaṭkoṇaṃ tatra devyaḥ subhīṣaṇāḥ  || 23.132 ||

pūjayedyakṣiṇīmūlā ḍāmaryantaṃ vidurbudhāḥ  |
kusuminyā sahaikatvamātmānaṃ madhyato nyaset || 23.133 ||

māṃsakhaṇḍaiḥ prapūjyeta raktenārghaṃ pradāpayet |
bhedayitvā tu aṣṭāṅgaṃ viṣṭhamūtrasamekataḥ  || 23.134 ||

kiñcidalisamāyuktamarghapātraṃ niyojayet |
kṣmāpalenātha nāreṇa kṛṣṇavastrodbhavena ca  || 23.135 ||

pūjayedvātha naivedyairdhātuṃ dattvā svakāṃ svakām  |
raktapātraṃ pṛthakkuryānnaivedyāni pṛthakpṛthak || 23.136 ||

kapālaśakalaiḥ sarvaṃ pātrādau dhūpakāvadhim  |
sānnidhyakaraṇārthaṃ tu dhūpānyaṃ sanniyojayet || 23.137 ||

yenākṛṣṭāḥ prayāntyāśu sannidhānā bhavanti hi  |
kṣmāpalaṃ hi ca kṣmāpittaṃ narāsthi śailamadrajam  || 23.138 ||

kiñcidalisamāyuktaṃ dhūpo'yaṃ paramārthataḥ  |
evopacārayogena dhūpayitvā samuccaret || 23.139 ||

vidyāṃ svadhātusaṃyuktāṃ yasya tasya śataṃ japet || 23.140 ||

aiṃ śrīṃ hāṃ hīṃ hūṃ kusumamālinīye idaṃ pradhānadhātuṃ gṛhṇa gṛhṇa devadattasya udaragatamāṇimāri vaśaṅkari sarvaśatrūṇāṃ svāhā  || 23.140ā,1 ||
aiṃ śrīṃ yāṃ yīṃ yūṃ yakṣiṇī jambhaya jambhaya sarvaśatrūṇāṃ devadattānāmasthi bhañja bhañja āṇimāri vaśīkuru kuru svāhā  || 23.140ā,2 ||
aiṃ śrīṃ śāṃ śīṃ śūṃ śaṅkhinī śaṅkhagrahena sarvaśatrūṇāṃ devadattānāmāṇimāri vaśīkuru kuru majjaṃ gṛhṇa gṛhṇa svāhā  || 23.140ā,3 ||
aiṃ śrīṃ kāṃ kīṃ kūṃ kākinī kāyaṃ saṃhāraya saṃhāraya medaṃ sarvaśatrūṇāṃ devadattānāmāṇimāri vaśīkuru kuru svāhā  || 23.140ā,4 ||
aiṃ śrīṃ lāṃ līṃ lūṃ lākinī sarvaśatrūṇāṃ devadattānāmāṇimāri vaśīkuru kuru māṃsaṃ bhakṣaya bhakṣaya stambhaya stambhaya svāhā  || 23.140ā,5 ||
aiṃ śrīṃ rāṃ rīṃ rūṃ rākiṇī āṇimāri vaśīkuru kuru sarvaśatrūṇāṃ devadattānāṃ raktaṃ gṛhṇa gṛhṇa svāhā  || 23.140ā,6 ||
aiṃ śrīṃ ḍāṃ ḍīṃ ḍūṃ ḍākinī sarvaśatrūṇāṃ devadattānāmāṇimāri vaśīkuru kuru tvacadhātuṃ gṛhṇa gṛhṇa svāhā  || 23.140ā,7 ||

śataṃ śataṃ japitvā tu ekaikāyāḥ samarpayet |
gṛhṇantvidaṃ mayā dattamatrājñā pārameśvarī  || 23.141 ||
duḥkhito'haṃ virakto'haṃ bhraṣṭo'haṃ samayojjhitaḥ  |
gṛhṇantu devatāḥ kṣipraṃ mayā dattāṃ svakāṃ tanum  || 23.142 ||

ājñā yadi pramāṇo'sti pramāṇaṃ yadi cānvayam  |
tena satyena gṛhṇantu matpradattaṃ marīcayaḥ  || 23.143 ||

evaṃ viraktadehastu yāvatkuryāddine dine  |
tāvadāyānti yoginyaḥ saptame'hani bhāsurāḥ  || 23.144 ||

jñānasiddhiprasiddhasya saptarātrāntakāvadhim  |
kṣapayantyanyathā naiva śīghraṃ saṃhārayanti tāḥ  || 23.145 ||

atha cedduṣṭakarmāṇāṃ nigrahedaṃ prakārayet |
tadātmāṅgasamudbhūtaṃ kiñciddravyaṃ na gṛhṇayet || 23.146 ||

brahmaṇālepya-m-ātmānaṃ paścāddhyānaṃ niyojayet |
paramātmasvarūpo'haṃ bhairavo'haṃ mahāprabhuḥ  || 23.147 ||

iti matvā prayuñjīta ṣoḍhānyāsaṃ svake tanau  |
kṛtvā ṣoḍaśa vārāṇi tato vajratanurbhavet || 23.148 ||

athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi lohake sādhanaṃ yathā  |
ayutaikena siddhiḥ syālliṅge vai paścimāmukhe  || 23.149 ||

svayambhau bāṇaliṅge vā itare vāpi suvrate  |
tatra sthitvā japedevamekacittaḥ samāhitaḥ  || 23.150 ||

sadā kruddhena kartavyaṃ nigrahaṃ saptavāsaraiḥ  |
śatamaṣṭottareṇaiva yāvattannigraho bhavet || 23.151 ||

ṭha ṭha ya lā ba hā ma mi mi hi hi sa gra sa gra  || 23.151ā,1 ||
kaṃ mu a hūṃ hūṃ la jva la jva na śa nā ra gho  || 23.151ā,2 ||
pa rū ra gho ra gho a ra śva the ma pra tha ma pra  || 23.151ā,3 ||
tha ma pra śa vi ā śa vi ā da bhiṃ da bhiṃ ya  || 23.151ā,4 ||
śa dhvaṃ vi ya śa dhvaṃ vi tha ma tha ma sa gra  || 23.151ā,5 ||
sa gra kaṃ mu aka ha lo hi e hi e ya rā śva me ra  || 23.151ā,6 ||
pa ya rā pa rā pa ya vā de ya sā hā ṭṭa ṅgā  || 23.151ā,7 ||
li sphu vi ye ta pa dhi ṇā ga hā ma ya kā ha lo  || 23.151ā,8 ||
mo na ya rā śva'je ste ma na aiṃ  || 23.151ā,9 ||

atordhve yantrakarmāṇi nigrahānugrahaṃ prati  |
kṛtvā kuṇḍalikāstisro aṣṭau dvādaśa ṣoḍaśa  || 23.152 ||

ya madhye kṣe ma me da abhyantaracakre vidikṣu
hūṃkāreṇa nāma vidarbhya yacca ni rā [rā] ja sa
do [do] ru ṇa yo [yo] ni ra [ya] iti dvitīyacakre l
oṃ hrīḥ ṣṭrīḥ vikṛtānana hūṃ hūṃ phaṭphaṭ
amukaṃ māraya vidveṣaya uccāṭaya vaśīkuru ākṛṣṭiṃ
kuru śāntiṃ kuru puṣṭiṃ kuru stobhaṃ kuru stambhaṃ
kuru hūṃ hūṃ phaṭphaṭṭha ṭha tṛtīyacakre |
māraṇe hūṃ vidveṣe hraṃḥ uccāṭe hyaḥ vaśe
hsklīmākṛṣṭau hrīṃ śāntike sphauṃ pauṣṭike
sphaḥ stobhe hrūṃ mohe hlauṃ stambhe hlūṃ  || 23.152ā ||

madhye yakāralopasya lopye niṣkadvayasya ca  |
karmakarmāṇurūpeṇa śeṣā varṇā yathāsukham  || 23.153 ||

athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi svapnamānavakāmbikām  |
prāṇaṃ vahnisamārūḍhaṃ mātrādvādaśabheditam  || 23.154 ||

akṣarāntaritaṃ kṛtvā saṣaḍaṅgā bhavedume  |
hrasvā tyājyā prayatnena dīrghā grāhyā sulocane  || 23.155 ||

ayutaṃ pūrvasevāyāṃ pañcapraṇavasampuṭe  |
raktāśvamārakusumaiḥ sidhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 23.156 ||

aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā śayyārūḍho niśāsu ca  |
śubhāśubhaṃ vadedrātrau siddhavidyāṃ tu kaulikīm  || 23.157 ||

athānyamapi vakṣyāmi prayogaṃ mṛtyunāśanam  |
saṅkocya mūlacakran tu janmasthaṃ dhārayetkṣaṇāt || 23.158 ||

saṅghaṭṭe pīḍanaṃ kṛtvā lambakaṃ tu vidārayet |
lambakāmṛtasantṛpto jayenmṛtyuṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 23.159 ||

dāhaśoṣastu santāpo vaivarṇaṃ vā mahadbhutam  |
nāśayeta varārohe anenābhyāsayogataḥ  || 23.160 ||

rasanāṃ śūnyamadhyasthāṃ kṛtvā caiva nirāśrayam  |
na dantairdaśanān spṛṣṭvā oṣṭhau naiva parasparam  || 23.161 ||

tyajya sparśanameteṣāṃ jinenmṛtyuṃ na saṃśayaḥ  |
eṣa mṛtyuñjayo yogo na bhūto na bhaviṣyati  || 23.162 ||

nābhicakrādadhaścāgnirnivṛtte tu gamāgame  |
dvandvātītaṃ padaṃ devi cintātītaṃ pracakṣyate  || 23.163 ||

pṛṣṭhavaṃśādhastāttu spandane vilayaṃ gate  |
kālātītaṃ paraṃ sthānaṃ cintātītamihocyate  || 23.164 ||

gudaguhyāntare granthiḥ sīvanyā badirāsthivat |
jarātītaṃ padaṃ divyaṃ bhāvātītaṃ pracakṣyate  || 23.165 ||

gudaguhyāntare granthirguhādhāre sukhodaye  |
parānandapadaṃ divyaṃ cintātītaṃ tu kathyate  || 23.166 ||

rājadantadvayormadhye adhastātpīḍayedbhṛśam  |
ūrdhvadṛṣṭiṃ parāṃ kṛtvā evametatsamabhyaset || 23.167 ||

anena jayate mṛtyuṃ nātra kāryavicāraṇāt |
nādānte saṃsthitaṃ lakṣyaṃ pañcatattvasya madhyagam  || 23.168 ||

catuṣkalasamopetaṃ tatra sthitvā japetpriye  |
jarā mṛtyuśca rogāśca ītayo vividhāśca ye  || 23.169 ||

naśyante nātra sandehastuhinaṃ tu raveryathā  |
adhaḥ prāṇaṃ samānīya kuṇḍalīpadamadhyagam  || 23.170 ||

tatra rundhyātprayatnena vṛttirājavivarjitam  |
ye prāṇāste mahājīve gatāyurvīravandite  || 23.171 ||

tatrāsaktaḥ sadā devi mṛtyujidbhavate kṣaṇāt |
kathitaṃ sarahasyaṃ tu sugopyaṃ tu tavānaghe  || 23.172 ||

ṣoḍhānyāsavidhānaṃ tu pūrvaṃ tu kathitaṃ mayā  |
nirodhotkramaṇādīnāṃ kiṃ tvedaṃ na prakāśayet || 23.173 ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate triṣkālajñānamutkrāntisambandha [iti] trayoviṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************



śrīkubjikā uvāca

devadeva mahādeva śaśāṅkakṛtaśekhara  |
tadgrahākhye tu ye rudrāḥ sṛṣṭinyāsena me śrutāḥ  || 24.1 ||

saṃhāreṇa sureśāna kathayasva prasādataḥ  |
niścayārthaṃ mahādeva kṣakārādyā yathāsphuṭam  || 24.2 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

kathayāmi na sandehaḥ sphuṭārthaṃ tadgrahātmakam  |
pañcāśadvyutkrameṇaiva śṛṇuṣva gadato mama  || 24.3 ||

kṣa saṃvartaḥ sthitaḥ krodhe ha prāṇe lākulī sthitaḥ  |
sa bhṛguḥ śukrato nityaṃ ṣa śveto majjamāsthitaḥ  || 24.4 ||

śa bakīśo'sthimadhye tu va khaḍgīśaḥ sirānvitaḥ  |
la pinākī sthito māṃse ra bhujaṅgo'sṛgāsthitaḥ  || 24.5 ||

ya vālīśastvacāmadhye kathitaṃ tava suvrate  |
mahākālo makārastu hṛdaye sarvajantuṣu  || 24.6 ||

dviraṇḍastu bha nābhyāṃ tu ba vaṃśe chagalaṇḍinaḥ  |
pha śikhī vāmapārśve tu kathitaṃ tu varānane  || 24.7 ||

pakāro lohito rudro dakṣiṇe kukṣimāśritaḥ  |
na meṣo vāmapāde tu dha mīno jaṅghamāśritaḥ  || 24.8 ||

da dhātṛ jānumadhye tu saṃsthito varavarṇini  |
tha diṇḍī ūrudeśe tu ta āṣāḍhī tatordhvataḥ  || 24.9 ||
loṇa umākānta pāde tu dakṣiṇe ca mahābalaḥ
ḍhārdhanārī sthito devi jaṅghikāyāṃ varānane

ḍakāre dāruko rudro jānumadhye prakīrtitaḥ  |
ṭha lāṅgalyūrudeśe tu nātra kāryavicāraṇāt || 24.11 ||

someśvarasṭakāre tu jaṅghāmūle sthitastu saḥ  |
ña haste saṃsthitaḥ śarmā jhājeśo vāmabāhugaḥ  || 24.12 ||

ja caturmukha madhye tu cha ekākṣastu kakṣagaḥ  |
cakāraḥ kūrma evātra śikhare vāmake sthitaḥ  || 24.13 ||

atordhve dakṣiṇe haste ṅādikāntamataḥ śṛṇu  |
ṅakāra[ḥ] karāgre tu ekarudro vyavasthitaḥ  || 24.14 ||

gha śiveśaḥ karasyordhve ga pracaṇḍaśca kurpare  |
kha caṇḍo bāhumadhye tu ka krodhaḥ śikhare sthitaḥ  || 24.15 ||

aḥ mahāsenarudrastu visargabrahmarandhragaḥ  |
aṃ krūro madhyaghaṇṭānte au'nugrahīśordhvauṣṭhake  || 24.16 ||

o oṣṭhe sadyadevastu ai bhauktī dvija-m-ūrdhvagaḥ  |
e jhaṇṭīśo' dhapaṅktistu dvijabhūto vyavasthitaḥ  || 24.17 ||

ḹ haro vāmagaṇḍe tu ḻa sthāṇurdakṣagaṇḍagaḥ  |
ṝ tithīśa iḍāyāṃ tu ṛ bhārabhūti piṅgalā  || 24.18 ||

ū arghīśo vāmakarṇe u amarīśastu dakṣiṇe  |
ī trimūrtirvāmacakṣuṣi i sūkṣma dakṣacakṣugaḥ  || 24.19 ||

ā ananto maṇḍale vaktre a śrīkaṇṭho lalāṭagaḥ  |
tadgrahe rudra-m-ākhyātā mālinyāṃ śṛṇu sāmpratam  || 24.20 ||

vāme phetkārikā caiva dahanī dakṣapādagā  |
sāvitrī caiva gāyatrī au o jaṅghau prakīrtitau  || 24.21 ||

e ai jānū kriyā jñānī tārā takāramūrugā  |
śukradevītyanusvāraṃ śukraṃ devyāstu bhairavi  || 24.22 ||

śa guhyaṃ kusumākhyā ca mahākālī nitambagā  |
kṣa saṃhārī sthitā nābhau ṣodaraṃ lambanodarā  || 24.23 ||

ha prāṇe ambikā devī sakāre ca parāparā  |
āmoṭī tadgataṃ kṣīramā varṇaṃ parikīrtitam  || 24.24 ||

pūtanā chagalaṇḍā ca la-chau stanau prakīrtitau  |
pāvanī tu pa hṛllagnā jayantyā śūlajā smṛtā  || 24.25 ||

dīpanī śūladaṇḍā tu rephaṃ dakṣakare sthitam  |
kapālinī vāmakare ṭa varṇaḥ parikīrtitaḥ  || 24.26 ||

icchāśaktirvisargākhyā karapṛṣṭhāvubhāvapi  |
kurdanī jhaṅkarī caiva ña-jhāvaṅgulayaḥ kramāt || 24.27 ||

sampūrṇapūrṇimā caiva ṭhavarṇastalahastayoḥ  |
vināyakī ca lāmā ca ḍa-ḍhau bāhudvayaṃ priye  || 24.28 ||

vāyuvegā ca bhīṣaṇyā skandhayorubhayorapi  |
va kaṇṭhe śikhivāhinyā a vāgvāgeśvarī matā  || 24.29 ||

māyā devī i jihvā tu jihvā devyā virājate  |
khirvirā ghoraghoṣā ca śivā kālī ca kaṅkaṭā  || 24.30 ||

kavarge daśanāstīkṣṇā evaṃ devyā virājate  |
bakāraṃ vadanaṃ tasyā vajriṇī śaktiravyayā  || 24.31 ||

ī guhyaśakti nādasthā nāsāyāṃ netramadhyataḥ  |
prajñā ca mohanī caiva ū u bhūṣaṇa-m-īkṣagau  || 24.32 ||

nārāyaṇī ṇa karṇau tu vāmadakṣiṇayorubhau  |
priyadarśanā dha netrasthā ubhau netrau virājate  || 24.33 ||

cāmuṇḍā ca lalāṭasthā tha vaktraṃ grasanī smṛtā  |
ḹ ḻa ṛ ṝ tu śāntyādyāḥ śiromālā tu mālinī  || 24.34 ||
nādinī tu śikhāntasthā nakārākṣarasambhavā  |
mālinyāstadgraho hyeṣa śaktitrayamataḥ śṛṇu  || 24.35 ||

kriyā ca śukrasahitā bindvardhendusaṃyutā  |
nādaśaktiśikhākrāntā prathamaṃ bījamuttamam  || 24.36 ||

ambikā śūladaṇḍasthā guhyaśaktyā tvalaṅkṛtā  |
binduyuktaṃ dvitīyaṃ tu tṛtīyaṃ śṛṇu sāmpratam  || 24.37 ||

vāktattvaṃ kevalaṃ śuddhaṃ ghoraghoṣā tathāparā  |
dakṣajaṅghāsamāyuktā dīpanī dakṣajānugā  || 24.38 ||

ambikā dīpanīsaṃsthā guhyaśaktīcchayānvitā  |
ambikā ca parārūḍhā icchāyuktaṃ padaṃ bhavet || 24.39 ||

abhinnā pāvanī tadvacchūladaṇḍaṃ tathaiva ca  |
mahākālīsvarūpeṇa daśanaṃ tu caturthakam 1  || 24.40 ||

gāyatryā bheditaṃ kāryaṃ jñānabhinnā ca dīpanī  |
mohanyā bheditaṃ prāṇaṃ mahākālyā samāhitam  || 24.41 ||

ṣaḍakṣaraṃ dvitīyaṃ tu padaṃ devyāḥ samuddhṛtam  |
gāyatryā bheditaṃ kṛtvā ghoraghoṣā mahābalā  || 24.42 ||

dīpanīṃ kevalāṃ dadyātprajñayā śūladaṇḍakam  |
jñānabhinnaṃ tu hṛdayaṃ prāṇaṃ jīvasamanvitam  || 24.43 ||

sāvitrīsahitaṃ kāryaṃ padaṃ vidyāttṛtīyakam  |
dakṣajaṅghāsamāyuktā ghoraghoṣā kuleśvarī  || 24.44 ||

dīpanī kevalā cātra nitambaṃ mohanānvitam  |
kālikā jihvayā yuktā caturthaṃ caturakṣaram  || 24.45 ||

bhīṣaṇānāsamāyuktā mahākālī tu kevalā  |
bhīṣaṇā guhyaśaktisthā lambikā kevalāpyataḥ  || 24.46 ||

nārāyaṇī jñānaśaktyā yuktā syātpañcamaṃ padam  |
vajriṇī tu mahākālī dvirabhyāsaṃ tu kārayet || 24.47 ||
pāvanī māyayā bhinnā abhinnā śikhivāhinī  |
dvirabhyāsamidaṃ kāryaṃ prāṇamicchāsamanvitaṃ  || 24.48 ||

ṣaṣṭhamaṃ tu padaṃ devyā uddhṛtaṃ tu navākṣaram  |
ambikā jñānabhinnā vai dīpanyā ca catuṣṭayam  || 24.49 ||

bhūṣitaṃ bhūṣaṇenaiva vāmakarṇasya suvrate  |
dīpanyā kevalā caiva caturdhā tu prakalpayet || 24.50 ||

navavarṇamidaṃ devi saptamaṃ padamuddhṛtam  |
ambikā śūladaṇḍasthā guhyaśaktyā vibhūṣitā  || 24.51 ||

punareva tathāpyevaṃ śūladaṇḍāsane sthitā  |
prajñāyuktā tu kartavyā vāmapādaṃ tataḥ punaḥ  || 24.52 ||

śūladaṇḍāsanāsīnaṃ jñānadevyā hyalaṅkṛtam  |
prāṇaṃ jīvasamāyuktaṃ śūladaṇḍāsane sthitam  || 24.53 ||

sāvitryā sahitaṃ kāryaṃ bhūṣitaṃ bhūṣaṇena tu  |
vāmakarṇasya deveśi kriyādevyā tataḥ punaḥ  || 24.54 ||

binduyuktaṃ tu kartavyaṃ padaṃ devyāstu cāṣṭamam  |
kubje te prītipūrveṇa kathitaṃ tu viśeṣataḥ  || 24.55 ||

phetkārādiniyogena nādiphāntaṃ tu mālinī  |
vidyātrayaṃ tathāpyevaṃ [']ghoryāṣṭakasamanvitam  || 24.56 ||

dvādaśāṅgaṃ tu suśroṇi vidyāṅgāśca navātmakam  |
navātmāṅgasaṃyuktaṃ mālinyāṅgasamanvitam  || 24.57 ||

nigrahastu samākhyāto vilomenopadeśataḥ  |
athānyaṃ sampravakṣyāmi arcanaṃ vidhipūrvakam  || 24.58 ||

kaulikena vidhānena yathāśāstravidhānataḥ  |
susame bhūpradeśe tu gomayenopalepite  || 24.59 ||

yogapīṭhe'thavā ramye gandhadhūpasuvāsite  |
puṣpaprakarasaṅkīrṇe sugupte janavarjite  || 24.60 ||
tatrārcanaṃ samārabhya ekacitto dṛḍhavrataḥ  |
śuklavastraparīdhānaḥ suklayajñopavītinaḥ  || 24.61 ||

śucirbhūtvā susannaddhaḥ ṣoḍhānyāsena kubjike  |
sabāhyābhyantare dhyātvā ekacitto vyavasthitaḥ  || 24.62 ||

tataḥ karma samārabhya pūrvoktena vidhānavit |
kuṅkumākṣatasammiśraistrirasraṃ vartayetkramam  || 24.63 ||

raktacandanacūrṇena sindūreṇa-m-athāmbike  |
hiṅgulena tathā rakte likhyākṣaraṃ yathoditam  || 24.64 ||

nādabindusamāyuktaṃ ṣaṭprakārasamanvitam  |
sarvākārasamopetaṃ paramaṃ divyarūpiṇam  || 24.65 ||

tatra pūjā prakartavyā śāstroktena vidhānavit |
ādau pīṭhāni catvāri catvāraḥ pīṭhadevatāḥ  || 24.66 ||

yoginīpañcakaṃ caiva ḍādiyāntāḥ krameṇa tu  |
punarmahāntārikāḥ pañca jñānaṃ ṣaḍvidhamucyate  || 24.67 ||

sraṣṭāraḥ siddhasadbhāvāḥ siddhāścatvāryanukramāt |
oṃkārapīṭhamadhyasthaṃ devyāyā saha vinyaset || 24.68 ||

dakṣiṇe caiva jālākhyaṃ pūrṇapīṭhaṃ tathottare  |
kāmarūpaṃ tato'gre tu devyāsiddhasamanvitam  || 24.69 ||

ṣaṭprakāravidhānena kulāṣṭakamataḥ śṛṇu  |
prayāge tu mahākṣetre ā-kṣā-maṅgalasaṃyutā  || 24.70 ||

vairiñcī ādinā pūjyā pūrvabhāge vyavasthitā  |
vārāṇasyāṃ mahākṣetre ī-lā-carcikasaṃyutā  || 24.71 ||

māheśī kādinā pūjyā āgneyīṃ diśamāśritā  |
kolāgirye mahākṣetre ū-hā-yogīsamanvitā  || 24.72 ||

kaumārī cādinā pūjyā yāmyāyāṃ diśi saṃsthitā  |
aṭṭahāse mahākṣetre ṝ-sā-siddhiharānvitā  || 24.73 ||
ṭādinā vaiṣṇavī hyevaṃ nairṛtyakoṇamāśritā  |
jayantī ca mahākṣetre ḹ-ṣā-bhaṭṭasamanvitā  || 24.74 ||

vārāhī tādinā hyevaṃ vāruṇyāṃ diśi bhūṣitā  |
caritre ca mahākṣetre ai-śā-kilakilānvitā  || 24.75 ||

aindrī pādyena sampūjyā vāyavyakoṇake sthitā  |
ekāmrake mahākṣetre kālarātryā ca au-va-kā  || 24.76 ||

cāmuṇḍā yādinā pūjyā kauberīdiśi saṃsthitā  |
devikoṭṭe mahākṣetre aḥ-hlā-bhīṣaṇasaṃyutā  || 24.77 ||

lakṣmī śādyena sampūjyā aiśānyāṃ diśi bhūṣitā  |
kṣavarṇe kubjinīśānaṃ madhyasaṃsthaṃ prapūjayet || 24.78 ||

ḍakāre ḍākinī pūjyā rakāre rākṣasī tathā  |
lakāre lākinī'pyevaṃ kakāre kusumālikā  || 24.79 ||

śakāre śākinī viddhi yakāre yakṣiṇī matā  |
bhrāmaṇī madhyataḥ pūjyā dakṣaṣaṭkaṃ prakīrtitam  || 24.80 ||

uttaraṃ sampravakṣyāmi yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ  |
guhyākhyā ca mahākhyā ca balākhyā maṇicandrikā  || 24.81 ||

mālinī vidyayā sārdhaṃ ṣaṭkamuttarasaṃjñakam  |
ūrdhvataḥ siddhasantānaṃ mitrādau guravāvadhim  || 24.82 ||

ādhārīśastu oṃkāre kuraṅgīśastu jālake  |
cakrīśaḥ pūrṇagiryāyāṃ mathanaṃ kāmarūpake  || 24.83 ||

yoginyaśca yugāścaiva kramaśaḥ samprapūjayet |
caṇḍā ghaṇṭā mahānāsā sumukhī durmukhī balā  || 24.84 ||

revatī prathamā ghorā bhaumyā bhīmā mahābalā  |
jayā ca vijayā caiva'jitā caivāparājitā  || 24.85 ||

mahotkaṭā virūpākṣī śuṣkā cākāśamātarā  |
sehārī jātahārī ca daṃṣṭrālī śuṣkarevatī  || 24.86 ||
pipīlikā puṣpahārī aśanī śaspahārikā  |
bhadrakālī subhadrā ca bhadrabhīmā subhadrikā  || 24.87 ||

kādivarṇaiḥ prapūjyaitāḥ svaraiḥ pīṭhādhipāstathā  |
siddhakramamidaṃ devi siddhamātṭḥ prapūjayet || 24.88 ||

gopanīyaṃ prayatnena yadīcchecchirajīvitam  |
na deyaṃ duṣṭabuddhīnāmityājñā pārameśvarī  || 24.89 ||

pūjanīyaṃ prayatnena nityameva na saṃśayaḥ  |
yogācārasamo yogī mānasaṃ samprapūjayet || 24.90 ||

trikālamekakālaṃ vā ṣoḍhānyāsaprapūrvakam  |
ṣaṭprakāramidaṃ devi trisandhyaṃ samprapūjayet || 24.91 ||

svaraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhirdevyaḥ siddhāścaiva prapūjayet || 24.92 ||

pīṭhaṃ pīṭheśvarīmīśaṃ pīṭhādhipasapālakam  |
nāthadevyā samāyuktaṃ siddhadevyānvitaṃ yajet || 24.93 ||

pañcamaṃ pīṭhamadhyasthaṃ devīcatuṣṭayānvitam  |
siddhaiścaturbhiḥ saṃyuktaṃ pūjayetsamanukramāt || 24.94 ||

ṣaḍaṅgabhogasaṃsthānaṃ paṅkti ratnaṃ ca pañcakam  |
guhyaṣaṭkaṃ tathā ḍādi sthānaṣaṭkamataḥ param  || 24.95 ||

yoginīṣaṭkametaddhi pañcakaṃ ca tataḥ punaḥ  |
kṣetrāṣṭa-m-aṣṭakaṃ caiva aṣṭakaṃ ca catuṣṭayam  || 24.96 ||

ṣaṭkamanyattato bāhye pūjanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ  |
yo vetti yogyatā tasya anyathā nāmadhārakaḥ  || 24.97 ||

ṣoḍhānyāsaṃ tataḥ paścādvācanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ  |
svabhrā caiva nirabhrā ca bhūcarī khecarī tathā  || 24.98 ||

gocarī gaṇamukhyā ca yoginyaḥ ṣaṭkule sthitāḥ  |
sūkṣmā caiva susūkṣmā ca antimāmṛta-m-antimā  || 24.99 ||

kāmarūpāditaḥ kṛtvā yoginyaḥ siddhasaṃyutāḥ  |
kamalā barbarā caiva mahāntārī tṛtīyakā  || 24.100 ||

laghvinī ca caturthī syādbimbākhyā vṛddhapañcakam  |
raktākhyā ca karālākhyā caṇḍā ucchuṣmasaṃjñikā  || 24.101 ||

khaṇḍikā pañcamī jñeyā pañca devyā udāhṛtāḥ  |
mātaṅgī ca pulindā ca śabarī campakā tathā  || 24.102 ||

madhyataḥ kubjanāmā tu ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitā  |
viśuddhānāhataṃ caiva tathā ca maṇipūrakam  || 24.103 ||

svādhiṣṭhānaṃ tathādhāraṃ pañcaratnaṃ prapūjayet |
ṣoḍhānyāsasya tattvajño anyathā ca vilomakṛt || 24.104 ||

pūjyapūjakadigbhāge kramaśuddhikrameṇa tu  |
sa ca yogyo'nvayī śiṣyo anyathā nāmadhārakaḥ  || 24.105 ||

gandhaiśca vividhaiḥ puṣpairjavābandhūkapāṭalaiḥ  |
karavīrakubjakuṇḍaiśca jātīmallikacampakaiḥ  || 24.106 ||

saivalyotpalayūthībhiḥ sindūraiḥ kiṃśukaistathā  |
ebhiśca bahubhiścānyaiḥ sugandhairdhūpaguggulaiḥ  || 24.107 ||

vāmāmṛtādibhirdravyaiḥ kuṇḍagolodbhavaistathā  |
pañcāmṛtaistathā cānyairaliphalgusamanvitaiḥ  || 24.108 ||

mahāpiśitadhūpaistu nālājairdīpakaiḥ saha  |
evaṃ kuryādvidhānena arcanaṃ vidhipūrvakam  || 24.109 ||

mahāśaṅkhārghapātreṇa arghaṃ dattvā yathākramam  |
tasyāpi pūrvato devi maṇḍalānāṃ catuṣṭayam  || 24.110 ||

pītapuṣpaiḥ samabhyarcya ekaikasya krameṇa tu  |
punardakṣiṇato devi catvāraḥ kṛṣṇapuṣpakaiḥ  || 24.111 ||

uttare caiva catvāri raktapuṣpaiḥ prapūjayet |
catvāraḥ paścime devi śvetapuṣpaiḥ prapūjayet || 24.112 ||
praṇāmaḥ kriyate paścādaṣṭāṅgaṃ mānasena tu  |
stotraṃ paścātprakurvīta tacca devi vadāmyaham  || 24.113 ||

namo'stu te mahāmāye sūkṣmadehe parāpare  |
ekākini viśuddhātme nādākhye bindumālini  || 24.114 ||

adehācca samutpanne acale viśvadhāriṇi  |
mahākuṇḍalinī nitye haṃsamadhye vyavasthite  || 24.115 ||

somasūryāgnimadhyasthe vyomavyāpī parāpare  |
oṃkāravigrahāvasthe hakārārdhārdhadhāriṇi  || 24.116 ||

vālāgraśatadhāsūkṣme anante cākṣaye'vyaye  |
hakārārdhakalādhāre padmakiñjalkamāśrite  || 24.117 ||

sakalākhye mahāmāye varade lokapūjite  |
ekaikanāḍimadhyasthe marma-m-ekaikabhedini  || 24.118 ||

aṣṭatriṃśatkalā devi bhedini brahmarandhrage  |
brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca īśvaraśca sadāsivaḥ  || 24.119 ||

ete pañca mahāpretāḥ pādamūle vyavasthitāḥ  |
trailokyajananī devi namaste śaktirūpiṇī  || 24.120 ||

iḍāpiṅgalamadhyasthe mṛṇālatanturūpiṇi  |
bindumadhyagate devi kuṭile cārdhacandrike  || 24.121 ||

tuṣārakaṇikābhāse dvādaśāntāvalambini  |
umākhye hṛdgate gauri dvādaśādityavarcase  || 24.122 ||

śūnye śūnyāntarāvasthe haṃsākhye prāṇadhāriṇi  |
lambākhye parame devi dakṣiṇottaragāmini  || 24.123 ||

nāsāgre tu samuttīrṇe madhyasūtrapravāhini  |
hṛllekhe paramānande tālumūrdhni vyavasthite  || 24.124 ||

nādaghaṇṭikasaṅghṛṣṭe guṇāṣṭakasamanvite  |
sthūlasūkṣme tu saṅkṣubdhe dharmādharmapuṭadvaye  || 24.125 ||
kāryakāraṇakartṛtve triśūnye nādavigrahe  |
parāparapare śuddhe caitanye śāśvate dhruve  || 24.126 ||

sarvavarṇadharī devi guhyatattveti viśrute  |
aśarīre mahābhāge saṃsārārṇavatāriṇi  || 24.127 ||

jayā ca vijayā caiva jayantī cāparājitā-  |
tumburubījamadhyasthe namaste pāpamocani  || 24.128 ||

bandhamokṣakarī devi ṣoḍaśānte vyavasthite  |
bhrāmaṇī śaktiśūlena mahāvyūhasamanvite  || 24.129 ||

bhramaṇi bhrāmaṇī gauri māyāyantrapravāhini  |
svacchandabhairavī devi krodhonmattabhairavi  || 24.130 ||

pañcāśadvarṇarūpasthe tvayā rudrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ  |
amṛtākhye ruruścaṇḍe namaste jñānabhairavi  || 24.131 ||

daṃṣṭrotkaṭe vidyujjihve tārakākṣi bhayānake  |
namāmi devadeveśi aghore ghorarūpiṇi  || 24.132 ||

jvālāmukhī vegavatī umādevi sarasvati  |
haṃsasvarodvahe devi gomukhi śaktimālini  || 24.133 ||

kroṣṭuke subhage devi durge kātyāyanī tathā  |
nityaklinnāsamākhyāte rakte ekākṣare pare  || 24.134 ||

brāhmī māheśvarī caiva kaumārī vaiṣṇavī tathā  |
vārāhī caiva māhendrī cāmuṇḍā tvabhayānanā  || 24.135 ||

yogeśī tvaṃ hi deveśi kulāṣṭakavibhūṣite  |
aindrī caiva tu āgneyī yāmyā nairṛtyavāruṇī  || 24.136 ||

vāyavyā caiva kauberī īśānī samudāhṛtā  |
prayāgā varuṇā kollā aṭṭahāsā jayantikā  || 24.137 ||

caritrekāmrake caiva devikoṭṭaṃ tu cāṣṭadhā  |
tathā kālī umā devī devadūti namo'stu te  || 24.138 ||
bhadrakāli mahādevi carmamuṇḍe bhayāvahe  |
mahocchuṣme mahāśānte namaste śaktirūpiṇi  || 24.139 ||

bhūrbhuvaḥ sveti svāhānte dayāṃ nāthe kuruṣva me  |
jñānārthino mahāmāye etadicchāmi veditum  || 24.140 ||

yastvidaṃ paṭhate stotraṃ trisandhyaṃ caiva mānavaḥ  |
prāpnoti cintitān kāmān strīṇāṃ bhavati vallabhaḥ  || 24.141 ||

iti śivaśaktisamarasamahāmāyāstavaḥ samāptaḥ

*************************************************************************



śrīkubjikā uvāca

kulajānāṃ maheśāna pavitrārohaṇaṃ katham  |
kasmin kāle kathaṃ kāryaṃ kimarthaṃ vada me prabho  || 24.142 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

purā devāsurairdevi kṣīrodo mathito yadā  |
tatra netro mahābhāge kaśyapasya suto balī  || 24.143 ||

manthāne yojito bhadre viṣanidrāvimūrchitaḥ  |
na śaknoti talasyānte varṣāsu vasituṃ yadā  || 24.144 ||

tenāhaṃ rādhito devi pavitreṇa mahātmanā  |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu vāyubhakṣo mahābalaḥ  || 24.145 ||

tuṣṭo'haṃ tasya deveśi kiṃ kartavyaṃ puroditam  |
tato'sau daṇḍavadbhūmau mama pādāgrataḥ sthitaḥ  || 24.146 ||

prāvṛṭkāle na śaknomi talānte vasituṃ hara  |
tataḥ so'pi mayā devi karābhyāṃ gṛhya bhūtalāt || 24.147 ||

śirasā dhārito devi jaṭājūṭe varānane  |
tataḥ sarvaistu deveśi śirasā dhāritaḥ śuciḥ  || 24.148 ||

daśakoṭistu pūjānāṃ pavitrārohaṇe samā  |
vṛthā dīkṣā vṛthā jñānaṃ gurvārādhanameva ca  || 24.149 ||

harate nāgarājastu vinā devi pavitrakāt |
vṛthā pariśramastasya yo na kuryātpavitrakam  || 24.150 ||

tasmātsarvaprayatnena kartavyaṃ kulajaiḥ priye  |
āṣāḍhe śuklapakṣe tu mithunasthe divākare  || 24.151 ||

tadālābhe prakartavyaṃ karkaṭasthe divākare  |
avirodhena kartavyaṃ yāvatsyāttulapūrṇimā  || 24.152 ||

sauvarṇaṃ tu kṛtaṃ sūtraṃ sūkṣmaṃ tu triguṇīkṛtam  |
tatra tantuśataṃ proktaṃ granthipādaṃ gurormatam  || 24.153 ||

pūjyasya dvyadhikaṃ kāryaṃ pratipūjye caturādhikam  |
saptādhikaṃ śivasyoktaṃ yogeśīnāṃ ṣaḍuttaram  || 24.154 ||

vidyāpīṭhasya sarvasya kuryāttacca ṣaḍuttaram  |
pādukānāṃ prakartavyaṃ śatamaṣṭādhikaṃ priye  || 24.155 ||

aṣṭatriṃśacca granthyo vai pañcāśadvā vikalpanā  |
aṣṭādhikaṃ guroruktaṃ vaṭukasya tathā bhavet || 24.156 ||

athavā rājataṃ sūtramabhāvādvastrajaṃ bhavet |
śuklasūtraṃ samādāya triguṇaṃ triguṇīkṛtam  || 24.157 ||

tena tantuśataṃ kuryādaṣṭādhikaṃ mahātape  |
śrīkaṇṭhādi caturviṃśairajeśādyaistu ṣoḍaśa  || 24.158 ||

aṣṭābhiśca mahākālairvidyāmātā catuṣṭayam  |
ṣaṭtantu ḍādiṣaṭkasya kulāṣṭe cāṣṭatantukam  || 24.159 ||

granthayaśca yathāśobhā yathāśaktyā pavitrakam  |
gorocanā prakartavyā athavā kuṅkumena tu  || 24.160 ||
evaṃ niṣpādayitvā tu yāgaṃ kṛtvā varānane  |
dātavyaṃ bhaktiyuktena pūjānte tu pavitrakam  || 24.161 ||

gītaṃ nṛtyaṃ prakartavyaṃ sa evātra varānane  |
hindolaṃ vātha kartavyaṃ mandatārayutena tu  || 24.162 ||

prāptāḥ samayino ye tu te pūjyāstu prayatnataḥ  |
tatsarvaṃ tu prakartavyaṃ cāturmāsyaṃ varānane  || 24.163 ||

saptavāsaramevaṃ tu trīṇi vā ekameva vā  |
vīrakrīḍākṛte devi sampannaṃ bhavate priye  || 24.164 ||

tatpavitraṃ varārohe kṛtvā caiva kṣamāpayet |
bahuyajñaphalaṃ devi bahutīrthaphalaṃ tathā  || 24.165 ||

dānadharmasya deveśi kalāṃ nārghanti ṣoḍaśīm  |
pavitraṃ paramaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvadoṣavivarjitam  || 24.166 ||

tena kāryamidaṃ devi kulajaistu varānane  |
laṅghanaṃ samayānāṃ tu karma vidhivinākṛtam  || 24.167 ||

te doṣā nāśamāyānti pavitreṇa varānane  |
varṣe varṣe prakartavyaṃ yathāvibhavavistarāt || 24.168 ||

kāśaiḥ kuśaiḥ prakartavyaṃ bhaktiyuktena bhairavi  |
vittaśāṭhyaṃ na kartavyamihaiva tu kulāgame  || 24.169 ||

śāṭhyena yatkṛtaṃ karma na tatsiddhipadaṃ bhavet |
evaṃ jñātvā varārohe vittaśāṭhyaṃ na kārayet || 24.170 ||

tadgrahaśca tathā pūjā pavitrārohaṇaṃ param  |
etatsarvaṃ samākhyātaṃ kimanyatparipṛcchasi  || 24.171 ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate tadgrahapūjāpavitrārohaṇaṃ nama caturviṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ

*************************************************************************


next26 versesintroducingadhyāya 25
(maṇḍalavidhāna) onlyinEñṭ
fromappendix 4


śrīdevyuvāca

maṇḍalānāṃ vidhānaṃ tu prasādaṃ kuru bhairava  |
yena jānāmyahaṃ deva kathayasva vidhānataḥ  || 25.0*1 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

sāraṃ maṇḍalamākhyātaṃ phalaṃ sāraṃ parāparam |
lāti yasmādyamātītaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tena kīrtitam  || 25.0*2 ||

makāre mātaraḥ sarvā ḍakāre ḍāmarīgaṇam  |
lakāre lākinīvargaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tena cocyate  || 25.0*3 ||

makāre nityamātmānaṃ ḍakāre khecarīgaṇam  |
lakāre bhūcarīvargo maṇḍalaṃ tena kīrtitam  || 25.0*4 ||

makāre' nāma yo devo ḍakāre śaktiriṣyate  |
lakāre ṣaḍvidhā sṛṣṭirmaṇḍalaṃ tena kīrtitam  || 25.0*5 ||

makāraṃ śivatattvaṃ ca vidyātattvaṃ ḍakāragam  |
lakāramātmatattvaṃ tu kīrtitaṃ tena maṇḍalam  || 25.0*6 ||

makāre tu śivaṃ vidyādḍakāre parameśvarī  |
lakāre saptakoṭyastu mantrāṇāṃ parikīrtitam  || 25.0*7 ||

makāre kāraṇāḥ pañca ḍakāre tu parāparā  |
lakāre aparāḥ pañca tena maṇḍala kīrtitam  || 25.0*8 ||

brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca īśvaraśca sadāśivaḥ  |
ucyate maṇḍalenaite tena maṇḍalamucyate  || 25.0*9 ||

sarve grahāśca dikpālā nāgā vai bhairavādayaḥ  |
tiṣṭhanti maṇḍale līnāḥ sarvādhikyamato punaḥ  || 25.0*10 ||

anantādyāḥ śivāntādhvā ṣaḍvidhaḥ samprakīrtitaḥ  |
āste maṇḍalake so hi maṇḍalaṃ vyāpakaṃ tataḥ  || 25.0*11 ||

sarvavyāpitayā nānyo maṇḍalābhyadhiko yataḥ  |
tena maṇḍalamabhyarcya[ṃ] bhuktimuktiphalārthibhiḥ  || 25.0*12 ||

itīṣṭaṃ maṇḍalādhyāyaṃ nirdiṣṭaṃ kubjikāmate  |
kuryātpradakṣiṇaṃ devi apasavye visarjanam  || 25.0*13 ||

śrīmatasyāgrato devi tadevaṃ gurusannidhau  |
suprasiddhāṃ tato jyeṣṭhāṃ suparīkṣya prayatnataḥ  || 25.0*14 ||

saptāviṃśatibhirbhedaiḥ praśnametatkramāgatam  |
ṣaṭprakāratrayaścājñā yeṣāṃ tīvrāvalokanam  || 25.0*15 ||

subhaktā vatsalāḥ śāntā dambhamāyāvivarjitāḥ  |
teṣāṃ maṇḍalakaṃ kuryādanyathā tu vilomakṛt || 25.0*16 ||

tasya pūjākramaścāyaṃ jānunā bhūmisaṃsthitaḥ  |
vidhāya mārjanī śuddhe prokṣite caiva bhūtale  || 25.0*17 ||

dakṣahastatalaṃ bhrāmyaṃ maṇḍalaṃ vārinirmitam  |
supuṣpaprakarairyuktaṃ vilipya candanādibhiḥ  || 25.0*18 ||

tridhā tadyāgavidhinā dattvārghaṃ dravyasaṃyutam  |
śiṣyo'sya gandhadhūpādyaiḥ pūjāṃ kṛtvā purātmanaḥ  || 25.0*19 ||

sadvidhānasamāyuktaṃ tadabhyarcya samantataḥ  |
gurupādāmbhujau bhaktyā stutvā stotrādibhiḥ kramāt || 25.0*20 ||

paścāttriḥśuddhayā bhaktyā praṇamya tadanujñayā  |
utthāya śirasā dhāryaṃ bhuktimuktiphalārthinā  || 25.0*21 ||

gurupādasthapuṣpaṃ tu kāryaṃ śisyeṇa sādarāt |
nīrājanaṃ subhaktyātha śubhavastunivedanam  || 25.0*22 ||

dūrātkaroti paryāyātpārśvastho hi ca parvasu  |
svāminaṃ praṇamedbhaktyā ityājñā pārameśvarī  || 25.0*23 ||

puṣpāghrāya visarjyeta nītvā nāsamapuṣpatā  |
vidhānaṃ maṇḍalasyoktaṃ svāmipūjāvidhikramaḥ  || 25.0*24 ||

kramaṃ kuryādidaṃ bhaktyā yaḥ śiṣyaḥ kubjikāmate  |
svāmipādaprasādena divyājñāṃ labhate punaḥ  || 25.0*25 ||

etanmaṇḍalamākhyātaṃ yathārthaṃ tu mayā tava  |
idānīṃ brūhi deveśi kimanyaṃ kathayāmi te  || 25.0*26 ||

endof maṇḍalavidhāna insertion


*****************************************************************


śrīkubjikā uvāca

pañcātmānaḥ kathaṃ deva saṃsthitaḥ katamaḥ śivaḥ  |
kinniyogaratā deva kimpramāṇaṃ vyavasthitāḥ  || 25.1 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

atyantanipunaṃ devi pṛcchase guṇavistaram  |
savismayakaraṃ bhadre kathayāmyanupūrvaśaḥ  || 25.2 ||

paraḥ parāparaḥ siddhaḥ prasiddhaḥ pudgalātmakaḥ  |
sthānanirdeśato vakṣye śṛṇu tvaṃ varavarṇini  || 25.3 ||

śatakoṭipramāṇena paro hyātmā vyavasthitaḥ  |
ṣaṇnavatyeva koṭīnāṃ vijñeyastu parāparaḥ  || 25.4 ||

caturāśītipramāṇena siddho hyātmā vyavasthitaḥ  |
ṣaṭtriṃśatkoṭimadhyasthaḥ prasiddhaḥ kāraṇeśvaraḥ  || 25.5 ||

catvāriṃśāṣṭamānena saṃsthitaḥ pudgalātmakaḥ  |
laḍahaiva sa vijñeyaḥ parāparavibhāgaśaḥ  || 25.6 ||

prasiddhakandharārūḍhaḥ siddhasārathināhataḥ  |
sa yāti nīyate yatra parāparanirīkṣaṇāt || 25.7 ||

paramāṇusamādiṣṭaḥ karmavṛttau niyāmitaḥ  |
vāmādipathamārgeṇa prakṛtyarthabharālasaḥ  || 25.8 ||

prakṛtyākrāntaśakaṭo bhajyate mriyate gavi  |
rodate sārathistatra paśyate tu parāparaḥ  || 25.9 ||

vāmādipathamārūḍho nadyāmbhodhisaritsaraiḥ  |
yāti madhyena teṣāṃ vai pasyate varṣaṇādikam  || 25.10 ||

yānakrīḍāṃ ca paśyeta kaphākrāntabharo yadi  |
pudgalātmā vrajettatra siddhasārathineritaḥ  || 25.11 ||

pittadravyabharākrānto jyeṣṭhāpathi niyojitaḥ  |
jvālāvalīḍhamadhye tu jvalantaikapure'pi vā  || 25.12 ||

kṣutpipāsābhibhūtastu caurā gṛhṇanti tatpathe  |
kopasaṅgrāmasaṃrambhaṃ striyāliṅganacumbanam  || 25.13 ||

rājyopadravametaddhi paradārasamākulam  |
sārathistu bhavettatra vadhyate māryate tu saḥ  || 25.14 ||

parāparo rudatyāśu paraḥ paśyati tatra vai  |
yātyanekavidhopāyaiḥ jyeṣṭhāpathasamāśritaḥ  || 25.15 ||

kaphapittabharākrānto vātākrānto yathā punaḥ  |
pathi raudre niyukto'yaṃ yātyasau khecarāmukhaḥ  || 25.16 ||

svargapātālalokāntaṃ piśācabhuvanāni ca  |
vidyādharapuraṃ paśyetpuṣpitaṃ vanakānanam  || 25.17 ||

yoginīcakramelāpaṃ nṛtyagītaravākulam  |
rājyābhiṣekamāpannaṃ chattrotkṣepitacāmaram  || 25.18 ||
vātapittabharākrāntaḥ pudgalātmā pathi sthitaḥ  |
paśyate sārathiḥ sarvaṃ bhuñjate tu parāparaḥ  || 25.19 ||

atha vātabharākrānto yāti nīyati dūrataḥ  |
santrāsitastu ravinā viṣamaḥ samaparvatam  || 25.20 ||

khañjamāno'pyasau yatnādbhajyate mriyate tu saḥ  |
atha vātakaphākrāntaḥ parasārathineritaḥ  || 25.21 ||

sarpavyāghravṛkākīrṇaṃ mārgaṃ paśyati sarvathā  |
khādyate cāpyasau sarpairmriyate nīyate'pi vā  || 25.22 ||

evaṃ pudgalātmā vai prasiddhaskandhamāśritaḥ  |
siddhasārathinā yuktaḥ parāparavaśānugaḥ  || 25.23 ||

prerito'sau parātmānā krīḍate sa carācaram  |
kathitaṃ sarahasyaṃ tu parātmānirṇayaṃ sphuṭam  || 25.24 ||

yanna kasyacidākhyātaṃ bhrāntirūpaṃ jagasya ca  |
śrīmataṃ ye na vindanti bhrāntisteṣāṃ pade pade  || 25.25 ||

parākāśe paro hyātmā mantrākāśe parāparaḥ  |
śaktyākāśe susiddhastu padabhāve prasiddhadhīḥ  || 25.26 ||

bhūtākāśapathe saṃsthaḥ pudgalātmā nakiñcanaḥ  |
pañcaite śambhunādiṣṭāḥ śambhuḥ sarvatra samarasaḥ  || 25.27 ||

ṣaṣṭhanāthaḥ paraḥ sākṣātsarvajñaḥ sa parāparaḥ  |
alakṣaṇamasaṃjño'sau prasādācchambhuvastu saḥ  || 25.28 ||


śrīkubjikā uvāca

anujñāto'bhiṣiktastu vīro vīratvamicchatā  |
caredvidyāvrataṃ mantrī yathā tatkathayasva me  || 25.29 ||


śrībhairava uvāca
śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi vidyāyā vratamuttamam  |
jaṭī muṇḍī śikhī bhasmī brahmacārī tu snātakaḥ ll || 25.30 ||

vratastho'pyavratastho vā sarvāvastho'tha siddhidaḥ  |
pañcamudrādharo vāpi bhasmaniṣṭho digambaraḥ  || 25.31 ||

cīravalkaladhārī vā sarvābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ  |
malīmaso'tha śuklo vā vastrābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ  || 25.32 ||

yena yena hi veṣeṇa vartate sādhakottamaḥ  |
tattadeva vrataṃ proktamiti śāstrasya niścayaḥ  || 25.33 ||

yadyadābharaṇaṃ tasya yaṃ vā vadati vācayā  |
sā caryā kathitā tasya mantrāścaiva na saṃśayaḥ  || 25.34 ||

vidyā nāma parā śaktirdvibhirbhedairvyavasthitā  |
cicchaktirahitādhiṣṭhā avarṇā varṇagā śubhā  || 25.35 ||

vindate varṇagā yena tena vidyāvrataṃ priye  |
cīrṇacaryā jagatsarvaṃ varṇāvarṇairvyavasthitam  || 25.36 ||

vrataṃ bhāvamiti proktaṃ tena vidyāvrataṃ smṛtam  |
ciccinoti vida jñāne cicchaktipratibodhakam  || 25.37 ||

avarṇaṃ raktavatpiṇḍaṃ vidyāmantrātmavigraham  |
paśyanti ca vratāsaktāścīrṇavidyā sa ucyate  || 25.38 ||

saptakoṭisahasrāṇāṃ vidyānāmaprameyataḥ  |
cicchaktibodhanaṃ yasmādavarṇā varṇatāṃ gatā  || 25.39 ||

ekā eva parā proktā vidyārūpā tu kuṇḍalī  |
tena devi vrataṃ proktaṃ vidyāyāvaraṇaṃ śubham  || 25.40 ||

vidyāmārge caredyastu śāstradṛṣṭena karmaṇā  |
dhyānaṃ pūjā japo homaḥ samayānāṃ prapālanam  || 25.41 ||

etadvidyāvrataṃ proktaṃ nānyathā vīranāyike  |
vidyā jñeyā tu yonisthā carate dvādaśāntagam  || 25.42 ||
vratasthāneṣu sarveṣu tena vidyāvrataṃ priye  |
brahmā viṣṇustathā rudra īśvarastu sadāśivaḥ  || 25.43 ||

ete sthānā vratasyaiva yatra sā carate parā  |
taṃ jñātvā paramaṃ sthānaṃ cīrṇavidyāvrato hi saḥ  || 25.44 ||

pañcamudrā bhaveddevi pañcakāraṇakaṃ tataḥ  |
bhūṣito hṛdi tiṣṭheta pañcamudrāvyavasthitaḥ  || 25.45 ||

etaistu bhūṣito mantrī paryaṭetkṣetramāśritaḥ  |
śmaśāne kānane kūpe udyāne devakule'pi vā  || 25.46 ||

śūnye rājagṛhe mantrī parvatāgre catuṣpathe  |
tripathagrāmarathyāsu mahodadhitaṭe tathā  || 25.47 ||

nadīsaṅgamatīre vā ekavṛkṣe'tha kānane  |
ekaliṅge tathā ṣaṇḍe kṣetrairvā aṣṭabhiḥ kramāt || 25.48 ||

prayāgā varuṇā kolā bhīmanādā jayantikā  |
caritrekāmrakaṃ caiva koṭīvarṣaṃ tathāṣṭamam  || 25.49 ||

etaistu paryaṭenmantrī yoginīsiddhimicchatā  |
khaṭvāṅgadhāriṇo maunī vegātparyaṭate sadā  || 25.50 ||

ḍamaruṃ pāśakhaṭvāṅgaṃ triśūlaṃ kheṭakaṃ tathā  |
nārācā kartarī cakramaṅkuśaṃ muśalaṃ dhanuḥ  || 25.51 ||

gadā kaṭṭārikā śaktistathā daṇḍakamaṇḍalum  |
ete tu āyudhāḥ śreṣṭhāścaryākāle tu dhārayet || 25.52 ||

pañcadaivasikaṃ kāryamastrasamkhyā vratottamā  |
dvādasāhaṃ carenmantrī pakṣamāsādito'thavā  || 25.53 ||

ṣaṇmāsamathavābdaṃ ca dvirabdaṃ trīṇi-m-eva vā  |
catuḥ pañca tathā ṣaṭsu sapta aṣṭa tathāpi vā  || 25.54 ||

nava dvipañcakaṃ vātha ekādaśa-m-athāpi vā  |
dvādaśābdaṃ carenmantrī brahmaghno'pi sa sidhyati  || 25.55 ||
abda-m-ekena deveśi maṇḍalīkaiḥ prapūjyate  |
deśaṃ bhṛtyā[ḥ] puraṃ grāmaṃ samantrī sapurogamaḥ  || 25.56 ||

sāntaḥpuravaro rājā vaśyo bhavati śobhane  |
dvirabdairyakṣakanyāśca sidhyanti suranāyike  || 25.57 ||

trirabdātsaptapātālā yāstu daityāṅganāḥ śubhāḥ  |
paśyate madamattāstu madavibhrāntalocanāḥ  || 25.58 ||

mātaṅgamadagāminyo akṣayā yauvanodvahāḥ  |
kṣubhyanti sādhakendrasya prāṇānmuñcanti tatkṣaṇāt || 25.59 ||

catuḥ pañca tathā ṣaṭsu brahmalokādi sādhayet |
saptame'bde varārohe rudrāntā yā vyavasthitāḥ  || 25.60 ||

sidhyanti sādhakendrasya iti śāstre pracoditāḥ  |
aṣṭame īśvarākhyaṃ tu navame tu sadāśivam  || 25.61 ||

daśame vidyālayo bhūtvā krīḍate gagane mahān  |
daśamaikādaśe devi dvādaśairguṇasaṃyutaḥ  || 25.62 ||

aṇimādiguṇairyukto gacchate khecaraiḥ saha  |
akṣayo hyajayo yogī krīḍate sarvagaḥ śubhaḥ  || 25.63 ||

vratasthasya phalaṃ hyetatkathitaṃ tu mayā priye  |
sāmprataṃ yogamārgeṇa yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu  || 25.64 ||

śmaśānaṃ tu gṛhaṃ proktaṃ gṛho dehaḥ prakīrtitaḥ  |
aṭate tu aviśrāntaḥ śmaśānagatacetasaḥ  || 25.65 ||

kaṃ śarīramiti proktaṃ tasyānte nayate bhṛśam  |
paśyate mantrasaṃstho'pi vāṅmayaṃ sacarācaram  || 25.66 ||

kānanaṃ tena cākhyātaṃ kāyānte saṃsthitaṃ priye  |
manaḥ kūpaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ saṅkalpaṃ kurute bahūn  || 25.67 ||

tatrādhāro vrajetkṣetrī tena kūpeti viśrutaḥ  |
udyato mana nābhistho madhyataḥ sarvajantuṣu  || 25.68 ||
neti tatparamaṃ prāptamudyānastena ucyate  |
dadāti satataṃ dehe amṛtaṃ tu nabhogatam  || 25.69 ||

kulānte ca caredyena dharmādharmātmabandhanaiḥ  |
tena devi samākhyātaṃ dehī devakulaṃ sadā  || 25.70 ||

rājā cātmā samuddiṣṭaḥ ṣaṭtriṃśe'pyathavādhvani  |
śabdādiguṇabhūyiṣṭho manaḥkoṣṭhagataḥ prabhuḥ  || 25.71 ||

tena sthitena tiṣṭhanti tenaiva saha gacchati  |
unmanatve sadā yuktaḥ śūnyo mana-m-udāhṛtaḥ  || 25.72 ||

śūnyaṃ rājagṛhaṃ tena unmanatve sadā priye  |
parvataṃ guruvaktraṃ tu tasyāgramavalambayet || 25.73 ||

parvatāgraṃ smṛtaṃ tena paryāyena surārcite  |
catuṣpathaṃ bhaveddevi vāmā jyeṣṭhā ca raudrikā  || 25.74 ||

ambikāyā samāyuktamaṭanaṃ pudgalātmakam  |
catuṣkaṃ tena cākhyātaṃ pathametadudāhṛtam  || 25.75 ||

pathaṃ nāḍītrayaṃ proktamiḍādyā tu kuleśvari  |
tripathastho-r-aṭennityaṃ kurute gati-r-āgatim  || 25.76 ||

tripathasthaikabhāvastho yaḥ karoti sa sidhyati  |
grāmaṃ dehamiti proktamātmā dehamiti smṛtaḥ  || 25.77 ||

ālayaḥ sarvasattvānāṃ sukhaduḥkhaparāparaḥ  |
gacchate adha ūrdhvaṃ tu rathyādhāro jagatpatiḥ  || 25.78 ||

tena rathyā smṛtā nāḍī brahmādyā ātmanaśca tu  |
taṭaṃ tīraṃ samākhyātaṃ sindhūccāraṃ nigadyate  || 25.79 ||

duḥkhānte tu layātītaṃ taṭamudadhisaṃjñakam  |
vācānte vyāpinaṃ devaṃ śivaṃ paramakāraṇam  || 25.80 ||

tiṣṭhate satataṃ mantrī tatra caryā prakāśitā  |
nadate cāntarādhārā parā kuṇḍalinī tu yā  || 25.81 ||
sā nadī oghabhūtā tu vyomārṇe vahate sadā  |
saṅgamaṃ parayā yuktamunmanāyāḥ prakīrtitam  || 25.82 ||

saṅgamaṃ tena cākhyātaṃ nadī tu samudāhṛtā  |
tīraṃ tu samavāyinyā vibhvīyā sā parā kalā  || 25.83 ||

tadātīto bhavedvyāpī nadyāstīramudāhṛtam  |
ekavṛkṣaṃ samākhyātamekā śaktirihocyate  || 25.84 ||

vṛkṣamindriyamākhyātaṃ vṛkṣaṃ śaktiriti smṛtā  |
kṣayaṃ gatā pare vyomni amanaske nirāmaye  || 25.85 ||

tena devi mayā proktamekavṛkṣastu caryayā  |
ekameva paraṃ tattvaṃ liṅgādhāraṃ vibhuṃ priye  || 25.86 ||

paryaṭe[t] tu divā rātrau aviśrāntaḥ punaḥ punaḥ  |
ekaliṅgaṃ samākhyātaṃ ṣaṇḍaṃ tu kathayāmi te  || 25.87 ||

hṛdayaṃ tu saraḥ proktaṃ padmaṃ vai aṣṭapattrakam  |
udānena tu deveśi vikāśe tu ravistu saḥ  || 25.88 ||

sevyate pudgalālīnaṃ sarojaṃ hṛdayātmakam  |
ramate tatra haṃsākhyaḥ śaktirādyā manonmanī  || 25.89 ||

taṃ ṣaṇḍaṃ kathitaṃ śāstre kṣetrāṇi kathayāmi te  |
kṣetraṃ nāma paraṃ śāntaṃ śarīraṃ tattvasaṃyutam  || 25.90 ||

kṣetrajño aṭate nityaṃ sthānāṣṭakagatisthitaḥ  |
tenedaṃ kathitaṃ devi kṣetrāṣṭakamudāhṛtam  || 25.91 ||

ye pīṭhāste bhavetkṣetrāḥ kṣetrāḥ pīṭhā udāhṛtāḥ  |
nāmaparyāyasaṃjñā tu śāstre śāstre pṛthakpṛthak || 25.92 ||

prayāgaṃ nābhisaṃsthaṃ tu varuṇā hṛtpradeśataḥ  |
kolāgiryaṃ tu kaṇṭhasthaṃ bhīmanādaṃ ca tāluke  || 25.93 ||

bindusthāne jayantyākhyaṃ nādākhye tu caritrakam  |
ekāmraṃ śaktimadhye tu jñātavyaṃ viditātmakaiḥ  || 25.94 ||
guruvaktragataṃ proktaṃ koṭīvarṣaṃ tu cāṣṭamam  |
ete sthānā mayā proktā adhyātmaṃ pudgalāśritāḥ  || 25.95 ||

aṭate satataṃ yena hṛccakrasthaḥ sanātanaḥ  |
yāvadevaṃ na vindecca pīṭhamadhyātmikaṃ priye  || 25.96 ||

tāvattasya kutaḥ siddhiraṭato'pi jagattrayam  |
bahirantarabhāvaṃ tu antarambahiraṅgayoḥ  || 25.97 ||

lokapravṛttihetvarthe bahiḥpīṭhāḥ prakīrtitāḥ  |
antaraṅgaṃ yadā śuddhaṃ paśyate manasā priye  || 25.98 ||

tadā paśyati bāhye tu sūkṣmarūpā gabhastayaḥ  |
melakaṃ ca prayacchanti caruṃ vā pāśavīṃ vidhim  || 25.99 ||

sampradāyaṃ prayacchanti sthānaṃ vā kathayantyapi  |
aśuddhena tu bhāvena paryaṭetpṛthivīṃ yadi  || 25.100 ||

na tasya darśanaṃ devi dadate manasā kvacit |
paśyannapi ca deveśi paśyanto'pi na paśyati  || 25.101 ||

saṅkīrṇalakṣaṇā devyo miśrā jñātuṃ na śakyate  |
prabhāvo'syāḥ samuddiṣṭo vinā tāsāmanugrahāt || 25.102 ||

grāme grāme tathāraṇye nagare catvare pure  |
kheṭake caiva sandohe pīṭhakṣetre vane tathā  || 25.103 ||

udyānopavane caiva pūrvamukte tathaiva ca  |
deśe deśe'bhijāyante jñānarūpā gabhastayaḥ  || 25.104 ||

pārthivācaraṇe proktā āpe teje tathānile  |
ākāśe caiva suśroṇi tāsāṃ saṅkhyā na vidyate  || 25.105 ||

pīṭhāśrayavibhāgena utpadyante hyanekadhā  |
khānapānaratā nityaṃ krīḍante cāntyajeṣvapi  || 25.106 ||

tena devi mayā proktāḥ pīṭhā bāhyasvarūpataḥ  |
veśyāgṛhaṃ prayāgākhyaṃ varuṇā sauṇḍikī viduḥ  || 25.107 ||
kaivartikī bhavetkollā aṭṭahāsaṃ tu khaṭṭakī  |
jayantī kandukī vidyāccaritraṃ rajakīgṛham  || 25.108 ||

ekāmrakaṃ bhavecchippī koṭākhyeti ca kauṣaṭī  |
purasthitāni kṣetrāṇi jñānātmā lakṣayettu tā[ḥ]  || 25.109 ||

bāhyataḥ kathito bhedo gṛhasthaṃ śṛṇu sāmpratam  |
prayāgaṃ madhyadeśe tu varuṇā dvāramāsritā  || 25.110 ||

kolāpuraṃ tu kañjinyāṃ cullī caivāṭṭahāsakam  |
caritraṃ peṣaṇī jñeyā ekāmraṃ kaṇḍanī smṛtā  || 25.111 ||

devikoṭṭaṃ gharaṭṭaṃ ca upakṣetrāṇyataḥ sṛṇu  |
vardhamānī-m-upālambhī dehalyā muṣalaṃ tathā  || 25.112 ||

khaṭvā śūrpagharaṭṭaṃ ca vardhamānyāditaḥ kramāt |
pādenaitānna saṃspṛśya yadicchecchriyamātmanaḥ  || 25.113 ||

mārjanīśūrpavātaṃ vā dūrataḥ parivarjayet |
vātāviṣṭāḥ praviśyanti chidraṃ matvā tu sādhake  || 25.114 ||

vighnāni siddhayoginyaḥ śreyaṃ gṛhṇantyato'rthataḥ  |
