kaṇikā śiravākhyaṃ tu kālikālālayaṃ śivam  || 25.115 ||

kālañjaraṃ mahākālaṃ kṣetrasaṃsthānamāśritaḥ  |
pīṭhopapīṭhasandohaṃ purasthaṃ gṛhadehagam  || 25.116 ||

jñātavyaṃ cumbakenaiva bhuktimuktiphalapradam  |
bāhyataḥ kathitā hyevaṃ pīṭhāḥ kṣetrāstu suvrate  || 25.117 ||

paryaṭedeṣu sthāneṣu pūjanīyāḥ sadā budhaiḥ  |
bhakṣyabhojyānnapānaiśca tarpayenmantravitsadā  || 25.118 ||

eteṣāṃ saṃsthitisteṣāṃ yogināmaprameyatā  |
bhavantīha na sandeho varadāḥ sādhakasya tu  || 25.119 ||

tarpitāḥ pūjitā devyaḥ sādhakasya dadanti hi  |
ṣaṇmāsādyuktamārgasya samayavratapālake  || 25.120 ||
mantavyaṃ sādhakendreṇa toṣayitvā guruṃ priye  |
athānyaṃ sampravakṣyāmi paribhāṣāstravādinām  || 25.121 ||

adhyātmikaṃ bahiścaiva yathā jñāyanti tattvataḥ  |
tathā te kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvāyatalocane  || 25.122 ||

adhyātmaṃ kurute bāhyaṃ vratacaryā tu sādhanam  |
evaṃ kṛte bhavetsiddhiḥ satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 25.123 ||

khaṭvāṅgaṃ kathayiṣyāmi khagatīkaraṇaṃ param  |
āpādatalamūrdhnāntaṃ yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu  || 25.124 ||

śirādau sarva-m-aṅgeṣu aṅgapratyaṅgakeṣvapi  |
khaṭvāyate tu suśroṇi khaṭvāṅgī tena ucyate  || 25.125 ||

maunena vartayennityaṃ hṛdi gūḍhaṃ parāparam  |
tena maunīti vijñeyaḥ sarvabhāveṣu bhāvini  || 25.126 ||

vegena paryaṭeddehe aviśrāntaḥ punaḥ punaḥ  |
tena vegānmayākhyātamaṭanaṃ pudgalasya tu  || 25.127 ||

ḍamarukaṃ pravakṣyāmi yathā śāstre udāhṛtam  |
amā nāma parā sūkṣmā kalā cāmṛtavāhinī  || 25.128 ||

ātmā sañcarate tasmin rāvaṃ muñcanti binduke  |
visargastho mahātmāno vādayetkathayeti ca  || 25.129 ||

ḍamarukaṃ smṛtaṃ tena amanaske nirāmaye  |
nābhisthā yasya tiṣṭheta māyārūpā tu kuṇḍalī  || 25.130 ||

pāśametadvinirdiṣṭaṃ khaḍgaṃ caivādhunā śṛṇu  |
khasthā chinatti pāśāṃstu visargāstreṇa mohanī  || 25.131 ||

khaḍgavannirmalā yena khagamadhye kuleśvari  |
gatā sā brahmasāyujyaṃ ghaṭate śaktiravyayā  || 25.132 ||

tena khaḍgamiti proktamāyudhaṃ suranāyike  |
ekā eva parā śaktistripathā cakramaṇḍale  || 25.133 ||
vāmā jyeṣṭhā tathā raudrī icchājñānakriyātmikā  |
triśūlaṃ tripathaṃ khyātaṃ triśaktimanupūrvaśaḥ  || 25.134 ||

kharūpā vyomagā śāntā nirmalā aṭate priye  |
kheṭakaṃ tena nāmaṃ tu dvādaśānte vyavasthitam  || 25.135 ||

nārā ca śakti-r-uccāraṃ karaṇātmavyavasthitam  |
vedhate tu nirodhinyā kāraṇaṃ pañca eva tu  || 25.136 ||

tena nārācamākhyātaṃ paryāyeṇa varānane  |
kartarī jñānaśaktistu yena pāśāñchinattyasau  || 25.137 ||

sā kalā paramā sūkṣmā mantrāṇāṃ bodhanī parā  |
kartarī kartṛrūpeṇa jñātavyā sādhakena tu  || 25.138 ||

carate dvādaśānte tu kramāttattvāni muñcati  |
cakravedbhramate nityamaṅkuśasthā parāparā  || 25.139 ||

aṅgamaṅgagatā devī bahirantarasaṃsthitā  |
kurute satataṃ ceṣṭāmāsane śayane tathā  || 25.140 ||

dhāvanaṃ valganaṃ rodhamaṅkuśasthānamāśritaḥ  |
muṣalatve sthito nādo rekhākārordhvagaḥ priye  || 25.141 ||

taṃ bhittvā gamanaṃ cordhvaṃ muṣalākhyaṃ sadāśivam  |
dhanurlakṣye manākhyaṃ tu yena vedhayate param  || 25.142 ||

āpūrya savisargeṇa pareṇa manacakṣuṣā  |
karaṇena phaḍantena visargasthena susvane  || 25.143 ||

ṛtudvayaviśuddhena kānanāntargatena tu  |
anena karaṇāntena kaṭṭārikā parāparā  || 25.144 ||

prāpnoti tattvasāyojyaṃ gadayā ca sulocane  |
gatā hyekā parā randhraṃ dadate cāmṛtaṃ śubham  || 25.145 ||

gatāstu na nivartante ye gatā gadayā saha  |
śaktiḥ śaktisthabhāvena ātmānaṃ nayate sadā  || 25.146 ||
tadbhāvayogaviddhastu śaktinā tu surādhipe  |
daṇḍavadṛjurekhā tu nādāntapadamavyayam  || 25.147 ||

tena mārgeṇa gantavyaṃ daṇḍadhāreṇa suvrate  |
kaṃ śarīramiti khyātaṃ maṇḍalākārasaṃsthitam  || 25.148 ||

atrādhvā tu varārohe śodhanīyo manīṣibhiḥ  |
kulādyā yā parā śuddhā sarvādhvahṛdi sthitā  || 25.149 ||

tenedaṃ kathitaṃ bhadre kamaṇḍaluriti smṛtaḥ  |
ete tu āyudhāḥ sūkṣmāḥ paryāyātkathitāḥ sphuṭam  || 25.150 ||

samayinyagatā sūkṣmā kalā sūkṣmātināyikā  |
kathitāstu mayā devi parāparavibhāgaśaḥ  || 25.151 ||

bāhyataḥ kathayiṣyāmi dūtīnāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ subham  |
antaraṅge tathā hyevaṃ śrūyatāṃ teṣu niścayam  || 25.152 ||

mātā duhitā bhaginī sahajā tu tathā ntyajā  |
rajakī carmakārī ca mātaṅgī cāgrajanmikā  || 25.153 ||

annapānaṃ tathā bhakṣyamācarecchaktibhiḥ saha  |
antyajānāṃ dvijānāṃ ca ekatra carubhojanam  || 25.154 ||

kartavyaṃ sādhakenaiva yadicchetsiddhimuttamām  || 25.155 ||

śrīkubjikā uvāca

kutsitaṃ kathitaṃ deva ayuktaṃ śāstravādinām  |
paśūnāṃ yatsamākhyātamācāraṃ parameśvara  |
yatīnāṃ tu yadā so hi tadāścaryaṃ mahāprabho  || 25.156 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

sādhu devi mahāprājñe praśnametatsudurlabham  |
kathayāmi samāsena tvatprītyā suranāyike  || 25.157 ||

māteva saṃsthitā śaktirjagato yonirūpiṇī  |
atrotpannaṃ samastaṃ hi vāṅmayaṃ sacarācaram  || 25.158 ||

tena māteti vikhyātā kathitā parameśvarī  |
udbhavasthā duhitrī tu duhanāttu jagasya ca  || 25.159 ||

duhitrī tu dvitīyā tu bhaginī tvatha-m-ucyate  |
bhagarūpā parā sūkṣmā nānyena tu sunirmitā  || 25.160 ||

svatotpannā svayaṃ jātā tenoktā sahajā kalā  |
antasthā sarvabhūtānāṃ vartate cāntagā parā  || 25.161 ||

ante ca saṃsthitā hyekā antyajā parameśvarī  |
rajastamovinirmuktā mahānte rajakī ume  || 25.162 ||

carate carmagā yena svaraṅgena tu rañjitā  |
carmakārī tu sā caikā mātaṅgī ca tatocyate  || 25.163 ||

ātmasthā satataṃ nityaṃ gītasyāntapathe sthitā  |
mātaṅgī kathitā dūtī agrajanmī tathocyate  || 25.164 ||

sarveṣāṃ caiva śāstrāṇāmagrotpannā tu agraṇī  |
nānyattatra bhavetkiñcicchaktirādyā manonmanī  || 25.165 ||

agrajanmā samākhyātā parā hyamṛtavāhinī  |
paryāyātkathitā devi śaktistaddharmadharmiṇī  || 25.166 ||

ajñātvā dehajāṃ śaktiṃ bahusthānagatāṃ priye  |
ācaranti ca ye mūḍhāḥ paśavaḥ samudāhṛtāḥ  || 25.167 ||

apākteyā asambhāṣyāḥ śivavrataviḍambakāḥ  |
khānaṃ pānaṃ tathā devi kartavyaṃ na ca taiḥ saha  || 25.168 ||

evaṃ yuktaḥ sadā tiṣṭhenmadirānandacetasaḥ  |
madirā yā parā śakti rañjitaṃ tu jagattrayam  || 25.169 ||

ānandaṃ tatsamatvaṃ hi madirānandacetasaḥ  |
sidhyate nātra sandeho yathā bhairava-m-abravīt || 25.170 ||
jñānāmṛtena tṛptasya kṛtakṛtyasya yoginaḥ  |
naivāsti kiñcitkartavyamasti cenna sa tattvavit || 25.171 ||

athānyatsampravakṣyāmi avasthāṃ jñānabodhikām  |
ghoṣaṇī piṅgalā caiva vidyunmālā ca candriṇī  || 25.172 ||

mano'nugā ca sukṛtā saumyā caiva nirañjanā  |
nirālambā tathā devī anyā caiva mahābalā  || 25.173 ||

helā lolā tathā līlā bodhābodhavatīti ca  |
nirāmayāḥ samākhyātā etāḥ pratyakṣamātarāḥ  || 25.174 ||

ājñāsiddhipradātārā ājñāsiddhikulānvaye  |
ghoṣaṇī ghoṣamārgasthā śikhā dhūmrā ca piṅgalā  || 25.175 ||

rātrau dyotayate śuklaṃ vidyunmāleti cocyate  |
candriṇī candragarbheṇa sandhyābindu mano'nugā  || 25.176 ||

nimīlitākṣe yatpītaṃ sukṛtā samudāhṛtā  |
kaṃsadhvanistathā saumyā ghaṇṭā caiva nirañjanā  || 25.177 ||

haṃsākhyā tu nirālambā kiṅkiṇī tu mahābalā  |
gudadeśe prajāyeta sadā siddhipradāyikā  || 25.178 ||

ghoṣamārge tu yo haṃso helā nāmeti cocyate  |
tasya madhye tu yaḥ śabdo lolākhyā sā prakīrtitā  || 25.179 ||

līlā caivāṇavā proktā khecaratvapradāyinī  |
cītkṛtaṃ karṇadeśe tu bodhābodhavatī tu sā  || 25.180 ||

ātmānaṃ haṃsamityāhurmāyārūpā tu bodhanī  |
kuṇḍalī tu samākhyātā rudraśaktistu bindukam  || 25.181 ||

gagane dṛśyate yastu prabhākāreṇa suprabhaḥ  |
akhaṇḍamaṇḍalākāraṃ dyotayantaṃ nabhastalam  || 25.182 ||

acalaṃ tatsamākhyātamacalatvena saṃsthitam  |
etāvasthāḥ samākhyātā udayanti krameṇa tu  || 25.183 ||
ājñātatparabhāvajñaḥ sugupto gurupūjakaḥ  |
tasya siddhirbhavatyāśu nānyathā kubjike vacaḥ  || 25.184 ||

pitu[ḥ] prāptaṃ yathā saukhyaṃ tatsukhaṃ bhuñjate svayam  |
mṛtyuryena sukheneha tatsukhaṃ dhyānamucyate  || 25.185 ||


śrībhairava uvāca

samastedaṃ varārohe durlabhaṃ prakaṭīkṛtam  |
tvaṃ punaścāvatāritvā kramaughaṃ samprakāśaya  || 25.186 ||

sugopyaṃ gopanībhūtvā kramaṃ pūjyārihā bhava  || 25.187 ||

yā sā kubji parā mahaughajananī sañcodito'haṃ tvayā tvaṃ kubjā parakubjinī mama punastvāhaṃ mayā tvaṃ punaḥ  |
tvayādiṣṭacatuṣṭayaṃ kramapathaṃ teṣāṃ kramo vai yathā sampreṣyātmagataṃ kramaughaparamaṃ cājñā gṛhītānaghe  || 25.188 ||

śatakoṭisuvistīrṇaṃ tantredaṃ pārameśvaram  |
asya bhedopabhedāśca bhaviṣyanti hyanekadhā  || 25.189 ||

atra kalpe varārohe sūtrasaṅgrahalakṣaṇam  |
caturviṃśatisāhasraṃ kiṃ tu tatprakaṭaṃ na hi  || 25.190 ||

kartavyaṃ tu tvayā bhadre kiṃ tu pīṭhacatuṣṭaye  |
prakāśayasva cājñāto yathā gopyataraṃ bhavet || 25.191 ||

sudurlabhataraṃ devi tantredaṃ paramādbhutam  |
yatra vā tiṣṭhate deśe sa deśo bhuktibhāgbhavet || 25.192 ||

kiṃ punaḥ puramadhyasthaṃ gṛhāvasthagataṃ hṛdi  |
tiṣṭhate yasya'sau nāthe puraṃ pīṭhasamaṃ bhavet || 25.193 ||

gṛhaṃ tadyogapīṭhaṃ ca mantavyamanvayānvitaiḥ  |
āgamaṃ maṇḍalādyaistu pūjyoghaṃ pārameśvaram  || 25.194 ||

yatredaṃ tiṣṭhate sthāne divyāmnāyaṃ sudurlabham  |
divyaistu pūjyate so hi yadi gopyataraṃ bhavet || 25.195 ||

vidhānavihitā pūjā yadyaṣṭamyāṃ caturdaśī  |
pūjayetparamāmnāyaṃ pūjyate sa marīcibhiḥ  || 25.196 ||

yastvevaṃ vindate devi granthataścārthato'pi vā  |
sa jyeṣṭhaḥ kulasantāne pūjyo'sau bhairavo yathā  || 25.197 ||

avajñāṃ kurute yastu yasya tasya prakāśayet |
sāmarthyena satāṃ dviṣṭo bhraṣṭo duḥkhī sa sarvataḥ  || 25.198 ||

āgamādhārabhāṇḍasya dṛṣṭvāvajñāṃ karoti yaḥ  |
namaskāreṇa tatpīṭhaṃ tasyaivāyaṃ puroditam  || 25.199 ||

śrutvaivaṃ vismayāpannā ūcustvevaṃ kuleśvarī  |
prerayitvāditoddiṣṭā atrājñānavirodhinī  || 25.200 ||

ājñāśrutaṃ samastedaṃ dṛṣṭamasmādvirjṛmbhitam  |
vrajatoḍādisiddhānāṃ teṣu sarvaṃ samarpayet || 25.201 ||

samayinyaśca saṃyojya tasya tvāmoghaśālinī  |
bhaviṣyasi purāvasthā tadavasthāntare sthitā  || 25.202 ||

prathamaṃ madguṇairbhadre dvitīyaṃ ca khagāntare  |
tṛtīyaṃ bhūcarīnāthaḥ paścānantabhavātmikā  || 25.203 ||

trayānte gurupaṅktisthā ড়ṛthakpūjākrame sthitā  |
bhaviṣyasi purāvasthā hrāsyamānā pade pade  || 25.204 ||

jīvikopāyahetvarthamutkarṣārthamathāpi vā  |
teṣu tyajya parāmnāyaṃ bhūtāveśakarī bhava  || 25.205 ||

vyatikramaṃ yadā kāle bhaviṣyatkubjinīmate  |
tadā kāle tu taṃ hatvā samāpyevaṃ punarbhaja  || 25.206 ||

sārasaṅgrahametaddhi anāmāmatamuttamam  |
asyoccāraṃ na kartavyamuccārādayutaṃ japet || 25.207 ||

kubjikā yā varārohe pañcabhiḥ praṇavaiḥ saha  |
tairvinā na hi coccāraṃ kubjāmnāyamahādhvare  || 25.208 ||
yaistu tāni varārohe kathayāmi svarūpataḥ  |
binduyuktāni sarvāṇi jīvabhūtāni śāsane  || 25.209 ||

ādimaṃ ca tṛtīyaṃ ca daśamaṃ caikaviṃśakam  |
dvitīyaṃ caikaviṃśena varjitaṃ pañcamaṃ tu tat || 25.210 ||

kiṃ tu vāmena jaṅghāyā hataṃ bījena kārayet |
tadbījaṃ paramuddiṣṭaṃ sarvajñānāvatārakam  || 25.211 ||

ambikā bindunādaṃ ca kuṇḍalī ca paraḥ śivaḥ  |
ratnānāṃ pañcakaṃ devi vyāpayitvā sudurlabham  || 25.212 ||

anenābhyāsayogena khecarīkulanandanaḥ  |
sidhyate nātra sandeho guruvaktraprasādataḥ  || 25.213 ||

khecarā bhūcarā caiva dikcarā gocarā tathā  |
dadanti melakaṃ sarvaṃ yasyedaṃ hṛdi saṃsthitam  || 25.214 ||

devadevena devyāyā mataṃ divyaṃ mayā tava  |
kartavyaṃ tu tathā gopyamityājñā pārameśvarī  || 25.215 ||

pūjā cāsya prakartavyā viśeṣeṇa varānane  |
śuklapakṣe tṛtīyāyāṃ vaiśākhasya tathā punaḥ  || 25.216 ||

kṛṣṇapakṣe trayodaśyāṃ nabhasyanavamī punaḥ  |
āśvine śuklapakṣasya pūrṇimā phālgune matā  || 25.217 ||

āṣāḍhe śrāvaṇe caiva bhādrapadyāṃ tathaiva ca  |
śuklapakṣe caturdaśyāṃ kartavyaṃ ca pavitrakam  || 25.218 ||

ātmavittānusāreṇa uttamādhamamadhyamāḥ  |
guruparvamiti khyātaṃ pālanīyaṃ kulāmbike  || 25.219 ||

yugādayaḥ samākhyātā atra pīṭhāvatāraṇam  |
pīṭhamārgakramāyātamāgamo'yaṃ tadeva hi  || 25.220 ||

āgame pūjite sarvaṃ pūjitaṃ jñānasāgaram  |
yenedaṃ pustakaṃ devyāḥ pūrvoktaṃ yanmayā tava  || 25.221 ||
anyattatparamopāyaṃ siddhiparyāyaśāsane  |
divyasiddhipradātāraṃ divyabhāṣāvibhūṣitam  || 25.222 ||

kusumaṃ ca rajaṃ raktaṃ rathyaṃ śivakusumbhakam  |
taḍidamṛtamadhuraṃ kṣatajodbhavanetrajam  || 25.223 ||

kādambarī prasannā ca parisruṅmadirā surā  |
vāmāmṛtamaliścaiva somapānaṃ madālasī  || 25.224 ||

dhārāmṛtaṃ śivāmbuṃ ca rativiṣṇuvaruṇodbhavam  |
varco brahmā dvijanmā ca sarojaḥ kamalāsanaḥ  || 25.225 ||

bukapuṣpakaṇākhyaṃ ca liṅgapaṅkamalaṃ tathā  |
kuṇḍagolodbhavaṃ śukraṃ śaśiścaiva sitaṃ madhu  || 25.226 ||

kaṭaṃ māṃsaṃ palaṃ kravyaṃ piṣitaṃ phalguṣāmiṣam  |
jāṅgalaṃ devadāruṃ ca kṣmā vai khaḍgodbhavaṃ smṛtam  || 25.227 ||

tailaṃ vasā tathā snehaṃ kaṭutailaṃ tu tīkṣṇakam  |
turuṣkaṃ sihṇakaṃ proktaṃ kapālapuṭamadhyagam  || 25.228 ||

laśunaṃ nāsikāvasthaṃ tacca hiṅgu prakīrtitam  |
gajaṃ caiva tu kuṣmāṇḍaṃ palāṇḍuṃ ca viśeṣataḥ  || 25.229 ||

paryuṣitācchālyagaruṃ pippalyaḥ kṛṣṇataṇḍulāḥ  |
kṛṣṇacchāgo mahānetrī palalaṃ meṣātmakaṃ smṛtam  || 25.230 ||

sāmarthajñavidānāṃ ca iti pūjā prakīrtitā  |
siddhadravyaṃ samākhyātaṃ prasaṅgādyoginīkule  || 25.231 ||

nānena rahitā siddhirbhuktimuktirna vidyate  |
nirācārapadaṃ hyetattenedaṃ paramaṃ smṛtam  || 25.232 ||

iti kulālikāmnāye śrīkubjikāmate samastajñānāvabodhacaryānirdeśo nāma pañcaviṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ

iti caturviṃśatisāhasre sārātsārataraṃ śrīkubjikāmnāyaṃ śrīoḍiyānapīṭhavinirgataṃ pañcaviṃśatipaṭalaṃ sārdhatrīṇi sahasrāṇi śrīmataṃ parisamāptam







